' ============================================
' ПОЛУЧЕНИЕ ИМЕНИ ЧЕРТЕЖНОГО ВИДА
' ============================================
Private Function GetViewNameForAnnotation(swAnn As Object, sheetName As String) As String
    Dim viewName As String
    viewName = ""
    On Error Resume Next
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then
        GetViewNameForAnnotation = ""
        Exit Function
    End If
    Dim targetPos As Variant
    targetPos = swAnn.GetPosition()
    If IsEmpty(targetPos) Then
        GetViewNameForAnnotation = ""
        Exit Function
    End If
    Dim targetX As Double, targetY As Double
    targetX = targetPos(0)
    targetY = targetPos(1)
    Dim curSheet As String
    curSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    If curSheet <> sheetName Then swDraw.ActivateSheet sheetName
    Dim swSheet As Object
    Set swSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    If swSheet Is Nothing Then
        GetViewNameForAnnotation = ""
        If curSheet <> sheetName Then swDraw.ActivateSheet curSheet
        Exit Function
    End If
    ' v15.14: позиции всех аннотаций листа читаются один раз (кэш на лист),
    ' порядок обхода и правило выбора вида прежние.
    If gKC_VCSheet <> sheetName Then
        KC_ViewCacheBuild swSheet
        gKC_VCSheet = sheetName
    End If
    Dim i As Long, dist As Double, bestDist As Double, bestViewName As String
    bestDist = 1E+30
    bestViewName = ""
    For i = 1 To gKC_VCCount
        dist = Sqr((targetX - gKC_VCX(i)) ^ 2 + (targetY - gKC_VCY(i)) ^ 2)
        If dist < 0.00005 Then
            viewName = gKC_VCName(i)
            GoTo FoundView
        End If
        If dist < bestDist Then
            bestDist = dist
            bestViewName = gKC_VCName(i)
        End If
    Next i
    If bestDist < 0.05 And Len(bestViewName) > 0 Then
        viewName = bestViewName
    End If
FoundView:
    On Error GoTo 0
    If curSheet <> sheetName Then swDraw.ActivateSheet curSheet
    GetViewNameForAnnotation = viewName
End Function

Private Sub KC_ViewCacheReset()
    gKC_VCSheet = ""
    gKC_VCCount = 0
End Sub

Private Sub KC_ViewCacheAdd(ByVal vName As String, ByVal pos As Variant)
    If Not IsArray(pos) Then Exit Sub
    If UBound(pos) < 1 Then Exit Sub
    gKC_VCCount = gKC_VCCount + 1
    If gKC_VCCount > UBound(gKC_VCName) Then
        ReDim Preserve gKC_VCName(1 To gKC_VCCount + 256)
        ReDim Preserve gKC_VCX(1 To gKC_VCCount + 256)
        ReDim Preserve gKC_VCY(1 To gKC_VCCount + 256)
    End If
    gKC_VCName(gKC_VCCount) = vName
    gKC_VCX(gKC_VCCount) = CDbl(pos(0))
    gKC_VCY(gKC_VCCount) = CDbl(pos(1))
End Sub

' Тот же порядок, что и раньше: виды листа -> аннотации вида -> размеры вида.
Private Sub KC_ViewCacheBuild(ByVal swSheet As Object)
    Dim views As Variant, annots As Variant, i As Long, j As Long
    Dim swView As Object, vName As String, testAnn As Object, testPos As Variant
    Dim dd As Object, nextDD As Object, dimAnn As Object
    gKC_VCCount = 0
    ReDim gKC_VCName(1 To 256): ReDim gKC_VCX(1 To 256): ReDim gKC_VCY(1 To 256)
    On Error Resume Next
    views = swSheet.GetViews
    If IsEmpty(views) Or Not IsArray(views) Then Exit Sub
    For i = 0 To UBound(views)
        Set swView = Nothing
        Set swView = views(i)
        If Not swView Is Nothing Then
            vName = ""
            vName = swView.GetName2
            annots = Empty
            annots = swView.GetAnnotations
            If IsArray(annots) Then
                For j = 0 To UBound(annots)
                    Set testAnn = Nothing
                    Set testAnn = annots(j)
                    If Not testAnn Is Nothing Then
                        testPos = Empty
                        testPos = testAnn.GetPosition()
                        KC_ViewCacheAdd vName, testPos
                    End If
                Next j
            End If
            Set dd = Nothing
            Set dd = swView.GetFirstDisplayDimension5
            Do While Not dd Is Nothing
                Set dimAnn = Nothing
                Set dimAnn = dd.GetAnnotation
                If Not dimAnn Is Nothing Then
                    testPos = Empty
                    testPos = dimAnn.GetPosition()
                    KC_ViewCacheAdd vName, testPos
                End If
                Set nextDD = Nothing
                Set nextDD = dd.GetNext5
                Set dd = nextDD
            Loop
        End If
    Next i
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

' Ключ COM-объекта по указателю IUnknown: то же, что сравнение "Is", но за O(1).
Private Function KC_ObjKey(ByVal obj As Object) As String
    Dim unk As IUnknown
    Set unk = obj
    KC_ObjKey = CStr(ObjPtr(unk))
End Function

' Отключение перерисовки окна и дерева на время массовых API-вызовов.
Private Sub KC_GraphicsOff()
    If gKC_GfxOff Then Exit Sub
    On Error Resume Next
    Set gKC_GfxView = Nothing
    Set gKC_GfxView = swModel.ActiveView
    If Not gKC_GfxView Is Nothing Then gKC_GfxView.EnableGraphicsUpdate = False
    swModel.FeatureManager.EnableFeatureTree = False
    gKC_GfxOff = True
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Sub KC_GraphicsOn()
    If Not gKC_GfxOff Then Exit Sub
    On Error Resume Next
    gKC_GfxOff = False
    swModel.FeatureManager.EnableFeatureTree = True
    If Not gKC_GfxView Is Nothing Then gKC_GfxView.EnableGraphicsUpdate = True
    Set gKC_GfxView = Nothing
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

' ============================================
' УДАЛЕНИЕ МАСШТАБА ИЗ ИМЕНИ ВИДА И СЛОВА МЕСТНЫЙ
' ============================================
Private Function RemoveViewScale(viewName As String) As String
    Dim result As String
    result = viewName
    If Len(result) = 0 Then
        RemoveViewScale = ""
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    If Left(result, 8) = "Местный " Then
        result = Mid(result, 9)
    End If
    
    If Len(result) > 0 Then
        result = UCase(Left(result, 1)) & Mid(result, 2)
    End If
    
    Dim openPos As Long
    openPos = InStrRev(result, "(")
    If openPos > 0 Then
        Dim closePos As Long
        closePos = InStr(openPos, result, ")")
        If closePos > 0 Then
            Dim bracketContent As String
            bracketContent = Trim(Mid(result, openPos + 1, closePos - openPos - 1))
            If IsScalePattern(bracketContent) Then
                Dim afterBracket As String
                afterBracket = ""
                If closePos < Len(result) Then
                    afterBracket = Mid(result, closePos + 1)
                End If
                result = Trim(Left(result, openPos - 1) & afterBracket)
            End If
        End If
    End If
    
    RemoveViewScale = result
End Function

Private Function IsScalePattern(content As String) As Boolean
    IsScalePattern = False
    Dim s As String
    s = Trim(content)
    If Len(s) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim colonPos As Long
    colonPos = InStr(1, s, ":")
    If colonPos = 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim leftPart As String
    leftPart = Trim(Left(s, colonPos - 1))
    If Len(leftPart) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim rightPart As String
    rightPart = Trim(Mid(s, colonPos + 1))
    If Len(rightPart) = 0 Then Exit Function
    leftPart = Replace(leftPart, ",", ".")
    rightPart = Replace(rightPart, ",", ".")
    If IsNumericStr(leftPart) And IsNumericStr(rightPart) Then
        IsScalePattern = True
    End If
End Function

' ============================================
' ДИАЛОГ ВЫБОРА ТИПОВ
' ============================================
Private Function SelectAnnotationTypes() As Boolean
    Dim userChoice As String
    Dim msgText As String
    msgText = "Выберите типы аннотаций:" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        " 1 = Только РАЗМЕРЫ (14)" & vbCrLf & _
        " 2 = Только ГЕОМ. ДОПУСКИ (13)" & vbCrLf & _
        " 3 = Только СВАРКА (38)" & vbCrLf & _
        " 4 = Размеры + Геом. допуски" & vbCrLf & _
        " 5 = Размеры + Сварка" & vbCrLf & _
        " 6 = Геом. допуски + Сварка" & vbCrLf & _
        " 7 = ВСЕ ТИПЫ" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Заметки с """ & NOTE_MARK & """ учитываются всегда."
    userChoice = InputBox(msgText, "Выбор типов", "7")
    If userChoice = "" Then
        SelectAnnotationTypes = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    processDimensions = False
    processGtols = False
    processWelds = False
    processNotes = True
    Select Case Trim(userChoice)
        Case "1": processDimensions = True
        Case "2": processGtols = True
        Case "3": processWelds = True
        Case "4": processDimensions = True: processGtols = True
        Case "5": processDimensions = True: processWelds = True
        Case "6": processGtols = True: processWelds = True
        Case "7": processDimensions = True: processGtols = True: processWelds = True
        Case Else: ParseCustomTypes userChoice
    End Select
    If Not processDimensions And Not processGtols And Not processWelds Then
        MsgBox "Не выбран ни один тип!", vbExclamation
        SelectAnnotationTypes = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    SelectAnnotationTypes = True
End Function

Private Sub ParseCustomTypes(userInput As String)
    Dim parts As Variant, i As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    userInput = Replace(Replace(userInput, " ", ","), ";", ",")
    parts = Split(userInput, ",")
    For i = LBound(parts) To UBound(parts)
        If IsNumeric(Trim(parts(i))) Then
            Select Case CLng(Trim(parts(i)))
                Case 14: processDimensions = True
                Case 13: processGtols = True
                Case 38: processWelds = True
            End Select
        End If
    Next i
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

' ============================================
' РАБОЧАЯ ОБЛАСТЬ
' ============================================
Private Sub InitWorkArea(Optional sheetName As String = "")
    Dim swSheet As Object, sheetProps As Variant
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(sheetName) > 0 Then swDraw.ActivateSheet sheetName
    Set swSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    If swSheet Is Nothing Then
        workArea.IsValid = False
        Exit Sub
    End If
    sheetProps = swSheet.GetProperties()
    workArea.minX = FRAME_LEFT
    workArea.maxX = sheetProps(5) - FRAME_RIGHT
    workArea.minY = FRAME_BOTTOM
    workArea.maxY = sheetProps(6) - FRAME_TOP
    workArea.IsValid = True
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function IsInWorkArea(posX As Variant, posY As Variant) As Boolean
    Dim dx As Double, dy As Double
    On Error Resume Next
    dx = CDbl(posX)
    dy = CDbl(posY)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Not workArea.IsValid Then
        IsInWorkArea = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    IsInWorkArea = (dx >= workArea.minX And dx <= workArea.maxX And _
                    dy >= workArea.minY And dy <= workArea.maxY)
End Function

Private Sub ClampToWorkArea(ByRef posX As Double, ByRef posY As Double)
    If Not workArea.IsValid Then Exit Sub
    If posX + BALLOON_OFFSET_X < workArea.minX Then posX = workArea.minX
    If posX + BALLOON_OFFSET_X > workArea.maxX Then posX = workArea.maxX - BALLOON_OFFSET_X - 0.005
    If posY + BALLOON_OFFSET_Y < workArea.minY Then posY = workArea.minY
    If posY + BALLOON_OFFSET_Y > workArea.maxY Then posY = workArea.maxY - BALLOON_OFFSET_Y - 0.005
End Sub

' ============================================
' GDT ФУНКЦИИ
' ============================================
Private Function GetGDTSymbolFromText(fullText As String) As String
    Dim upper As String
    upper = UCase(fullText)
    If InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-PARA") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Параллельность"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-PERP") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Перпендикулярность"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-CONC") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Соосность"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-FLAT") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Плоскостность"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-SYMMETRY") > 0 Or InStr(1, upper, "GGTOL-SYMMETRY") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Симметричность"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-ANGULAR") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Угловатость"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-CYLR") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Цилиндричность"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-CIRC") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Круглость"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-STRAIGHT") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Прямолинейность"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-SURFPRO") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Профиль поверхности"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-LINEPRO") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Профиль линии"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-POSN") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Позиционный"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-ROUT") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Биение"
    ElseIf InStr(1, upper, "GTOL-TROUT") > 0 Then
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = "Полное биение"
    Else
        GetGDTSymbolFromText = ""
    End If
End Function

Private Function RemoveGtolTags(inputText As String) As String
    Dim result As String
    result = inputText
    Dim tags As Variant
    tags = Array("<GTOL-PARA>", "<GTOL-PERP>", "<GTOL-CONC>", "<GTOL-FLAT>", _
        "<GTOL-SYMMETRY>", "<GGTOL-SYMMETRY>", "<GTOL-ANGULAR>", _
        "<GTOL-CYLR>", "<GTOL-CIRC>", "<GTOL-STRAIGHT>", _
        "<GTOL-SURFPRO>", "<GTOL-LINEPRO>", "<GTOL-POSN>", _
        "<GTOL-ROUT>", "<GTOL-TROUT>", _
        "<MOD-DIAM>", "<MOD-SDIA>", "<MOD-MMC>", "<MOD-LMC>", _
        "<MOD-RFS>", "<MOD-PROJ>", "<MOD-FREE>", "<MOD-STAT>", _
        "<MOD-TANG>", "<MOD-UNREQ>")
    Dim t As Long
    For t = 0 To UBound(tags)
        result = Replace(result, CStr(tags(t)), "", , , vbTextCompare)
    Next t
    Dim startPos As Long, endPos As Long
    Do
        startPos = InStr(1, result, "<GTOL-")
        If startPos = 0 Then startPos = InStr(1, result, "<GGTOL-")
        If startPos = 0 Then startPos = InStr(1, result, "<MOD-")
        If startPos = 0 Then Exit Do
        endPos = InStr(startPos, result, ">")
        If endPos > 0 Then
            result = Left(result, startPos - 1) & Mid(result, endPos + 1)
        Else
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop
    Do While InStr(1, result, "  ") > 0
        result = Replace(result, "  ", " ")
    Loop
    RemoveGtolTags = Trim(result)
End Function

Private Function GetGDTSymbolName(symType As Long) As String
    Select Case symType
        Case 0: GetGDTSymbolName = "Плоскостность"
        Case 1: GetGDTSymbolName = "Параллельность"
        Case 2: GetGDTSymbolName = "Перпендикулярность"
        Case 3: GetGDTSymbolName = "Наклон"
        Case 4: GetGDTSymbolName = "Цилиндричность"
        Case 5: GetGDTSymbolName = "Круглость"
        Case 6: GetGDTSymbolName = "Прямолинейность"
        Case 7: GetGDTSymbolName = "Профиль поверхности"
        Case 8: GetGDTSymbolName = "Профиль линии"
        Case 9: GetGDTSymbolName = "Позиционный"
        Case 10: GetGDTSymbolName = "Соосность"
        Case 11: GetGDTSymbolName = "Симметричность"
        Case 12: GetGDTSymbolName = "Биение"
        Case 13: GetGDTSymbolName = "Полное биение"
        Case Else: GetGDTSymbolName = "GDT(" & symType & ")"
    End Select
End Function

Private Function AutoSelectTool(pd As PositionData) As String
    Dim mainTool As String, extraTool As String
    KC28_SelectTools pd, mainTool, extraTool
    AutoSelectTool = mainTool
End Function

' Выбор шаблона сварщика по величине катета (таблица СИ для тавровых/нахлёсточных швов по ГОСТ 14771-76)


Private Function GetSecondaryTool(pd As PositionData) As String
    Dim mainTool As String, extraTool As String
    KC28_SelectTools pd, mainTool, extraTool
    GetSecondaryTool = extraTool
End Function

' ============================================
' ДОПУСКИ КАТЕТА ПО ГОСТ
' ============================================
Private Sub AssignWeldLegToleranceByGOST(ByRef pd As PositionData)
    If pd.tolPlus <> 0 Or pd.tolMinus <> 0 Then Exit Sub
    If pd.weldLeg = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Len(pd.weldType) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim leg As Double
    leg = pd.weldLeg
    If leg <= 5 Then
        pd.tolPlus = 1: pd.tolMinus = -0.5: pd.WeldLegTolPlus = 1: pd.WeldLegTolMinus = -0.5
    ElseIf leg <= 8 Then
        pd.tolPlus = 2: pd.tolMinus = -1: pd.WeldLegTolPlus = 2: pd.WeldLegTolMinus = -1
    ElseIf leg <= 12 Then
        pd.tolPlus = 2.5: pd.tolMinus = -1.5: pd.WeldLegTolPlus = 2.5: pd.WeldLegTolMinus = -1.5
    Else
        pd.tolPlus = 3: pd.tolMinus = -2: pd.WeldLegTolPlus = 3: pd.WeldLegTolMinus = -2
    End If
End Sub

Private Function FormatNum(val As Double) As String
    If val = Int(val) Then
        FormatNum = CStr(CLng(val))
    Else
        FormatNum = CStr(val)
    End If
End Function

Private Function IsNumericStr(s As String) As Boolean
    Dim ts As String, i As Long, ch As String
    Dim hasDot As Boolean, hasDigit As Boolean
    ts = Trim(s)
    If Len(ts) = 0 Then
        IsNumericStr = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    hasDot = False: hasDigit = False
    For i = 1 To Len(ts)
        ch = Mid(ts, i, 1)
        If ch Like "[0-9]" Then
            hasDigit = True
        ElseIf ch = "." And Not hasDot Then
            hasDot = True
        Else
            IsNumericStr = False
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
    IsNumericStr = hasDigit
End Function

Private Function BuildTolText(tolPlus As Double, tolMinus As Double) As String
    BuildTolText = ""
    If tolPlus = 0 And tolMinus = 0 Then Exit Function
    If tolPlus > 0 And tolMinus < 0 And Abs(tolPlus) = Abs(tolMinus) Then
        BuildTolText = Chr(177) & FormatNum(tolPlus)
    ElseIf tolPlus <> 0 And tolMinus <> 0 Then
        BuildTolText = "+" & FormatNum(tolPlus) & "/" & FormatNum(tolMinus)
    ElseIf tolPlus <> 0 Then
        BuildTolText = "+" & FormatNum(tolPlus)
    ElseIf tolMinus <> 0 Then
        BuildTolText = FormatNum(tolMinus)
    End If
End Function

' ============================================
' БУКВЕННЫЙ РАЗМЕР
' ============================================
Private Function IsLetterDimension(displayText As String) As Boolean
    Dim txt As String
    txt = Trim(displayText)
    IsLetterDimension = False
    If Len(txt) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim startPos As Long: startPos = 1
    Dim fc As String: fc = Mid(txt, 1, 1)
    If fc = ChrW(216) Or fc = ChrW(248) Then
        startPos = 2
    ElseIf UCase(Left(txt, 10)) = "<MOD-DIAM>" Then
        startPos = 11
    End If
    If startPos > Len(txt) Then Exit Function
    Dim core As String: core = Trim(Mid(txt, startPos))
    If Len(core) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim firstCore As String: firstCore = Mid(core, 1, 1)
    If firstCore Like "[A-Za-z]" Or firstCore Like "[А-Яа-яЁё]" Then
        Dim letterPart As String, digitPart As String, restPart As String
        letterPart = "": digitPart = "": restPart = ""
        Dim ci As Long, cc As String
        ci = 1
        Do While ci <= Len(core)
            cc = Mid(core, ci, 1)
            If cc Like "[A-Za-z]" Or cc Like "[А-Яа-яЁё]" Then
                letterPart = letterPart & cc: ci = ci + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        Do While ci <= Len(core)
            cc = Mid(core, ci, 1)
            If cc Like "[0-9]" Then
                digitPart = digitPart & cc: ci = ci + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        restPart = Trim(Mid(core, ci))
        If Len(letterPart) >= 1 And Len(letterPart) <= 3 Then
            If Len(digitPart) <= 2 Then
                If Len(restPart) = 0 Then
                    IsLetterDimension = True
                ElseIf Left(restPart, 1) = Chr(177) Or Left(restPart, 1) = "+" Or _
                       Left(restPart, 1) = "-" Or Left(restPart, 1) = " " Then
                    IsLetterDimension = True
                End If
            End If
        End If
    End If
End Function

' ============================================
' ПАРСИНГ ТЕКСТА DISPLAYDATA ДЛЯ РАЗМЕРА
' ============================================
Private Sub ParseDimensionFromDisplayText(displayText As String, ByRef pd As PositionData)
    Dim txt As String: txt = Trim(displayText)
    If Len(txt) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim pos As Long: pos = 1
    Dim firstChar As String: firstChar = Mid(txt, 1, 1)
    If firstChar = ChrW(216) Or firstChar = ChrW(248) Then
        pd.DimPrefix = "<MOD DIAM>": pos = 2
    ElseIf UCase(Left(txt, 10)) = "<MOD-DIAM>" Then
        pd.DimPrefix = "<MOD DIAM>": pos = 11
    ElseIf UCase(firstChar) = "R" Then
        If Len(txt) > 1 Then
            If Mid(txt, 2, 1) Like "[0-9]" Then
                pd.DimPrefix = "R": pos = 2
            End If
        End If
    End If
    Do While pos <= Len(txt) And Mid(txt, pos, 1) = " "
        pos = pos + 1
    Loop
    Dim numStr As String: numStr = ""
    Dim ch As String
    Do While pos <= Len(txt)
        ch = Mid(txt, pos, 1)
        If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
            If ch = "," Then ch = "."
            numStr = numStr & ch: pos = pos + 1
        Else
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop
    If Len(numStr) > 0 Then pd.NominalValue = val(numStr)
    Dim remainder As String: remainder = Mid(txt, pos)
    If Len(remainder) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    remainder = Replace(Replace(remainder, "(", " "), ")", " ")
    Do While InStr(1, remainder, "  ") > 0
        remainder = Replace(remainder, "  ", " ")
    Loop
    remainder = Trim(remainder)
    If Len(remainder) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim remFirstChar As String: remFirstChar = Mid(remainder, 1, 1)
    If remFirstChar Like "[A-Za-z]" Then
        Dim tempFit As String: tempFit = ""
        Dim fp As Long: fp = 1
        Do While fp <= Len(remainder)
            ch = Mid(remainder, fp, 1)
            If ch Like "[A-Za-z]" Then
                tempFit = tempFit & ch: fp = fp + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        Dim qualStr As String: qualStr = ""
        Do While fp <= Len(remainder)
            ch = Mid(remainder, fp, 1)
            If ch Like "[0-9]" Then
                qualStr = qualStr & ch: fp = fp + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        Dim fitPattern As String: fitPattern = ""
        If Len(qualStr) > 0 Then
            tempFit = tempFit & qualStr
            fitPattern = tempFit
        End If
        If fp <= Len(remainder) Then
            If Mid(remainder, fp, 1) = "/" Then
                fp = fp + 1
                Dim tempFit2 As String: tempFit2 = ""
                Do While fp <= Len(remainder)
                    ch = Mid(remainder, fp, 1)
                    If ch Like "[A-Za-z]" Then
                        tempFit2 = tempFit2 & ch: fp = fp + 1
                    Else
                        Exit Do
                    End If
                Loop
                Dim qualStr2 As String: qualStr2 = ""
                Do While fp <= Len(remainder)
                    ch = Mid(remainder, fp, 1)
                    If ch Like "[0-9]" Then
                        qualStr2 = qualStr2 & ch: fp = fp + 1
                    Else
                        Exit Do
                    End If
                Loop
                If Len(qualStr2) > 0 Then fitPattern = fitPattern & "/" & tempFit2 & qualStr2
            End If
        End If
        If Len(fitPattern) > 0 Then
            Dim firstFitChar As String: firstFitChar = UCase(Left(fitPattern, 1))
            If InStr(1, "ABCDEFGHJKMNPRSTUVXYZ", firstFitChar) > 0 Then
                pd.TolText = fitPattern
                Exit Sub
            End If
        End If
    End If
    Dim pi As Long
    Dim pmPos As Long: pmPos = InStr(1, remainder, Chr(177))
    If pmPos > 0 Then
        Dim pmNum As String: pmNum = ""
        For pi = pmPos + 1 To Len(remainder)
            ch = Mid(remainder, pi, 1)
            If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
                If ch = "," Then ch = "."
                pmNum = pmNum & ch
            ElseIf ch = " " And Len(pmNum) = 0 Then
                ' skip
            ElseIf Len(pmNum) > 0 Then
                Exit For
            Else
                Exit For
            End If
        Next pi
        If Len(pmNum) > 0 Then
            Dim pmVal As Double: pmVal = val(pmNum)
            pd.tolPlus = pmVal: pd.tolMinus = -pmVal
            pd.TolText = Chr(177) & FormatNum(pmVal)
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    Dim foundPlus As Boolean, foundMinus As Boolean
    Dim plusVal As Double, minusVal As Double
    foundPlus = False: foundMinus = False: plusVal = 0: minusVal = 0
    pi = 1
    Do While pi <= Len(remainder)
        ch = Mid(remainder, pi, 1)
        If ch = "+" Then
            Dim pNumStr As String: pNumStr = ""
            Dim pj As Long: pj = pi + 1
            Do While pj <= Len(remainder) And Mid(remainder, pj, 1) = " "
                pj = pj + 1
            Loop
            Do While pj <= Len(remainder)
                ch = Mid(remainder, pj, 1)
                If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
                    If ch = "," Then ch = "."
                    pNumStr = pNumStr & ch: pj = pj + 1
                Else
                    Exit Do
                End If
            Loop
            If Len(pNumStr) > 0 Then plusVal = val(pNumStr): foundPlus = True
            pi = pj
        ElseIf ch = "-" Then
            Dim mNumStr As String: mNumStr = ""
            Dim mj As Long: mj = pi + 1
            Do While mj <= Len(remainder) And Mid(remainder, mj, 1) = " "
                mj = mj + 1
            Loop
            Do While mj <= Len(remainder)
                ch = Mid(remainder, mj, 1)
                If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
                    If ch = "," Then ch = "."
                    mNumStr = mNumStr & ch: mj = mj + 1
                Else
                    Exit Do
                End If
            Loop
            If Len(mNumStr) > 0 Then minusVal = val(mNumStr): foundMinus = True
            pi = mj
        Else
            pi = pi + 1
        End If
    Loop
    If foundPlus Then pd.tolPlus = plusVal
    If foundMinus Then pd.tolMinus = -minusVal
    If pd.tolPlus <> 0 Or pd.tolMinus <> 0 Then
        pd.TolText = BuildTolText(pd.tolPlus, pd.tolMinus)
    End If
End Sub

' ============================================
' ПРОВЕРКА: DisplayData буквенный размер
' ============================================
Private Function IsDisplayDataLetterDim(swAnn As Object) As Boolean
    IsDisplayDataLetterDim = False
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim dispData As Object
    Set dispData = swAnn.GetDisplayData
    If dispData Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim txtCount As Long: txtCount = dispData.GetTextCount
    If txtCount = 0 Then Exit Function
    ' Первый фрагмент бывает пустым (напр. один пробел), когда символ-модификатор/буква
    ' находятся в более поздних фрагментах - ищем первый непустой.
    Dim idx0 As Long: idx0 = -1
    Dim fi0 As Long, tmpTxt0 As Variant
    For fi0 = 0 To txtCount - 1
        tmpTxt0 = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(fi0)
        If Not IsNull(tmpTxt0) Then
            If Len(Trim(CStr(tmpTxt0))) > 0 Then
                idx0 = fi0
                Exit For
            End If
        End If
    Next fi0
    If idx0 < 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim txt As String: txt = Trim(CStr(dispData.GetTextAtIndex(idx0)))
    If Len(txt) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim startPos As Long: startPos = 1
    Dim symbolPrefix As Boolean: symbolPrefix = False
    If Mid(txt, 1, 1) = ChrW(216) Or Mid(txt, 1, 1) = ChrW(248) Then
        startPos = 2
        symbolPrefix = True
    ElseIf UCase(Left(txt, 10)) = "<MOD-DIAM>" Then
        startPos = 11
        symbolPrefix = True
    End If
    Dim core As String
    If startPos <= Len(txt) Then
        core = Trim(Mid(txt, startPos))
    Else
        core = ""
    End If
    If Len(core) = 0 Then
        ' Символ ChrW(216)/R/<MOD-DIAM> иногда занимает целый фрагмент отдельно,
        ' а буквенное обозначение ("Ш1" и т.п.) - в следующем непустом фрагменте.
        If symbolPrefix Then
            Dim fi1 As Long, nextTxt0 As Variant
            For fi1 = idx0 + 1 To txtCount - 1
                nextTxt0 = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(fi1)
                If Not IsNull(nextTxt0) Then
                    If Len(Trim(CStr(nextTxt0))) > 0 Then
                        core = Trim(CStr(nextTxt0))
                        Exit For
                    End If
                End If
            Next fi1
        End If
        If Len(core) = 0 Then Exit Function
    End If
    Dim fc As String: fc = Mid(core, 1, 1)
    If Not (fc Like "[A-Za-z]" Or fc Like "[А-Яа-яЁё]") Then Exit Function
    Dim ci As Long, cc As String
    Dim letterCount As Long, digitCount As Long
    letterCount = 0: digitCount = 0
    ci = 1
    Do While ci <= Len(core)
        cc = Mid(core, ci, 1)
        If cc Like "[A-Za-z]" Or cc Like "[А-Яа-яЁё]" Then
            letterCount = letterCount + 1: ci = ci + 1
        Else
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop
    Do While ci <= Len(core)
        cc = Mid(core, ci, 1)
        If cc Like "[0-9]" Then
            digitCount = digitCount + 1: ci = ci + 1
        Else
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop
    If letterCount >= 1 And letterCount <= 3 And digitCount <= 2 Then
        IsDisplayDataLetterDim = True
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

' ============================================
' СБОРКА ТЕКСТА ИЗ DISPLAYDATA
' ============================================
Private Function CollectDisplayDataText(swAnn As Object) As String
    Dim dispData As Object
    Dim txt As Variant
    Dim dimText As String, i As Long, txtCount As Long
    Dim txtParts(0 To 4) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    dimText = ""
    Set dispData = swAnn.GetDisplayData
    If dispData Is Nothing Then
        CollectDisplayDataText = ""
        Exit Function
    End If
    txtCount = dispData.GetTextCount
    Dim pi As Long
    For pi = 0 To 4
        If pi < txtCount Then
            txt = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(pi)
            If Not IsNull(txt) Then txtParts(pi) = Trim(CStr(txt))
        End If
    Next pi
    ' Если самый первый непустой фрагмент - это только символ ChrW(216)/R/<MOD-DIAM> сам по себе (без буквы/числа),
    ' не выводим его - буквенное обозначение ("Ш1" и т.п.) идёт в следующем непустом фрагменте.
    ' Первый фрагмент (index 0) иногда бывает пустым (напр. один пробел) - ищем первый непустой.
    Dim explicitPrefix As String: explicitPrefix = ""
    Dim prefixIdx As Long: prefixIdx = -1
    Dim ppi As Long
    For ppi = 0 To 4
        If Len(txtParts(ppi)) > 0 Then
            prefixIdx = ppi
            Exit For
        End If
    Next ppi
    If prefixIdx >= 0 Then
        If Left(txtParts(prefixIdx), 1) = ChrW(216) Or Left(txtParts(prefixIdx), 1) = ChrW(248) Then
            explicitPrefix = "<MOD DIAM>"
            txtParts(prefixIdx) = Mid(txtParts(prefixIdx), 2)
        ElseIf UCase(Left(txtParts(prefixIdx), 10)) = "<MOD-DIAM>" Then
            explicitPrefix = "<MOD DIAM>"
            txtParts(prefixIdx) = Mid(txtParts(prefixIdx), 11)
        End If
    End If
    If Len(txtParts(0)) > 0 Then dimText = txtParts(0)
    If Len(txtParts(1)) > 0 Then dimText = dimText & " " & txtParts(1)
    If Len(txtParts(3)) > 0 Then dimText = dimText & " " & txtParts(3)
    If Len(txtParts(2)) > 0 Then dimText = dimText & " " & txtParts(2)
    If Len(txtParts(4)) > 0 Then dimText = dimText & " " & txtParts(4)
    For i = 5 To txtCount - 1
        txt = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(i)
        If Not IsNull(txt) Then
            If Len(Trim(CStr(txt))) > 0 Then dimText = dimText & " " & Trim(CStr(txt))
        End If
    Next i
    On Error GoTo 0
    dimText = Trim(dimText)
    If Len(explicitPrefix) > 0 Then dimText = explicitPrefix & dimText
    CollectDisplayDataText = dimText
End Function

' ============================================
' ТЕКСТ РАЗМЕРА - ОСНОВНАЯ ФУНКЦИЯ
' ============================================
Private Function GetDimensionTextEx(swDispDim As Object, ByRef pd As PositionData) As String
    Dim swAnn As Object
    Dim swDimObj As Object
    Dim dimType As Long
    Dim dispData As Object
    Dim txtFragments() As String
    Dim fragCount As Long
    Dim totalTxtCount As Long
    Dim fi As Long
    Dim tmpVal As Variant
    Dim nominalText As String
    Dim tolFromFrag0 As Boolean
    Dim skipTolSearch As Boolean
    Dim resultText As String
    On Error Resume Next
    Set swAnn = swDispDim.GetAnnotation
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then
        GetDimensionTextEx = "Размер"
        Exit Function
    End If
    dimType = swDispDim.Type2
    Select Case dimType
        Case 5: pd.DimPrefix = "<MOD DIAM>"
        Case 4: pd.DimPrefix = "R"
        Case Else: pd.DimPrefix = ""
    End Select
    If IsDisplayDataLetterDim(swAnn) Then
        Dim letterText As String
        letterText = CollectDisplayDataText(swAnn)
        Set swDimObj = swDispDim.GetDimension2(0)
        If Not swDimObj Is Nothing Then
            pd.NominalValue = Round(swDimObj.value, 1)
        End If
        pd.UnitStr = "мм"
        ' Буквенный размер (напр. "Ш1") не должен автоматически наследовать префикс диаметра/радиуса из метаданных SW (Type2 = 5/4),
        ' но если тег модификатора явно встретился как текст (CollectDisplayDataText уже добавил "<MOD DIAM>" в letterText) - сохраняем его и в pd.DimPrefix.
        If Left(letterText, Len("<MOD DIAM>")) = "<MOD DIAM>" Then
            pd.DimPrefix = "<MOD DIAM>"
        Else
            pd.DimPrefix = ""
        End If
        On Error GoTo 0
        If Len(Trim(letterText)) = 0 Then letterText = "Размер"
        GetDimensionTextEx = Trim(letterText)
        Exit Function
    End If
    Set dispData = swAnn.GetDisplayData
    fragCount = 0
    If Not dispData Is Nothing Then
        totalTxtCount = dispData.GetTextCount
        If totalTxtCount > 0 Then
            ReDim txtFragments(0 To totalTxtCount - 1)
            For fi = 0 To totalTxtCount - 1
                tmpVal = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(fi)
                If Not IsNull(tmpVal) Then
                    txtFragments(fi) = Trim(CStr(tmpVal))
                Else
                    txtFragments(fi) = ""
                End If
            Next fi
            fragCount = totalTxtCount
        End If
    End If
    nominalText = ""
    tolFromFrag0 = False
    skipTolSearch = False
    Dim nomFragIdx As Long: nomFragIdx = -1
    Dim fzi As Long
    For fzi = 0 To fragCount - 1
        If Len(txtFragments(fzi)) > 0 Then
            nomFragIdx = fzi
            Exit For
        End If
    Next fzi
    If nomFragIdx >= 0 Then nominalText = txtFragments(nomFragIdx)
    If Len(nominalText) > 0 Then
        Dim nomPos As Long: nomPos = 1
        Dim nomFC As String: nomFC = Mid(nominalText, 1, 1)
        If nomFC = ChrW(216) Or nomFC = ChrW(248) Then
            pd.DimPrefix = "<MOD DIAM>": nomPos = 2
        ElseIf UCase(Left(nominalText, 10)) = "<MOD-DIAM>" Then
            pd.DimPrefix = "<MOD DIAM>": nomPos = 11
        ElseIf UCase(nomFC) = "R" Then
            If Len(nominalText) > 1 Then
                If Mid(nominalText, 2, 1) Like "[0-9]" Then
                    pd.DimPrefix = "R": nomPos = 2
                End If
            End If
        End If
        If nomPos > Len(nominalText) Then
            ' Весь фрагмент был только префиксом (напр. отдельный тег "<MOD-DIAM>" или символ диаметра) -
            ' само число находится в следующем непустом фрагменте.
            Dim nfi2 As Long
            For nfi2 = nomFragIdx + 1 To fragCount - 1
                If Len(txtFragments(nfi2)) > 0 Then
                    nominalText = txtFragments(nfi2)
                    nomPos = 1
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next nfi2
        End If
        Do While nomPos <= Len(nominalText) And Mid(nominalText, nomPos, 1) = " "
            nomPos = nomPos + 1
        Loop
        Dim nomNumStr As String: nomNumStr = ""
        Dim nch As String
        Do While nomPos <= Len(nominalText)
            nch = Mid(nominalText, nomPos, 1)
            If nch Like "[0-9]" Or nch = "." Or nch = "," Then
                If nch = "," Then nch = "."
                nomNumStr = nomNumStr & nch: nomPos = nomPos + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        If Len(nomNumStr) > 0 Then pd.NominalValue = val(nomNumStr)
        Dim nomRemainder As String: nomRemainder = ""
        If nomPos <= Len(nominalText) Then nomRemainder = Trim(Mid(nominalText, nomPos))
        If Len(nomRemainder) > 0 Then
            Dim remFirst As String: remFirst = Mid(nomRemainder, 1, 1)
            If remFirst Like "[A-Za-z]" Then
                pd.TolText = nomRemainder
                skipTolSearch = True
            ElseIf remFirst = Chr(177) Then
                Dim pmFromFrag As String: pmFromFrag = ""
                If Len(nomRemainder) > 1 Then pmFromFrag = Replace(Trim(Mid(nomRemainder, 2)), ",", ".")
                If Len(pmFromFrag) > 0 And IsNumericStr(pmFromFrag) Then
                    Dim pmFV As Double: pmFV = val(pmFromFrag)
                    pd.tolPlus = pmFV: pd.tolMinus = -pmFV
                    pd.TolText = Chr(177) & FormatNum(pmFV)
                    tolFromFrag0 = True
                End If
            ElseIf remFirst = "+" Then
                Dim pFromFrag As String: pFromFrag = ""
                If Len(nomRemainder) > 1 Then pFromFrag = Replace(Trim(Mid(nomRemainder, 2)), ",", ".")
                If Len(pFromFrag) > 0 And IsNumericStr(pFromFrag) Then
                    pd.tolPlus = val(pFromFrag)
                    pd.TolText = "+" & FormatNum(pd.tolPlus)
                    tolFromFrag0 = True
                End If
            ElseIf remFirst = "-" Then
                Dim mFromFrag As String: mFromFrag = ""
                If Len(nomRemainder) > 1 Then mFromFrag = Replace(Trim(Mid(nomRemainder, 2)), ",", ".")
                If Len(mFromFrag) > 0 And IsNumericStr(mFromFrag) Then
                    pd.tolMinus = -val(mFromFrag)
                    pd.TolText = FormatNum(pd.tolMinus)
                    tolFromFrag0 = True
                End If
            Else
                Dim tmpPD0 As PositionData
                ParseDimensionFromDisplayText nominalText, tmpPD0
                If tmpPD0.tolPlus <> 0 Or tmpPD0.tolMinus <> 0 Then
                    pd.tolPlus = tmpPD0.tolPlus
                    pd.tolMinus = tmpPD0.tolMinus
                    pd.TolText = BuildTolText(pd.tolPlus, pd.tolMinus)
                    tolFromFrag0 = True
                End If
            End If
        End If
    End If
    If pd.NominalValue = 0 Then
        Set swDimObj = swDispDim.GetDimension2(0)
        If Not swDimObj Is Nothing Then
            pd.NominalValue = Round(swDimObj.value, 1)
        End If
    End If
    If Len(pd.TolText) > 0 Then
        If Mid(pd.TolText, 1, 1) Like "[A-Za-z]" Then skipTolSearch = True
    End If
    If tolFromFrag0 Then skipTolSearch = True
    If Not skipTolSearch Then
        Dim tokenCount As Long: tokenCount = 0
        Dim tokens() As String
        ReDim tokens(0 To 20)
        Dim fragI As Long
        For fragI = 1 To fragCount - 1
            Dim frag As String: frag = Trim(txtFragments(fragI))
            If Len(frag) > 0 Then
                tokenCount = tokenCount + 1
                If tokenCount > UBound(tokens) Then ReDim Preserve tokens(0 To tokenCount + 10)
                tokens(tokenCount) = frag
            End If
        Next fragI
        Dim tolFound As Boolean: tolFound = False
        Dim tok As String
        If Not tolFound And tokenCount >= 2 Then
            If UCase(tokens(1)) = "<MOD-PM>" Then
                Dim pmNumStr As String: pmNumStr = Replace(tokens(2), ",", ".")
                If IsNumericStr(pmNumStr) Then
                    Dim pmVal2 As Double: pmVal2 = val(pmNumStr)
                    pd.tolPlus = pmVal2: pd.tolMinus = -pmVal2
                    pd.TolText = Chr(177) & FormatNum(pmVal2)
                    tolFound = True
                End If
            End If
        End If
        If Not tolFound And tokenCount >= 1 Then
            If Len(tokens(1)) > 8 Then
                If Left(UCase(tokens(1)), 8) = "<MOD-PM>" Then
                    Dim pmSlitNum As String: pmSlitNum = Replace(Trim(Mid(tokens(1), 9)), ",", ".")
                    If IsNumericStr(pmSlitNum) Then
                        Dim pmSlitVal As Double: pmSlitVal = val(pmSlitNum)
                        pd.tolPlus = pmSlitVal: pd.tolMinus = -pmSlitVal
                        pd.TolText = Chr(177) & FormatNum(pmSlitVal)
                        tolFound = True
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
        If Not tolFound And tokenCount >= 1 Then
            Dim signCount As Long: signCount = 0
            Dim numCount As Long: numCount = 0
            Dim signs() As String, nums() As String
            ReDim signs(0 To 10): ReDim nums(0 To 10)
            Dim ti As Long
            For ti = 1 To tokenCount
                tok = tokens(ti)
                If UCase(tok) = "<MOD-PM>" Or tok = Chr(177) Then
                    signCount = signCount + 1
                    If signCount > UBound(signs) Then ReDim Preserve signs(0 To signCount + 5)
                    signs(signCount) = "pm"
                ElseIf Left(tok, 1) = Chr(177) And Len(tok) > 1 Then
                    Dim pmSlitNum2 As String: pmSlitNum2 = Replace(Mid(tok, 2), ",", ".")
                    If IsNumericStr(pmSlitNum2) Then
                        Dim pmSlitVal2 As Double: pmSlitVal2 = val(pmSlitNum2)
                        pd.tolPlus = pmSlitVal2: pd.tolMinus = -pmSlitVal2
                        pd.TolText = Chr(177) & FormatNum(pmSlitVal2)
                        tolFound = True
                    End If
                ElseIf tok = "+" Then
                    signCount = signCount + 1
                    If signCount > UBound(signs) Then ReDim Preserve signs(0 To signCount + 5)
                    signs(signCount) = "+"
                ElseIf tok = "-" Then
                    signCount = signCount + 1
                    If signCount > UBound(signs) Then ReDim Preserve signs(0 To signCount + 5)
                    signs(signCount) = "-"
                ElseIf Left(tok, 1) = "+" And Len(tok) > 1 Then
                    Dim pSlitNum As String: pSlitNum = Replace(Mid(tok, 2), ",", ".")
                    If IsNumericStr(pSlitNum) Then
                        pd.tolPlus = val(pSlitNum): tolFound = True
                    End If
                ElseIf Left(tok, 1) = "-" And Len(tok) > 1 Then
                    Dim mSlitNum As String: mSlitNum = Replace(Mid(tok, 2), ",", ".")
                    If IsNumericStr(mSlitNum) Then
                        pd.tolMinus = -val(mSlitNum): tolFound = True
                    End If
                Else
                    Dim cleanNum As String: cleanNum = Replace(tok, ",", ".")
                    If IsNumericStr(cleanNum) Then
                        numCount = numCount + 1
                        If numCount > UBound(nums) Then ReDim Preserve nums(0 To numCount + 5)
                        nums(numCount) = cleanNum
                    End If
                End If
            Next ti
            If Not tolFound And signCount > 0 And numCount > 0 Then
                Dim si2 As Long
                For si2 = 1 To signCount
                    If si2 <= numCount Then
                        Dim sv As Double: sv = val(nums(si2))
                        If signs(si2) = "+" Then
                            pd.tolPlus = sv: tolFound = True
                        ElseIf signs(si2) = "-" Then
                            pd.tolMinus = -sv: tolFound = True
                        ElseIf signs(si2) = "pm" Then
                            pd.tolPlus = sv: pd.tolMinus = -sv
                            pd.TolText = Chr(177) & FormatNum(sv)
                            tolFound = True
                        End If
                    End If
                Next si2
            End If
        End If
        If Not tolFound Then
            Dim tolType As Long: tolType = swDispDim.GetToleranceType
            If tolType >= 2 Then
                Err.Clear
                Dim tVals As Variant: tVals = swDispDim.GetToleranceValues
                If Err.number = 0 And Not IsEmpty(tVals) Then
                    If IsArray(tVals) Then
                        If UBound(tVals) >= 1 Then
                            Dim apiTP As Double: apiTP = Round(tVals(0) * 1000, 3)
                            Dim apiTM As Double: apiTM = Round(tVals(1) * 1000, 3)
                            If apiTP <> 0 Or apiTM <> 0 Then
                                pd.tolPlus = apiTP: pd.tolMinus = apiTM: tolFound = True
                            End If
                        ElseIf UBound(tVals) >= 0 Then
                            Dim apiSym As Double: apiSym = Round(Abs(tVals(0)) * 1000, 3)
                            If apiSym <> 0 Then
                                pd.tolPlus = apiSym: pd.tolMinus = -apiSym: tolFound = True
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                Err.Clear
            End If
        End If
    End If
    If Len(pd.TolText) = 0 Then
        pd.TolText = BuildTolText(pd.tolPlus, pd.tolMinus)
    End If
    If Len(pd.DimPrefix) = 0 Then
        Select Case dimType
            Case 5: pd.DimPrefix = "<MOD DIAM>"
            Case 4: pd.DimPrefix = "R"
        End Select
    End If
    pd.UnitStr = "мм"
    resultText = ""
    If Len(pd.DimPrefix) > 0 Then resultText = pd.DimPrefix
    resultText = resultText & FormatNum(pd.NominalValue)
    If Len(pd.TolText) > 0 Then resultText = resultText & pd.TolText
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Len(Trim(resultText)) = 0 Then resultText = "Размер"
    GetDimensionTextEx = Trim(resultText)
End Function

' ============================================
' ТЕКСТ ГЕОМ. ДОПУСКА
' ============================================
Private Function GetGtolTextEx(swGtol As Object, ByRef pd As PositionData) As String
    Dim gtolText As String, swAnn As Object, dispData As Object
    Dim txtCount As Long, i As Long, txt As Variant, rawFullText As String
    On Error Resume Next
    gtolText = ""
    rawFullText = ""
    Set swAnn = swGtol.GetAnnotation
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then
        GetGtolTextEx = "Геом.допуск"
        Exit Function
    End If
    Dim txtFormat As Long
    For txtFormat = 0 To 3
        Dim tempTxt As String: tempTxt = ""
        tempTxt = swAnn.GetText(txtFormat)
        If Len(tempTxt) > 0 Then rawFullText = rawFullText & " " & tempTxt
    Next txtFormat
    Dim frameCount As Long: frameCount = 1
    Err.Clear
    frameCount = swGtol.GetFrameCount
    If Err.number <> 0 Then Err.Clear: frameCount = 1
    If frameCount < 1 Then frameCount = 1
    Dim fi As Long, frmVal As String
    For fi = 0 To frameCount - 1
        Err.Clear: frmVal = ""
        frmVal = swGtol.GetFrameValue(fi)
        If Err.number <> 0 Then
            Err.Clear: frmVal = ""
            frmVal = swGtol.GetFrameValue
            If Err.number <> 0 Then Err.Clear: frmVal = ""
        End If
        If Len(frmVal) > 0 Then rawFullText = rawFullText & " " & frmVal
    Next fi
    Set dispData = swAnn.GetDisplayData
    If Not dispData Is Nothing Then
        txtCount = dispData.GetTextCount
        For i = 0 To txtCount - 1
            txt = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(i)
            If Not IsNull(txt) Then
                Dim sTxt As String: sTxt = Trim(CStr(txt))
                If Len(sTxt) > 0 Then
                    rawFullText = rawFullText & " " & sTxt
                    If Len(gtolText) > 0 Then gtolText = gtolText & " "
                    gtolText = gtolText & sTxt
                End If
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    Dim tagSymbol As String: tagSymbol = GetGDTSymbolFromText(rawFullText)
    If Len(tagSymbol) > 0 Then
        pd.GDTSymbolName = tagSymbol
    Else
        Dim symType As Long
        Err.Clear
        symType = swGtol.GetFrameSymbol(0)
        If Err.number <> 0 Then
            Err.Clear
            symType = swGtol.GetFrameSymbol
            If Err.number <> 0 Then Err.Clear: symType = -1
        End If
        If symType >= 0 Then
            pd.GDTSymbolName = GetGDTSymbolName(symType)
        Else
            pd.GDTSymbolName = "Геом.допуск"
        End If
    End If
    Dim gdtVal As String: gdtVal = ""
    Err.Clear
    gdtVal = swGtol.GetFrameValue(0)
    If Err.number <> 0 Then
        Err.Clear: gdtVal = ""
        gdtVal = swGtol.GetFrameValue
        If Err.number <> 0 Then Err.Clear: gdtVal = ""
    End If
    pd.GDTValue = gdtVal
    pd.NominalValue = val(gdtVal)
    Dim datumStr As String: datumStr = ""
    Dim d As Long
    For d = 0 To 2
        Dim datumItem As String: datumItem = ""
        Err.Clear
        datumItem = swGtol.GetFrameDatumIdentifier(0, d)
        If Err.number <> 0 Then Err.Clear: datumItem = ""
        If Len(datumItem) > 0 Then
            If Len(datumStr) > 0 Then datumStr = datumStr & ", "
            datumStr = datumStr & datumItem
        End If
    Next d
    pd.GDTDatums = datumStr
    If Len(pd.GDTValue) = 0 And Len(gtolText) > 0 Then
        Dim ch As String, extractedNum As String: extractedNum = ""
        For i = 1 To Len(gtolText)
            ch = Mid(gtolText, i, 1)
            If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
                extractedNum = extractedNum & ch
            ElseIf Len(extractedNum) > 0 Then
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        If Len(extractedNum) > 0 Then
            extractedNum = Replace(extractedNum, ",", ".")
            pd.GDTValue = extractedNum
            pd.NominalValue = val(extractedNum)
        End If
    End If
    Dim cleanText As String: cleanText = RemoveGtolTags(gtolText)
    If Len(Trim(cleanText)) = 0 Then
        cleanText = pd.GDTSymbolName
        If Len(pd.GDTValue) > 0 Then cleanText = cleanText & " " & pd.GDTValue
    Else
        If InStr(1, cleanText, pd.GDTSymbolName) = 0 And Len(pd.GDTSymbolName) > 0 Then
            cleanText = pd.GDTSymbolName & " " & cleanText
        End If
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Len(Trim(cleanText)) = 0 Then cleanText = "Геом.допуск"
    GetGtolTextEx = Trim(cleanText)
End Function

' ============================================
' УДАЛЕНИЕ ШАГА ШВА
' ============================================
Private Function RemoveWeldSpacing(inputText As String) As String
    Dim result As String: result = inputText
    Dim i As Long, slashPos As Long, numAfter As String, numBefore As String
    Dim ch As String, j As Long, startCut As Long, endCut As Long
    i = 1
    Do
        slashPos = InStr(i, result, "/")
        If slashPos = 0 Then Exit Do
        numAfter = ""
        For j = slashPos + 1 To Len(result)
            ch = Mid(result, j, 1)
            If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
                numAfter = numAfter & ch
            ElseIf ch = " " And Len(numAfter) = 0 Then
                ' skip
            Else
                Exit For
            End If
        Next j
        If Len(numAfter) = 0 Then
            i = slashPos + 1: GoTo NextSlash
        End If
        endCut = j - 1
        numBefore = ""
        Dim numStartW As Long: numStartW = slashPos
        For j = slashPos - 1 To 1 Step -1
            ch = Mid(result, j, 1)
            If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
                numBefore = ch & numBefore: numStartW = j
            ElseIf ch = " " And Len(numBefore) = 0 Then
                ' skip
            Else
                Exit For
            End If
        Next j
        If Len(numBefore) = 0 Then
            i = slashPos + 1: GoTo NextSlash
        End If
        Dim preNumPos As Long: preNumPos = numStartW - 1
        Do While preNumPos >= 1
            If Mid(result, preNumPos, 1) = " " Then preNumPos = preNumPos - 1 Else Exit Do
        Loop
        If preNumPos >= 1 Then
            If Mid(result, preNumPos, 1) = "-" Then
                Dim prePrePos As Long: prePrePos = preNumPos - 1
                Do While prePrePos >= 1
                    If Mid(result, prePrePos, 1) = " " Then prePrePos = prePrePos - 1 Else Exit Do
                Loop
                Dim isSpacing As Boolean: isSpacing = True
                If prePrePos >= 1 Then
                    Dim ppChar As String: ppChar = Mid(result, prePrePos, 1)
                    If ppChar = "+" Then
                        isSpacing = False
                    ElseIf ppChar Like "[0-9]" Then
                        Dim k As Long
                        For k = prePrePos - 1 To 1 Step -1
                            ch = Mid(result, k, 1)
                            If ch = "+" Then
                                isSpacing = False: Exit For
                            ElseIf ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Or ch = " " Then
                                ' continue
                            Else
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next k
                    End If
                End If
                If isSpacing Then
                    startCut = preNumPos
                    result = Left(result, startCut - 1) & Mid(result, endCut + 1)
                    i = startCut: GoTo NextSlash
                End If
            End If
        End If
        i = slashPos + 1
NextSlash:
    Loop
    Do While InStr(1, result, "  ") > 0
        result = Replace(result, "  ", " ")
    Loop
    RemoveWeldSpacing = Trim(result)
End Function

' ============================================
' ТЕКСТ СВАРКИ (С ПРОВЕРКОЙ ЗНАКА КАТЕТА)
' ============================================
Private Function GetWeldTextEx(swWeld As Object, ByRef pd As PositionData) As String
    Dim weldText As String, swAnn As Object, dispData As Object
    Dim txtCount As Long, i As Long
    Dim tmpTxt As Variant, tmpStr As String
    On Error Resume Next
    weldText = ""
    Set swAnn = swWeld.GetAnnotation
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then
        GetWeldTextEx = "Сварка"
        Exit Function
    End If
    Set dispData = swAnn.GetDisplayData
    If dispData Is Nothing Then
        GetWeldTextEx = "Сварка"
        Exit Function
    End If
    txtCount = dispData.GetTextCount
    Dim pi As Long
    Dim placesCaptured As Boolean: placesCaptured = False
    pd.weldPlaces = 0
    For pi = 0 To txtCount - 1
        tmpTxt = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(pi)
        If Not IsNull(tmpTxt) Then
            tmpStr = Trim(CStr(tmpTxt))
            If Len(tmpStr) > 0 Then
                ' Значение катета иногда приходит отдельным блоком текста (чистые цифры) сразу после тега <GWLD-FILL>.
                ' В этом случае блок цифр — это значение катета, а не «кол-во мест», даже если оно выглядит так же, как и кол-во мест.
                If (Not placesCaptured) And IsPlacesSegment(tmpStr) And Not EndsWithOpenFillTag(weldText) Then
                    pd.weldPlaces = ExtractPlacesNumber(tmpStr)
                    placesCaptured = True
                Else
                    If pi < 3 Then
                        weldText = weldText & tmpStr
                    Else
                        weldText = weldText & " | " & tmpStr
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next pi
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Len(Trim(weldText)) = 0 Then weldText = "Сварка"
    Dim fullTxt As String: fullTxt = Trim(weldText)
    pd.WeldIntermittentSpec = KC34_IntermittentSpec(fullTxt)
    Dim explicitGOST As Boolean: explicitGOST = False

    Dim bodyTxt As String, tailTxt As String
    Dim barPos As Long
    barPos = InStr(1, fullTxt, "|")
    If barPos > 0 Then
        bodyTxt = Trim(Left(fullTxt, barPos - 1))
        tailTxt = Trim(Mid(fullTxt, barPos))
    Else
        bodyTxt = fullTxt
        tailTxt = ""
    End If

    ' Двузначные типы швов проверяются первыми
    Dim wTypes As Variant
    wTypes = Array( _
        "С10", "С11", "С12", "С13", "С14", "С15", "С16", "С17", "С18", "С19", _
        "С20", "С21", "С22", "С23", "С24", "С25", "С26", "С27", "С28", "С29", _
        "С30", "С31", "С32", "С33", "С34", "С35", "С36", "С37", "С38", "С39", _
        "У10", "У11", "У12", "У13", "У14", "У15", "У16", "У17", "У18", "У19", "У20", _
        "Т10", "Т11", "Т12", "Т13", "Т14", "Т15", "Т16", "Т17", "Т18", "Т19", "Т20", _
        "Н10", "Н11", "Н12", _
        "Т1", "Т2", "Т3", "Т4", "Т5", "Т6", "Т7", "Т8", "Т9", _
        "С1", "С2", "С3", "С4", "С5", "С6", "С7", "С8", "С9", _
        "Н1", "Н2", "Н3", "Н4", _
        "У1", "У2", "У3", "У4", "У5", "У6", "У7", "У8", "У9")

    Dim tp As Long
    For tp = 0 To UBound(wTypes)
        If InStr(1, bodyTxt, CStr(wTypes(tp))) > 0 Then
            pd.weldType = CStr(wTypes(tp))
            Exit For
        End If
    Next tp

    pd.weldLeg = 0
    pd.tolPlus = 0
    pd.tolMinus = 0
    pd.WeldLegTolPlus = 0
    pd.WeldLegTolMinus = 0

    Dim fillPos As Long
    fillPos = InStr(1, bodyTxt, "<GWLD-FILL>", vbTextCompare)
    If fillPos = 0 Then fillPos = InStr(1, bodyTxt, "<WELD-FILL>", vbTextCompare)
    If fillPos = 0 Then fillPos = InStr(1, bodyTxt, "<GWLD-FILA>", vbTextCompare)
    If fillPos = 0 Then fillPos = InStr(1, bodyTxt, "<GWLD-FILB>", vbTextCompare)

    If fillPos > 0 Then
        Dim legSearchTxt As String
        legSearchTxt = Mid(bodyTxt, fillPos)
        Dim tEnd As Long
        tEnd = InStr(1, legSearchTxt, ">")
        If tEnd > 0 Then legSearchTxt = Mid(legSearchTxt, tEnd + 1)

        Dim p As Long, ch As String, numStr As String, foundNum As Boolean
        numStr = "": foundNum = False
        For p = 1 To Len(legSearchTxt)
            ch = Mid(legSearchTxt, p, 1)
            If ch Like "[0-9]" Or (ch = "," And foundNum) Or (ch = "." And foundNum) Then
                numStr = numStr & ch: foundNum = True
            ElseIf ch = " " And Not foundNum Then
                ' skip
            ElseIf foundNum Then
                Exit For
            End If
        Next p

        If Len(numStr) > 0 Then
            pd.weldLeg = val(Replace(numStr, ",", "."))
            pd.NominalValue = pd.weldLeg

            Dim tolSearchText As String
            If p <= Len(legSearchTxt) Then
                tolSearchText = Mid(legSearchTxt, p)
            Else
                tolSearchText = ""
            End If

            Dim plusPos As Long, pp As Long
            plusPos = InStr(1, tolSearchText, "+")
            If plusPos > 0 Then
                Dim plusNum As String: plusNum = ""
                For pp = plusPos + 1 To Len(tolSearchText)
                    ch = Mid(tolSearchText, pp, 1)
                    If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
                        plusNum = plusNum & ch
                    ElseIf ch = " " And Len(plusNum) = 0 Then
                        ' skip
                    ElseIf Len(plusNum) > 0 Then
                        Exit For
                    Else
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next pp
                If Len(plusNum) > 0 Then
                    pd.WeldLegTolPlus = val(Replace(plusNum, ",", "."))
                    pd.tolPlus = pd.WeldLegTolPlus
                End If
            End If

            Dim minusPos As Long
            minusPos = InStr(1, tolSearchText, "-")
            If minusPos > 0 Then
                Dim minusNum As String: minusNum = ""
                For pp = minusPos + 1 To Len(tolSearchText)
                    ch = Mid(tolSearchText, pp, 1)
                    If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
                        minusNum = minusNum & ch
                    ElseIf ch = " " And Len(minusNum) = 0 Then
                        ' skip
                    ElseIf Len(minusNum) > 0 Then
                        Exit For
                    Else
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next pp
                ' "катет-длина/шаг" (прерывистый/шахматный шов, напр. "8-40/80") — это НЕ допуск катета.
                ' Отличаем по "/" сразу после числа при отсутствии знака "+" (у допуска всегда есть "+").
                Dim isPitchSpec As Boolean: isPitchSpec = False
                If Len(minusNum) > 0 And plusPos = 0 And pp <= Len(tolSearchText) Then
                    Do While pp <= Len(tolSearchText)
                        If Mid$(tolSearchText, pp, 1) <> " " And Mid$(tolSearchText, pp, 1) <> ChrW(160) Then Exit Do
                        pp = pp + 1
                    Loop
                    If pp <= Len(tolSearchText) Then
                        ch = Mid$(tolSearchText, pp, 1)
                        If ch = "/" Or ch = "Z" Or ch = "z" Then isPitchSpec = True
                    End If
                End If
                If Len(minusNum) > 0 And Not isPitchSpec Then
                    pd.WeldLegTolMinus = -val(Replace(minusNum, ",", "."))
                    pd.tolMinus = pd.WeldLegTolMinus
                ElseIf isPitchSpec Then
                    Dim pitchNum As String: pitchNum = ""
                    Dim qq As Long
                    For qq = pp + 1 To Len(tolSearchText)
                        ch = Mid(tolSearchText, qq, 1)
                        If ch Like "[0-9]" Or ch = "." Or ch = "," Then
                            pitchNum = pitchNum & ch
                        ElseIf ch = " " And Len(pitchNum) = 0 Then
                            ' skip
                        ElseIf Len(pitchNum) > 0 Then
                            Exit For
                        Else
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next qq
                    If Len(pitchNum) > 0 Then
                        If val(Replace(minusNum, ",", ".")) > 0 And val(Replace(pitchNum, ",", ".")) > 0 Then _
                            pd.WeldIntermittentSpec = "-" & minusNum & "/" & pitchNum
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    End If

    Dim gostPos As Long
    gostPos = InStr(1, fullTxt, "ГОСТ", vbTextCompare)
    If gostPos = 0 Then gostPos = InStr(1, fullTxt, "GOST", vbTextCompare)
    
    If gostPos > 0 Then
        Dim gostEndPos As Long: gostEndPos = gostPos
        Dim gch As String
        Do While gostEndPos <= Len(fullTxt)
            gch = Mid(fullTxt, gostEndPos, 1)
            If gch Like "[А-Яа-яA-Za-z]" Then
                gostEndPos = gostEndPos + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        Do While gostEndPos <= Len(fullTxt)
            If Mid(fullTxt, gostEndPos, 1) = " " Then
                gostEndPos = gostEndPos + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        Dim gostNumStr As String: gostNumStr = ""
        Do While gostEndPos <= Len(fullTxt)
            gch = Mid(fullTxt, gostEndPos, 1)
            If gch Like "[0-9]" Or gch = "-" Then
                gostNumStr = gostNumStr & gch
                gostEndPos = gostEndPos + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        If Len(gostNumStr) > 0 Then
            pd.WeldStandard = Trim(Mid(fullTxt, gostPos, gostEndPos - gostPos))
            explicitGOST = True
        End If
    End If
    
    If Not explicitGOST Then
        If Len(pd.weldType) > 0 Then
            If pd.weldType = "Т1" And pd.weldLeg = 0 Then
                pd.WeldStandard = "ГОСТ 23518-79"
            Else
                pd.WeldStandard = "ГОСТ 14771-76"
            End If
        End If
    End If

    If pd.weldLeg > 0 Then
        AssignWeldLegToleranceByGOST pd
    End If

    GetWeldTextEx = fullTxt
End Function

' ============================================
' ОЧИСТКА ТЕКСТА НЕСТАНДАРТНОГО ШВА
' ============================================
Private Function CleanNonStandardWeldText(inputText As String) As String
    Dim result As String
    result = RemoveWeldTags(inputText)
    result = RemoveWeldSpacing(result)

    Do While Len(result) > 0
        Dim lastCh As String: lastCh = Right(result, 1)
        If lastCh Like "[0-9]" Then
            If Len(result) > 1 Then
                Dim prevCh As String: prevCh = Mid(result, Len(result) - 1, 1)
                If prevCh Like "[0-9]" Or prevCh = "." Or prevCh = "," Or prevCh = "-" Then
                    Exit Do
                End If
            End If
            result = Left(result, Len(result) - 1)
            result = Trim(result)
        Else
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop

    Do While Len(result) > 0
        If Right(result, 1) = "." Or Right(result, 1) = " " Or Right(result, 1) = "|" Then
            result = Left(result, Len(result) - 1)
        Else
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop

    CleanNonStandardWeldText = Trim(result)
End Function

' ============================================
' ГРУППИРОВКА С СОРТИРОВКОЙ ШВОВ
' ============================================
Private Sub GroupPositions()
    If positionsCount < 2 Then Exit Sub
    Dim nonWeldCount As Long, weldCountG As Long, noteCountG As Long
    Dim nonWeldIdx() As Long, weldIdx() As Long, noteIdx() As Long
    ReDim nonWeldIdx(1 To positionsCount)
    ReDim weldIdx(1 To positionsCount)
    ReDim noteIdx(1 To positionsCount)
    nonWeldCount = 0: weldCountG = 0: noteCountG = 0
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        If positionsData(i).AnnotationType = swSelNOTES Then
            noteCountG = noteCountG + 1
            noteIdx(noteCountG) = i
        ElseIf positionsData(i).AnnotationType = swSelWELDSYMBOLS Then
            weldCountG = weldCountG + 1
            weldIdx(weldCountG) = i
        Else
            nonWeldCount = nonWeldCount + 1
            nonWeldIdx(nonWeldCount) = i
        End If
    Next i
    Dim weldKeys() As String
    If weldCountG > 0 Then
        ReDim weldKeys(1 To weldCountG)
        Dim wi As Long, idx As Long, wKey As String
        For wi = 1 To weldCountG
            idx = weldIdx(wi)
            wKey = ""
            If Len(positionsData(idx).weldType) > 0 Then
                If Len(positionsData(idx).WeldStandard) > 0 Then
                    wKey = positionsData(idx).WeldStandard
                Else
                    wKey = "NO_GOST"
                End If
                wKey = wKey & "|" & positionsData(idx).weldType
                wKey = wKey & "|" & FormatLegForSort(positionsData(idx).weldLeg)
            Else
                wKey = "ZZZZ_RAW|" & positionsData(idx).AnnotationText & "|000.000"
            End If
            weldKeys(wi) = wKey
        Next wi
        Dim j As Long
        Dim tempIdx As Long, tempKey As String
        For i = 2 To weldCountG
            tempIdx = weldIdx(i)
            tempKey = weldKeys(i)
            j = i - 1
            Do While j >= 1
                If weldKeys(j) > tempKey Then
                    weldKeys(j + 1) = weldKeys(j)
                    weldIdx(j + 1) = weldIdx(j)
                    j = j - 1
                Else
                    Exit Do
                End If
            Loop
            weldKeys(j + 1) = tempKey
            weldIdx(j + 1) = tempIdx
        Next i
    End If
    Dim sortedData() As PositionData
    ReDim sortedData(1 To positionsCount)
    Dim outPos As Long: outPos = 0
    ' Заметки с "@" идут первыми - перед размерами, геом. допусками и сваркой
    For i = 1 To noteCountG
        outPos = outPos + 1
        sortedData(outPos) = positionsData(noteIdx(i))
    Next i
    For i = 1 To nonWeldCount
        outPos = outPos + 1
        sortedData(outPos) = positionsData(nonWeldIdx(i))
    Next i
    For i = 1 To weldCountG
        outPos = outPos + 1
        sortedData(outPos) = positionsData(weldIdx(i))
    Next i
    Dim startN As Long: startN = positionsData(1).number
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        positionsData(i) = sortedData(i)
        positionsData(i).number = startN + i - 1
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function FormatLegForSort(leg As Double) As String
    Dim intPart As Long
    Dim fracPart As Long
    intPart = Int(leg)
    fracPart = Int((leg - intPart) * 1000 + 0.5)
    FormatLegForSort = Right("000" & CStr(intPart), 3) & "." & Right("000" & CStr(fracPart), 3)
End Function

' ============================================
' УНИВЕРСАЛЬНАЯ ОБРАБОТКА АННОТАЦИИ
' ============================================
Private Function ProcessAnnotation(selType As Long, selectedObj As Object, _
                                   sheetName As String, ByRef counter As Long, _
                                   Optional ByVal owningViewName As String = "") As Boolean
    ProcessAnnotation = False
    Dim swAnn As Object
    Dim pos As Variant
    Dim annotText As String
    Select Case selType
        Case swSelDIMENSIONS
            If Not processDimensions Then Exit Function
            Dim swDD As Object: Set swDD = selectedObj
            If swDD Is Nothing Then Exit Function
            Set swAnn = swDD.GetAnnotation
            If swAnn Is Nothing Then Exit Function
            pos = swAnn.GetPosition()
            If IsEmpty(pos) Then Exit Function
            If Not IsInWorkArea(pos(0), pos(1)) Then Exit Function
            ' Не экспортировать размер с # в любой редактируемой части или отображаемом тексте.
            ' Ошибка чтения KC_DimHasHash прерывает режим вместо ошибочного включения размера.
            If KC_DimHasHash(swDD) Then Exit Function
            If ShouldSkipDimension(swDD) Then Exit Function

            If DimHasStar(swDD) Then
                ProcessAnnotation = False
                Exit Function
            End If

            positionsCount = positionsCount + 1
            If positionsCount > UBound(positionsData) Then ReDim Preserve positionsData(1 To positionsCount + 200)
            With positionsData(positionsCount)
                .number = counter
                .AnnotationType = swSelDIMENSIONS
                Set .SourceAnnotation = swAnn
                .sourceName = KC_AnnotationName(swAnn)
                .posX = pos(0): .posY = pos(1)
                .sheetName = sheetName
                .volumeStr = "100%"
            End With
            annotText = GetDimensionTextEx(swDD, positionsData(positionsCount))
            positionsData(positionsCount).AnnotationText = annotText
            positionsData(positionsCount).ToolName = AutoSelectTool(positionsData(positionsCount))
            positionsData(positionsCount).viewName = RemoveViewScale(GetViewNameForAnnotation(swAnn, sheetName))
            counter = counter + 1
            ProcessAnnotation = True
        Case swSelGTOLS
            If Not processGtols Then Exit Function
            Dim swGO As Object: Set swGO = selectedObj
            If swGO Is Nothing Then Exit Function
            Set swAnn = swGO.GetAnnotation
            If swAnn Is Nothing Then Exit Function
            pos = swAnn.GetPosition()
            If IsEmpty(pos) Then Exit Function
            If Not IsInWorkArea(pos(0), pos(1)) Then Exit Function
            positionsCount = positionsCount + 1
            If positionsCount > UBound(positionsData) Then ReDim Preserve positionsData(1 To positionsCount + 200)
            With positionsData(positionsCount)
                .number = counter
                .AnnotationType = swSelGTOLS
                Set .SourceAnnotation = swAnn
                .sourceName = KC_AnnotationName(swAnn)
                .posX = pos(0): .posY = pos(1)
                .sheetName = sheetName
                .volumeStr = "100%"
            End With
            annotText = GetGtolTextEx(swGO, positionsData(positionsCount))
            positionsData(positionsCount).AnnotationText = annotText
            positionsData(positionsCount).ToolName = AutoSelectTool(positionsData(positionsCount))
            positionsData(positionsCount).viewName = RemoveViewScale(GetViewNameForAnnotation(swAnn, sheetName))
            counter = counter + 1
            ProcessAnnotation = True
        Case swSelWELDSYMBOLS
            If Not processWelds Then Exit Function
            Dim swWO As Object: Set swWO = selectedObj
            If swWO Is Nothing Then Exit Function
            Set swAnn = swWO.GetAnnotation
            If swAnn Is Nothing Then Exit Function
            pos = swAnn.GetPosition()
            If IsEmpty(pos) Then Exit Function
            If Not IsInWorkArea(pos(0), pos(1)) Then Exit Function
            positionsCount = positionsCount + 1
            If positionsCount > UBound(positionsData) Then ReDim Preserve positionsData(1 To positionsCount + 200)
            With positionsData(positionsCount)
                .number = counter
                .AnnotationType = swSelWELDSYMBOLS
                Set .SourceAnnotation = swAnn
                .sourceName = KC_AnnotationName(swAnn)
                .posX = pos(0): .posY = pos(1)
                .sheetName = sheetName
                .volumeStr = "100%"
            End With
            annotText = GetWeldTextEx(swWO, positionsData(positionsCount))
            positionsData(positionsCount).AnnotationText = annotText
            positionsData(positionsCount).ToolName = AutoSelectTool(positionsData(positionsCount))
            positionsData(positionsCount).viewName = RemoveViewScale(GetViewNameForAnnotation(swAnn, sheetName))
            counter = counter + 1
            ProcessAnnotation = True
        Case swSelNOTES
            If Not processNotes Then Exit Function
            Dim swNO As Object: Set swNO = selectedObj
            If swNO Is Nothing Then Exit Function
            If Not NoteHasMark(swNO) Then Exit Function
            annotText = GetMarkedNoteText(swNO)
            If Len(annotText) = 0 Then Exit Function
            Set swAnn = swNO.GetAnnotation
            If swAnn Is Nothing Then Exit Function
            pos = swAnn.GetPosition()
            If IsEmpty(pos) Then Exit Function
            If Not IsInWorkArea(pos(0), pos(1)) Then Exit Function
            positionsCount = positionsCount + 1
            If positionsCount > UBound(positionsData) Then ReDim Preserve positionsData(1 To positionsCount + 200)
            With positionsData(positionsCount)
                .number = counter
                .AnnotationType = swSelNOTES
                Set .SourceAnnotation = swAnn
                .sourceName = KC_AnnotationName(swAnn)
                .posX = pos(0): .posY = pos(1)
                .sheetName = sheetName
                .volumeStr = "100%"
                .AnnotationText = annotText
            End With
            positionsData(positionsCount).ToolName = AutoSelectTool(positionsData(positionsCount))
            positionsData(positionsCount).viewName = RemoveViewScale(owningViewName)
            counter = counter + 1
            ProcessAnnotation = True
    End Select
End Function

' ============================================
' ОБРАБОТКА ЛИСТА
' ============================================
' v15.14: лист уже активирован и InitWorkArea выполнен вызывающим (один раз на лист).
' Заодно с аннотациями собираются существующие баллоны листа (один обход заметок).
Private Sub ProcessSheetAuto(sName As String, ByRef counter As Long, _
                             ByRef dCnt As Long, ByRef gCnt As Long, ByRef wCnt As Long, ByRef skipCnt As Long, _
                             ByRef nCnt As Long, _
                             ByRef bNotes() As Object, ByRef bNums() As Long, _
                             ByRef bPosX() As Double, ByRef bPosY() As Double, ByRef bSheets() As String, _
                             ByRef bTypes() As Long, ByRef bPtrs() As Long, ByRef bTotal As Long, ByRef bMax As Long, _
                             ByVal balloonSeen As Object)
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    swModel.Extension.SelectAll
    Set swSelMgr = swModel.SelectionManager
    Dim selCount As Long: selCount = swSelMgr.GetSelectedObjectCount2(-1)
    Dim i As Long, selType As Long, prevCount As Long
    ' Keep the existing dimension/GDT/weld scan; notes use a separate view walk.
    For i = 1 To selCount
        selType = swSelMgr.GetSelectedObjectType3(i, -1)
        If selType = swSelDIMENSIONS Or selType = swSelGTOLS Or selType = swSelWELDSYMBOLS Then
            prevCount = positionsCount
            If ProcessAnnotation(selType, swSelMgr.GetSelectedObject6(i, -1), sName, counter) Then
                Select Case selType
                    Case swSelDIMENSIONS: dCnt = dCnt + 1
                    Case swSelGTOLS: gCnt = gCnt + 1
                    Case swSelWELDSYMBOLS: wCnt = wCnt + 1
                End Select
            Else
                If positionsCount = prevCount Then skipCnt = skipCnt + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    ' Run even when SelectAll returned zero objects.
    KC_ScanSheetNotes sName, processNotes, counter, nCnt, skipCnt, _
        bNotes, bNums, bPosX, bPosY, bSheets, bTypes, bPtrs, bTotal, bMax, balloonSeen
End Sub

' ============================================
' БАЛЛОНЫ
' ============================================
Private Sub CreateBalloonsMultiSheet()
    Dim i As Long, currentSheet As String: currentSheet = ""
    Dim swNote As Object, swNoteAnn As Object, textFormat As Object
    Dim balloonX As Double, balloonY As Double
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        If positionsData(i).sheetName <> currentSheet Then
            swDraw.ActivateSheet positionsData(i).sheetName
            currentSheet = positionsData(i).sheetName
            InitWorkArea currentSheet
        End If
        balloonX = positionsData(i).posX
        balloonY = positionsData(i).posY
        ClampToWorkArea balloonX, balloonY
        Set swNote = swModel.InsertNote(CStr(positionsData(i).number))
        If Not swNote Is Nothing Then
            Set swNoteAnn = swNote.GetAnnotation
