            If Not swNoteAnn Is Nothing Then
                swNoteAnn.SetPosition2 balloonX + BALLOON_OFFSET_X, balloonY + BALLOON_OFFSET_Y, 0
                swNote.SetBalloon 1, 2
                swNote.SetTextJustification 1
                Set textFormat = swNoteAnn.GetTextFormat(0)
                If Not textFormat Is Nothing Then
                    textFormat.CharHeight = 0.0035
                    swNoteAnn.SetTextFormat 0, False, textFormat
                End If
                SetBalloonStableKey swNote, BuildAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(i))
            End If
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

' ============================================
' ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНЫЕ
' ============================================
Private Sub ParseSheetSelection(userInput As String, ByRef selected() As Boolean, maxCount As Long)
    Dim parts As Variant, i As Long, part As String
    On Error Resume Next
    userInput = Replace(userInput, " ", "")
    parts = Split(userInput, ",")
    For i = LBound(parts) To UBound(parts)
        part = Trim(CStr(parts(i)))
        If InStr(1, part, "-") > 0 Then
            Dim rp As Variant: rp = Split(part, "-")
            If UBound(rp) >= 1 Then
                Dim rs As Long, re As Long, ri As Long
                rs = CLng(Trim(rp(0))): re = CLng(Trim(rp(1)))
                For ri = rs To re
                    If ri >= 1 And ri <= maxCount Then selected(ri - 1) = True
                Next ri
            End If
        Else
            If IsNumeric(part) Then
                Dim num As Long: num = CLng(part)
                If num >= 1 And num <= maxCount Then selected(num - 1) = True
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function ShouldSkipDimension(swDispDim As Object) As Boolean
    ShouldSkipDimension = False
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim swAnn As Object: Set swAnn = swDispDim.GetAnnotation
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then ShouldSkipDimension = True: Exit Function
    Dim dispData As Object: Set dispData = swAnn.GetDisplayData
    If dispData Is Nothing Then ShouldSkipDimension = True: Exit Function
    Dim txtCount As Long: txtCount = dispData.GetTextCount
    Dim fullText As String: fullText = ""
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To txtCount - 1
        Dim txt As Variant: txt = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(i)
        If Not IsNull(txt) Then fullText = fullText & Trim(CStr(txt))
    Next i
    Dim starCount As Long: starCount = 0
    Dim pos As Long
    For pos = 1 To Len(fullText)
        If Mid(fullText, pos, 1) = "#" Then starCount = starCount + 1
    Next pos
    If starCount > 0 Then ShouldSkipDimension = True: Exit Function
    If Len(Trim(fullText)) = 0 Then ShouldSkipDimension = True: Exit Function
    Dim swDimObj As Object: Set swDimObj = swDispDim.GetDimension2(0)
    If Not swDimObj Is Nothing Then
        If swDimObj.value = 0 Then ShouldSkipDimension = True: Exit Function
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function NoteHasExactlyOneStar(swNote As Object) As Boolean
    NoteHasExactlyOneStar = False
    On Error Resume Next
    If swNote Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim noteText As String: noteText = ""
    noteText = swNote.GetText
    Dim starCount As Long: starCount = 0
    Dim p As Long
    For p = 1 To Len(noteText)
        If Mid(noteText, p, 1) = "#" Then starCount = starCount + 1
    Next p
    If starCount = 1 Then NoteHasExactlyOneStar = True
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

' ============================================
' ЗАМЕТКИ С МАРКЕРОМ "@"
' ============================================
Private Function NoteHasMark(swNote As Object) As Boolean
    NoteHasMark = False
    On Error Resume Next
    If swNote Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim noteText As String: noteText = ""
    noteText = CStr(swNote.GetText)
    If Err.number <> 0 Then Err.Clear: Exit Function
    If InStr(1, noteText, NOTE_MARK) = 0 Then Exit Function
    If IsNumeric(Trim(noteText)) Then Exit Function   ' баллон
    NoteHasMark = True
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function GetMarkedNoteText(swNote As Object) As String
    GetMarkedNoteText = ""
    On Error Resume Next
    If swNote Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim t As String: t = ""
    t = CStr(swNote.GetText)
    If Err.number <> 0 Then Err.Clear: Exit Function
    On Error GoTo 0
    t = Replace(t, NOTE_MARK, "")
    t = Replace(t, vbCrLf, " ")
    t = Replace(t, vbCr, " ")
    t = Replace(t, vbLf, " ")
    t = Replace(t, vbTab, " ")
    t = Replace(t, "<MOD-DIAM>", ChrW(216), , , vbTextCompare)
    t = Replace(t, "<MOD-PM>", Chr(177), , , vbTextCompare)
    t = Replace(t, "<MOD-DEG>", ChrW(176), , , vbTextCompare)
    ' убрать прочие служебные теги вида <...> без пробелов
    Dim p1 As Long, p2 As Long, searchFrom As Long
    searchFrom = 1
    Do
        p1 = InStr(searchFrom, t, "<")
        If p1 = 0 Then Exit Do
        p2 = InStr(p1 + 1, t, ">")
        If p2 = 0 Then Exit Do
        If InStr(1, Mid(t, p1, p2 - p1 + 1), " ") = 0 And (p2 - p1) <= 40 Then
            t = Left(t, p1 - 1) & Mid(t, p2 + 1)
            searchFrom = p1
        Else
            searchFrom = p1 + 1
        End If
    Loop
    Do While InStr(1, t, "  ") > 0: t = Replace(t, "  ", " "): Loop
    t = Replace(t, " .", ".")
    t = Replace(t, " ,", ",")
    GetMarkedNoteText = Trim(t)
End Function

Private Function GetMestWord(count As Long) As String
    Dim lastTwo As Long, lastOne As Long
    lastTwo = count Mod 100: lastOne = count Mod 10
    If lastTwo >= 11 And lastTwo <= 19 Then
        GetMestWord = "мест"
    ElseIf lastOne = 1 Then
        GetMestWord = "место"
    ElseIf lastOne >= 2 And lastOne <= 4 Then
        GetMestWord = "места"
    Else
        GetMestWord = "мест"
    End If
End Function

Private Function IsPlacesSegment(s As String) As Boolean
    Dim t As String: t = Trim(s)
    Dim i As Long, ch As String, digits As String, rest As String
    digits = "": i = 1
    Do While i <= Len(t)
        ch = Mid(t, i, 1)
        If ch Like "[0-9]" Then
            digits = digits & ch
            i = i + 1
        Else
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop
    rest = LCase(Trim(Mid(t, i)))
    IsPlacesSegment = (Len(digits) > 0) And _
        (rest = "" Or rest = "место" Or rest = "места" Or rest = "мест")
End Function

Private Function ExtractPlacesNumber(s As String) As Long
    Dim numStr As String, i As Long, ch As String
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        ch = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If ch Like "[0-9]" Then
            numStr = numStr & ch
        ElseIf Len(numStr) > 0 Then
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If Len(numStr) > 0 Then ExtractPlacesNumber = CLng(numStr) Else ExtractPlacesNumber = 0
End Function

' Проверяет, заканчивается ли накопленный текст открытым тегом <GWLD-FILL>/<GWLD-FILA>/<GWLD-FILB>
' (значение катета ещё не вставлено в этот текст).
Private Function EndsWithOpenFillTag(s As String) As Boolean
    EndsWithOpenFillTag = (Right(s, 11) = "<GWLD-FILL>") Or _
                          (Right(s, 11) = "<WELD-FILL>") Or _
                          (Right(s, 11) = "<GWLD-FILA>") Or _
                          (Right(s, 11) = "<GWLD-FILB>")
End Function

Private Function RemoveWeldTags(inputText As String) As String
    Dim result As String: result = inputText
    Dim tags As Variant
    tags = Array("<GWLD-BEAD>", "<GWLD-PLUG>", "<GWLD-SPOT>", "<GWLD-SEAM>", "<GWLD-BACK>", "<GWLD-MELT>", _
        "<GWLD-FLNG>", "<GWLD-SURF>", "<GWLD-FLARE>", "<GWLD-SCRF>", "<GWLD-BRZE>", "<GWLD-EDGE>", _
        "<GWLD-J>", "<GWLD-U>", "<GWLD-V>", "<GWLD-SQ>", "<GWLD-FLRV>", "<GWLD-FLRB>", _
        "<GWLD-STUD>", "<GWLD-SPTF>", "<GWLD-SPTP>", "<GWLD-NAA>", "<GWLD-NAB>", "<GWLD-NAC>", _
        "<GWLD-FLDA>", "<GWLD-FLDB>", "<GWLD-WRPA>", "<GWLD-WRPB>", _
        "<GWLD-MTHRU>", "<GWLD-FFIN>", "<GWLD-CNVX>", "<GWLD-FLAT>", "<GWLD-CNCV>")
    Dim t As Long
    For t = 0 To UBound(tags)
        result = Replace(result, CStr(tags(t)), "", , , vbTextCompare)
    Next t
    Dim startPos As Long, endPos As Long, tagContent As String, searchFrom As Long
    searchFrom = 1
    Do
        startPos = InStr(searchFrom, result, "<GWLD-", vbTextCompare)
        If startPos = 0 Then Exit Do
        endPos = InStr(startPos, result, ">")
        If endPos > 0 Then
            tagContent = UCase(Mid(result, startPos, endPos - startPos + 1))
            If tagContent = "<GWLD-FILL>" Or tagContent = "<WELD-FILL>" Or tagContent = "<GWLD-FILA>" Or tagContent = "<GWLD-FILB>" Then
                searchFrom = endPos + 1
            Else
                result = Left(result, startPos - 1) & Mid(result, endPos + 1)
                searchFrom = startPos
            End If
        Else
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop
    result = Replace(result, " | ", " ")
    result = Trim(result)
    Do While Left(result, 1) = "|": result = Trim(Mid(result, 2)): Loop
    Do While Right(result, 1) = "|": result = Trim(Left(result, Len(result) - 1)): Loop
    Do While InStr(1, result, "  ") > 0: result = Replace(result, "  ", " "): Loop
    RemoveWeldTags = Trim(result)
End Function

Private Function ParseWeldPlaces(ByRef textToClean As String) As Long
    Dim barPos As Long, afterBar As String, numStr As String, ch As String, i As Long
    ParseWeldPlaces = 0
    barPos = InStrRev(textToClean, "|")
    If barPos = 0 Then barPos = InStrRev(textToClean, "¦")
    If barPos = 0 Then Exit Function
    afterBar = Trim(Mid(textToClean, barPos + 1))
    numStr = ""
    For i = 1 To Len(afterBar)
        ch = Mid(afterBar, i, 1)
        If ch Like "[0-9]" Then
            numStr = numStr & ch
        ElseIf Len(numStr) > 0 Then
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If Len(numStr) > 0 And IsNumeric(numStr) Then
        ParseWeldPlaces = CLng(numStr)
        textToClean = Trim(Left(textToClean, barPos - 1))
    End If
End Function

Private Function BuildParamText(pd As PositionData) As String
    Dim paramText As String
    paramText = CStr(pd.number) & ". "
    Select Case pd.AnnotationType
        Case swSelNOTES
            Dim noteTxt As String: noteTxt = Trim(pd.AnnotationText)
            If Len(noteTxt) > 0 Then
                If InStr(1, ".!?", Right(noteTxt, 1)) = 0 Then noteTxt = noteTxt & "."
            End If
            paramText = paramText & noteTxt
            If Len(pd.viewName) > 0 Then paramText = paramText & " " & pd.viewName & "."
        Case swSelDIMENSIONS
            paramText = paramText & pd.AnnotationText & " мм."
            If Len(pd.viewName) > 0 Then paramText = paramText & " " & pd.viewName & "."
        Case swSelGTOLS
            paramText = paramText & pd.GDTSymbolName
            If Len(pd.GDTValue) > 0 Then
                paramText = paramText & " " & pd.GDTValue
            ElseIf pd.NominalValue <> 0 Then
                paramText = paramText & " " & CStr(pd.NominalValue)
            End If
            paramText = paramText & " мм."
            If Len(pd.viewName) > 0 Then paramText = paramText & " " & pd.viewName & "."
        Case swSelWELDSYMBOLS
            Dim weldDesc As String: weldDesc = ""
            If Len(pd.WeldStandard) > 0 Then weldDesc = pd.WeldStandard
            If Len(pd.weldType) > 0 Then
                If Len(weldDesc) > 0 Then weldDesc = weldDesc & " "
                weldDesc = weldDesc & pd.weldType
            End If
            
            If pd.weldLeg > 0 Then
                weldDesc = weldDesc & "-<GWLD-FILL>" & FormatNum(pd.weldLeg)
                
                Dim tolDisplay As String: tolDisplay = ""
                If pd.tolPlus <> 0 And pd.tolMinus <> 0 Then
                    Dim atp As String, atm As String
                    atp = FormatNum(Abs(pd.tolPlus)): atm = FormatNum(Abs(pd.tolMinus))
                    If Abs(pd.tolPlus) = Abs(pd.tolMinus) Then
                        tolDisplay = Chr(177) & atp
                    Else
                        tolDisplay = "+" & atp & "/-" & atm
                    End If
                ElseIf pd.tolPlus <> 0 Then
                    tolDisplay = "+" & FormatNum(Abs(pd.tolPlus))
                ElseIf pd.tolMinus <> 0 Then
                    tolDisplay = "-" & FormatNum(Abs(pd.tolMinus))
                End If
                weldDesc = weldDesc & tolDisplay
            End If
            
            If Len(Trim(weldDesc)) = 0 Then
                Dim rawText As String: rawText = pd.AnnotationText
                weldDesc = CleanNonStandardWeldText(rawText)
            End If
            
            Dim pc As Long: pc = pd.weldPlaces
            
            Dim isNonStdWeld As Boolean
            isNonStdWeld = (Len(pd.weldType) = 0) And _
                (InStr(1, pd.WeldStandard, "14771") = 0) And _
                (InStr(1, pd.WeldStandard, "23518") = 0)

            If isNonStdWeld Then
                Dim nonStdViewPart As String
                If Len(pd.viewName) > 0 Then
                    nonStdViewPart = pd.viewName & "."
                Else
                    nonStdViewPart = ""
                End If
                paramText = paramText & "Размер нестандартного шва. " & nonStdViewPart
            Else
                If pd.weldLeg > 0 Then
                    paramText = paramText & "Размер шва " & weldDesc & " мм."
                    If Len(pd.WeldIntermittentSpec) > 0 Then
                        Dim ispec As String: ispec = pd.WeldIntermittentSpec
                        If Left(ispec, 1) = "-" Then ispec = Mid(ispec, 2)
                        paramText = paramText & " - " & ispec & " мм."
                    End If
                Else
                    paramText = paramText & "Размер шва " & weldDesc & "."
                End If
            End If
            
            If pc > 0 Then paramText = paramText & " " & CStr(pc) & " " & GetMestWord(pc) & "."
            
            If Not isNonStdWeld Then
                If Len(pd.viewName) > 0 Then paramText = paramText & " " & pd.viewName & "."
            End If
    End Select
    BuildParamText = paramText
End Function


' ============================================
' ОБНОВЛЕНИЕ НОМЕРА ПОЗИЦИИ В СОХРАНЁННОМ ТЕКСТЕ
' ============================================
Private Function UpdateParamTextPositionNumber(paramText As String, newNumber As Long) As String
    Dim dotPos As Long
    Dim oldNumberText As String
    dotPos = InStr(1, paramText, ".")
    If dotPos > 1 Then
        oldNumberText = Trim(Left(paramText, dotPos - 1))
        If IsNumeric(oldNumberText) Then
            UpdateParamTextPositionNumber = CStr(newNumber) & Mid(paramText, dotPos)
            Exit Function
        End If
    End If
    UpdateParamTextPositionNumber = paramText
End Function

' ============================================
' НОРМАЛИЗАЦИЯ ТЕКСТА ДЛЯ СРАВНЕНИЯ
' ============================================
Private Function NormalizeForCompare(text As String) As String
    Dim s As String
    s = LCase(Trim(text))
    s = Replace(s, ChrW(216), "d")
    s = Replace(s, ChrW(248), "d")
    s = Replace(s, Chr(177), "+-")
    s = Replace(s, ",", ".")
    Do While InStr(1, s, "  ") > 0
        s = Replace(s, "  ", " ")
    Loop
    NormalizeForCompare = s
End Function

' ============================================
' СТАБИЛЬНЫЕ КЛЮЧИ БАЛЛОНОВ
' ============================================
Private Function FormatInvariantDouble(val As Double, Optional digits As Long = 4) As String
    Dim mult As Double
    mult = 10 ^ digits
    FormatInvariantDouble = Replace(CStr(Round(val * mult) / mult), ",", ".")
End Function

Private Function StableHash(rawText As String) As String
    Dim h1 As Double, h2 As Double
    Dim i As Long, code As Long
    Dim modVal As Double
    modVal = 2147483647#
    h1 = 5381#
    h2 = 7#
    For i = 1 To Len(rawText)
        code = AscW(Mid(rawText, i, 1))
        If code < 0 Then code = code + 65536
        h1 = h1 * 33# + code
        h1 = h1 - Int(h1 / modVal) * modVal
        h2 = h2 * 131# + code
        h2 = h2 - Int(h2 / modVal) * modVal
    Next i
    StableHash = Right("00000000" & Hex(CLng(h1)), 8) & Right("00000000" & Hex(CLng(h2)), 8)
End Function

' Старый ключ v15.12 (с координатами). Используется только для совместимости
' и как запасной вариант для аннотаций без однозначного имени.
Private Function BuildLegacyAnnotationStableKey(pd As PositionData) As String
    Dim rawKey As String
    rawKey = CStr(pd.AnnotationType) & "|" & _
             NormalizeForCompare(pd.sheetName) & "|" & _
             NormalizeForCompare(pd.viewName) & "|" & _
             NormalizeForCompare(pd.AnnotationText) & "|" & _
             FormatInvariantDouble(pd.NominalValue, 3) & "|" & _
             FormatInvariantDouble(pd.tolPlus, 3) & "|" & _
             FormatInvariantDouble(pd.tolMinus, 3) & "|" & _
             NormalizeForCompare(pd.DimPrefix) & "|" & _
             NormalizeForCompare(pd.GDTSymbolName) & "|" & _
             NormalizeForCompare(pd.GDTValue) & "|" & _
             NormalizeForCompare(pd.WeldStandard) & "|" & _
             NormalizeForCompare(pd.weldType) & "|" & _
             FormatInvariantDouble(pd.weldLeg, 3) & "|" & _
             FormatInvariantDouble(pd.posX, 4) & "|" & _
             FormatInvariantDouble(pd.posY, 4)
    BuildLegacyAnnotationStableKey = "KC_B_" & StableHash(rawKey)
End Function

' v15.13: ключ баллона = тип + лист + внутреннее имя исходной аннотации SolidWorks.
' Координаты, текст и вид в ключ не входят, поэтому перемещение размера/заметки
' (вручную, расстановкой или откатом пробного сдвига) не ломает привязку.
Private Function BuildAnnotationStableKey(pd As PositionData) As String
    If Len(pd.stableKey) > 0 Then
        BuildAnnotationStableKey = pd.stableKey
    Else
        BuildAnnotationStableKey = BuildLegacyAnnotationStableKey(pd)
    End If
End Function

Private Function KC_AnnotationName(ByVal ann As Object) As String
    Dim nameText As String
    On Error Resume Next
    If ann Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    nameText = CStr(ann.GetName)
    If Err.number <> 0 Then nameText = ""
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
    ' Баллон КК не может быть источником.
    If Left$(nameText, 5) = "KC_B_" Or Left$(nameText, 6) = "KC_B2_" Then nameText = ""
    KC_AnnotationName = Trim$(nameText)
End Function

Private Function KC_IdentityRaw(pd As PositionData) As String
    KC_IdentityRaw = "KC_ID2|" & CStr(pd.AnnotationType) & "|" & _
                     NormalizeForCompare(pd.sheetName) & "|" & LCase$(Trim$(pd.sourceName))
End Function

' Назначает каждой позиции уникальный ключ. Совпадающие имена уточняются
' persistent reference; если и он недоступен — старый ключ с координатами.
Private Sub KC_AssignStableKeys(ByRef identityCount As Long, ByRef fallbackCount As Long)
    Dim seen As Object, usedKeys As Object, i As Long, raw As String, hexText As String, key As String, pinned As String
    identityCount = 0: fallbackCount = 0
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set usedKeys = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        positionsData(i).stableKey = ""
        If Len(positionsData(i).sourceName) > 0 Then
            raw = KC_IdentityRaw(positionsData(i))
            If seen.Exists(raw) Then
                seen(raw) = CLng(seen(raw)) + 1
            Else
                seen.Add raw, 1
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    ' Global source counts are collected once from all ordinary KE, independent of selected types/sheets.
    If Not gKRSourceCounts Is Nothing Then Set seen = gKRSourceCounts
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        key = "": pinned = KR_PinnedKey(gKRBalloonPlans, positionsData(i).SourceAnnotation)
        If Len(pinned) > 0 Then
            If usedKeys.Exists(pinned) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4101, , "Повторное назначение одного ключа баллона."
            positionsData(i).stableKey = pinned: usedKeys.Add pinned, i
            identityCount = identityCount + 1
            GoTo NextAssignedKey
        End If
        If Len(positionsData(i).sourceName) > 0 Then
            raw = KC_IdentityRaw(positionsData(i))
            If Not seen.Exists(raw) Then seen.Add raw, 1
            If CLng(seen(raw)) > 1 Then
                hexText = ""
                If Not positionsData(i).SourceAnnotation Is Nothing Then hexText = KC_GetPersistentHex(positionsData(i).SourceAnnotation)
                If Len(hexText) > 0 Then raw = raw & "|" & hexText Else raw = ""
            End If
            If Len(raw) > 0 Then
                key = "KC_B2_" & StableHash(raw)
                If usedKeys.Exists(key) Then key = ""
            End If
        End If
        If Len(key) = 0 Then
            key = BuildLegacyAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(i))
            fallbackCount = fallbackCount + 1
        Else
            identityCount = identityCount + 1
        End If
        If Len(key) = 0 Or usedKeys.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4701, , _
            "Неуникальный или пустой ключ источника. Номера не менялись; проверьте дубли исходных объектов."
        usedKeys.Add key, i
        positionsData(i).stableKey = key
NextAssignedKey:
    Next i
End Sub

' Для запасного ключа с координатами — пересчитать после сдвига исходной аннотации.
Private Sub KC_RefreshFallbackKey(ByVal idx As Long)
    If Left$(positionsData(idx).stableKey, 5) = "KC_B_" Then
        positionsData(idx).stableKey = BuildLegacyAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(idx))
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub SetBalloonStableKey(swNote As Object, stableKey As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    If swNote Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If Len(stableKey) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim swAnn As Object
    Set swAnn = swNote.GetAnnotation
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Err.Clear
    swAnn.SetName2 stableKey
    Err.Clear
    If GetBalloonStableKey(swNote) <> stableKey Then
        Err.Clear
        swAnn.SetName stableKey
    End If
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function GetBalloonStableKey(swNote As Object) As String
    GetBalloonStableKey = ""
    On Error Resume Next
    If swNote Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim swAnn As Object
    Set swAnn = swNote.GetAnnotation
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim annName As String
    annName = ""
    Err.Clear
    annName = CStr(swAnn.GetName)
    If Err.number <> 0 Then Err.Clear: annName = ""
    If Len(annName) > 0 Then
        If Left(annName, 5) = "KC_B_" Or Left(annName, 6) = "KC_B2_" Then GetBalloonStableKey = annName
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub ResetPreparedMerge()
    gUsePreparedMerge = False
    gPreparedMergeMode = 0
    gPreparedOldCount = 0
    gPreparedOldBlockCount = 0
    gPreparedOldFilePath = ""
    Erase gPreparedOldData
    Erase gPreparedOldCardBlocks
    Erase gPreparedMatchResults
    Erase gPreparedMatchIdx
End Sub

' ============================================
' ПОИСК СОВПАДЕНИЯ В СТАРОМ EXCEL
' ============================================
Private Function FindMatchInOldData(newAnnotText As String, newNominal As Double, _
                                     newPrefix As String, newType As Long, _
                                     oldData() As OldExcelRow, oldCount As Long, _
                                     ByRef matchType As Long, _
                                     Optional newSheetName As String = "", _
                                     Optional newViewName As String = "") As Long
    
    FindMatchInOldData = 0
    matchType = 0
    Dim newNorm As String
    newNorm = NormalizeForCompare(newAnnotText)
    Dim newTypeStr As String
    Select Case newType
        Case swSelDIMENSIONS: newTypeStr = "Размер"
        Case swSelGTOLS: newTypeStr = "Геом.допуск"
        Case swSelWELDSYMBOLS: newTypeStr = "Сварка"
        Case swSelNOTES: newTypeStr = "Заметка"
        Case Else: newTypeStr = ""
    End Select
    
    Dim i As Long
    Dim bestIdx As Long: bestIdx = 0
    Dim bestScore As Long: bestScore = -1
    Dim score As Long
    
    For i = 1 To oldCount
        If Not oldData(i).matched Then
            If NormalizeForCompare(oldData(i).AnnotationText) = newNorm Then
                score = 100
                If Len(newTypeStr) > 0 And oldData(i).AnnotationType = newTypeStr Then score = score + 30
                If Len(newSheetName) > 0 And Len(oldData(i).sheetName) > 0 Then
                    If NormalizeForCompare(oldData(i).sheetName) = NormalizeForCompare(newSheetName) Then score = score + 20
                End If
                If Len(newViewName) > 0 And Len(oldData(i).viewName) > 0 Then
                    If NormalizeForCompare(oldData(i).viewName) = NormalizeForCompare(newViewName) Then score = score + 10
                End If
                If Abs(oldData(i).NominalValue - newNominal) < 0.01 Then score = score + 5
                If NormalizeForCompare(oldData(i).DimPrefix) = NormalizeForCompare(newPrefix) Then score = score + 5
                If score > bestScore Then
                    bestScore = score
                    bestIdx = i
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    If bestIdx > 0 Then
        FindMatchInOldData = bestIdx
        matchType = 1
        oldData(bestIdx).matched = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' Для заметок частичное совпадение (по номиналу) не имеет смысла - только точное по тексту
    If newType = swSelNOTES Then Exit Function
    
    bestIdx = 0
    bestScore = -1
    For i = 1 To oldCount
        If Not oldData(i).matched Then
            If oldData(i).AnnotationType = newTypeStr Then
                If Abs(oldData(i).NominalValue - newNominal) < 0.01 Then
                    If NormalizeForCompare(oldData(i).DimPrefix) = NormalizeForCompare(newPrefix) Then
                        score = 50
                        If Len(newSheetName) > 0 And Len(oldData(i).sheetName) > 0 Then
                            If NormalizeForCompare(oldData(i).sheetName) = NormalizeForCompare(newSheetName) Then score = score + 20
                        End If
                        If Len(newViewName) > 0 And Len(oldData(i).viewName) > 0 Then
                            If NormalizeForCompare(oldData(i).viewName) = NormalizeForCompare(newViewName) Then score = score + 10
                        End If
                        If score > bestScore Then
                            bestScore = score
                            bestIdx = i
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    If bestIdx > 0 Then
        FindMatchInOldData = bestIdx
        matchType = 2
        oldData(bestIdx).matched = True
    End If
End Function

' ============================================
' ЧТЕНИЕ СТАРОГО EXCEL
' ============================================
Private Function ReadOldExcelData(filePath As String, ByRef oldData() As OldExcelRow, _
    ByRef oldCount As Long, ByRef oldCardBlocks() As OldCardBlock, ByRef oldBlockCount As Long) As Boolean
    
    ReadOldExcelData = False
    oldCount = 0
    oldBlockCount = 0
    Dim xlApp As Object, xlWB As Object
    Dim owns As Boolean, detail As String
    Dim wsRaw As Object, wsCard As Object
    Dim rawValues As Variant, cardValues As Variant, introLast As Long
    
    On Error GoTo ReadError
    If Not gWorkBook Is Nothing Then
        Set xlApp = gWorkApp: Set xlWB = gWorkBook
    Else
        Set xlApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
        xlApp.Visible = True: xlApp.AutomationSecurity = 3: xlApp.EnableEvents = False
        Set xlWB = xlApp.Workbooks.Open(filePath, 0, True)
        owns = True
    End If

    Dim wsFound As Boolean: wsFound = False
    Dim ws As Object
    For Each ws In xlWB.sheets
        If ws.name = "Данные из чертежа" Then
            Set wsRaw = ws
            wsFound = True
            Exit For
        End If
    Next
    
    If wsFound Then
        Dim lastRow As Long
        lastRow = wsRaw.cells(wsRaw.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        If lastRow >= 2 Then
            oldCount = lastRow - 1
            ReDim oldData(1 To oldCount)
            rawValues = wsRaw.Range("A2:Z" & CStr(lastRow)).value
            Dim r As Long
            For r = 1 To oldCount
                Dim xlR As Long: xlR = r + 1
                On Error Resume Next
                oldData(r).posNumber = CLng(val(CStr(rawValues(r, 1))))
                If oldData(r).posNumber = 0 Then oldData(r).posNumber = r
                oldData(r).AnnotationText = Trim(CStr(rawValues(r, 3)))
                oldData(r).NominalValue = 0
                Dim tmpNom As Variant
                tmpNom = rawValues(r, 4)
                If IsNumeric(tmpNom) Then oldData(r).NominalValue = CDbl(tmpNom)
                oldData(r).DimPrefix = Trim(CStr(rawValues(r, 8)))
                oldData(r).AnnotationType = Trim(CStr(rawValues(r, 2)))
                oldData(r).ToolName = Trim(CStr(rawValues(r, 17)))
                oldData(r).ToolCode = ""
                oldData(r).volumeStr = Trim(CStr(rawValues(r, 19)))
                oldData(r).timeTv = Trim(CStr(rawValues(r, 20)))
                oldData(r).sheetName = Trim(CStr(rawValues(r, 23)))
                oldData(r).viewName = Trim(CStr(rawValues(r, 24)))
                oldData(r).paramText = ""
                oldData(r).matched = False
                On Error GoTo ReadError
            Next r
        End If
    End If
    
    Dim wsCardFound As Boolean: wsCardFound = False
    For Each ws In xlWB.sheets
        If ws.name = "Карта контроля" Then
            Set wsCard = ws
            wsCardFound = True
            Exit For
        End If
    Next
    
    If wsCardFound Then
        Dim lastCardRow As Long
        lastCardRow = wsCard.cells(wsCard.rows.count, 2).End(-4162).row
        If lastCardRow >= 7 Then cardValues = wsCard.Range("A7:E" & CStr(lastCardRow)).value
        introLast = KC15_IntroLast(wsCard)
        Dim endDataRow As Long: endDataRow = lastCardRow
        Dim scanRow As Long
        For scanRow = 7 To lastCardRow
            On Error Resume Next
            Dim cellA As String
            cellA = Trim(CStr(cardValues(scanRow - 6, 1)))
            On Error GoTo ReadError
            If cellA = "ОК" Or cellA = "OK" Then
                endDataRow = scanRow - 1
                Exit For
            End If
        Next scanRow
        
        If endDataRow >= 7 Then
            Dim maxBlocks As Long: maxBlocks = (endDataRow - 6) \ 1 + 1
            ReDim oldCardBlocks(1 To maxBlocks)
            Dim blockIdx As Long: blockIdx = 0
            Dim cRow As Long: cRow = 7
            
            Do While cRow <= endDataRow
                Dim cellAVal As String
                On Error Resume Next
                cellAVal = Trim(CStr(cardValues(cRow - 6, 1)))
                On Error GoTo ReadError
                
                If Len(cellAVal) > 0 And cellAVal <> "ОК" And cellAVal <> "OK" _
                    And cRow > introLast Then
                    blockIdx = blockIdx + 1
                    If blockIdx > UBound(oldCardBlocks) Then
                        ReDim Preserve oldCardBlocks(1 To blockIdx + 50)
                    End If
                    
                    On Error Resume Next
                    oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).posNumber = CLng(val(cellAVal))
                    oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).paramText = Trim(CStr(cardValues(cRow - 6, 2)))
                    oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).mainTool = Trim(CStr(cardValues(cRow - 6, 3)))
                    oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).volumeStr = Trim(CStr(cardValues(cRow - 6, 4)))
                    oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).timeTv = Trim(CStr(cardValues(cRow - 6, 5)))
                    oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).extraToolCount = 0
                    On Error GoTo ReadError
                    
                    Dim subRow As Long
                    For subRow = 1 To 3
                        If cRow + subRow > endDataRow Then Exit For
                        Dim subAVal As String
                        On Error Resume Next
                        subAVal = Trim(CStr(cardValues(cRow + subRow - 6, 1)))
                        On Error GoTo ReadError
                        If Len(subAVal) > 0 And subAVal <> "0" Then Exit For
                        Dim extraTool As String
                        On Error Resume Next
                        extraTool = Trim(CStr(cardValues(cRow + subRow - 6, 3)))
                        On Error GoTo ReadError
                        If Len(extraTool) > 0 Then
                            oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).extraToolCount = oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).extraToolCount + 1
                            If oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).extraToolCount <= 3 Then
                                oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).extraTools(oldCardBlocks(blockIdx).extraToolCount) = extraTool
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next subRow
                    cRow = cRow + 1
                Else
                    cRow = cRow + 1
                End If
            Loop
            oldBlockCount = blockIdx
        End If
        
        If oldCount > 0 And oldBlockCount > 0 Then
            Dim bi As Long
            For bi = 1 To oldBlockCount
                If bi <= oldCount Then
                    If Len(oldCardBlocks(bi).mainTool) > 0 Then
                        oldData(bi).ToolName = oldCardBlocks(bi).mainTool
                    End If
                    If Len(oldCardBlocks(bi).timeTv) > 0 Then
                        oldData(bi).timeTv = oldCardBlocks(bi).timeTv
                    End If
                    oldData(bi).paramText = oldCardBlocks(bi).paramText
                    Dim extraStr As String: extraStr = ""
                    Dim ei As Long
                    For ei = 1 To oldCardBlocks(bi).extraToolCount
                        If ei <= 3 Then
                            If Len(extraStr) > 0 Then extraStr = extraStr & "|"
                            extraStr = extraStr & oldCardBlocks(bi).extraTools(ei)
                        End If
                    Next ei
                    oldData(bi).ToolCode = extraStr
                End If
            Next bi
        End If
    End If
    
    Set wsRaw = Nothing: Set wsCard = Nothing: Set ws = Nothing
    If owns Then
        xlWB.Close False
        xlApp.Quit
    End If
    Set xlWB = Nothing: Set xlApp = Nothing
    ReadOldExcelData = True
    Exit Function
ReadError:
    detail = "Чтение прежних данных: " & Err.number & " | " & Err.Description
    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, detail
    MsgBox detail & vbCrLf & "Книга не закрывается автоматически. Сохраните копию при необходимости.", vbCritical
    ReadOldExcelData = False
End Function

' ============================================
' ПОДГОТОВКА СТАРОГО EXCEL
' ============================================
Private Function PrepareOldExcelMergeForCurrentPositions() As Boolean
    Dim pr As Long, mType As Long, idx As Long
    ResetPreparedMerge
    If gWorkBook Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3020, , "Нет рабочей книги."
    KC14_Snapshot gWorkBook
    If Not ReadOldExcelData(gIntroConfigPath, gPreparedOldData, gPreparedOldCount, gPreparedOldCardBlocks, gPreparedOldBlockCount) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3021, , "Не удалось прочитать предыдущие данные рабочей книги; обновление отменено."
    If gPreparedOldCount <= 0 Then Exit Function
    ReDim gPreparedMatchResults(1 To positionsCount): ReDim gPreparedMatchIdx(1 To positionsCount)
    Dim j As Long, candidates As Long, kind As String
    Dim oldText() As String, oldPrefix() As String, currentText As String, currentPrefix As String
    ReDim oldText(1 To gPreparedOldCount): ReDim oldPrefix(1 To gPreparedOldCount)
    For j = 1 To gPreparedOldCount
        oldText(j) = NormalizeForCompare(gPreparedOldData(j).AnnotationText)
        oldPrefix(j) = NormalizeForCompare(gPreparedOldData(j).DimPrefix)
    Next j
    For pr = 1 To positionsCount
        currentText = NormalizeForCompare(positionsData(pr).AnnotationText)
        currentPrefix = NormalizeForCompare(positionsData(pr).DimPrefix)
        idx = 0: candidates = 0
        Select Case positionsData(pr).AnnotationType
            Case swSelDIMENSIONS: kind = "Размер"
            Case swSelGTOLS: kind = "Геом.допуск"
            Case swSelWELDSYMBOLS: kind = "Сварка"
            Case swSelNOTES: kind = "Заметка"
            Case Else: kind = ""
        End Select
        For j = 1 To gPreparedOldCount
            If oldText(j) = currentText And _
                gPreparedOldData(j).AnnotationType = kind And _
                StrComp(gPreparedOldData(j).sheetName, positionsData(pr).sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 And _
                StrComp(gPreparedOldData(j).viewName, positionsData(pr).viewName, vbTextCompare) = 0 And _
                Abs(gPreparedOldData(j).NominalValue - positionsData(pr).NominalValue) < 0.000001 And _
                oldPrefix(j) = currentPrefix Then
                candidates = candidates + 1: idx = j
            End If
        Next j
        If candidates <> 1 Then idx = 0
        If idx > 0 Then
            If gPreparedOldData(idx).matched Then idx = 0
        End If
        If idx > 0 Then
            gPreparedOldData(idx).matched = True
            gPreparedMatchResults(pr) = 1
        End If
        gPreparedMatchIdx(pr) = idx
    Next pr
    KC50_MatchMovedOldRows
    For j = 1 To gPreparedOldCount
        If Not gPreparedOldData(j).matched Then gMergeSkipped = gMergeSkipped + 1
    Next j
    gUsePreparedMerge = True: gPreparedMergeMode = 3
    gPreparedOldFilePath = gIntroConfigPath
    PrepareOldExcelMergeForCurrentPositions = True
End Function

' ============================================
' ТОЧКА ВХОДА
' ============================================
Sub Main()
    KC53_MainCore
    KC53_Finish
End Sub

Private Sub KC53_MainCore()
    On Error GoTo RunError
    Dim modeInput As String, detail As String
    KC_ProgressConnect
    Do
        KC_ProgressWrite "menu", 0, "Выбор режима — ответьте в окне SolidWorks"
        Set swApp = Application.SldWorks
        Set swModel = swApp.ActiveDoc
        If swModel Is Nothing Then
            MsgBox "Откройте чертёж!", vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If swModel.GetType <> 3 Then
            MsgBox "Это не чертёж!", vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        Set swDraw = swModel
        KC28_Load swApp
        Set swSelMgr = swModel.SelectionManager
        modeInput = InputBox("Выберите режим работы:" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            " 1 = Баллоны и рабочая карта: создать недостающие," & vbCrLf & _
            "      перенумеровать и обновить карту" & vbCrLf & _
            " 2 = Управление маркерами # и @" & vbCrLf & _
            " 3 = Настройки и дополнительные действия" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "После режимов 2 и 3 это меню откроется снова." & vbCrLf & _
            "Отмена = завершить макрос.", "Карта контроля", "1")
        If modeInput = "" Then
            KC_ProgressWrite "menu", 0, "Выбор режима закрыт. Макрос завершён."
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If Len(swModel.GetPathName) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3060, , "Сначала сохраните чертёж."
        Select Case Trim$(modeInput)
            Case "1"
                KA_Reset
                gBackupMade = False: gMergeSkipped = 0
                KC_ResetIntroConfiguration
                gKCProgressDone = False: gKCCompleted = 0
                RunMode1_BalloonsAndCard
                KC_GraphicsOn
                If Not gKCProgressDone Then KC_ProgressWrite "stopped", gKCCompleted, _
                    "Режим 1 завершён без подтверждения полного выполнения. Проверьте сообщения."
                Exit Sub
            Case "2"
                gStage = "Управление маркерами # и @"
                KC_ProgressWrite "menu", 0, gStage
                RunMode3_StarDims
            Case "3"
                gStage = "Настройки и дополнительные действия"
                KC_ProgressWrite "menu", 0, gStage
                KC14_Advanced
            Case Else
                MsgBox "Неверный выбор!", vbExclamation
        End Select
        KC_GraphicsOn
    Loop
RunError:
    detail = "Этап: " & gStage & vbCrLf & "Ошибка " & Err.number & ": " & Err.Description
    KC_ProgressWrite "error", gKCCompleted, "Ошибка: " & gStage
    On Error Resume Next
    KC_GraphicsOn
    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, detail
    On Error GoTo 0
    MsgBox detail & vbCrLf & "Автоматического закрытия Excel и общего отката чертежа нет. Проверьте текущий результат.", vbCritical
End Sub

Private Sub KC_ProgressConnect()
    Dim shell As Object, fso As Object, stream As Object, descriptor As String
    gKCProgressPath = "": gKCProgressToken = "": gKCCompleted = 0: gKCProgressDone = False
    On Error GoTo Finished
    Set shell = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    descriptor = fso.BuildPath(shell.ExpandEnvironmentStrings("%TEMP%"), "KC_Start_progress_session.txt")
    If Not fso.FileExists(descriptor) Then Exit Sub
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(descriptor, 1, False, -1)
    If stream.ReadLine <> "KC_PROGRESS_V1" Then GoTo Finished
    gKCProgressPath = stream.ReadLine
    gKCProgressToken = stream.ReadLine
    If Len(gKCProgressToken) <> 32 Then gKCProgressPath = ""
Finished:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
End Sub

Private Sub KC_ProgressWrite(ByVal status As String, ByVal completed As Long, ByVal label As String)
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object
    If Len(gKCProgressPath) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ' UI failure must never change drawing/export control flow.
    On Error GoTo Finished
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    Set stream = fso.CreateTextFile(gKCProgressPath, True, True)
    label = Replace(Replace(label, vbCr, " "), vbLf, " ")
    stream.WriteLine gKCProgressToken
    stream.WriteLine status
    stream.WriteLine CStr(completed)
    stream.WriteLine "8"
    stream.WriteLine label
Finished:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
End Sub

Private Sub KC_ProgressStage(ByVal number As Long, ByVal label As String)
    gKCCompleted = number - 1
    KC_ProgressWrite "running", gKCCompleted, label
End Sub

' ============================================
' РЕЖИМ 1: БАЛЛОНЫ + ПЕРЕНУМЕРАЦИЯ + РАБОЧАЯ КАРТА (бывшие режимы 1 и 2)
' ============================================
Private Sub RunMode1_BalloonsAndCard()
    Dim kc46Phase As Double
    kc46Phase = Timer: KC46_Trace "--- Режим 1: начало ---"
    gStage = "Согласование состава КЭ до выбора листов и маркеров"
    KC_ProgressWrite "running", 0, gStage
    KC_GraphicsOff
    If Not KC37_PrepareRegularKE() Then KC_GraphicsOn: Exit Sub
    KC_GraphicsOn
    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Согласование КЭ (включая диалоги)"
    gStage = "Предварительная проверка имён видов"
    KC_ProgressStage 1, "Проверка имён видов на выбранных листах"
    Dim allSheetNames As Variant: allSheetNames = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    Dim si As Long
    Dim selectedSheets() As Boolean
    If gKDForceRebuild Then
        KD_SelectAll allSheetNames, selectedSheets
    Else
        If Not KC14_LoadSelection(allSheetNames, selectedSheets) Then Exit Sub
    End If
    Dim anySelected As Boolean: anySelected = False
    For si = 0 To UBound(allSheetNames)
        If selectedSheets(si) Then anySelected = True
    Next si
    If Not anySelected Then
        MsgBox "Не выбран ни один лист!", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    KC_GraphicsOff
    If Not KC26_CheckViewNames(allSheetNames, selectedSheets) Then KC_GraphicsOn: Exit Sub
    KC_GraphicsOn
    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Выбор листов и проверка видов (включая диалоги)"
    gStage = "Чтение карты и настроек"
    KC_ProgressWrite "running", 0, "Подготовка карты, настроек и маркеров"
    ResetPreparedMerge
    KC_ResetAutomaticMarkers
    If gKDForceRebuild Then
        processDimensions = True: processGtols = True: processWelds = True: processNotes = True
    Else
        If Not KC14_LoadTypes() Then Exit Sub
    End If
    If Not KC_PrepareIntroConfiguration(True) Then Exit Sub
    If KC37_RegularSnapshot(swDraw) <> gKC37PreparedLayout Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3705, , _
        "Состав КЭ изменился во время подготовки карты. Повторите режим 1; маркеры ещё не восстанавливались."
    Dim startNum As Long: startNum = gIntroFirstBalloon
    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Чтение карты и вводных (включая диалоги)"
    KC_GraphicsOff
    gStage = "Восстановление маркеров # и @"
    Set gKC34RefScope = KC_MarkerScope(allSheetNames, selectedSheets)
    If Not KC_PrepareAutomaticMarkers(allSheetNames, selectedSheets) Then Exit Sub
    ' Определение места выполняется ДО перенумерации баллонов и записи Excel.
    If Not KC41_PrepareStampView() Then Exit Sub
    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Восстановление маркеров и место клеймения (включая диалоги)"

    gStage = "Сканирование листов: баллоны и аннотации"
    KC_ProgressStage 2, "Сканирование чертежа: баллоны и аннотации"
    ' v15.14: один проход по всем листам. Выбранные листы: аннотации + баллоны (один обход заметок);
    ' невыбранные листы: только баллоны для проверки пересечения номеров.
    Dim bNotes() As Object, bNums() As Long, bPosX() As Double, bPosY() As Double
    Dim bSheets() As String, bTypes() As Long, bPtrs() As Long
    Dim bTotal As Long: bTotal = 0
    Dim bMax As Long: bMax = 500
    ReDim bNotes(1 To bMax): ReDim bNums(1 To bMax): ReDim bPosX(1 To bMax)
    ReDim bPosY(1 To bMax): ReDim bSheets(1 To bMax): ReDim bTypes(1 To bMax): ReDim bPtrs(1 To bMax)
    Dim oNotes() As Object, oNums() As Long, oPosX() As Double, oPosY() As Double
    Dim oSheets() As String, oTypes() As Long, oPtrs() As Long
    Dim oTotal As Long: oTotal = 0
    Dim oMax As Long: oMax = 500
    ReDim oNotes(1 To oMax): ReDim oNums(1 To oMax): ReDim oPosX(1 To oMax)
    ReDim oPosY(1 To oMax): ReDim oSheets(1 To oMax): ReDim oTypes(1 To oMax): ReDim oPtrs(1 To oMax)
    Dim bSeen As Object: Set bSeen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim oSeen As Object: Set oSeen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim otherScanError As String: otherScanError = ""
    Dim sName As String

    ReDim positionsData(1 To 500)
    positionsCount = 0
    Dim annCounter As Long: annCounter = startNum
    Dim dCnt As Long, gCnt As Long, wCnt As Long, skipCnt As Long, nCnt As Long

    Dim origSheet As String
    origSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    For si = 0 To UBound(allSheetNames)
        sName = CStr(allSheetNames(si))
        If selectedSheets(si) Then
            KC47_RequireSheet swDraw, sName
            InitWorkArea
            KC_ViewCacheReset
            ProcessSheetAuto sName, annCounter, dCnt, gCnt, wCnt, skipCnt, nCnt, _
                bNotes, bNums, bPosX, bPosY, bSheets, bTypes, bPtrs, bTotal, bMax, bSeen
        ElseIf Len(otherScanError) = 0 Then
            otherScanError = KC_ScanOtherSheetBalloons(sName, oNotes, oNums, oPosX, oPosY, _
                oSheets, oTypes, oPtrs, oTotal, oMax, oSeen)
        End If
    Next si
    KC_ViewCacheReset
    KC47_RequireSheet swDraw, origSheet
    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Сканирование листов"
    If positionsCount = 0 Then
        MsgBox "Аннотации не найдены!", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' v15.14: ключи существующих баллонов читаются из SolidWorks один раз.
    Dim bKeys() As String, keyIndex As Object, bi As Long
    ReDim bKeys(1 To IIf(bTotal > 0, bTotal, 1))
    Set keyIndex = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For bi = 1 To bTotal
        bKeys(bi) = GetBalloonStableKey(bNotes(bi))
        If Len(bKeys(bi)) > 0 Then
            If Not keyIndex.Exists(bKeys(bi)) Then keyIndex.Add bKeys(bi), New Collection
            keyIndex.item(bKeys(bi)).Add bi
        End If
    Next bi

    GroupPositions
    Dim identityKeys As Long, fallbackKeys As Long
    KC_AssignStableKeys identityKeys, fallbackKeys
    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Ключи и группировка"

    gStage = "Сопоставление прежней карты"
    KC_ProgressStage 3, "Сопоставление карты, баллонов и проверка номеров"
    gMergeSkipped = 0
    PrepareOldExcelMergeForCurrentPositions
    ' Источник старого номера — последняя успешная выгрузка, а не B4,
    ' которое могло быть записано на предыдущем незавершённом запуске.
    If gPreparedOldCount > 0 Then
        If gPreparedOldData(1).posNumber < 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4424, , "Неверный первый старый номер в листе данных."
        gIntroPreviousCount = gPreparedOldData(1).posNumber - 1
        Dim oldRowIndex As Long
        For oldRowIndex = 1 To gPreparedOldCount
            If gPreparedOldData(oldRowIndex).posNumber <> gIntroPreviousCount + oldRowIndex Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4424, , "Старые номера листа данных непоследовательны. Перенумерация остановлена."
        Next oldRowIndex
    End If
    KD_PrepareMerge
    KD_CheckMerge
    If gMergeSkipped > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4702, , _
        "Не сопоставлены прежние блоки карты: " & gMergeSkipped & _
        ". Перенумерация остановлена для сохранения ручных данных; чертёж и карта не изменены." & _
        vbCrLf & KC52_MergeReport()
    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Сопоставление прежней карты"

    gStage = "Сопоставление баллонов"
    Dim annToBalloon() As Long
    Dim assignedByKey As Long, assignedByLegacy As Long, assignedByExcel As Long, assignedByDistance As Long
    BuildStableBalloonAssignments annToBalloon, keyIndex, bNums, bPosX, bPosY, bSheets, _
        bTotal, assignedByKey, assignedByLegacy, assignedByExcel, assignedByDistance

    Dim preflightReason As String, orphanCount As Long
    If Not KC_ValidateBalloonAssignments(annToBalloon, bNums, bSheets, bTotal, preflightReason, orphanCount) Then
        MsgBox preflightReason & vbCrLf & "Обработка номеров не начата. Раннее согласование имён КЭ и ключей уже могло быть выполнено.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If (gKDForceRebuild Or (gPreparedOldCount > 0 And gIntroPointCount <> gIntroPreviousCount)) And orphanCount > 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4202, , "Остались несопоставленные баллоны: " & orphanCount & _
        ". Перенумерация остановлена до изменения номеров; проверьте привязки."
    If gPreparedOldCount > 0 And gIntroPointCount <> gIntroPreviousCount Then
        If gMergeSkipped > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4425, , _
            "Изменилось количество вводных, но не сопоставлены прежние строки карты: " & gMergeSkipped & _
            ". Перенумерация остановлена во избежание потери ручных правок."
    End If
    If Not KC_CheckOtherSheetNumbers(otherScanError, oNums, oSheets, oTotal, startNum, startNum + positionsCount - 1, preflightReason) Then
        MsgBox preflightReason & vbCrLf & "Обработка номеров не начата. Раннее согласование имён КЭ и ключей уже могло быть выполнено.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Сопоставление баллонов и проверки"
    Dim pi As Long, di As Long, newCount As Long
    For pi = 1 To positionsCount
        If annToBalloon(pi) = 0 Then newCount = newCount + 1
    Next pi

    Dim previewMsg As String
    previewMsg = "Создать недостающие баллоны, перенумеровать и обновить рабочую карту?" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Аннотаций: " & positionsCount & "; вводных пунктов: " & gIntroPointCount & _
        IIf(gPreparedOldCount > 0, " (ранее " & CStr(gIntroPreviousCount) & ")", "") & _
        "; номера: " & startNum & "–" & (startNum + positionsCount - 1) & "." & vbCrLf & _
        "Существующие баллоны: по ключу — " & assignedByKey & _
        "; по старому ключу с координатами — " & assignedByLegacy & _
        "; по прежней карте — " & assignedByExcel & _
        "; по расстоянию — " & assignedByDistance & "." & vbCrLf & _
        "Будет создано новых баллонов: " & newCount & "." & vbCrLf & _
        IIf(orphanCount > 0, "Баллонов вне сопоставления (не меняются): " & orphanCount & "." & vbCrLf, "") & _
        IIf(fallbackKeys > 0, "Аннотаций без однозначного имени (ключ по координатам): " & fallbackKeys & "." & vbCrLf, "") & _
        "Требуют проверки прежних правок: " & gMergeSkipped & "." & vbCrLf & _
        "Работайте на копии чертежа."
    KC_GraphicsOn
    If MsgBox(previewMsg, vbYesNo + vbQuestion, "Карта контроля") <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Предпросмотр (включая ожидание пользователя)"
    KC_GraphicsOff

    gWorkBook.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2 = "PENDING"
    gWorkBook.Save
    KC20_WriteIntroSync swModel, "PENDING"

    gStage = "Перенумерация баллонов"
    KC_ProgressStage 4, "Перенумерация существующих баллонов"
    ' v15.14: по листам — одна активация на лист; ключ пишется, только если он отличается.
    Dim newNum As Long, key As String, currentSheet As String, sheetActive As Boolean
    Dim changed As Long, unchangedNum As Long, renumberErrors As Long, keyWarnings As Long
    For si = 0 To UBound(allSheetNames)
        sName = CStr(allSheetNames(si))
        sheetActive = False
        For pi = 1 To positionsCount
            di = annToBalloon(pi)
            If di > 0 Then
                If bSheets(di) = sName Then
                    If Not sheetActive Then
                        KC47_RequireSheet swDraw, sName
                        sheetActive = True
                    End If
                    newNum = startNum + pi - 1
                    If bNums(di) <> newNum Then
                        If KC_TrySetNoteNumber(bNotes(di), newNum) Then
                            changed = changed + 1
                            bNums(di) = newNum
                        Else
                            renumberErrors = renumberErrors + 1
                        End If
                    Else
                        unchangedNum = unchangedNum + 1
                    End If
                    key = BuildAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(pi))
                    If bKeys(di) <> key Then
                        SetBalloonStableKey bNotes(di), key
                        bKeys(di) = GetBalloonStableKey(bNotes(di))
                        If bKeys(di) <> key Then keyWarnings = keyWarnings + 1
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next pi
    Next si
    KC_GraphicsOn

    KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "PENDING и перенумерация баллонов"
    gStage = "Создание новых баллонов"
    KC_ProgressStage 5, "Создание недостающих баллонов"
    Dim created As Long, failedCount As Long, visionTargets As Long, keyOK As Boolean
    Dim detail As String, report As String
    gKV_AutoCount = 0: gKV_DefaultCount = 0: gKV_SourceMovedCount = 0: gKV_Notes = ""
    report = "Режим 1: баллоны, перенумерация и рабочая карта." & vbCrLf
    If newCount > 0 Then
        KC_VisionBegin
        ' v16.5.15: листы, на которых будут новые баллоны: только они нужны в PDF для поиска места.
        If Not gKV_NeedSheets Is Nothing Then
            For pi = 1 To positionsCount
                If annToBalloon(pi) = 0 Then gKV_NeedSheets(positionsData(pi).sheetName) = True
            Next pi
        End If
        ' v15.14: по листам — одна активация и один InitWorkArea на лист.
        For si = 0 To UBound(allSheetNames)
        sName = CStr(allSheetNames(si))
        currentSheet = ""
        For pi = 1 To positionsCount
            If annToBalloon(pi) = 0 And positionsData(pi).sheetName = sName Then
                If currentSheet <> sName Then
                    currentSheet = sName
                    KC47_RequireSheet swDraw, currentSheet
                    InitWorkArea
                End If
                KC_VisionBeforeNew currentSheet
                keyOK = False: detail = ""
                If KC_CreateControlBalloon(pi, startNum + pi - 1, keyOK, detail) Then
                    created = created + 1
                    If positionsData(pi).AnnotationType = swSelDIMENSIONS Or positionsData(pi).AnnotationType = swSelWELDSYMBOLS Then
                        visionTargets = visionTargets + 1
                    End If
                    If Not keyOK Then keyWarnings = keyWarnings + 1
                Else
                    failedCount = failedCount + 1
                End If
                report = report & currentSheet & " | " & positionsData(pi).viewName & " | " & _
                         positionsData(pi).AnnotationText & " | " & detail & vbCrLf
            End If
        Next pi
        Next si
        KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Создание новых баллонов (включая PDF для расстановки): " & created
        KC_ProgressStage 6, "Поиск свободных мест и проверка переносов"
        KC_VisionFinish report
        KC46_Mark kc46Phase, "Поиск свободных мест и переносы"
        gKV_DefaultCount = visionTargets - gKV_AutoCount
        KC_WriteMarkerReport report, "balloons"
    End If

    KC47_RequireSheet swDraw, origSheet
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2

    If renumberErrors > 0 Or failedCount > 0 Or keyWarnings > 0 Then
        MsgBox "Баллоны обработаны с ошибками. Экспорт в рабочую карту заблокирован." & vbCrLf & _
            "Ошибок перенумерации: " & renumberErrors & "; ошибок создания: " & failedCount & "; ошибок ключей: " & keyWarnings & vbCrLf & _
            "Часть номеров могла измениться; автоматического отката нет. Проверьте чертёж или восстановите резервную копию." & vbCrLf & _
            "Маркеры сохранены.", vbCritical
        Exit Sub
    End If

    gStage = "Проверка итоговых ключей после удаления КЭ"
    KD_CheckResult
    gStage = "Экспорт в рабочую карту"
    KC_ProgressStage 7, "Обновление и сохранение рабочей карты"
    Dim refSummary As String
    If ExportToExcel() Then
        KC_ProgressStage 8, "Согласование карты и завершение работы с маркерами"
        If Not KC33_ApplyNoteRefs(refSummary) Then
            KC46_Trace "НЕ ЗАВЕРШЕНО: ссылки; " & refSummary
            MsgBox "Карта сохранена, но ссылки не согласованы. Состояние PENDING; общего успеха нет." & vbCrLf & _
                refSummary & vbCrLf & "Маркеры не снимались. Проверьте копии перед повтором.", vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If Not KC_CleanupMarkersAfterExport() Then
            KC46_Trace "НЕ ЗАВЕРШЕНО: снятие маркеров; PENDING"
            MsgBox "Снятие маркеров не завершено. Состояние PENDING; резервы не отмечены завершёнными.", vbExclamation
            Exit Sub
        End If
        KC47_RequireSheet swDraw, origSheet
        KC47_CommitCard
        KA_AfterSuccess gWorkBook
        gKCCompleted = 8: gKCProgressDone = True
        KC_ProgressWrite "done", 8, "Выполнение завершено. Проверьте итоговый отчёт."
        MsgBox "Готово. Баллоны: " & startNum & "–" & (startNum + positionsCount - 1) & vbCrLf & _
            IIf(gKDForceRebuild, "Пересборка после удаления: исключено из активной карты позиций " & gKDRemovedCount & "; осталось " & positionsCount & ". Архив сохранён." & vbCrLf, "") & _
            "Создано новых баллонов: " & created & vbCrLf & _
            "Перенумеровано существующих: " & changed & "; без изменения номера: " & unchangedNum & vbCrLf & _
            "Привязано по расстоянию (проверьте): " & assignedByDistance & vbCrLf & _
            IIf(created > 0, "Размещено у текста/линии: " & gKV_AutoCount & "; в стандартных местах: " & gKV_DefaultCount & _
                "; исходных размеров/швов сдвинуто: " & gKV_SourceMovedCount & vbCrLf, "") & _
            IIf(keyWarnings > 0, "ВНИМАНИЕ: не сохранён ключ привязки у баллонов: " & keyWarnings & vbCrLf, "") & _
            IIf(orphanCount > 0, "Баллонов вне сопоставления (номера вне диапазона, не менялись): " & orphanCount & vbCrLf, "") & _
            IIf(Len(gKV_Notes) > 0, "Внимание: " & Left$(gKV_Notes, 220) & vbCrLf, "") & _
            IIf(Len(refSummary) > 0, refSummary & vbCrLf, "") & _
            "Рабочая карта обновлена. Требуют проверки прежних правок: " & gMergeSkipped & "." & vbCrLf & _
            "Чертёж автоматически НЕ сохранён. READY не подтверждает сохранение двух файлов. Сохраните и проверьте копии.", vbInformation
    End If
    If Not gKCProgressDone Then KC47_RequireSheet swDraw, origSheet
End Sub

' ============================================
' СОПОСТАВЛЕНИЕ БАЛЛОНОВ
' ============================================
' v15.14: поиск по словарю ключ -> индексы баллонов (в порядке сканирования).
' Правило прежнее: сначала свободный баллон того же листа, затем любой свободный.
Private Function FindBalloonByStableKey(stableKey As String, _
                    ByVal keyIndex As Object, _
                    ByRef bSheets() As String, _
                    ByRef assignedBal() As Boolean, _
                    sheetName As String) As Long
    FindBalloonByStableKey = 0
    If Len(stableKey) = 0 Then Exit Function
    If keyIndex Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If Not keyIndex.Exists(stableKey) Then Exit Function
    Dim list As Collection, item As Variant, fb As Long
    Set list = keyIndex(stableKey)
    For Each item In list
        fb = CLng(item)
        If Not assignedBal(fb) Then
            If bSheets(fb) = sheetName Then
                FindBalloonByStableKey = fb
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
    Next item
    For Each item In list
        fb = CLng(item)
        If Not assignedBal(fb) Then
            FindBalloonByStableKey = fb
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next item
End Function

Private Function FindBalloonByOldNumber(oldNum As Long, _
                    ByRef bNums() As Long, _
                    ByRef bSheets() As String, _
                    ByRef assignedBal() As Boolean, _
                    bTotal As Long, sheetName As String) As Long
    FindBalloonByOldNumber = 0
    If oldNum <= 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim fbn As Long
    For fbn = 1 To bTotal
        If Not assignedBal(fbn) Then
            If bNums(fbn) = oldNum And bSheets(fbn) = sheetName Then
                FindBalloonByOldNumber = fbn
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
    Next fbn
    For fbn = 1 To bTotal
        If Not assignedBal(fbn) Then
            If bNums(fbn) = oldNum Then
                FindBalloonByOldNumber = fbn
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
    Next fbn
End Function

Private Sub AssignRemainingBalloonsByDistance(ByRef annToBalloon() As Long, _
                    ByRef assignedAnn() As Boolean, _
                    ByRef assignedBal() As Boolean, _
                    ByRef bPosX() As Double, _
                    ByRef bPosY() As Double, _
                    ByRef bSheets() As String, _
                    bTotal As Long, _
                    ByRef assignedByDistance As Long)
    ' v15.14: массив пар растёт по мере надобности (раньше выделялось positionsCount * bTotal).
    If positionsCount <= 0 Or bTotal <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim maxPairs As Long: maxPairs = 256
    Dim pairPi() As Long, pairDi() As Long, pairDist() As Double
    ReDim pairPi(1 To maxPairs): ReDim pairDi(1 To maxPairs): ReDim pairDist(1 To maxPairs)
    Dim actualPairs As Long: actualPairs = 0
    Dim ap As Long, ad As Long
    For ap = 1 To positionsCount
        If Not assignedAnn(ap) Then
            For ad = 1 To bTotal
                If Not assignedBal(ad) Then
                    If positionsData(ap).sheetName = bSheets(ad) Then
                        Dim pairD As Double
                        pairD = Sqr((bPosX(ad) - positionsData(ap).posX) ^ 2 + _
                                    (bPosY(ad) - positionsData(ap).posY) ^ 2)
                        If pairD <= KC_DISTANCE_MATCH_MAX_M Then
                            actualPairs = actualPairs + 1
                            If actualPairs > maxPairs Then
                                maxPairs = maxPairs * 2
                                ReDim Preserve pairPi(1 To maxPairs): ReDim Preserve pairDi(1 To maxPairs)
                                ReDim Preserve pairDist(1 To maxPairs)
                            End If
                            pairPi(actualPairs) = ap
                            pairDi(actualPairs) = ad
                            pairDist(actualPairs) = pairD
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            Next ad
        End If
    Next ap
    If actualPairs = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim si As Long, sj As Long
    Dim tmpPi2 As Long, tmpDi2 As Long, tmpDist2 As Double
    For si = 2 To actualPairs
        tmpPi2 = pairPi(si): tmpDi2 = pairDi(si): tmpDist2 = pairDist(si)
        sj = si - 1
        Do While sj >= 1
            If pairDist(sj) > tmpDist2 Then
                pairPi(sj + 1) = pairPi(sj): pairDi(sj + 1) = pairDi(sj): pairDist(sj + 1) = pairDist(sj)
                sj = sj - 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        pairPi(sj + 1) = tmpPi2: pairDi(sj + 1) = tmpDi2: pairDist(sj + 1) = tmpDist2
    Next si
    Dim ai As Long
    For ai = 1 To actualPairs
        ap = pairPi(ai): ad = pairDi(ai)
        If Not assignedAnn(ap) And Not assignedBal(ad) Then
            assignedAnn(ap) = True
            assignedBal(ad) = True
            annToBalloon(ap) = ad
            assignedByDistance = assignedByDistance + 1
        End If
    Next ai
End Sub

Private Sub BuildStableBalloonAssignments(ByRef annToBalloon() As Long, _
                    ByVal keyIndex As Object, _
                    ByRef bNums() As Long, _
                    ByRef bPosX() As Double, _
                    ByRef bPosY() As Double, _
                    ByRef bSheets() As String, _
                    bTotal As Long, _
                    ByRef assignedByKey As Long, _
                    ByRef assignedByLegacy As Long, _
                    ByRef assignedByExcel As Long, _
                    ByRef assignedByDistance As Long)
    ReDim annToBalloon(1 To positionsCount)
    assignedByKey = 0: assignedByLegacy = 0: assignedByExcel = 0: assignedByDistance = 0
    If bTotal <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim assignedAnn() As Boolean, assignedBal() As Boolean
    ReDim assignedAnn(1 To positionsCount): ReDim assignedBal(1 To bTotal)

    Dim bp As Long, bd As Long, keyVal As String, legacyVal As String
    ' 1) Ключ по имени исходной аннотации: не зависит от её перемещения.
    For bp = 1 To positionsCount
        keyVal = BuildAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(bp))
        bd = FindBalloonByStableKey(keyVal, keyIndex, bSheets, assignedBal, positionsData(bp).sheetName)
        If bd > 0 Then
            assignedAnn(bp) = True
            assignedBal(bd) = True
            annToBalloon(bp) = bd
            assignedByKey = assignedByKey + 1
        End If
    Next bp
    ' 2) Совместимость: старый ключ v15.12 с координатами (баллоны прежних запусков).
    For bp = 1 To positionsCount
        If Not assignedAnn(bp) Then
            legacyVal = BuildLegacyAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(bp))
            If legacyVal <> BuildAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(bp)) Then
                bd = FindBalloonByStableKey(legacyVal, keyIndex, bSheets, assignedBal, positionsData(bp).sheetName)
                If bd > 0 Then
                    assignedAnn(bp) = True
                    assignedBal(bd) = True
                    annToBalloon(bp) = bd
                    assignedByLegacy = assignedByLegacy + 1
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next bp

    ' Deletion rebuild: never attach a surviving balloon by old position or distance.
    If gKDForceRebuild Then Exit Sub

    ' 3) Номер позиции из прежней рабочей карты (совпадение текста/вида/номинала).
    If gUsePreparedMerge Then
        For bp = 1 To positionsCount
            If Not assignedAnn(bp) Then
                If bp <= UBound(gPreparedMatchIdx) Then
                    Dim oldIdx As Long
                    oldIdx = gPreparedMatchIdx(bp)
                    If oldIdx > 0 And oldIdx <= gPreparedOldCount Then
                        Dim oldNum As Long
                        oldNum = gPreparedOldData(oldIdx).posNumber
                        bd = FindBalloonByOldNumber(oldNum, bNums, bSheets, assignedBal, bTotal, positionsData(bp).sheetName)
                        If bd > 0 Then
                            assignedAnn(bp) = True
                            assignedBal(bd) = True
                            annToBalloon(bp) = bd
                            assignedByExcel = assignedByExcel + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next bp
    End If

    ' При изменении числа вводных не угадываем привязку по расстоянию.
    ' Несопоставленный старый баллон будет обнаружен проверкой до записи.
    If gPreparedOldCount > 0 And gIntroPointCount <> gIntroPreviousCount Then Exit Sub
    ' 4) Остаток — ближайшие пары на том же листе.
    AssignRemainingBalloonsByDistance annToBalloon, assignedAnn, assignedBal, _
        bPosX, bPosY, bSheets, bTotal, assignedByDistance
End Sub

' ============================================
' СКАНИРОВАНИЕ ЛИСТА НА БАЛЛОНЫ v2
' ============================================
Private Sub ScanSheetForBalloons_v2(sheetName As String, _
                    ByRef bNotes() As Object, _
                    ByRef bNums() As Long, _
                    ByRef bPosX() As Double, _
                    ByRef bPosY() As Double, _
                    ByRef bSheets() As String, _
                    ByRef bTypes() As Long, _
                    ByRef bPtrs() As Long, _
                    ByRef bTotal As Long, _
                    ByRef bMax As Long, _
                    Optional ByVal seen As Object = Nothing)
    Dim view As Object, note As Object
    Set view = swDraw.GetFirstView
    Do While Not view Is Nothing
        Set note = view.GetFirstNote
        Do While Not note Is Nothing
            CheckAndAddBalloon_v2 note, sheetName, bNotes, bNums, bPosX, bPosY, _
                                 bSheets, bTypes, bPtrs, bTotal, bMax, seen
            Set note = note.GetNext
        Loop
        Set view = view.GetNextView
    Loop
End Sub

Private Sub CheckAndAddBalloon_v2(swNote As Object, _
                    sheetName As String, _
                    ByRef bNotes() As Object, _
                    ByRef bNums() As Long, _
                    ByRef bPosX() As Double, _
                    ByRef bPosY() As Double, _
                    ByRef bSheets() As String, _
                    ByRef bTypes() As Long, _
                    ByRef bPtrs() As Long, _
                    ByRef bTotal As Long, _
                    ByRef bMax As Long, _
                    Optional ByVal seen As Object = Nothing)
    Dim txt As String, noteNum As Long, ann As Object, pos As Variant, noteKey As String
    Dim i As Long, code As Long, isBalloon As Boolean, style As Long
    On Error GoTo InvalidNote
    If swNote Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    txt = Trim$(swNote.GetText)
    If Len(txt) = 0 Or Len(txt) > 10 Then Exit Sub
    For i = 1 To Len(txt)
        code = AscW(Mid$(txt, i, 1))
        If code < 48 Or code > 57 Then Exit Sub
    Next i
    noteNum = CLng(txt)
    If noteNum <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    Set ann = swNote.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    pos = ann.GetPosition
    If Not IsArray(pos) Then Exit Sub
    If Not IsInWorkArea(pos(0), pos(1)) Then Exit Sub
    ' swBS_Circular = 1. Plain numbers, font size and other frames do not count.
    style = swNote.GetBalloonStyle
    If style <> 1 Then Exit Sub
    ' Font height and default balloon size do not identify a balloon.
    If seen Is Nothing Then
        For i = 1 To bTotal
            If bNotes(i) Is swNote Then Exit Sub
        Next i
    Else
        noteKey = KC_ObjKey(swNote)
        If seen.Exists(noteKey) Then Exit Sub
        seen.Add noteKey, True
    End If
    bTotal = bTotal + 1
    If bTotal > bMax Then
        bMax = bMax + 200
        ReDim Preserve bNotes(1 To bMax): ReDim Preserve bNums(1 To bMax)
        ReDim Preserve bPosX(1 To bMax): ReDim Preserve bPosY(1 To bMax)
        ReDim Preserve bSheets(1 To bMax): ReDim Preserve bTypes(1 To bMax): ReDim Preserve bPtrs(1 To bMax)
    End If
    Set bNotes(bTotal) = swNote
    bNums(bTotal) = noteNum: bPosX(bTotal) = CDbl(pos(0)): bPosY(bTotal) = CDbl(pos(1))
    bSheets(bTotal) = sheetName: bTypes(bTotal) = style
    Exit Sub
InvalidNote:
    ' Unsupported/non-numeric notes are not treated as control balloons.
End Sub

' ============================================
' РЕЖИМ 2: УПРАВЛЕНИЕ МАРКЕРАМИ # И @ (лист "Маркеры КК" рабочей книги)
' ============================================
Private Sub RunMode3_StarDims()
    Dim subMode As String
    subMode = InputBox( _
        "Управление разметкой КК (# и @ вместе):" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        " 1 = Запомнить все # и @ (команда 2.1)" & vbCrLf & _
        " 2 = Восстановить # и @ из списка (2.2)" & vbCrLf & _
        " 3 = Снять # и @ по списку (2.3)" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Список хранится листом «Маркеры КК» в рабочей книге" & vbCrLf & _
        "карты контроля этого чертежа (.xlsm).", _
        "Режим 2 - Разметка КК", "2")
    Select Case Trim$(subMode)
        Case "": Exit Sub
        Case "1": KC_SaveCombinedMarkers
        Case "2": KC_CombinedFileAction False
        Case "3": KC_CombinedFileAction True
        Case Else: MsgBox "Неверный выбор!", vbExclamation
    End Select
End Sub


' ============================================
' LEGACY: сохранение отдельного файла (не вызывается новым меню)
' ============================================
Private Sub RunMode3_SaveStars(Optional ByVal marker As String = "#")
    Dim allSheets As Variant, si As Long, origSheet As String
    Dim sDims() As Object, sAnns() As Object, sTexts() As String, sSheets() As String
    Dim sViews() As String, sNames() As String, sPosX() As Double, sPosY() As Double
    Dim sCount As Long, sMax As Long, ri As Long
    Dim xlApp As Object, xlWB As Object, ws As Object
    Dim savePath As Variant, errText As String, refText As String, refCount As Long
    Dim saveStage As String, sheetTitle As String, schema As String, suffix As String
    Dim writeIndex As Long, keep As Boolean
    If marker = "@" Then
        sheetTitle = "Заметки @": schema = "KC_AT_1": suffix = "_at_notes.xlsx"
    Else
        marker = "#": sheetTitle = "Звёздочки": schema = "KC_HASH_2": suffix = "_hashes.xlsx"
    End If
    On Error GoTo SaveError
    saveStage = "Чтение текущего листа"
    origSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    saveStage = "Чтение списка листов"
    allSheets = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    sMax = 200
    ReDim sDims(1 To sMax): ReDim sAnns(1 To sMax)
    ReDim sTexts(1 To sMax): ReDim sSheets(1 To sMax)
    ReDim sViews(1 To sMax): ReDim sNames(1 To sMax)
    ReDim sPosX(1 To sMax): ReDim sPosY(1 To sMax)
    For si = 0 To UBound(allSheets)
        saveStage = "Активация листа"
        swDraw.ActivateSheet CStr(allSheets(si))
        saveStage = "Чтение границ листа"
        InitWorkArea
        saveStage = "Сбор объектов с " & marker
        CollectAllDims CStr(allSheets(si)), sDims, sAnns, sTexts, sSheets, _
                        sViews, sNames, sPosX, sPosY, sCount, sMax
    Next si
    swDraw.ActivateSheet origSheet
    ' Filter after enumeration. @ applies only to notes; one note = one record.
    For ri = 1 To sCount
        keep = False
        If marker = "@" Then
            If KC_ObjectKind(sDims(ri)) = "NOTE" Then keep = NoteHasMark(sDims(ri))
        Else
            keep = (InStr(1, sTexts(ri), "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0)
        End If
        If keep Then
            writeIndex = writeIndex + 1
            Set sDims(writeIndex) = sDims(ri): Set sAnns(writeIndex) = sAnns(ri)
            sTexts(writeIndex) = sTexts(ri): sSheets(writeIndex) = sSheets(ri)
            sViews(writeIndex) = sViews(ri): sNames(writeIndex) = sNames(ri)
            sPosX(writeIndex) = sPosX(ri): sPosY(writeIndex) = sPosY(ri)
        End If
    Next ri
    sCount = writeIndex
    If sCount = 0 Then
        MsgBox "Объекты с " & marker & " не найдены.", vbInformation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If Len(swModel.GetPathName) = 0 Then
        MsgBox "Сначала сохраните сам чертёж, затем сохраните список " & marker & ".", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    saveStage = "Запуск Excel"
    Set xlApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
    xlApp.Visible = False
    saveStage = "Диалог выбора файла Excel"
    savePath = xlApp.GetSaveAsFilename(swModel.GetPathName & suffix, _
               "Excel Files (*.xlsx),*.xlsx", , "Сохранить " & marker)
    If VarType(savePath) = vbBoolean Then GoTo SaveCleanup
    saveStage = "Создание книги Excel"
    Set xlWB = xlApp.Workbooks.Add
    Set ws = xlWB.sheets(1)
    saveStage = "Подготовка листа Excel"
    ws.name = sheetTitle
    ' A:G retain the old layout. H:K are additional identity fields.
    ws.cells(1, 1).value = "№"
    ws.cells(1, 2).value = IIf(marker = "@", "Текст заметки", "Текст размера")
    ws.cells(1, 3).value = "Лист"
    ws.cells(1, 4).value = "Вид"
    ws.cells(1, 5).value = "Поз X"
    ws.cells(1, 6).value = "Поз Y"
    ws.cells(1, 7).value = "Идентификатор"
    ws.cells(1, 8).value = "Тип объекта"
    ws.cells(1, 9).value = "PersistentReferenceHex"
    ws.cells(1, 10).value = "HashSchema"
    ws.cells(1, 11).value = "Путь чертежа"
    ws.Columns("B:D").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Columns("G:K").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("A1:K1").Font.Bold = True
    saveStage = "Запись объектов в Excel"
    For ri = 1 To sCount
        refText = KC_GetPersistentHex(sAnns(ri))
        If Len(refText) > 0 Then refCount = refCount + 1
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 1).Value2 = ri
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 2).Value2 = sTexts(ri)
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 3).Value2 = sSheets(ri)
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 4).Value2 = sViews(ri)
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 5).Value2 = sPosX(ri)
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 6).Value2 = sPosY(ri)
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 7).Value2 = sNames(ri)
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 8).Value2 = KC_ObjectKind(sDims(ri))
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 9).Value2 = refText
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 10).Value2 = schema
        ws.cells(ri + 1, 11).Value2 = swModel.GetPathName
    Next ri
    ws.Columns("A:H").AutoFit
