    ws.Columns("I:K").ColumnWidth = 24
    saveStage = "Сохранение книги Excel"
    xlWB.SaveAs CStr(savePath), 51
    MsgBox "Сохранено объектов с " & marker & ": " & sCount & vbCrLf & _
           "С persistent reference: " & refCount & vbCrLf & _
           "Без неё используется уникальное совпадение листа, вида, имени и типа." & vbCrLf & _
           "Перемещение объекта не мешает восстановлению " & marker & "." & vbCrLf & _
           "Файл: " & CStr(savePath), vbInformation
SaveCleanup:
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = Nothing
    If Not xlWB Is Nothing Then xlWB.Close False
    If Not xlApp Is Nothing Then xlApp.Quit
    Set xlWB = Nothing: Set xlApp = Nothing
    If Len(origSheet) > 0 Then swDraw.ActivateSheet origSheet
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Len(errText) > 0 Then MsgBox errText, vbCritical
    Exit Sub
SaveError:
    errText = "Ошибка сохранения " & marker & " (" & CStr(Err.number) & ")." & vbCrLf & _
              "Этап: " & saveStage & vbCrLf & "Источник: " & Err.source & vbCrLf & Err.Description
    Resume SaveCleanup
End Sub

' ============================================
' LEGACY: восстановление отдельного файла (не вызывается новым меню)
' ============================================
Private Sub RunMode3_RestoreStars()
    KC_ApplyHashesFromFile False
End Sub

' ============================================
' LEGACY: снятие из отдельного файла (не вызывается новым меню)
' ============================================
Private Sub RunMode3_RemoveAllStars()
    KC_ApplyHashesFromFile True
End Sub

' ============================================
' УНИВЕРСАЛЬНЫЕ МЕТОДЫ РАБОТЫ С #
' ============================================
Private Function ItemIsNote(ByVal obj As Object) As Boolean
    ItemIsNote = False
    On Error Resume Next
    If obj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim tn As String: tn = LCase(TypeName(obj))
    If InStr(1, tn, "note") > 0 Then ItemIsNote = True: Exit Function
    If InStr(1, tn, "dim") > 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim bs As Long: bs = obj.GetBalloonStyle
    If Err.number = 0 Then ItemIsNote = True
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function AddStarToNote(swNote As Object) As Boolean
    Dim t As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If swNote Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    t = swNote.GetText
    If InStr(1, t, "#", vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then swNote.SetText t & "#"
    AddStarToNote = (InStr(1, swNote.GetText, "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0)
    Exit Function
Failed:
    AddStarToNote = False
End Function

Private Function RemoveStarFromNote(swNote As Object) As Boolean
    Dim t As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If swNote Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    t = swNote.GetText
    If InStr(1, t, "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then swNote.SetText Replace(t, "#", "")
    RemoveStarFromNote = (InStr(1, swNote.GetText, "#", vbBinaryCompare) = 0)
    Exit Function
Failed:
    RemoveStarFromNote = False
End Function

Private Function ObjHasExactlyOneStar(ByVal obj As Object, ByVal selType As Long) As Boolean
    ObjHasExactlyOneStar = False
    On Error Resume Next
    If obj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If selType = 15 Or ItemIsNote(obj) Then
        ObjHasExactlyOneStar = NoteHasExactlyOneStar(obj)
        Exit Function
    End If
    Dim swAnnU As Object: Set swAnnU = obj.GetAnnotation
    If swAnnU Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim fullText As String: fullText = CollectDisplayDataText(swAnnU)
    Dim starCount As Long, p As Long: starCount = 0
    For p = 1 To Len(fullText)
        If Mid(fullText, p, 1) = "#" Then starCount = starCount + 1
    Next p
    If starCount = 1 Then ObjHasExactlyOneStar = True
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function ObjDisplayText(ByVal obj As Object, ByVal selType As Long) As String
    ObjDisplayText = ""
    On Error Resume Next
    If obj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If selType = 15 Or ItemIsNote(obj) Then
        ObjDisplayText = Trim(obj.GetText)
        Exit Function
    End If
    Dim swAnnU As Object: Set swAnnU = obj.GetAnnotation
    If swAnnU Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    ObjDisplayText = CollectDisplayDataText(swAnnU)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function AddStarToDim(swDispDim As Object) As Boolean
    Dim t As String, isHole As Boolean, attempted As Boolean, detail As String
    gKC_MarkerDetail = ""
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If swDispDim Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4705, , "Не получен объект размера."
    If ItemIsNote(swDispDim) Then
        AddStarToDim = AddStarToNote(swDispDim)
        Exit Function
    End If
    If KC_DimHasHash(swDispDim) Then
        AddStarToDim = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    If Not KC42_IsHole(swDispDim, isHole) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4705, , "Не определён тип размера."
    If isHole Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4705, , "Запись маркера в обозначение отверстия запрещена."
    t = CStr(swDispDim.GetText(2))
    attempted = True
    swDispDim.SetText 2, t & "#"
    If CStr(swDispDim.GetText(2)) <> (t & "#") Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4705, , "Не подтверждена запись суффикса."
    If Not KC_DimHasHash(swDispDim) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4705, , "Не подтверждён маркер после записи."
    AddStarToDim = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = Err.Description
    If attempted Then
        If Not KC47_RestoreTextPart(swDispDim, 2, t) Then detail = detail & _
            "; НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЁН ОТКАТ суффикса; восстановите исходный текст на копии."
    End If
    gKC_MarkerDetail = detail
    AddStarToDim = False
End Function

Private Sub EnumSheetAnns(ByVal onlyStars As Boolean, sheetName As String, _
                ByRef sDims() As Object, ByRef sAnns() As Object, ByRef sTexts() As String, _
                ByRef sSheets() As String, ByRef sViews() As String, ByRef sNames() As String, _
                ByRef sPosX() As Double, ByRef sPosY() As Double, ByRef sCount As Long, ByRef sMax As Long)
    Dim swView As Object, swDD As Object, swNote As Object
    Dim viewName As String, stage As String, n As Long, msg As String
    On Error GoTo EnumError
    ' First view is the sheet itself; then follow all drawing views.
    stage = "IDrawingDoc.GetFirstView"
    Set swView = swDraw.GetFirstView
    Do While Not swView Is Nothing
        stage = "IView.GetName2"
        viewName = swView.GetName2
        stage = "IView.GetFirstDisplayDimension5"
        Set swDD = swView.GetFirstDisplayDimension5
        Do While Not swDD Is Nothing
            stage = "AddCollectedObj: размер"
            AddCollectedObj swDD, onlyStars, sheetName, sDims, sAnns, sTexts, _
                sSheets, sViews, sNames, sPosX, sPosY, sCount, sMax, viewName
            stage = "IDisplayDimension.GetNext5"
            Set swDD = swDD.GetNext5
        Loop
        stage = "IView.GetFirstNote"
        Set swNote = swView.GetFirstNote
        Do While Not swNote Is Nothing
            stage = "AddCollectedObj: заметка"
            AddCollectedObj swNote, onlyStars, sheetName, sDims, sAnns, sTexts, _
                sSheets, sViews, sNames, sPosX, sPosY, sCount, sMax, viewName
            stage = "INote.GetNext"
            Set swNote = swNote.GetNext
        Loop
        stage = "IView.GetNextView"
        Set swView = swView.GetNextView
    Loop
    Exit Sub
EnumError:
    n = Err.number: msg = Err.Description
    Err.Raise n, "EnumSheetAnns", "Лист: " & sheetName & "; вид: " & viewName & _
              vbCrLf & "Этап: " & stage & vbCrLf & msg
End Sub

Private Sub AddCollectedObj(swObj As Object, ByVal onlyStars As Boolean, sheetName As String, _
                ByRef sDims() As Object, ByRef sAnns() As Object, ByRef sTexts() As String, _
                ByRef sSheets() As String, ByRef sViews() As String, ByRef sNames() As String, _
                ByRef sPosX() As Double, ByRef sPosY() As Double, ByRef sCount As Long, ByRef sMax As Long, _
                Optional ByVal actualViewName As String = "")
    Dim swAnn As Object, pos As Variant, txt As String, selType As Long
    Dim annName As String, keyAnn As String, keyObj As String
    If swObj Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If ItemIsNote(swObj) Then selType = 15 Else selType = 14
    txt = ObjDisplayText(swObj, selType)
    If onlyStars And InStr(1, txt, "#", vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Set swAnn = swObj.GetAnnotation
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    pos = swAnn.GetPosition()
    If Not IsArray(pos) Then Exit Sub
    If Not IsInWorkArea(pos(0), pos(1)) Then Exit Sub
    ' Deduplicate by COM object identity only, not by name or coordinates.
    ' v15.14: словарь по IUnknown вместо перебора всех собранных объектов.
    If sCount = 0 Or gKC_CollectSeen Is Nothing Then Set gKC_CollectSeen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    keyAnn = "A" & KC_ObjKey(swAnn): keyObj = "O" & KC_ObjKey(swObj)
    If gKC_CollectSeen.Exists(keyAnn) Or gKC_CollectSeen.Exists(keyObj) Then Exit Sub
    On Error Resume Next
    annName = swAnn.GetName
    On Error GoTo 0
    sCount = sCount + 1
    If sCount > sMax Then
        sMax = sMax + 100
        ReDim Preserve sDims(1 To sMax): ReDim Preserve sAnns(1 To sMax)
        ReDim Preserve sTexts(1 To sMax): ReDim Preserve sSheets(1 To sMax)
        ReDim Preserve sViews(1 To sMax): ReDim Preserve sNames(1 To sMax)
        ReDim Preserve sPosX(1 To sMax): ReDim Preserve sPosY(1 To sMax)
    End If
    Set sDims(sCount) = swObj: Set sAnns(sCount) = swAnn
    gKC_CollectSeen(keyAnn) = True: gKC_CollectSeen(keyObj) = True
    sTexts(sCount) = txt: sSheets(sCount) = sheetName
    sViews(sCount) = actualViewName: sNames(sCount) = annName
    sPosX(sCount) = CDbl(pos(0)): sPosY(sCount) = CDbl(pos(1))
End Sub

Private Sub CollectAllDims(sheetName As String, _
                ByRef sDims() As Object, ByRef sAnns() As Object, ByRef sTexts() As String, _
                ByRef sSheets() As String, ByRef sViews() As String, ByRef sNames() As String, _
                ByRef sPosX() As Double, ByRef sPosY() As Double, ByRef sCount As Long, ByRef sMax As Long)
    EnumSheetAnns False, sheetName, sDims, sAnns, sTexts, sSheets, sViews, sNames, sPosX, sPosY, sCount, sMax
End Sub

Private Sub CollectStarDims(sheetName As String, _
                ByRef sDims() As Object, ByRef sAnns() As Object, ByRef sTexts() As String, _
                ByRef sSheets() As String, ByRef sViews() As String, ByRef sNames() As String, _
                ByRef sPosX() As Double, ByRef sPosY() As Double, ByRef sCount As Long, ByRef sMax As Long)
    EnumSheetAnns True, sheetName, sDims, sAnns, sTexts, sSheets, sViews, sNames, sPosX, sPosY, sCount, sMax
End Sub


Private Function DimHasStar(swDispDim As Object) As Boolean
    DimHasStar = False
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim swAnn As Object: Set swAnn = swDispDim.GetAnnotation
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim dispData As Object: Set dispData = swAnn.GetDisplayData
    If dispData Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim txtCount As Long: txtCount = dispData.GetTextCount
    Dim fullText As String: fullText = ""
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To txtCount - 1
        Dim txt As Variant: txt = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(i)
        If Not IsNull(txt) Then fullText = fullText & Trim(CStr(txt))
    Next i
    If InStr(1, fullText, "*") > 0 Then DimHasStar = True
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function DimHasExactlyOneStar(swDispDim As Object) As Boolean
    DimHasExactlyOneStar = False
    On Error Resume Next
    If ItemIsNote(swDispDim) Then
        DimHasExactlyOneStar = NoteHasExactlyOneStar(swDispDim)
        Exit Function
    End If
    Dim swAnn As Object: Set swAnn = swDispDim.GetAnnotation
    If swAnn Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim dispData As Object: Set dispData = swAnn.GetDisplayData
    If dispData Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Dim txtCount As Long: txtCount = dispData.GetTextCount
    Dim fullText As String: fullText = ""
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To txtCount - 1
        Dim txt As Variant: txt = dispData.GetTextAtIndex(i)
        If Not IsNull(txt) Then fullText = fullText & Trim(CStr(txt))
    Next i
    Dim starCount As Long, p As Long: starCount = 0
    For p = 1 To Len(fullText)
        If Mid(fullText, p, 1) = "#" Then starCount = starCount + 1
    Next p
    If starCount = 1 Then DimHasExactlyOneStar = True
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function RemoveStarFromDim(swDispDim As Object) As Boolean
    Dim part As Long, txt(1 To 4) As String, wanted(1 To 4) As String
    Dim changed(1 To 4) As Boolean, p As Long, changedCount As Long
    Dim wasOff As Boolean, errInfo As String, rollbackFailures As Long
    Dim lowerBefore As String, lowerAfter As String, lowerOK As Boolean, lowerChanged As Boolean
    Dim isHole As Boolean, holeKnown As Boolean
    gKC_MarkerDetail = ""
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If swDispDim Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4410, , "Не получен объект размера."
    If ItemIsNote(swDispDim) Then
        RemoveStarFromDim = RemoveStarFromNote(swDispDim)
        Exit Function
    End If
    holeKnown = KC42_IsHole(swDispDim, isHole)
    If Not holeKnown Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4703, , "Не определён тип размера; изменение маркера запрещено."
    If holeKnown And isHole Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4422, , _
        "Это обозначение отверстия. SolidWorks не поддерживает GetText/SetText для него; автоматическое удаление запрещено. " & KC42_Diagnostic(swDispDim)
    lowerOK = KC42_GetLower(swDispDim, lowerBefore)
    If Not lowerOK Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4704, , "Не прочитан нижний текст; изменение маркера запрещено."
    For part = 1 To 4
        txt(part) = CStr(swDispDim.GetText(part))
        wanted(part) = Replace(txt(part), "#", "")
    Next part
    For part = 1 To 4
        If wanted(part) <> txt(part) Then
            changed(part) = True
            changedCount = changedCount + 1
            swDispDim.SetText part, wanted(part)
        End If
    Next part
    If lowerOK And InStr(1, lowerBefore, "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
        ' GetLowerText/SetLowerText — отдельный документированный канал.
        lowerChanged = True: changedCount = changedCount + 1
        lowerAfter = Replace(lowerBefore, "#", "")
        swDispDim.SetLowerText lowerAfter
    End If
    If changedCount > 0 Then
        ' SetText не всегда обновляет IAnnotation.GetDisplayData немедленно.
        ' Не используем SetText 0: он уничтожает составной текст размера.
        wasOff = gKC_GfxOff
        If wasOff Then KC_GraphicsOn
        swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
        If wasOff Then KC_GraphicsOff
        For part = 1 To 4
            If CStr(swDispDim.GetText(part)) <> wanted(part) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4411, , "SolidWorks не подтвердил текст части " & CStr(part) & "."
        Next part
        If lowerChanged Then
            Dim lowerCheck As String
            If Not KC42_GetLower(swDispDim, lowerCheck) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4423, , "Не прочитан нижний текст после изменения."
            If lowerCheck <> lowerAfter Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4423, , "Нижний текст не подтвердился после изменения."
        End If
    End If
    If KC_DimHasHash(swDispDim) Then
        If changedCount = 0 Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4412, , "# только в отображении, в обычных редактируемых полях не найден; изменение запрещено. " & KC42_Diagnostic(swDispDim)
        Else
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4413, , "После перерисовки # всё ещё виден в тексте размера."
        End If
    End If
    RemoveStarFromDim = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errInfo = Err.Description
    If Len(errInfo) = 0 Then errInfo = "SolidWorks не подтвердил изменение текста размера."
    On Error Resume Next
    If wasOff And Not gKC_GfxOff Then KC_GraphicsOff
    On Error GoTo 0
    For p = 1 To 4
        If changed(p) Then
            If Not KC47_RestoreTextPart(swDispDim, p, txt(p)) Then rollbackFailures = rollbackFailures + 1
        End If
    Next p
    If lowerChanged Then
        If Not KC47_RestoreTextPart(swDispDim, -1, lowerBefore) Then rollbackFailures = rollbackFailures + 1
    End If
    If rollbackFailures > 0 Then errInfo = errInfo & "; НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЁН ОТКАТ частей: " & rollbackFailures & _
        ". Не сохраняйте поверх исходника; проверьте копию и восстановите исходный текст."
    gKC_MarkerDetail = errInfo
    RemoveStarFromDim = False
End Function

' ============================================
' ЭКСПОРТ В EXCEL
' ============================================
Private Function ExportToExcel() As Boolean
    Dim xlApp As Object, xlWB As Object, wsRaw As Object, wsCard As Object, wsTools As Object
    Dim r As Long, row As Long, h As Long, pd As PositionData
    Dim exportErrorText As String, finishChanges As New Collection, finishRollback As Long
    Dim restoreEvents As Boolean, restoreScreen As Boolean, restoreCalculation As Long, settingsCaptured As Boolean
    Dim rawBuffer As Variant, headerBuffer As Variant, cardBuffer As Variant, cardLength As Long
    Dim perfStarted As Double, perfPhase As Double, perfReport As String
    perfStarted = Timer
    On Error GoTo ExcelError
    If Not gIntroReady Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2720, , "Вводные пункты не подтверждены."
    For r = 1 To positionsCount
        If positionsData(r).number <> gIntroFirstBalloon + r - 1 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2721, , "Номера позиций не согласованы с вводными пунктами. Повторите режим 1."
    Next r

    Dim useMerge As Boolean: useMerge = False
    Dim mergeMode As Long: mergeMode = 0
    Dim oldData() As OldExcelRow, oldCount As Long: oldCount = 0
    Dim oldCardBlocks() As OldCardBlock, oldBlockCount As Long: oldBlockCount = 0
    Dim oldFilePath As String
    Dim matchResults() As Long, matchIdx() As Long
    ReDim matchResults(1 To positionsCount): ReDim matchIdx(1 To positionsCount)
    
    If gUsePreparedMerge Then
        useMerge = True
        mergeMode = gPreparedMergeMode
        oldCount = gPreparedOldCount
        oldBlockCount = gPreparedOldBlockCount
        oldFilePath = gPreparedOldFilePath
        If oldCount > 0 Then
            ReDim oldData(1 To oldCount)
            Dim copyOld As Long
            For copyOld = 1 To oldCount
                oldData(copyOld) = gPreparedOldData(copyOld)
            Next copyOld
        End If
        If positionsCount > 0 Then
            For r = 1 To positionsCount
                matchIdx(r) = gPreparedMatchIdx(r)
                matchResults(r) = gPreparedMatchResults(r)
            Next r
        End If
    End If
    
    gStage = "Обновление общей рабочей книги"
    Set xlApp = gWorkApp: Set xlWB = gWorkBook
    restoreEvents = xlApp.EnableEvents
    restoreScreen = xlApp.ScreenUpdating: restoreCalculation = xlApp.calculation
    settingsCaptured = True
    xlApp.EnableEvents = False: xlApp.ScreenUpdating = False: xlApp.calculation = -4135
    perfPhase = Timer
    Set wsCard = xlWB.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    KC47_CheckCardFormulas wsCard
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsRaw = xlWB.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    On Error GoTo ExcelError
    If wsRaw Is Nothing Then
        Set wsRaw = xlWB.Worksheets.Add(After:=wsCard)
        wsRaw.name = "Данные из чертежа"
    End If
    wsRaw.cells.Clear
    Dim clearLast As Long
    clearLast = LastOKContinuationRow(wsCard)
    If clearLast >= 7 Then
        wsCard.Range("A7:E" & CStr(clearLast)).UnMerge
        wsCard.Range("A7:E" & CStr(clearLast)).Clear
    End If
    Dim headers As Variant
    headers = Array("№ поз.", "Тип", "Текст аннотации", "Номинал мм", "Допуск +", "Допуск -", _
        "Текст допуска", "Префикс", "GDT символ", "GDT значение", "GDT базы", _
        "ГОСТ сварки", "Тип шва", "Катет шва", "Доп.катета +", "Доп.катета -", _
        "Средство ТО", "Код ТО", "Объём", "То/Тв", "Кол-во мест", _
        "Ссылка эскиз/вид", "Лист", "Чертёжный вид", "Буквенный", "Совпадение")
    ReDim headerBuffer(1 To 1, 1 To UBound(headers) + 1)
    For h = 0 To UBound(headers): headerBuffer(1, h + 1) = headers(h): Next h
    wsRaw.Range("A1:Z1").Value2 = headerBuffer
    If positionsCount > 0 Then ReDim rawBuffer(1 To positionsCount, 1 To 26)
    wsRaw.Range("A1:" & ColLetter(UBound(headers) + 1) & "1").Font.Bold = True
    wsRaw.Range("A1:" & ColLetter(UBound(headers) + 1) & "1").Interior.Color = RGB(180, 210, 255)
    wsRaw.Range("A1:" & ColLetter(UBound(headers) + 1) & "1").WrapText = True
    wsRaw.rows(1).RowHeight = 40
    wsRaw.Columns("C").NumberFormat = "@"
    wsRaw.Columns("S").NumberFormat = "@"
    
    For r = 1 To positionsCount
        row = r + 1
        pd = positionsData(r)
        
        If useMerge And matchIdx(r) > 0 Then
            Dim oi As Long: oi = matchIdx(r)
            If Len(oldData(oi).ToolName) > 0 Then pd.ToolName = oldData(oi).ToolName
            If mergeMode = 2 Or mergeMode = 3 Then
                If Len(oldData(oi).volumeStr) > 0 Then pd.volumeStr = oldData(oi).volumeStr
                If Len(oldData(oi).timeTv) > 0 Then pd.timeTv = oldData(oi).timeTv
            End If
            positionsData(r) = pd
        End If
        
        rawBuffer(r, 1) = pd.number
        Select Case pd.AnnotationType
            Case swSelDIMENSIONS: rawBuffer(r, 2) = "Размер"
            Case swSelGTOLS: rawBuffer(r, 2) = "Геом.допуск"
            Case swSelWELDSYMBOLS: rawBuffer(r, 2) = "Сварка"
            Case swSelNOTES: rawBuffer(r, 2) = "Заметка"
        End Select
        rawBuffer(r, 3) = pd.AnnotationText
        rawBuffer(r, 4) = pd.NominalValue
        rawBuffer(r, 5) = pd.tolPlus
        rawBuffer(r, 6) = pd.tolMinus
        rawBuffer(r, 7) = pd.TolText
        rawBuffer(r, 8) = pd.DimPrefix
        rawBuffer(r, 9) = pd.GDTSymbolName
        rawBuffer(r, 10) = pd.GDTValue
        rawBuffer(r, 11) = ""
        rawBuffer(r, 12) = pd.WeldStandard
        rawBuffer(r, 13) = pd.weldType
        rawBuffer(r, 14) = pd.weldLeg
        rawBuffer(r, 15) = pd.WeldLegTolPlus
        rawBuffer(r, 16) = pd.WeldLegTolMinus
        rawBuffer(r, 17) = pd.ToolName
        rawBuffer(r, 18) = pd.ToolCode
        rawBuffer(r, 19) = pd.volumeStr
        rawBuffer(r, 20) = pd.timeTv
        rawBuffer(r, 21) = pd.QuantityStr
        rawBuffer(r, 22) = pd.SketchRef
        rawBuffer(r, 23) = pd.sheetName
        rawBuffer(r, 24) = pd.viewName
        If pd.IsLetterDim Then rawBuffer(r, 25) = "Да" Else rawBuffer(r, 25) = ""
        
        If useMerge Then
            Select Case matchResults(r)
                Case 1: rawBuffer(r, 26) = "Точное"
                Case 2
                    rawBuffer(r, 26) = "Частичное"
                    ' Row colour is applied after the array has been written.
                Case Else
                    rawBuffer(r, 26) = "Новая"
                    ' Row colour is applied after the array has been written.
            End Select
        End If
    Next r
    If positionsCount > 0 Then wsRaw.Range("A2").Resize(positionsCount, 26).Value2 = rawBuffer
    If useMerge Then
        For r = 1 To positionsCount
            Select Case matchResults(r)
                Case 2: wsRaw.Range("A" & CStr(r + 1) & ":Z" & CStr(r + 1)).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 200, 100)
                Case 1
                Case Else: wsRaw.Range("A" & CStr(r + 1) & ":Z" & CStr(r + 1)).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 255, 150)
            End Select
        Next r
    End If
    wsRaw.Range("A1:Z" & CStr(positionsCount + 1)).Columns.AutoFit
    perfReport = "исходные данные=" & KCPerf_Seconds(perfPhase)
    perfPhase = Timer
    
    Set wsTools = xlWB.Worksheets("СписокТО")
    Dim hRow As Long: hRow = 6
    Dim cardRow As Long
    WriteFixedCardBlock wsCard, gIntroFixed, gIntroMass
    KC_WriteIntroSummary wsCard
    cardLength = KCPerf_BuildCard(cardBuffer)
    cardRow = 6 + cardLength
    wsCard.Range("A7:A" & CStr(cardRow)).NumberFormat = "@"
    wsCard.Range("D7:D" & CStr(cardRow)).NumberFormat = "@"
    wsCard.Range("A7").Resize(cardLength, 5).Value2 = cardBuffer
    KC15_SetIntroArea wsCard, 6 + gIntroRows
    perfReport = perfReport & "; сборка/запись карты=" & KCPerf_Seconds(perfPhase)
    perfPhase = Timer

    ' Финал ОК
    wsCard.cells(cardRow, 1).value = "ОК"
    wsCard.cells(cardRow, 1).Font.Bold = True
    wsCard.Range("B" & cardRow & ":D" & cardRow).Merge
    wsCard.cells(cardRow, 2).value = "Операционная карта контроля"
    wsCard.cells(cardRow, 2).Font.Bold = True
    wsCard.cells(cardRow, 2).Font.Italic = True
    
    Dim tableRange As Object
    Set tableRange = wsCard.Range("A" & hRow & ":E" & cardRow)
    Dim edge As Long
    For edge = 7 To 12
        tableRange.Borders(edge).LineStyle = 1
        tableRange.Borders(edge).Weight = 2
    Next edge
    wsCard.Range("A" & hRow & ":A" & cardRow).Borders(10).LineStyle = 1
    wsCard.Range("B" & hRow & ":B" & cardRow).Borders(10).LineStyle = 1
    wsCard.Range("C" & hRow & ":C" & cardRow).Borders(10).LineStyle = 1
    wsCard.Range("D" & hRow & ":D" & cardRow).Borders(10).LineStyle = 1
    wsCard.Range("E" & hRow & ":E" & cardRow).Borders(10).LineStyle = 1
    
    ' ОДИН И ТОТ ЖЕ формат и ToolsList для вводных и остальных операций.
    KC_CopyConfirmedTools xlWB
    EnsureCardToolsList xlWB
    KC28_AppendRuleTools xlWB
    KCPerf_Layout wsCard, cardBuffer, 7, cardLength - 1
    perfReport = perfReport & "; оформление=" & KCPerf_Seconds(perfPhase)
    perfPhase = Timer
    KC_RunEditorCommand xlApp, xlWB, "OK_RefreshButtons"
    perfReport = perfReport & "; редактор Excel=" & KCPerf_Seconds(perfPhase)
    perfPhase = Timer
    wsCard.Visible = True: wsCard.Activate
    wsRaw.Visible = True: wsTools.Visible = False
    xlWB.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B9").Value2 = gIntroPointCount
    KC37_StampLayout xlWB, swDraw, swModel.GetPathName
    KD_Finish xlWB, finishChanges
    xlWB.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2 = "PENDING"
    xlWB.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B11").Value2 = Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")
    gStage = "Сохранение рабочей карты"
    xlApp.calculation = restoreCalculation
    xlWB.Save
    If Not xlWB.saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4204, , "Пересобранная карта не сохранена."
    perfReport = perfReport & "; сохранение=" & KCPerf_Seconds(perfPhase) & "; всего экспорт=" & KCPerf_Seconds(perfStarted)
    KCPerf_RestoreExcel xlApp, restoreCalculation, restoreEvents, restoreScreen
    settingsCaptured = False
    ExportToExcel = True
    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, "Карта обновлена: " & gIntroConfigPath & "; позиций " & positionsCount & "; PERF v15.1: " & perfReport
    Exit Function
ExcelError:
    exportErrorText = "Этап: " & gStage & "; ошибка " & Err.number & ": " & Err.Description
    ExportToExcel = False
    On Error Resume Next
    finishRollback = KC35_RestoreCells(finishChanges)
    If Not xlWB Is Nothing Then xlWB.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2 = "PENDING"
    On Error GoTo 0
    If settingsCaptured Then KCPerf_RestoreExcel xlApp, restoreCalculation, restoreEvents, restoreScreen
    settingsCaptured = False
    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, exportErrorText
    MsgBox exportErrorText & vbCrLf & "Книга оставлена открытой. Сохраните её копию вручную. Чертёж мог измениться; общего отката нет.", vbCritical
    On Error Resume Next
    If settingsCaptured Then KCPerf_RestoreExcel xlApp, restoreCalculation, restoreEvents, restoreScreen
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

' ============================================
' ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНЫЕ ФУНКЦИИ EXCEL
' ============================================
Private Function ColLetter(colNum As Long) As String
    If colNum <= 26 Then
        ColLetter = Chr(64 + colNum)
    Else
        ColLetter = Chr(64 + Int((colNum - 1) / 26)) & Chr(64 + ((colNum - 1) Mod 26) + 1)
    End If
End Function









' ============================================
' KC_HASH_2: safe identity matching for # only
' ============================================
Private Function KC_ObjectKind(ByVal obj As Object) As String
    If ItemIsNote(obj) Then KC_ObjectKind = "NOTE" Else KC_ObjectKind = "DIM"
End Function

Private Function KC_CleanHashText(ByVal txt As String) As String
    txt = Replace(txt, "#", "")
    txt = Replace(txt, vbCrLf, vbLf)
    txt = Replace(txt, vbCr, vbLf)
    txt = Replace(txt, ChrW(160), " ")
    KC_CleanHashText = Trim$(txt)
End Function

Private Function KC_DimHasHash(ByVal obj As Object) As Boolean
    Dim part As Long, ann As Object, disp As Object, txt As String, fragment As Variant, i As Long, count As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If obj Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2601, , "Размер не получен."
    For part = 1 To 4
        txt = CStr(obj.GetText(part))
        If InStr(1, txt, "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then KC_DimHasHash = True: Exit Function
    Next part
    Dim lowerText As String
    If KC42_GetLower(obj, lowerText) Then
        If InStr(1, lowerText, "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then KC_DimHasHash = True: Exit Function
    End If
    Set ann = obj.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2601, , "Аннотация размера не получена."
    Set disp = ann.GetDisplayData
    If disp Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2601, , "Отображаемый текст размера не получен."
    count = disp.GetTextCount
    For i = 0 To count - 1
        fragment = disp.GetTextAtIndex(i)
        If Not IsNull(fragment) Then
            If InStr(1, CStr(fragment), "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then KC_DimHasHash = True: Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
    Exit Function
Failed:
    ' Ошибка чтения не означает отсутствие # и не разрешает добавление нового #.
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2601, "KC_DimHasHash", "Не удалось надёжно проверить текст размера."
End Function

Private Function KC_GetPersistentHex(ByVal ann As Object) As String
    KC_GetPersistentHex = KC35_GetRef(swModel, ann)
End Function

Private Function KC_ResolvePersistentHex(ByVal hexText As String) As Object
    Set KC_ResolvePersistentHex = KC35_ResolveRef(swModel, hexText)
End Function

Private Function KC_HashCandidate(ByVal savedSheet As String, ByVal savedView As String, _
        ByVal savedName As String, ByVal savedText As String, ByVal savedKind As String, _
        ByVal savedRef As String, ByVal savedX As Double, ByVal savedY As Double, _
        ByVal newFormat As Boolean, ByRef objs() As Object, ByRef anns() As Object, _
        ByRef sheets() As String, ByRef views() As String, ByRef names() As String, _
        ByRef texts() As String, ByRef xs() As Double, ByRef ys() As Double, _
        ByVal total As Long, ByRef why As String) As Long
    Dim i As Long, hits As Long, candidate As Long, target As Object
    Dim viewOK As Boolean
    If Len(savedRef) > 0 Then
        Set target = KC_ResolvePersistentHex(savedRef)
        If target Is Nothing Then
            why = "Persistent reference не разрешилась; подбор по близости запрещён."
            Exit Function
        End If
        For i = 1 To total
            If KC39_SameObject(anns(i), target) Then
                If sheets(i) = savedSheet And KC_ObjectKind(objs(i)) = savedKind Then
                    candidate = i: hits = hits + 1
                End If
            End If
        Next i
        If hits = 1 Then
            KC_HashCandidate = candidate
            why = "Точная persistent reference."
        Else
            why = "Объект ссылки не найден на сохранённом листе или найден неоднозначно."
        End If
        Exit Function
    End If
    ' No-reference matching must NOT depend on annotation position.
    ' A unique identity is sheet + owning view + annotation name + object kind.
    If Len(savedName) = 0 Then
        why = "Не сохранено имя объекта; безопасное сопоставление невозможно."
        Exit Function
    End If
    If Not newFormat And Len(savedView) = 0 Then
        why = "В старом файле не указан вид; автоматическое сопоставление запрещено."
        Exit Function
    End If
    For i = 1 To total
        If sheets(i) = savedSheet And names(i) = savedName Then
            If newFormat Then
                viewOK = (views(i) = savedView)
            Else
                viewOK = (RemoveViewScale(views(i)) = savedView)
            End If
            If viewOK Then
                If Len(savedKind) = 0 Or KC_ObjectKind(objs(i)) = savedKind Then
                    hits = hits + 1: candidate = i
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If hits = 1 Then
        ' Legacy files have no object kind; require text as an extra check.
        If Not newFormat Then
            If Len(KC_CleanHashText(savedText)) = 0 Or _
               KC_CleanHashText(texts(candidate)) <> KC_CleanHashText(savedText) Then
                why = "Старый файл: текст не совпал; пересохраните список # в новом формате."
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
        KC_HashCandidate = candidate
        why = "Уникальное совпадение листа, вида, имени и типа (если указан); координаты не учитывались."
    ElseIf hits = 0 Then
        why = "Объект с сохранённым именем не найден в указанном листе и виде."
    Else
        why = "Одинаковое имя у нескольких объектов в одном виде; изменение запрещено."
    End If
End Function

Private Sub KC_ApplyHashesFromFile(ByVal removeHashes As Boolean, Optional ByVal marker As String = "#")
    Dim xlApp As Object, xlWB As Object, ws As Object, filePath As Variant
    Dim lastRow As Long, rowCount As Long, ri As Long, si As Long, di As Long
    Dim fSheets() As String, fViews() As String, fNames() As String, fTexts() As String
    Dim fKinds() As String, fRefs() As String, fNew() As Boolean, fX() As Double, fY() As Double
    Dim plan() As Long, reasons() As String, uses() As Long
    Dim objs() As Object, anns() As Object, texts() As String, sheets() As String
    Dim views() As String, names() As String, xs() As Double, ys() As Double
    Dim total As Long, capacity As Long, allSheets As Variant, origSheet As String
    Dim successCount As Long, skipCount As Long, errorCount As Long, ok As Boolean
    Dim savedDoc As String, errText As String, actionTitle As String, report As String
    Dim sheetTitle As String, schema As String
    If marker = "@" Then
        sheetTitle = "Заметки @": schema = "KC_AT_1"
    Else
        marker = "#": sheetTitle = "Звёздочки": schema = "KC_HASH_2"
    End If
    Dim reportPath As String, reportError As String, fso As Object, reportFile As Object
    On Error GoTo ApplyError
    If removeHashes Then actionTitle = "Удаление " & marker Else actionTitle = "Восстановление " & marker
    origSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    Set xlApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
    xlApp.Visible = False
    ' Prevent workbook macros and external-link updates while reading a selection.
    xlApp.AutomationSecurity = 3
    filePath = KC_PickHashFile(xlApp, actionTitle)
    If VarType(filePath) = vbBoolean Then GoTo ApplyCleanup
    Set xlWB = xlApp.Workbooks.Open(CStr(filePath), 0, True)
    Set ws = xlWB.Worksheets(sheetTitle)
    lastRow = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If lastRow < 2 Then
        MsgBox "Файл пуст.", vbExclamation
        GoTo ApplyCleanup
    End If
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Лист" Or _
       CStr(ws.cells(1, 7).Value2) <> "Идентификатор" Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2602, , "Неожиданный формат файла " & marker & "."
    End If
    rowCount = lastRow - 1
    ReDim fSheets(1 To rowCount): ReDim fViews(1 To rowCount)
    ReDim fNames(1 To rowCount): ReDim fTexts(1 To rowCount)
    ReDim fKinds(1 To rowCount): ReDim fRefs(1 To rowCount)
    ReDim fNew(1 To rowCount): ReDim fX(1 To rowCount): ReDim fY(1 To rowCount)
    ReDim plan(1 To rowCount): ReDim reasons(1 To rowCount)
    For ri = 1 To rowCount
        fSheets(ri) = CStr(ws.cells(ri + 1, 3).Value2)
        fViews(ri) = CStr(ws.cells(ri + 1, 4).Value2)
        fNames(ri) = CStr(ws.cells(ri + 1, 7).Value2)
        fTexts(ri) = CStr(ws.cells(ri + 1, 2).Value2)
        If Not IsNumeric(ws.cells(ri + 1, 5).Value2) Or _
           Not IsNumeric(ws.cells(ri + 1, 6).Value2) Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2603, , "Некорректные координаты в строке " & (ri + 1)
        End If
        fX(ri) = CDbl(ws.cells(ri + 1, 5).Value2)
        fY(ri) = CDbl(ws.cells(ri + 1, 6).Value2)
        fNew(ri) = (CStr(ws.cells(ri + 1, 10).Value2) = schema)
        If marker = "@" And Not fNew(ri) Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2610, , "Выбран не файл сохранённых @ заметок."
        End If
        If fNew(ri) Then
            fKinds(ri) = CStr(ws.cells(ri + 1, 8).Value2)
            fRefs(ri) = CStr(ws.cells(ri + 1, 9).Value2)
            If marker = "@" And fKinds(ri) <> "NOTE" Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2611, , "В файле @ допустимы только заметки."
            End If
            savedDoc = CStr(ws.cells(ri + 1, 11).Value2)
            If Len(savedDoc) = 0 Or StrComp(savedDoc, swModel.GetPathName, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2604, , _
                    "Файл " & marker & " относится к другому пути чертежа (строка " & (ri + 1) & ")." & vbCrLf & _
                    "После переименования или Save As создайте новый файл маркеров из исходного чертежа."
            End If
            If fKinds(ri) <> "NOTE" And fKinds(ri) <> "DIM" Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2605, , "Неизвестный тип объекта в строке " & (ri + 1)
            End If
        End If
    Next ri
    Set ws = Nothing
    xlWB.Close False: Set xlWB = Nothing
    xlApp.Quit: Set xlApp = Nothing
    capacity = 500
    ReDim objs(1 To capacity): ReDim anns(1 To capacity)
    ReDim texts(1 To capacity): ReDim sheets(1 To capacity)
    ReDim views(1 To capacity): ReDim names(1 To capacity)
    ReDim xs(1 To capacity): ReDim ys(1 To capacity)
    allSheets = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For si = LBound(allSheets) To UBound(allSheets)
        swDraw.ActivateSheet CStr(allSheets(si))
        InitWorkArea
        ' Both actions use the same complete candidate list.
        CollectAllDims CStr(allSheets(si)), objs, anns, texts, sheets, _
                       views, names, xs, ys, total, capacity
    Next si
    If total = 0 Then
        MsgBox "На чертеже не найдено объектов для сопоставления.", vbExclamation
        GoTo ApplyCleanup
    End If
    ReDim uses(1 To total)
    ' Build the entire plan BEFORE modifying text or filtering used objects.
    For ri = 1 To rowCount
        plan(ri) = KC_HashCandidate(fSheets(ri), fViews(ri), fNames(ri), fTexts(ri), _
                   fKinds(ri), fRefs(ri), fX(ri), fY(ri), fNew(ri), _
                   objs, anns, sheets, views, names, texts, xs, ys, total, reasons(ri))
        If plan(ri) > 0 Then uses(plan(ri)) = uses(plan(ri)) + 1
    Next ri
    ' Reject ALL rows targeting the same object, not just the second one.
    For ri = 1 To rowCount
        If plan(ri) > 0 Then
            If uses(plan(ri)) > 1 Then
                plan(ri) = 0
                reasons(ri) = "Несколько строк файла указывают на один объект; все пропущены."
            End If
        End If
    Next ri
    If MsgBox(actionTitle & vbCrLf & "Строк в файле: " & rowCount & vbCrLf & _
              "Применить только однозначные соответствия?", vbYesNo + vbQuestion) <> vbYes Then GoTo ApplyCleanup
    report = actionTitle & vbCrLf & "Файл: " & CStr(filePath) & vbCrLf & _
             "Чертёж: " & swModel.GetPathName & vbCrLf & String$(60, "-") & vbCrLf
    For ri = 1 To rowCount
        di = plan(ri)
        If di = 0 Then
            skipCount = skipCount + 1
            report = report & "ПРОПУСК"
        Else
            swDraw.ActivateSheet sheets(di)
            If marker = "@" Then
                ok = KC_SetAtMarker(objs(di), removeHashes)
            Else
                If removeHashes Then ok = RemoveStarFromDim(objs(di)) Else ok = AddStarToDim(objs(di))
            End If
            If ok Then
                successCount = successCount + 1
                report = report & "OK"
            Else
                errorCount = errorCount + 1
                report = report & "ОШИБКА изменения текста"
            End If
        End If
        report = report & " | строка Excel " & (ri + 1) & " | " & fSheets(ri) & _
                 " | " & fViews(ri) & " | " & fNames(ri) & " | " & reasons(ri) & vbCrLf
    Next ri
    swModel.ForceRebuild3 False
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    On Error Resume Next
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If KC28_LogOn("Logs.MarkerActions") Then
    KC53_Add "ОТЧЁТ маркеры" & vbCrLf & report
    If KC53_Flush() Then reportPath = KC53_JournalPath() Else Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4900, , "журнал в папке карты недоступен"
    If Err.number <> 0 Then
        reportError = "Не удалось записать отчёт: " & Err.Description
        reportPath = ""
        Err.Clear
    End If
    Else
        reportPath = "": reportError = "Запись отчёта отключена в настройках."
    End If
    On Error GoTo ApplyError
    MsgBox actionTitle & ": завершено." & vbCrLf & _
           "Выполнено / уже в нужном состоянии: " & successCount & vbCrLf & _
           "Пропущено: " & skipCount & vbCrLf & "Ошибок изменения: " & errorCount & vbCrLf & _
           IIf(Len(reportPath) > 0, "Отчёт: " & reportPath, reportError), vbInformation
ApplyCleanup:
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = Nothing
    If Not xlWB Is Nothing Then xlWB.Close False
    If Not xlApp Is Nothing Then xlApp.Quit
    Set xlWB = Nothing: Set xlApp = Nothing
    If Len(origSheet) > 0 Then swDraw.ActivateSheet origSheet
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Len(errText) > 0 Then MsgBox errText, vbCritical
    Exit Sub
ApplyError:
    errText = actionTitle & ": " & Err.Description & vbCrLf & _
              "Успешных операций до ошибки: " & successCount & "." & vbCrLf & _
              "Изменения автоматически не сохранялись."
    Resume ApplyCleanup
End Sub

' Open the file picker in the current drawing folder, including UNC paths.
Private Function KC_PickHashFile(ByVal excelApp As Object, ByVal dialogTitle As String) As Variant
    Dim picker As Object, documentPath As String, initialFolder As String
    Dim slashPos As Long, errNumber As Long, errDescription As String
    On Error GoTo PickError
    KC_PickHashFile = False
    documentPath = swModel.GetPathName
    slashPos = InStrRev(documentPath, "\")
    If slashPos > 0 Then initialFolder = Left$(documentPath, slashPos)
    ' 3 = msoFileDialogFilePicker. Late binding: no Office reference required.
    Set picker = excelApp.FileDialog(3)
    With picker
        .title = dialogTitle
        .AllowMultiSelect = False
        .Filters.Clear
        .Filters.Add "Файлы Excel (*.xlsx;*.xls)", "*.xlsx;*.xls"
        If Len(initialFolder) > 0 Then .InitialFileName = initialFolder
        If .Show = -1 Then KC_PickHashFile = CStr(.SelectedItems(1))
    End With
    Set picker = Nothing
    Exit Function
PickError:
    errNumber = Err.number: errDescription = Err.Description
    Err.Raise errNumber, "KC_PickHashFile", "Не удалось открыть выбор файла." & vbCrLf & _
              "Начальная папка: " & initialFolder & vbCrLf & errDescription
End Function

' Direct note enumeration for the whole-note @ marker (modes 1 and 2).
' v15.14: один обход заметок листа: баллоны КК + заметки с @ (бывшие ScanSheetForBalloons_v2
' и KC_CollectMarkedNotes). Проверка дублей — словарь вместо перебора коллекции.
Private Sub KC_ScanSheetNotes(ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal collectMarked As Boolean, _
                             ByRef counter As Long, ByRef noteCount As Long, ByRef skipCount As Long, _
                             ByRef bNotes() As Object, ByRef bNums() As Long, _
                             ByRef bPosX() As Double, ByRef bPosY() As Double, ByRef bSheets() As String, _
                             ByRef bTypes() As Long, ByRef bPtrs() As Long, ByRef bTotal As Long, ByRef bMax As Long, _
                             ByVal balloonSeen As Object)
    Dim view As Object, note As Object, ann As Object
    Dim seen As Object, annKey As String
    Dim firstView As Boolean, viewName As String, stage As String
    Dim eNumber As Long, eDescription As String
    On Error GoTo NoteError
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    firstView = True
    stage = "IDrawingDoc.GetFirstView"
    Set view = swDraw.GetFirstView
    Do While Not view Is Nothing
        viewName = ""
        If collectMarked And Not firstView Then
            stage = "IView.GetName2"
            viewName = view.GetName2
        End If
        stage = "IView.GetFirstNote"
        Set note = view.GetFirstNote
        Do While Not note Is Nothing
            stage = "Поиск баллона"
            CheckAndAddBalloon_v2 note, sheetName, bNotes, bNums, bPosX, bPosY, _
                                 bSheets, bTypes, bPtrs, bTotal, bMax, balloonSeen
            If collectMarked Then
                stage = "INote.GetAnnotation"
                Set ann = note.GetAnnotation
                If Not ann Is Nothing Then
                    annKey = KC_ObjKey(ann)
                    If Not seen.Exists(annKey) Then
                        seen.Add annKey, ann
                        stage = "Проверка маркера @"
                        If NoteHasMark(note) Then
                            If InStr(1, CStr(note.GetText), "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
                                skipCount = skipCount + 1
                            Else
                                stage = "Обработка заметки с @"
                                If ProcessAnnotation(swSelNOTES, note, sheetName, counter, viewName) Then
                                    noteCount = noteCount + 1
                                Else
                                    skipCount = skipCount + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            stage = "INote.GetNext"
            Set note = note.GetNext
        Loop
        firstView = False
        stage = "IView.GetNextView"
        Set view = view.GetNextView
    Loop
    Exit Sub
NoteError:
    eNumber = Err.number: eDescription = Err.Description
    Err.Raise eNumber, "KC_ScanSheetNotes", "Поиск заметок @ и баллонов." & vbCrLf & _
              "Лист: " & sheetName & "; вид: " & viewName & vbCrLf & _
              "Этап: " & stage & vbCrLf & eDescription
End Sub

' @ marks a whole note. Restore a single prefix; never append another @.
Private Function KC_SetAtMarker(ByVal obj As Object, ByVal removeMarker As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim txt As String, result As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If obj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If Not ItemIsNote(obj) Then Exit Function
    txt = KC33_NoteRaw(obj)
    If removeMarker Then
        result = Replace(txt, "@", "")
    Else
        result = txt
        If InStr(1, txt, "@", vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then result = "@" & txt
    End If
    If result <> txt Then obj.SetText result
    KC_SetAtMarker = (KC33_NoteRaw(obj) = result)
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC_SetAtMarker = False
End Function

Private Function KC_WriteMarkerReport(ByVal report As String, ByVal suffix As String) As String
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, path As String, folder As String
    If (StrComp(suffix, "balloons", vbTextCompare) = 0 And Not KC28_LogOn("Logs.Balloons")) Or _
       (StrComp(suffix, "balloons", vbTextCompare) <> 0 And Not KC28_LogOn("Logs.MarkerConflicts")) Then
        KC_WriteMarkerReport = "запись отключена в настройках"
        Exit Function
    End If
    KC53_Add "ОТЧЁТ " & suffix & vbCrLf & report
    If KC53_Flush() Then
        KC_WriteMarkerReport = KC53_JournalPath()
    Else
        KC_WriteMarkerReport = "журнал в папке карты недоступен"
    End If
End Function

' One insertion attempt; do not count a failed or partly formatted note as success.
Private Function KC_CreateControlBalloon(ByVal positionIndex As Long, ByVal number As Long, _
                                        ByRef keySaved As Boolean, ByRef detail As String) As Boolean
    Dim note As Object, ann As Object, fmt As Object, visionSheet As Object
    Dim x As Double, y As Double, key As String, stage As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    keySaved = False
    stage = "снятие выделения"
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    stage = "InsertNote"
    Set note = swModel.InsertNote(CStr(number))
    If note Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2620, , "InsertNote вернул Nothing."
    Set ann = note.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2621, , "Не получена аннотация новой заметки."
    stage = "круглая рамка"
    note.SetBalloon 1, 2
    If note.GetBalloonStyle <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2622, , "Не установлена круглая рамка."
    note.SetTextJustification 1
    stage = "без выноски"
    ann.SetLeader3 0, 0, False, False, False, False
    If ann.GetLeaderStyle <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2624, , "Не удалось убрать выноску нового баллона."
    stage = "формат текста"
    Set fmt = ann.GetTextFormat(0)
    If Not fmt Is Nothing Then
        fmt.CharHeight = 0.0035
        ann.SetTextFormat 0, False, fmt
    End If
    stage = "координаты"
    x = positionsData(positionIndex).posX
    y = positionsData(positionIndex).posY
    ClampToWorkArea x, y
    If Not ann.SetPosition2(x + BALLOON_OFFSET_X, y + BALLOON_OFFSET_Y, 0) Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2623, , "SetPosition2 вернул False."
    End If
    stage = "ключ привязки"
    key = BuildAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(positionIndex))
    SetBalloonStableKey note, key
    keySaved = (GetBalloonStableKey(note) = key)
    detail = "СОЗДАН: номер " & number
    If Not keySaved Then detail = detail & "; ВНИМАНИЕ: ключ не сохранён, возможен дубликат при повторном запуске."
    KC_VisionTrack note, ann, positionIndex
    KC_CreateControlBalloon = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = "ОШИБКА: номер " & number & "; этап: " & stage & "; " & Err.Description
    If Not note Is Nothing Then detail = detail & "; частичная заметка могла остаться на листе."
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not note Is Nothing And gKV_Enabled Then
        If gKV_Sheets.Exists(positionsData(positionIndex).sheetName) Then
            Set visionSheet = gKV_Sheets(positionsData(positionIndex).sheetName)
            visionSheet("OK") = False
            gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Лист исключён из расстановки: частично созданный баллон. " & detail & vbCrLf
        End If
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    KC_CreateControlBalloon = False
End Function

' ============================================
' KC_MARKERS_1: one file, additive restore, scoped cleanup after saved export
' ============================================
' ============================================
' v15.13: маркеры # и @ хранятся листом "Маркеры КК" в рабочей книге карты контроля (.xlsm).
' Прежний отдельный файл ИмяЧертежа.SLDDRW_markers.xlsx читается и переносится автоматически.
' ============================================
Private Function KC_LegacyMarkerPath() As String
    Dim path As String
    path = swModel.GetPathName
    If Len(path) > 0 Then KC_LegacyMarkerPath = path & "_markers.xlsx"
End Function

' Путь рабочей книги карты контроля, в которой хранится лист маркеров.
Private Function KC_CombinedMarkerPath() As String
    Dim drawing As String
    drawing = swModel.GetPathName
    If Len(drawing) = 0 Then Exit Function
    KC_CombinedMarkerPath = KC14_Resolve(drawing, False)
End Function

Private Function KC_MarkerSheet(ByVal book As Object) As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set KC_MarkerSheet = book.Worksheets(MARKER_SHEET_NAME)
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function KC_FindOpenBook(ByVal path As String) As Object
    Dim app As Object, item As Object, same As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not gWorkBook Is Nothing Then
        same = False
        same = KC_SamePath(gWorkBook.FullName, path)
        If Err.number <> 0 Then same = False: Err.Clear
        If same Then
            Set KC_FindOpenBook = gWorkBook
            Exit Function
        End If
    End If
    Set app = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    Err.Clear
    If Not app Is Nothing Then
        For Each item In app.Workbooks
            same = False
            same = KC_SamePath(item.FullName, path)
            If Err.number <> 0 Then same = False: Err.Clear
            If same Then
                Set KC_FindOpenBook = item
                Exit For
            End If
        Next item
    End If
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

' Открывает рабочую книгу: уже открытую (рабочий Excel макроса или пользователя) или
' скрытую копию. allowCreate = создать книгу из шаблона, если её ещё нет.
Private Function KC_OpenMarkerBook(ByVal forWrite As Boolean, ByVal allowCreate As Boolean, _
        ByRef app As Object, ByRef book As Object, ByRef ownApp As Boolean, ByRef created As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim path As String, fso As Object, drawing As String, meta As Object
    ownApp = False: created = False
    Set app = Nothing: Set book = Nothing
    drawing = swModel.GetPathName
    path = KC_CombinedMarkerPath()
    If Len(path) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Set book = KC_FindOpenBook(path)
    If Not book Is Nothing Then
        Set app = book.Application
    Else
        Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
        If fso.FileExists(path) Then
            Set app = KC53_TakeExcel()
            ownApp = True
            app.Visible = False: app.DisplayAlerts = False
            app.AutomationSecurity = 3: app.EnableEvents = False
            Set book = app.Workbooks.Open(path, 0, Not forWrite)
        ElseIf allowCreate Then
            If Not fso.FileExists(INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3090, , "Нет шаблона рабочей книги: " & INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH
            Set app = KC53_TakeExcel()
            ownApp = True
            app.Visible = False: app.DisplayAlerts = False
            app.AutomationSecurity = 3: app.EnableEvents = False
            Set book = app.Workbooks.Open(INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH, 0, True)
            Set meta = KC_EditorMeta(book)
            If Len(Trim$(CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2))) > 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3091, , "Шаблон уже содержит привязку к чертежу: " & INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH
            meta.Range("B2").Value2 = drawing
            book.SaveAs path, 52
            KC_WriteIntroBinding drawing, path
            created = True
        Else
            Exit Function
        End If
    End If
    If Not KC_SamePath(CStr(KC_EditorMeta(book).Range("B2").Value2), drawing) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3092, , "Рабочая книга принадлежит другому чертежу: " & path
    If forWrite And book.ReadOnly Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3093, , "Рабочая книга открыта только для чтения. Закройте другую копию Excel: " & path
    gKC53Card = path
    KC_OpenMarkerBook = True
End Function

Private Sub KC_CloseMarkerBook(ByRef app As Object, ByRef book As Object, ByVal ownApp As Boolean)
    On Error Resume Next
    If ownApp Then
        If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
        If Not app Is Nothing Then app.Quit
    End If
    Set book = Nothing: Set app = Nothing
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

' Записывает лист "Маркеры КК". rows — текущие объекты чертежа или строки прежнего файла.
' Старый лист удаляется только после полной записи нового.
Private Sub KC_WriteMarkerSheet(ByVal book As Object, ByVal rows As Collection)
    Dim ws As Object, oldWs As Object, prevActive As Object, rec As Object, xl As Object
    Dim headers As Variant, i As Long, row As Long, alerts As Boolean, refText As String
    Dim errNum As Long, errText As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set xl = book.Application
    alerts = xl.DisplayAlerts
    On Error Resume Next
    Set prevActive = book.activeSheet
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set oldWs = KC_MarkerSheet(book)
    If Not oldWs Is Nothing Then oldWs.name = "МаркерыКК_" & Format$(Now, "hhnnss")
    Set ws = book.Worksheets.Add(After:=book.Worksheets(book.Worksheets.count))
    ws.name = MARKER_SHEET_NAME
    ws.Range("A1:B2").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.cells(1, 1).Value2 = "KC_MARKERS_1"
    ws.cells(1, 2).Value2 = "Общий список # и @ (служебный лист первого макроса, не редактировать вручную)"
    ws.cells(2, 1).Value2 = "Чертёж"
    ws.cells(2, 2).Value2 = swModel.GetPathName
    ws.cells(3, 1).Value2 = "Сохранено"
    ws.cells(3, 2).Value2 = Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")
    headers = Array("№", "Тип", "Лист", "Вид", "Имя", "Текст", "X", "Y", "PersistentReferenceHex", "#", "@")
    For i = 0 To UBound(headers): ws.cells(5, i + 1).Value2 = headers(i): Next i
    ws.Range("A5:K5").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Columns("B:F").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Columns("I").NumberFormat = "@"
    row = 6
    For Each rec In rows
        If CBool(rec("Hash")) Or CBool(rec("At")) Then
            If rec.Exists("Ref") Then
                refText = CStr(rec("Ref"))
            Else
                refText = KC_GetPersistentHex(rec("Ann"))
            End If
            ws.cells(row, 1).Value2 = row - 5
            ws.cells(row, 2).Value2 = CStr(rec("Kind"))
            ws.cells(row, 3).Value2 = CStr(rec("Sheet"))
            ws.cells(row, 4).Value2 = CStr(rec("View"))
            ws.cells(row, 5).Value2 = CStr(rec("Name"))
            ws.cells(row, 6).Value2 = CStr(rec("Text"))
            If rec.Exists("X") Then ws.cells(row, 7).Value2 = rec("X")
            If rec.Exists("Y") Then ws.cells(row, 8).Value2 = rec("Y")
            ws.cells(row, 9).Value2 = refText
            ws.cells(row, 10).Value2 = CBool(rec("Hash"))
            ws.cells(row, 11).Value2 = CBool(rec("At"))
            row = row + 1
        End If
    Next rec
    ws.Columns("A:E").AutoFit
    ws.Columns("F").ColumnWidth = 55
    ws.Columns("F").WrapText = True
    ws.Columns("G:H").ColumnWidth = 18
    ws.Columns("I").ColumnWidth = 24
    ws.Columns("J:K").ColumnWidth = 8
    If Not oldWs Is Nothing Then
        xl.DisplayAlerts = False
        oldWs.Delete
        xl.DisplayAlerts = alerts
        Set oldWs = Nothing
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not prevActive Is Nothing Then prevActive.Activate
    On Error GoTo 0
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errNum = Err.number: errText = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    xl.DisplayAlerts = False
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then ws.Delete
    If Not oldWs Is Nothing Then oldWs.name = MARKER_SHEET_NAME
    xl.DisplayAlerts = alerts
    If Not prevActive Is Nothing Then prevActive.Activate
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errNum, "KC_WriteMarkerSheet", errText
End Sub

' Читает список маркеров: лист рабочей книги, иначе прежний отдельный файл.
' found = False — списка ещё нет (это не ошибка).
Private Function KC_LoadMarkerRows(ByRef rows As Collection, ByRef found As Boolean, _
        ByRef sourceText As String, ByRef errText As String, ByVal allowMigrate As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim app As Object, book As Object, ownApp As Boolean, created As Boolean
    Dim legacyPath As String, fso As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    found = False: sourceText = "": errText = ""
    Set rows = Nothing
    If KC_OpenMarkerBook(False, False, app, book, ownApp, created) Then
        If Not KC_MarkerSheet(book) Is Nothing Then
            KC_ParseMarkerBook book, rows, True
            found = True
            sourceText = book.FullName & " — лист «" & MARKER_SHEET_NAME & "»"
        End If
    End If
    KC_CloseMarkerBook app, book, ownApp
    If Not found Then
        legacyPath = KC_LegacyMarkerPath()
        Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
        If Len(legacyPath) > 0 Then
            If fso.FileExists(legacyPath) Then
                If Not KC_LoadCombinedRows(legacyPath, rows, errText, True) Then Exit Function
                found = True
                sourceText = legacyPath & " (прежний отдельный файл)"
                If allowMigrate Then KC_MigrateLegacyMarkers rows, legacyPath, sourceText
            End If
        End If
    End If
    KC_LoadMarkerRows = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errText = "Чтение маркеров: " & Err.Description
    KC_CloseMarkerBook app, book, ownApp
End Function

Private Sub KC_MigrateLegacyMarkers(ByVal rows As Collection, ByVal legacyPath As String, ByRef sourceText As String)
    Dim app As Object, book As Object, ownApp As Boolean, created As Boolean, errText As String, bookPath As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    ' Книги ещё нет — продолжаем читать прежний файл; перенос при следующем запуске.
    If Not KC_OpenMarkerBook(True, False, app, book, ownApp, created) Then Exit Sub
    bookPath = book.FullName
    KC_WriteMarkerSheet book, rows
    book.Save
    KC_CloseMarkerBook app, book, ownApp
    sourceText = bookPath & " — лист «" & MARKER_SHEET_NAME & "» (перенесено из " & legacyPath & ")"
    MsgBox "Маркеры # и @ перенесены из отдельного файла" & vbCrLf & legacyPath & vbCrLf & _
           "в лист «" & MARKER_SHEET_NAME & "» рабочей книги:" & vbCrLf & bookPath & vbCrLf & _
           "Отдельный файл больше не используется; его можно удалить.", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errText = Err.Description
    KC_CloseMarkerBook app, book, ownApp
    MsgBox "Не удалось перенести маркеры в рабочую книгу: " & errText & vbCrLf & _
           "Пока используется прежний файл: " & legacyPath, vbExclamation
End Sub

Private Sub KC_SaveCombinedMarkers()
    Dim rows As Collection, rec As Object, scope As Object, fso As Object
    Dim app As Object, book As Object, ownApp As Boolean, created As Boolean
    Dim countHash As Long, countAt As Long, errText As String, stage As String
    Dim bookPath As String, legacyNote As String, lostText As String, lost As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Len(swModel.GetPathName) = 0 Then
        MsgBox "Сначала сохраните сам чертёж.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    stage = "Сбор разметки"
    Set rows = KC_CurrentMarkerObjects(scope)
    For Each rec In rows
        If rec("Hash") Then countHash = countHash + 1
        If rec("At") Then countAt = countAt + 1
    Next rec
    If countHash + countAt = 0 Then
        If MsgBox("На чертеже сейчас нет # и @." & vbCrLf & _
                  "Сохранить ПУСТОЙ список? Старые правила будут заменены пустыми.", _
                  vbYesNo + vbExclamation, "Запоминание маркеров") <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
    End If
    stage = "Открытие рабочей книги"
    If Not KC_OpenMarkerBook(True, True, app, book, ownApp, created) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3094, , "Не найдена рабочая книга карты контроля."
    bookPath = book.FullName
    If Not KC_MarkerSheet(book) Is Nothing Then lost = KC51_LostRules(book, rows, lostText)
    If lost > 0 Then
        If MsgBox("ВНИМАНИЕ: в сохранённом списке " & lost & " объект(ов) есть на чертеже, но сейчас БЕЗ маркеров." & vbCrLf & _
            "Обычно это значит, что режим 1 снял маркеры после выгрузки. При сохранении они будут УДАЛЕНЫ из списка," & vbCrLf & _
            "и следующий режим 1 перестанет их учитывать." & lostText & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "Правильный порядок: 2.2 (восстановить) -> изменить разметку -> 2.1." & vbCrLf & _
            "Да = всё равно заменить список; Нет = ничего не менять.", _
            vbYesNo + vbExclamation + vbDefaultButton2, "Запоминание маркеров") <> vbYes Then GoTo Cleanup
        KC46_Trace "MARKERS_21_OVERWRITE | пользователь подтвердил удаление правил: " & lost
    ElseIf countHash + countAt > 0 And Not KC_MarkerSheet(book) Is Nothing Then
        If MsgBox("Заменить сохранённый список текущими # и @?" & vbCrLf & _
                  "Для правки правил сначала восстановите ВСЮ разметку через 2.2, затем измените её." & vbCrLf & _
                  "Размеров/заметок с #: " & countHash & "; заметок с @: " & countAt & vbCrLf & _
                  bookPath & " — лист «" & MARKER_SHEET_NAME & "»", vbYesNo + vbQuestion) <> vbYes Then GoTo Cleanup
    End If
    stage = "Запись листа «" & MARKER_SHEET_NAME & "»"
    KC_WriteMarkerSheet book, rows
    stage = "Сохранение рабочей книги"
    book.Save
    KC_CloseMarkerBook app, book, ownApp
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If fso.FileExists(KC_LegacyMarkerPath()) Then
        legacyNote = vbCrLf & "Прежний отдельный файл больше не используется, его можно удалить:" & vbCrLf & KC_LegacyMarkerPath()
    End If
    MsgBox "Маркеры сохранены в лист «" & MARKER_SHEET_NAME & "» рабочей книги." & vbCrLf & _
           IIf(created, "Рабочая книга создана из шаблона." & vbCrLf, "") & _
           "С #: " & countHash & "; с @: " & countAt & vbCrLf & bookPath & vbCrLf & _
           "Режим 1 будет читать их автоматически." & legacyNote, vbInformation
    Exit Sub
Cleanup:
    KC_CloseMarkerBook app, book, ownApp
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errText = "Не удалось сохранить маркеры." & vbCrLf & "Этап: " & stage & vbCrLf & Err.Description
    KC_CloseMarkerBook app, book, ownApp
    MsgBox errText, vbCritical
End Sub

Private Function KC_InMarkerScope(ByVal scope As Object, ByVal sheet As String) As Boolean
    If scope Is Nothing Then
        KC_InMarkerScope = True
    Else
        KC_InMarkerScope = scope.Exists(sheet)
    End If
End Function

Private Function KC_MarkerScope(ByVal allSheets As Variant, ByRef selected() As Boolean) As Object
    Dim scope As Object, i As Long
    Set scope = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For i = LBound(allSheets) To UBound(allSheets)
        If selected(i) Then scope.Add CStr(allSheets(i)), True
    Next i
    ' Nothing means ALL sheets, including missing saved sheets that must be reported.
    If scope.count = UBound(allSheets) - LBound(allSheets) + 1 Then Exit Function
    Set KC_MarkerScope = scope
End Function

Private Sub KC_ResetAutomaticMarkers()
    Set gKC_AutoRows = Nothing
    Set gKC_AutoScope = Nothing
    gKC_AutoPath = ""
End Sub

Private Function KC_CurrentMarkerObjects(ByVal scope As Object) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, rec As Object, origSheet As String
    Dim objs() As Object, anns() As Object, texts() As String, sheets() As String
    Dim views() As String, names() As String, xs() As Double, ys() As Double
    Dim capacity As Long, total As Long, allSheets As Variant, si As Long, i As Long
    Dim errNum As Long, errDesc As String, hashFlag As Boolean, atFlag As Boolean, txt As String
    Dim gfxWasOff As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    gfxWasOff = gKC_GfxOff
    KC_GraphicsOff
    origSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    capacity = 200
    ReDim objs(1 To capacity): ReDim anns(1 To capacity)
    ReDim texts(1 To capacity): ReDim sheets(1 To capacity)
    ReDim views(1 To capacity): ReDim names(1 To capacity)
    ReDim xs(1 To capacity): ReDim ys(1 To capacity)
    allSheets = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For si = LBound(allSheets) To UBound(allSheets)
        If KC_InMarkerScope(scope, CStr(allSheets(si))) Then
            swDraw.ActivateSheet CStr(allSheets(si))
            InitWorkArea
            CollectAllDims CStr(allSheets(si)), objs, anns, texts, sheets, _
                           views, names, xs, ys, total, capacity
        End If
    Next si
    Dim currentOwnerSheet As String
    For i = 1 To total
        ' Чтение COM-свойств размера только при активном листе владельца.
        If currentOwnerSheet <> sheets(i) Then
            If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(sheets(i)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4420, , "Не активирован лист " & sheets(i)
            currentOwnerSheet = sheets(i)
        End If
        Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
        rec.Add "Obj", objs(i): rec.Add "Ann", anns(i)
        rec.Add "Kind", KC_ObjectKind(objs(i))
        rec.Add "Sheet", sheets(i): rec.Add "View", views(i): rec.Add "Name", names(i)
        rec.Add "Text", texts(i): rec.Add "X", xs(i): rec.Add "Y", ys(i)
        hashFlag = False: atFlag = False
        If rec("Kind") = "NOTE" Then
            ' v15.14: текст заметки уже прочитан при сборе (Trim не влияет на поиск # и @).
            txt = texts(i)
            hashFlag = (InStr(1, txt, "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0)
            atFlag = (InStr(1, txt, "@", vbBinaryCompare) > 0)
        Else
            hashFlag = KC_DimHasHash(objs(i))
        End If
        rec.Add "Hash", hashFlag: rec.Add "At", atFlag
        result.Add rec
    Next i
    swDraw.ActivateSheet origSheet
    If Not gfxWasOff Then KC_GraphicsOn
    Set KC_CurrentMarkerObjects = result
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errNum = Err.number: errDesc = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(origSheet) > 0 Then swDraw.ActivateSheet origSheet
    If Not gfxWasOff Then KC_GraphicsOn
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errNum, "KC_CurrentMarkerObjects", errDesc
End Function

' Разбор листа маркеров в открытой книге. Ошибки передаются вызывающему.
Private Sub KC_ParseMarkerBook(ByVal book As Object, ByRef rows As Collection, ByVal requireCombined As Boolean)
    Dim ws As Object, rec As Object
    Dim row As Long, firstDataRow As Long, lastRow As Long, kind As String, legacyKind As String, schema As String
    Dim isCombined As Boolean, savedPath As String, hasHash As Boolean, hasAt As Boolean
    Set rows = New Collection
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = book.Worksheets(MARKER_SHEET_NAME)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        isCombined = True
        If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "KC_MARKERS_1" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2700, , "Неизвестный формат списка маркеров."
        savedPath = CStr(ws.cells(2, 2).Value2)
        If Len(savedPath) = 0 Or StrComp(savedPath, swModel.GetPathName, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2701, , "Список маркеров относится к другому пути чертежа."
        End If
        If CStr(ws.cells(5, 10).Value2) <> "#" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 11).Value2) <> "@" Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2702, , "Повреждены заголовки списка маркеров."
        End If
        firstDataRow = 6
    Else
        If requireCombined Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2709, , "Ожидался общий файл с листом Маркеры КК."
        ' Manual import of old separate files. Automatic mode reads only the common file.
        On Error Resume Next
        Set ws = book.Worksheets("Звёздочки")
        If Not ws Is Nothing Then legacyKind = "#"
        If ws Is Nothing Then
            Set ws = book.Worksheets("Заметки @")
            If Not ws Is Nothing Then legacyKind = "@"
        End If
        On Error GoTo 0
        If ws Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2703, , "Не найден лист маркеров КК, # или @."
        If CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(ws.cells(1, 7).Value2) <> "Идентификатор" Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2704, , "Неизвестный старый формат."
        End If
        firstDataRow = 2
    End If
    lastRow = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    For row = firstDataRow To lastRow
        If isCombined Then
            If book.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range(ws.cells(row, 1), ws.cells(row, 11))) = 0 Then GoTo ArchivedMarkerNext
        End If
        Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
        rec.Add "ExcelRow", row
        If isCombined Then
            kind = CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2)
            rec.Add "Kind", kind: rec.Add "Sheet", CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2)
            rec.Add "View", CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2): rec.Add "Name", CStr(ws.cells(row, 5).Value2)
            rec.Add "Text", CStr(ws.cells(row, 6).Value2): rec.Add "Ref", CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
            rec.Add "Legacy", False
            hasHash = CBool(ws.cells(row, 10).Value2): hasAt = CBool(ws.cells(row, 11).Value2)
        Else
            schema = CStr(ws.cells(row, 10).Value2)
            kind = CStr(ws.cells(row, 8).Value2)
            savedPath = CStr(ws.cells(row, 11).Value2)
            If schema = "KC_HASH_2" Or schema = "KC_AT_1" Then
                If StrComp(savedPath, swModel.GetPathName, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2705, , "Старый файл относится к другому пути чертежа."
                End If
            Else
                kind = "" ' Very old files lack an object kind; text must also match.
            End If
            rec.Add "Kind", kind: rec.Add "Sheet", CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2)
            rec.Add "View", CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2): rec.Add "Name", CStr(ws.cells(row, 7).Value2)
            rec.Add "Text", CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2): rec.Add "Ref", CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
            rec.Add "Legacy", (Len(kind) = 0)
            hasHash = (legacyKind = "#"): hasAt = (legacyKind = "@")
        End If
        If kind <> "DIM" And kind <> "NOTE" And Not CBool(rec("Legacy")) Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2706, , "Неверный тип в строке " & row
        End If
        If hasAt And kind = "DIM" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2707, , "@ недопустим для размера."
        If Not hasHash And Not hasAt Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2708, , "Пустые признаки маркеров в строке " & row
        rec.Add "Hash", hasHash: rec.Add "At", hasAt
        rows.Add rec
ArchivedMarkerNext:
    Next row
End Sub

Private Function KC_LoadCombinedRows(ByVal path As String, ByRef rows As Collection, ByRef errText As String, _
                                     Optional ByVal requireCombined As Boolean = False) As Boolean
    Dim app As Object, book As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set rows = New Collection
    Set app = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
    app.Visible = False: app.AutomationSecurity = 3
    Set book = app.Workbooks.Open(path, 0, True)
    KC_ParseMarkerBook book, rows, requireCombined
    KC_LoadCombinedRows = True
Cleanup:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
    If Not app Is Nothing Then app.Quit
    On Error GoTo 0
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errText = "Чтение файла маркеров: " & Err.Description
    KC_LoadCombinedRows = False
    Resume Cleanup
End Function

' v15.14: индекс "лист -> номера объектов" (в исходном порядке), чтобы не перебирать все листы.
Private Function KC_BuildSheetIndex(ByVal current As Collection) As Object
    ' Элемент = Array(номер в current, запись): без медленного доступа к Collection по номеру.
    Dim idx As Object, i As Long, sheetKey As String, rec As Object
    Set idx = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each rec In current
        i = i + 1
        sheetKey = CStr(rec("Sheet"))
        If Not idx.Exists(sheetKey) Then idx.Add sheetKey, New Collection
        idx.item(sheetKey).Add Array(i, rec)
    Next rec
    Set KC_BuildSheetIndex = idx
End Function

Private Function KC_FindCombinedObject(ByVal saved As Object, ByVal current As Collection, ByRef reason As String, _
                                       Optional ByVal sheetIndex As Object = Nothing) As Long
    Dim i As Long, rec As Object, ann As Object, target As Object, hits As Long, found As Long
    Dim useRef As Boolean, viewOK As Boolean, kindOK As Boolean
    Dim candidates As Collection, item As Variant, k As Long
    useRef = (Len(CStr(saved("Ref"))) > 0)
    If useRef Then
        Set target = KC_ResolvePersistentHex(CStr(saved("Ref")))
        If target Is Nothing Then
            reason = "Не разрешилась persistent reference."
            Exit Function
        End If
    ElseIf Len(CStr(saved("Name"))) = 0 Then
        reason = "Не сохранено имя объекта."
        Exit Function
    End If
    If sheetIndex Is Nothing Then
        Set candidates = New Collection
        For Each rec In current
            k = k + 1
            candidates.Add Array(k, rec)
        Next rec
    ElseIf sheetIndex.Exists(CStr(saved("Sheet"))) Then
        Set candidates = sheetIndex.item(CStr(saved("Sheet")))
    Else
        Set candidates = New Collection
    End If
    For Each item In candidates
        i = CLng(item(0))
        Set rec = item(1)
        If rec("Sheet") = saved("Sheet") Then
            kindOK = (rec("Kind") = saved("Kind") Or Len(CStr(saved("Kind"))) = 0)
            If kindOK Then
                If useRef Then
                    Set ann = rec("Ann")
                    If KC39_SameObject(ann, target) Then hits = hits + 1: found = i
                Else
                    If CBool(saved("Legacy")) Then
                        viewOK = (RemoveViewScale(CStr(rec("View"))) = CStr(saved("View")))
                    Else
                        viewOK = (rec("View") = saved("View"))
                    End If
                    If viewOK And rec("Name") = saved("Name") Then hits = hits + 1: found = i
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next item
    If hits = 1 Then
        Set rec = current(found)
        If CBool(saved("Legacy")) Then
            If Len(CStr(saved("View"))) = 0 Or KC_CleanHashText(CStr(rec("Text"))) <> KC_CleanHashText(CStr(saved("Text"))) Then
                reason = "Старый файл: не подтверждены вид и текст."
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
        If CBool(saved("At")) And rec("Kind") <> "NOTE" Then
            reason = "@ сопоставился не с заметкой."
            Exit Function
        End If
        KC_FindCombinedObject = found
        reason = "Однозначная привязка; координаты не учитывались."
    ElseIf hits = 0 Then
        reason = "Объект не найден в указанном листе и виде."
    Else
        reason = "Несколько объектов с одинаковыми признаками."
    End If
End Function

Private Sub KC_CaptureMarkerText(ByVal rec As Object)
    Dim obj As Object, parts As Variant, i As Long, lowerText As String, lowerOK As Boolean
    Set obj = rec("Obj")
    If rec("Kind") = "NOTE" Then
        rec.Add "Before", KC33_NoteRaw(obj)
    Else
        parts = Array("", "", "", "")
        For i = 0 To 3: parts(i) = CStr(obj.GetText(i + 1)): Next i
        rec.Add "Before", parts
        lowerOK = KC42_GetLower(obj, lowerText)
        rec.Add "BeforeLowerOK", lowerOK
        rec.Add "BeforeLower", lowerText
    End If
End Sub

Private Function KC_RevertMarkerText(ByVal rec As Object) As Boolean
    Dim obj As Object, parts As Variant, i As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set obj = rec("Obj")
    If rec("Kind") = "NOTE" Then
