        If Not KC34_SetNoteRaw(obj, CStr(rec("Before"))) Then Exit Function
    Else
        parts = rec("Before")
        If CBool(rec("BeforeLowerOK")) Then
            Dim lowerNow As String
            If Not KC42_GetLower(obj, lowerNow) Then Exit Function
            If lowerNow <> CStr(rec("BeforeLower")) Then obj.SetLowerText CStr(rec("BeforeLower"))
        End If
        ' Не переписывать без нужды части размера, особенно со связями.
        For i = 0 To 3
            If CStr(obj.GetText(i + 1)) <> CStr(parts(i)) Then obj.SetText i + 1, CStr(parts(i))
        Next i
        For i = 0 To 3
            If CStr(obj.GetText(i + 1)) <> CStr(parts(i)) Then Exit Function
        Next i
        If CBool(rec("BeforeLowerOK")) Then
            If Not KC42_GetLower(obj, lowerNow) Then Exit Function
            If lowerNow <> CStr(rec("BeforeLower")) Then Exit Function
        End If
    End If
    KC_RevertMarkerText = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC_RevertMarkerText = False
End Function

Private Function KC_ChangeCombinedObject(ByVal current As Object, ByVal saved As Object, ByVal remove As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim obj As Object, txt As String, result As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set obj = current("Obj")
    If current("Kind") = "NOTE" Then
        txt = KC33_NoteRaw(obj): result = txt   ' v16.2: не разрушать $PRP-связи
        If CBool(saved("Hash")) Then
            If remove Then
                result = KC34_WithoutMarker(result, "#")
            ElseIf KC34_WithoutMarker(result, "#") = result Then
                result = result & "#"
            End If
        End If
        If CBool(saved("At")) Then
            If remove Then
                result = KC34_WithoutMarker(result, "@")
            ElseIf KC34_WithoutMarker(result, "@") = result Then
                result = "@" & result
            End If
        End If
        If result <> txt Then
            If Not KC34_SetNoteRaw(obj, result) Then Exit Function
        End If
        KC_ChangeCombinedObject = (KC33_NoteRaw(obj) = result)
    ElseIf CBool(saved("Hash")) Then
        If remove Then KC_ChangeCombinedObject = RemoveStarFromDim(obj) Else KC_ChangeCombinedObject = AddStarToDim(obj)
    Else
        KC_ChangeCombinedObject = True
    End If
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC_ChangeCombinedObject = False
End Function

Private Function KC_ApplyCombinedRows(ByVal savedRows As Collection, ByVal scope As Object, _
                                     ByVal remove As Boolean, ByVal quiet As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim current As Collection, saved As Object, rec As Object, counts As Object
    Dim plan() As Long, reasons() As String, i As Long, j As Long, active As Long, problems As Long
    Dim applied As Long, rollbackErrors As Long, report As String, reportPath As String
    Dim origSheet As String, errText As String, action As String
    Dim sheetIndex As Object, activeSheet As String, gfxWasOff As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If remove Then action = "Снятие # и @" Else action = "Восстановление # и @"
    gfxWasOff = gKC_GfxOff
    KC_GraphicsOff
    origSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    Set current = KC_CurrentMarkerObjects(Nothing)
    KC35_RefreshMarkerOwners savedRows, current
    Set sheetIndex = KC_BuildSheetIndex(current)
    Dim curArr() As Object, ci As Long
    ReDim curArr(0 To current.count)
    For Each rec In current
        ci = ci + 1
        Set curArr(ci) = rec
    Next rec
    Set counts = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    ReDim plan(0 To savedRows.count): ReDim reasons(0 To savedRows.count)
    For i = 1 To savedRows.count
        Set saved = savedRows(i)
        If KC_InMarkerScope(scope, CStr(saved("Sheet"))) Then
            active = active + 1
            plan(i) = KC_FindCombinedObject(saved, current, reasons(i), sheetIndex)
            If plan(i) > 0 Then
                If counts.Exists(CStr(plan(i))) Then
                    counts(CStr(plan(i))) = CLng(counts(CStr(plan(i)))) + 1
                Else
                    counts.Add CStr(plan(i)), 1
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    report = action & vbCrLf
    For i = 1 To savedRows.count
        Set saved = savedRows(i)
        If KC_InMarkerScope(scope, CStr(saved("Sheet"))) Then
            If plan(i) > 0 Then
                If CLng(counts(CStr(plan(i)))) > 1 Then
                    plan(i) = 0: reasons(i) = "Несколько строк файла указывают на один объект."
                End If
            End If
            If plan(i) = 0 Then problems = problems + 1
            report = report & CStr(saved("Sheet")) & " | " & CStr(saved("View")) & " | " & _
                     CStr(saved("Name")) & " | " & reasons(i) & vbCrLf
        End If
    Next i
    If problems > 0 Then
        reportPath = KC_WriteMarkerReport(report, "marker_conflicts")
        MsgBox action & " не выполнено: неоднозначных/не найденных записей: " & problems & "." & vbCrLf & _
               "Ничего не изменено. Исправьте разметку/файл перед запуском режима." & vbCrLf & _
               "Отчёт: " & reportPath, vbExclamation
        GoTo Cleanup
    End If
    ' Capture ALL originals before the first mutation, including both flags on a note.
    For i = 1 To savedRows.count
        If plan(i) > 0 Then
            Set rec = curArr(plan(i))
            If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(CStr(rec("Sheet"))) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4421, , "Не активирован лист для снимка маркера: " & CStr(rec("Sheet"))
            KC_CaptureMarkerText rec
        End If
    Next i
    activeSheet = ""
    For i = 1 To savedRows.count
        If plan(i) > 0 Then
            Set rec = curArr(plan(i)): Set saved = savedRows(i)
            If activeSheet <> CStr(rec("Sheet")) Then
                If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(CStr(rec("Sheet"))) Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2710, , "Не активирован лист маркера: " & CStr(rec("Sheet"))
                activeSheet = CStr(rec("Sheet"))
            End If
            ' Если ранее при активном листе # был виден, а теперь не читается,
            ' не добавляем второй маркер вслепую: останавливаем операцию с откатом.
            If Not remove And CStr(rec("Kind")) = "DIM" And CBool(saved("Hash")) Then
                If InStr(1, CStr(rec("Text")), "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
                    If Not KC_DimHasHash(rec("Obj")) Then _
                        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2710, , "Несогласованное чтение #; повторное добавление запрещено: " & _
                            CStr(rec("Sheet")) & "/" & CStr(rec("Name"))
                End If
            End If
            applied = i
            gKC_MarkerDetail = ""
            If Not KC_ChangeCombinedObject(rec, saved, remove) Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2710, , "Не удалось изменить маркеры: " & CStr(rec("Sheet")) & "/" & CStr(rec("Name")) & _
                    IIf(Len(gKC_MarkerDetail) > 0, vbCrLf & gKC_MarkerDetail, "")
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    swModel.ForceRebuild3 False
    KC_ApplyCombinedRows = True
    If Not quiet Then MsgBox action & " завершено. Объектов по файлу: " & active & ".", vbInformation
Cleanup:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(origSheet) > 0 Then swDraw.ActivateSheet origSheet
    If Not gfxWasOff Then KC_GraphicsOn
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    On Error GoTo 0
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errText = action & ": " & Err.Description
    ' Best-effort rollback of the current and all previously attempted objects.
    On Error Resume Next
    For j = 1 To applied
        If plan(j) > 0 Then
            Set rec = curArr(plan(j))
            swDraw.ActivateSheet CStr(rec("Sheet"))
            If Not KC_RevertMarkerText(rec) Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
        End If
    Next j
    On Error GoTo 0
    MsgBox errText & vbCrLf & "Выполнен возврат исходного текста." & vbCrLf & _
           "Ошибок возврата: " & rollbackErrors & ". Чертёж автоматически не сохранялся.", vbCritical
    GoTo Cleanup
End Function

Private Function KC_PrepareAutomaticMarkers(ByVal allSheets As Variant, ByRef selected() As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim fso As Object, rows As Collection, scope As Object, errText As String
    Dim found As Boolean, sourceText As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    KC_ResetAutomaticMarkers
    If Len(swModel.GetPathName) = 0 Then
        KC_PrepareAutomaticMarkers = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    If Not KC_LoadMarkerRows(rows, found, sourceText, errText, True) Then
        MsgBox errText & vbCrLf & "Режим остановлен до сбора аннотаций.", vbExclamation
        Exit Function
    End If
    If Not found Then
        Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
        If fso.FileExists(swModel.GetPathName & "_hashes.xlsx") Or fso.FileExists(swModel.GetPathName & "_at_notes.xlsx") Then
            If MsgBox("Лист «" & MARKER_SHEET_NAME & "» в рабочей книге пока не создан; найдены старые раздельные файлы." & vbCrLf & _
                      "Список создаётся командой 2.1 по видимым # и @. Старые раздельные файлы не подбираются." & vbCrLf & _
                      "Продолжить сейчас только по видимым # и @?", vbYesNo + vbExclamation) <> vbYes Then Exit Function
        End If
        KC_PrepareAutomaticMarkers = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    Set scope = KC_MarkerScope(allSheets, selected)
    If Not KC_ApplyCombinedRows(rows, scope, False, True) Then Exit Function
    Set gKC_AutoRows = rows: Set gKC_AutoScope = scope: gKC_AutoPath = sourceText
    KC_PrepareAutomaticMarkers = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    MsgBox "Автовосстановление маркеров: " & Err.Description & vbCrLf & "Режим остановлен.", vbCritical
End Function

Private Function KC_CleanupMarkersAfterExport() As Boolean
    Dim current As Collection, rec As Object, remaining As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If gKC_AutoRows Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "Excel сохранён. Маркеры оставлены: список «Маркеры КК» не был загружен." & vbCrLf & _
               "Сначала запомните разметку через режим 2.1.", vbInformation
        Exit Function
    End If
    If Not KC_ApplyCombinedRows(gKC_AutoRows, gKC_AutoScope, True, True) Then
        MsgBox "Excel уже сохранён, но автоматическое снятие маркеров не завершено. Проверьте чертёж.", vbExclamation
        Exit Function
    End If
    Set current = KC_CurrentMarkerObjects(gKC_AutoScope)
    For Each rec In current
        If rec("Hash") Or rec("At") Then remaining = remaining + 1
    Next rec
    MsgBox "Excel сохранён. Маркеры из списка «Маркеры КК» сняты на обработанных листах." & vbCrLf & _
           "Объектов с оставшимися маркерами: " & remaining & "." & vbCrLf & _
           "Новая разметка вне файла не удаляется; запомните её через 2.1." & vbCrLf & _
           "Список правил не перезаписывался. Сохраните чертёж обычным способом.", vbInformation
    KC_CleanupMarkersAfterExport = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    MsgBox "Excel сохранён, но при проверке снятия маркеров произошла ошибка: " & Err.Description, vbExclamation
End Function

Private Sub KC_CombinedFileAction(ByVal remove As Boolean)
    Dim rows As Collection, scope As Object, errText As String
    Dim found As Boolean, sourceText As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Len(swModel.GetPathName) = 0 Then
        MsgBox "Сначала сохраните сам чертёж. Без его пути рабочую книгу определить нельзя.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If Not KC_LoadMarkerRows(rows, found, sourceText, errText, True) Then
        MsgBox errText & vbCrLf & "Маркеры не изменены.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If Not found Then
        MsgBox "Список маркеров не найден: в рабочей книге нет листа «" & MARKER_SHEET_NAME & "»." & vbCrLf & _
               KC_CombinedMarkerPath() & vbCrLf & _
               "Сначала расставьте нужные # и @ и запомните их через режим 2.1." & vbCrLf & _
               "Маркеры не изменены.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    ' No picker or extra confirmation. All saved sheets; preflight remains mandatory.
    Call KC_ApplyCombinedRows(rows, scope, remove, False)
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    MsgBox "Операция со списком маркеров: " & Err.Description, vbCritical
End Sub


Private Function KC_TrySetNoteNumber(ByVal note As Object, ByVal number As Long) As Boolean
    Dim original As String, captured As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    original = CStr(note.GetText): captured = True
    note.SetText CStr(number)
    If Trim$(CStr(note.GetText)) <> CStr(number) Then GoTo Failed
    KC_TrySetNoteNumber = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    On Error Resume Next
    If captured Then note.SetText original
    KC_TrySetNoteNumber = False
End Function


' ============================================
' New-balloon placement prototype: local PDF + Python/OpenCV, no existing moves.
' ============================================
Private Function KC_VisionQuote(ByVal text As String) As String
    KC_VisionQuote = Chr$(34) & text & Chr$(34)
End Function

Private Sub KC_VisionBegin()
    Dim fso As Object, root As String, debugRoot As String
    Set gKV_Items = New Collection
    Set gKV_Sheets = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    gKV_Enabled = False: gKV_Folder = "": gKV_Notes = "": gKV_TimedOut = False
    gKV_AutoCount = 0: gKV_DefaultCount = 0: gKV_SourceMovedCount = 0
    gKV_Diagnostics = ""
    gKV_AllPDFPath = "": gKV_AllPDFState = 0: gKV_AllPDFNames = Empty
    Set gKV_NeedSheets = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): gKV_ExportNames = Empty
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not KC28_B("Vision.Enabled") Then Exit Sub
    root = KC28_S("Paths.VisionRoot")
    gKV_Python = root & "\SW_Balloons\SW_Balloons.exe"
    gKV_Script = "" ' EXE contains the Python handler and dependencies.
    gKV_Config = root & "\settings.json"
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Not fso.FileExists(gKV_Python) Then
        gKV_Notes = "Обработчик не установлен. Новые баллоны оставлены в стандартных местах."
        Exit Sub
    End If
    ' v16.5.15: рабочие файлы обработчика на локальном диске, а не в сетевой папке; удаляются после работы.
    debugRoot = fso.BuildPath(fso.GetSpecialFolder(2), "KC_Vision")
    If Not fso.FolderExists(debugRoot) Then fso.CreateFolder debugRoot
    gKV_Folder = fso.BuildPath(debugRoot, Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss") & "_" & fso.GetTempName)
    fso.CreateFolder gKV_Folder
    gKV_Enabled = True
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    gKV_Notes = "Подготовка обработчика: " & Err.Description
End Sub

Private Sub KC_VisionBeforeNew(ByVal sheetName As String)
    Dim rec As Object, sheet As Object, props As Variant, stage As String
    Dim ok As Boolean, activeName As String, i As Long, pageIndex As Long, matches As Long
    Dim errNumber As Long, errText As String
    If Not gKV_Enabled Then Exit Sub
    If gKV_Sheets.Exists(sheetName) Then Exit Sub
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "OK", False: rec.Add "PDF", "": rec.Add "W", 0#: rec.Add "H", 0#
    rec.Add "PDFReady", False: rec.Add "PageIndex", -1: rec.Add "PageCount", 0
    gKV_Sheets.Add sheetName, rec
    ' The first call is before the first NEW note in this run, on any sheet.
    stage = "общий PDF до вставки новых кругов"
    If Not KC_VisionExportAllPDF() Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2855, , "Общий PDF недоступен; повторный экспорт после вставки кругов не выполняется."
    End If
    rec("PDF") = gKV_AllPDFPath: rec("PDFReady") = True
    stage = "сопоставление листа со страницей"
    For i = LBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) To UBound(gKV_AllPDFNames)
        If StrComp(CStr(gKV_AllPDFNames(i)), sheetName, vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then
            matches = matches + 1: pageIndex = i - LBound(gKV_AllPDFNames)
        End If
    Next i
    If matches <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2856, , "Лист не сопоставлен однозначно с порядком страниц PDF."
    rec("PageIndex") = pageIndex
    rec("PageCount") = UBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) - LBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) + 1
    stage = "активация листа и размеры"
    ok = swDraw.ActivateSheet(sheetName)
    If Not ok Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2840, , "Не активирован лист для проверки размеров."
    Set sheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    activeName = CStr(sheet.GetName)
    If StrComp(activeName, sheetName, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2807, , "Активен другой лист: " & activeName
    End If
    props = sheet.GetProperties
    rec("W") = CDbl(props(5)): rec("H") = CDbl(props(6))
    If CDbl(rec("W")) <= 0 Or CDbl(rec("H")) <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2841, , "Не получены размеры листа."
    rec("OK") = True
    gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF MAP | sheet=" & sheetName & "; page=" & (pageIndex + 1) & _
                      "; total=" & rec("PageCount") & "; W=" & CStr(rec("W")) & "; H=" & CStr(rec("H")) & vbCrLf
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errNumber = Err.number: errText = Err.Description
    gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF SHEET_FAILED | sheet=" & sheetName & "; stage=" & stage & _
                      "; Err.Number=" & errNumber & "; " & errText & vbCrLf
    gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Лист " & sheetName & ": этап " & stage & "; " & errText & vbCrLf
End Sub

Private Sub KC_VisionTrack(ByVal note As Object, ByVal ann As Object, ByVal index As Long)
    Dim rec As Object, pos As Variant
    If Not gKV_Enabled Then Exit Sub
    On Error GoTo Failed
    pos = ann.GetPosition
    If Not IsArray(pos) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2803, , "Не получено положение новой заметки."
    If UBound(pos) < 2 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2804, , "Неполное положение новой заметки."
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "Note", note: rec.Add "Ann", ann: rec.Add "Index", index
    rec.Add "X", CDbl(pos(0)): rec.Add "Y", CDbl(pos(1)): rec.Add "Z", CDbl(pos(2))
    rec.Add "CX", 0#: rec.Add "CY", 0#: rec.Add "R", 0#: rec.Add "GeometryOK", False
    rec.Add "Status", "FALLBACK": rec.Add "Reason", "STANDARD_POSITION"
    rec.Add "TX", 0#: rec.Add "TY", 0#: rec.Add "Moved", False
    gKV_Items.Add rec
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Учёт нового баллона " & index & ": " & Err.Description & vbCrLf
    If gKV_Sheets.Exists(positionsData(index).sheetName) Then
        Set rec = gKV_Sheets(positionsData(index).sheetName)
        rec("OK") = False
    End If
End Sub

Private Function KC_VisionClean(ByVal text As String) As String
    text = Replace(text, vbTab, " ")
    text = Replace(text, vbCr, " "): text = Replace(text, vbLf, " ")
    KC_VisionClean = text
End Function

Private Sub KC_VisionWriteUTF8(ByVal path As String, ByVal text As String)
    Dim stream As Object
    Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stream.Type = 2: stream.Charset = "utf-8": stream.Open
    stream.WriteText text
    stream.SaveToFile path, 2
    stream.Close
End Sub

Private Function KC_VisionNum(ByVal value As Double) As String
    KC_VisionNum = Replace(CStr(value), ",", ".")
End Function

Private Function KC_VisionParseNum(ByVal text As String) As Double
    Dim decimalSeparator As String, localText As String
    decimalSeparator = Mid$(CStr(1.5), 2, 1)
    localText = Replace(text, ".", decimalSeparator)
    If Not IsNumeric(localText) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2824, , "Некорректное число ответа."
    KC_VisionParseNum = CDbl(localText)
End Function

Private Sub KC_VisionFinish(ByRef report As String)
    Dim fso As Object, shell As Object, stream As Object, rec As Object, sheetRec As Object
    Dim ann As Object, info As Variant, line As String, cols As Variant, text As String
    Dim requestPath As String, responsePath As String, donePath As String, command As String
    Dim i As Long, index As Long, movable As Boolean, eligible As Long, code As Long
    Dim start As Date, seen As Object, errText As String, applyFailed As Boolean
    Dim rollbackErrors As Long, cfgStream As Object, configText As String, rollbackOK As Boolean
    Dim measuredX As Double, measuredY As Double, measuredR As Double, measureDetail As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not gKV_Enabled Then GoTo Cleanup
    KC_VisionPrepareGeometry report
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    text = "id" & vbTab & "pdf" & vbTab & "width_m" & vbTab & "height_m" & vbTab & "kind" & vbTab & _
           "source_x" & vbTab & "source_y" & vbTab & "text" & vbTab & "center_x" & vbTab & _
           "center_y" & vbTab & "radius_m" & vbTab & "movable" & vbTab & "page_index" & vbTab & "page_count" & vbCrLf
    For i = 1 To gKV_Items.count
        Set rec = gKV_Items(i): index = CLng(rec("Index"))
        Set sheetRec = gKV_Sheets(positionsData(index).sheetName)
        If CBool(sheetRec("OK")) Then
            movable = (positionsData(index).AnnotationType = swSelDIMENSIONS Or positionsData(index).AnnotationType = swSelWELDSYMBOLS)
            If movable Then eligible = eligible + 1
            text = text & i & vbTab & CStr(sheetRec("PDF")) & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(CDbl(sheetRec("W"))) & vbTab & _
                   KC_VisionNum(CDbl(sheetRec("H"))) & vbTab & positionsData(index).AnnotationType & vbTab & _
                   KC_VisionNum(positionsData(index).posX) & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(positionsData(index).posY) & vbTab & _
                   KC_VisionClean(positionsData(index).AnnotationText) & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(CDbl(rec("CX"))) & vbTab & _
                   KC_VisionNum(CDbl(rec("CY"))) & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(CDbl(rec("R"))) & vbTab & IIf(movable, "1", "0") & vbTab & _
                   CStr(sheetRec("PageIndex")) & vbTab & CStr(sheetRec("PageCount")) & vbCrLf
            seen.Add CStr(i), False
        Else
            If Not CBool(sheetRec("PDFReady")) Then
                rec("Reason") = "PDF_NOT_CREATED"
            ElseIf CStr(rec("Reason")) = "STANDARD_POSITION" Then
                rec("Reason") = "SHEET_NOT_READY"
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If eligible = 0 Then GoTo Cleanup
    requestPath = fso.BuildPath(gKV_Folder, "request.tsv")
    responsePath = fso.BuildPath(gKV_Folder, "response.tsv")
    donePath = fso.BuildPath(gKV_Folder, "done.txt")
    KC_VisionWriteUTF8 requestPath, text
    command = KC_VisionQuote(gKV_Python) & _
              " --request " & KC_VisionQuote(requestPath) & " --response " & KC_VisionQuote(responsePath) & _
              " --done " & KC_VisionQuote(donePath) & " --protocol multi-sheet-v1"
    If fso.FileExists(gKV_Config) Then command = command & " --config " & KC_VisionQuote(gKV_Config)
    Set shell = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    shell.Run command, 0, False
    start = Now
    Do While Not fso.FileExists(donePath)
        DoEvents
        KC_VisionSleep 30
        If DateDiff("s", start, Now) > KC28_N("Vision.TimeoutSeconds") Then
            gKV_TimedOut = True
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2810, , "Истекло время ожидания обработчика. Все новые баллоны оставлены на стандартных местах."
        End If
    Loop
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(donePath, 1, False)
    code = CLng(stream.ReadLine)
    If code <> 0 Then
        errText = stream.ReadAll
        stream.Close
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2811, , "Python вернул код " & code & ": " & errText
    End If
    stream.Close
    If Not fso.FileExists(responsePath) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2812, , "Нет ответа обработчика."
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(responsePath, 1, False)
    If stream.AtEndOfStream Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2813, , "Пустой ответ обработчика."
    line = stream.ReadLine
    If line <> "id" & vbTab & "status" & vbTab & "x" & vbTab & "y" & vbTab & "reason" Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2814, , "Неизвестный формат ответа."
    End If
    Do While Not stream.AtEndOfStream
        line = stream.ReadLine
        If Len(line) > 0 Then
            cols = Split(line, vbTab)
            If UBound(cols) <> 4 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2815, , "Повреждена строка ответа."
            If Not seen.Exists(CStr(cols(0))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2816, , "Неизвестный номер ответа."
            If CBool(seen(CStr(cols(0)))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2817, , "Дубликат номера ответа."
            seen(CStr(cols(0))) = True
            i = CLng(cols(0)): Set rec = gKV_Items(i): index = CLng(rec("Index"))
            rec("Status") = CStr(cols(1)): rec("Reason") = CStr(cols(4))
            If cols(1) = "AUTO" Then
                If positionsData(index).AnnotationType <> swSelDIMENSIONS And positionsData(index).AnnotationType <> swSelWELDSYMBOLS Then
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2818, , "Попытка переместить запрещённый тип аннотации."
                End If
                Set sheetRec = gKV_Sheets(positionsData(index).sheetName)
                rec("TX") = KC_VisionParseNum(CStr(cols(2))): rec("TY") = KC_VisionParseNum(CStr(cols(3)))
                If CDbl(rec("TX")) < CDbl(rec("R")) Or CDbl(rec("TX")) > CDbl(sheetRec("W")) - CDbl(rec("R")) Or _
                   CDbl(rec("TY")) < CDbl(rec("R")) Or CDbl(rec("TY")) > CDbl(sheetRec("H")) - CDbl(rec("R")) Then
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2819, , "Координаты ответа вне листа."
                End If
            ElseIf cols(1) <> "FALLBACK" Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2820, , "Неизвестный статус ответа."
            End If
        End If
    Loop
    stream.Close
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In seen.Keys
        If Not CBool(seen(key)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2821, , "Неполный ответ обработчика."
    Next key
    ' Only this run's newly inserted notes are stored here. Existing notes cannot enter this loop.
    For i = 1 To gKV_Items.count
        Set rec = gKV_Items(i)
        If rec("Status") = "AUTO" Then
            index = CLng(rec("Index"))
            swDraw.ActivateSheet positionsData(index).sheetName
            Set ann = rec("Ann")
            rec("Moved") = True
            If Not ann.SetPosition2(CDbl(rec("X")) + CDbl(rec("TX")) - CDbl(rec("CX")), _
                                    CDbl(rec("Y")) + CDbl(rec("TY")) - CDbl(rec("CY")), CDbl(rec("Z"))) Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2822, , "SolidWorks отклонил новое положение баллона."
            End If
            Dim newNote As Object
            Set newNote = rec("Note")
            swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
            If Not KC_VisionReadGeometry(newNote, ann, measuredX, measuredY, measuredR, measureDetail) Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2823, , "Не подтверждена геометрия после перемещения: " & measureDetail
            End If
            If Abs(measuredX - CDbl(rec("TX"))) > 0.0005 Or Abs(measuredY - CDbl(rec("TY"))) > 0.0005 Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2823, , "Не подтверждено положение центра на листе: " & measureDetail
            End If
            If Abs(measuredR - CDbl(rec("R"))) > 0.00025 Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2823, , "Изменился размер круга после перемещения: " & measureDetail
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    KC_VisionAdjustSources report
    GoTo Cleanup
Failed:
    errText = Err.Description
    gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & errText & vbCrLf
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
    If Not gKV_Items Is Nothing Then
        For i = 1 To gKV_Items.count
            Set rec = gKV_Items(i): index = CLng(rec("Index"))
            If CBool(rec("Moved")) Then
                swDraw.ActivateSheet positionsData(index).sheetName
                Set ann = rec("Ann")
                rollbackOK = False
                Err.Clear
                rollbackOK = ann.SetPosition2(CDbl(rec("X")), CDbl(rec("Y")), CDbl(rec("Z")))
                If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackOK = False
                If rollbackOK Then
                    Err.Clear
                    Set newNote = rec("Note")
                    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
                    If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackOK = False
                    If rollbackOK Then
                        rollbackOK = KC_VisionReadGeometry(newNote, ann, measuredX, measuredY, measuredR, measureDetail)
                        If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackOK = False
                        If Abs(measuredX - CDbl(rec("CX"))) > 0.0005 Or Abs(measuredY - CDbl(rec("CY"))) > 0.0005 Then
                            rollbackOK = False
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                If Not rollbackOK Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
            End If
            rec("Status") = "FALLBACK": rec("Reason") = "BATCH_ERROR"
        Next i
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    If rollbackErrors > 0 Then gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Не удалось вернуть новых баллонов: " & rollbackErrors & ". Проверьте вручную." & vbCrLf
Cleanup:
    gKV_AutoCount = 0: gKV_DefaultCount = 0
    If Not gKV_Items Is Nothing Then
        For i = 1 To gKV_Items.count
            Set rec = gKV_Items(i): index = CLng(rec("Index"))
            If positionsData(index).AnnotationType = swSelDIMENSIONS Or positionsData(index).AnnotationType = swSelWELDSYMBOLS Then
                If rec("Status") = "AUTO" Then gKV_AutoCount = gKV_AutoCount + 1 Else gKV_DefaultCount = gKV_DefaultCount + 1
            End If
            report = report & "РАЗМЕЩЕНИЕ | " & positionsData(index).sheetName & " | " & _
                     positionsData(index).AnnotationText & " | " & CStr(rec("Status")) & " | " & CStr(rec("Reason")) & vbCrLf
        Next i
    End If
    report = report & "Экспорт PDF (до создания новых баллонов):" & vbCrLf & gKV_Diagnostics & vbCrLf
    report = report & "Диагностика размещения: " & gKV_Notes & vbCrLf
    On Error Resume Next
    KC_VisionRemovePDFs report
    If Len(gKV_Folder) > 0 Then
        ' v16.5.15: диагностика идёт текстом в журнал папки карты; файлы остаются только при зависании обработчика.
        If Not gKV_TimedOut Then
            CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject").DeleteFolder gKV_Folder, True
        Else
            report = report & "Диагностические файлы: " & gKV_Folder & vbCrLf
        End If
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function KC_VisionArrayText(ByVal values As Variant) As String
    Dim i As Long, text As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not IsArray(values) Then
        KC_VisionArrayText = "<not array>"
        Exit Function
    End If
    For i = LBound(values) To UBound(values)
        If i > LBound(values) Then text = text & "; "
        text = text & CStr(values(i))
    Next i
    KC_VisionArrayText = "(" & text & ")"
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC_VisionArrayText = "<unreadable: " & Err.Description & ">"
End Function

Private Function KC_VisionReadGeometry(ByVal note As Object, ByVal ann As Object, _
                                      ByRef cx As Double, ByRef cy As Double, ByRef radius As Double, _
                                      ByRef detail As String) As Boolean
    Dim extent As Variant, pos As Variant, raw As Variant
    Dim width As Double, height As Double, diameter As Double, rawRadius As Double
    Dim x0 As Double, x1 As Double, y0 As Double, y1 As Double, delta As Double
    On Error GoTo Failed
    extent = note.GetExtent
    pos = ann.GetPosition
    raw = note.GetBalloonInfo
    detail = "GetExtent=" & KC_VisionArrayText(extent) & "; GetPosition=" & KC_VisionArrayText(pos) & _
             "; GetBalloonInfo=" & KC_VisionArrayText(raw)
    If Not IsArray(extent) Or Not IsArray(pos) Or Not IsArray(raw) Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2831, , "Не получены массивы геометрии."
    End If
    If LBound(extent) <> 0 Or UBound(extent) < 5 Or UBound(pos) < 2 Or UBound(raw) < 6 Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2832, , "Неполные массивы геометрии."
    End If
    If note.GetBalloonStyle <> 1 Or ann.GetLeaderStyle <> 0 Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2833, , "Ожидается круглый баллон без выноски."
    End If
    x0 = CDbl(extent(0)): x1 = CDbl(extent(3))
    y0 = CDbl(extent(1)): y1 = CDbl(extent(4))
    width = Abs(x1 - x0): height = Abs(y1 - y0)
    diameter = width
    If height > diameter Then diameter = height
    If diameter < 0.001 Or diameter > 0.06 Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2834, , "Недопустимый размер границ баллона."
    End If
    If Abs(width - height) > diameter * 0.1 Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2835, , "Границы заметки не подтверждают окружность."
    End If
    rawRadius = Abs(CDbl(raw(6)))
    If rawRadius < 0.0005 Or rawRadius > 0.03 Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2836, , "Не подтверждён радиус круглой рамки."
    End If
    ' Never confuse a nearly square TEXT-only box with the enclosing circle.
    If diameter < 2# * rawRadius * 0.7 Or diameter > 2# * rawRadius * 1.45 Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2837, , "Размер GetExtent не согласуется с радиусом рамки."
    End If
    cx = (x0 + x1) / 2#: cy = (y0 + y1) / 2#: radius = diameter / 2#
    delta = Sqr((cx - CDbl(pos(0))) ^ 2 + (cy - CDbl(pos(1))) ^ 2)
    If delta > 2# * radius + 0.003 Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2838, , "Границы заметки удалены от её положения на листе."
    End If
    detail = "SHEET_EXTENT_CENTER=(" & CStr(cx) & "; " & CStr(cy) & "); R=" & CStr(radius) & "; " & detail
    KC_VisionReadGeometry = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = Err.Description & " | " & detail
    KC_VisionReadGeometry = False
End Function

Private Sub KC_VisionPrepareGeometry(ByRef report As String)
    Dim sheetKey As Variant, sheetRec As Object, rec As Object, ann As Object, note As Object
    Dim i As Long, index As Long, cx As Double, cy As Double, radius As Double, detail As String
    If Not gKV_Enabled Then Exit Sub
    For Each sheetKey In gKV_Sheets.Keys
        Set sheetRec = gKV_Sheets(CStr(sheetKey))
        If CBool(sheetRec("OK")) Then
            If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(CStr(sheetKey)) Then
                sheetRec("OK") = False
                gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Не активирован лист для геометрии: " & CStr(sheetKey) & vbCrLf
            Else
                ' Read only after all new notes have been inserted and this sheet redrawn.
                swModel.ClearSelection2 True
                swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
                For i = 1 To gKV_Items.count
                    Set rec = gKV_Items(i): index = CLng(rec("Index"))
                    If positionsData(index).sheetName = CStr(sheetKey) Then
                        Set ann = rec("Ann"): Set note = rec("Note")
                        If KC_VisionReadGeometry(note, ann, cx, cy, radius, detail) Then
                            rec("CX") = cx: rec("CY") = cy: rec("R") = radius
                            rec("TX") = cx: rec("TY") = cy
                            rec("GeometryOK") = True
                            report = report & "ГЕОМЕТРИЯ OK | " & index & " | " & CStr(sheetKey) & " | " & detail & vbCrLf
                        Else
                            ' An unmeasured new circle is an unknown obstacle: skip this sheet.
                            sheetRec("OK") = False
                            rec("Reason") = "GEOMETRY_UNCONFIRMED"
                            gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Геометрия нового баллона " & index & ": " & detail & vbCrLf
                        End If
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
        End If
    Next sheetKey
End Sub

Private Function KC_VisionExportAllPDF() As Boolean
    Dim fso As Object, ok As Boolean, stage As String, fileBytes As Double
    Dim errors As Long, warnings As Long, i As Long, errNumber As Long, errText As String
    If gKV_AllPDFState <> 0 Then
        KC_VisionExportAllPDF = (gKV_AllPDFState = 1)
        Exit Function
    End If
    ' Never retry after new notes may have been created: the snapshot must predate them.
    gKV_AllPDFState = -1
    On Error GoTo Failed
    stage = "список листов общего PDF"
    gKV_AllPDFNames = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    If Not IsArray(gKV_AllPDFNames) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2850, , "Не получен порядок листов."
    If UBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) < LBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2851, , "Пустой список листов."
    End If
    KC54_PdfSubset
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    gKV_AllPDFPath = fso.BuildPath(gKV_Folder, "drawing_all.pdf")
    gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF BEGIN | ALL_SHEETS | path=" & gKV_AllPDFPath & vbCrLf
    For i = LBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) To UBound(gKV_AllPDFNames)
        gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF PAGE_ORDER | page=" & (i - LBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) + 1) & _
                          "; sheet=" & CStr(gKV_AllPDFNames(i)) & vbCrLf
    Next i
    stage = "обновление графики до создания новых баллонов"
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    stage = "SaveAs: листы для расстановки, ViewPdfAfterSaving=False"
    gKV_PdfStarted = Timer
    gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF SAVE_BEGIN | ALL_SHEETS | ViewPdfAfterSaving=False" & vbCrLf
    errors = 0: warnings = 0
    ' Explicit export data prevents the PDF viewer from being launched.
    ok = KC_VisionSavePDFQuiet(gKV_AllPDFPath)
    If Not ok And IsArray(gKV_ExportNames) Then
        ' Новые баллоны ещё не создавались: безопасно повторить прежним способом (все листы).
        gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF SUBSET_FAILED | повтор на всех листах" & vbCrLf
        KC46_Trace "PDF: экспорт выбранных листов не подтверждён, выполнен экспорт всех листов"
        gKV_ExportNames = Empty
        gKV_AllPDFNames = swDraw.GetSheetNames
        ok = KC_VisionSavePDFQuiet(gKV_AllPDFPath)
    End If
    gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF SAVE_RESULT | result=" & CStr(ok) & "; errors=" & errors & _
                      "; warnings=" & warnings & "; exists=" & CStr(fso.FileExists(gKV_AllPDFPath)) & vbCrLf
    If Not ok Or errors <> 0 Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2852, , "Не сохранён общий PDF: result=" & CStr(ok) & "; errors=" & errors & "; warnings=" & warnings
    End If
    stage = "проверка общего PDF на диске"
    If Not fso.FileExists(gKV_AllPDFPath) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2853, , "Общий PDF не создан."
    fileBytes = CDbl(fso.GetFile(gKV_AllPDFPath).size)
    If fileBytes <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2854, , "Общий PDF пуст."
    gKV_AllPDFState = 1
    KC46_Trace "PDF для расстановки сохранён: " & KCPerf_Seconds(gKV_PdfStarted) & "; листов=" & (UBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) - LBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) + 1)
    gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF CREATED | ALL_SHEETS | bytes=" & CStr(fileBytes) & _
                      "; expected_pages=" & (UBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) - LBound(gKV_AllPDFNames) + 1) & "; path=" & gKV_AllPDFPath & vbCrLf
    KC_VisionExportAllPDF = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errNumber = Err.number: errText = Err.Description
    gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF FAILED | ALL_SHEETS | stage=" & stage & "; Err.Number=" & errNumber & _
                      "; " & errText & "; path=" & gKV_AllPDFPath & vbCrLf
    gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Общий PDF: этап " & stage & "; " & errText & vbCrLf
    KC_VisionExportAllPDF = False
End Function

' 2.8.7: no PDF viewer; no source motion based on an edited raster.
' Every source trial is performed in SW, then checked using a NEW PDF.
Private Function KC_VisionSavePDFQuiet(ByVal path As String) As Boolean
    Dim data As Object, errors As Long, warnings As Long, allSheets As Variant, fso As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set data = swApp.GetExportFileData(1) ' swExportPdfData
    If data Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2870, , "Не получены параметры тихого экспорта PDF."
    data.ViewPdfAfterSaving = False
    data.ExportAs3D = False
    ' New requirement: suppress the viewer explicitly. Do not export with Nothing.
    ' A fresh export object normally has all sheets selected; no named arrays.
    If IsArray(gKV_ExportNames) Then
        ' v16.5.15: только листы с новыми баллонами (swExportData_ExportSpecifiedSheets = 3).
        ' Тот же набор и порядок для первого и проверочных PDF, поэтому номера страниц согласованы.
        If Not data.SetSheets(3, gKV_ExportNames) Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2871, , "Не установлен экспорт выбранных листов."
        End If
        If CLng(data.GetWhichSheets) <> 3 Or CBool(data.ViewPdfAfterSaving) Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2872, , "Параметры экспорта выбранных листов не подтверждены."
        End If
    Else
        If CLng(data.GetWhichSheets) <> 1 Then
            allSheets = Empty
            If Not data.SetSheets(1, allSheets) Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2871, , "Не установлен экспорт всех листов без просмотра."
            End If
        End If
        If CLng(data.GetWhichSheets) <> 1 Or CBool(data.ViewPdfAfterSaving) Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2872, , "Параметры тихого экспорта не подтверждены."
        End If
    End If
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    If Not swModel.Extension.SaveAs(path, 0, 1, data, errors, warnings) Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2873, , "SaveAs PDF: errors=" & errors & "; warnings=" & warnings
    End If
    If errors <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2874, , "PDF errors=" & errors
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Not fso.FileExists(path) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2875, , "Не создан PDF."
    If CDbl(fso.GetFile(path).size) <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2876, , "Пустой PDF."
    KC_VisionSavePDFQuiet = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Тихий экспорт PDF: " & Err.Description & vbCrLf
End Function

Private Function KC_ShiftSnapshot(ByVal path As String) As Boolean
    Dim hidden As Collection, rec As Object, entry As Object, a As Object
    Dim i As Long, idx As Long, originalSheet As String, result As Boolean, restoreErrors As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set hidden = New Collection
    originalSheet = CStr(swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName)
    ' Hide ONLY circles created in THIS run. Existing circles stay in the PDF.
    For i = 1 To gKV_Items.count
        Set rec = gKV_Items(i): idx = CLng(rec("Index"))
        If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(positionsData(idx).sheetName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2877, , "Не активирован лист скрытия."
        Set a = rec("Ann")
        Set entry = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
        entry.Add "Ann", a: entry.Add "State", CLng(a.Visible): entry.Add "Sheet", positionsData(idx).sheetName
        hidden.Add entry
        a.Visible = 3 ' swAnnotationHidden, NOT 0 (unknown)
        If CLng(a.Visible) <> 3 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2878, , "Новый круг не скрыт для проверки."
    Next i
    result = KC_VisionSavePDFQuiet(path)
    GoTo Restore
Failed:
    gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Снимок после сдвига: " & Err.Description & vbCrLf
    result = False
Restore:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not hidden Is Nothing Then
        For Each entry In hidden
            Err.Clear
            swDraw.ActivateSheet CStr(entry("Sheet"))
            Set a = entry("Ann")
            a.Visible = CLng(entry("State"))
            If Err.number <> 0 Then
                restoreErrors = restoreErrors + 1
            ElseIf CLng(a.Visible) <> CLng(entry("State")) Then
                restoreErrors = restoreErrors + 1
            End If
        Next entry
    End If
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then swDraw.ActivateSheet originalSheet
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    On Error GoTo 0
    If restoreErrors > 0 Then
        gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "ВНИМАНИЕ: не подтверждён возврат видимости новых кругов: " & restoreErrors & vbCrLf
        result = False
    End If
    KC_ShiftSnapshot = result
End Function

Private Function KC_ShiftRun(ByVal requestPath As String, ByVal responsePath As String, _
                             ByVal donePath As String, ByVal arguments As String) As Boolean
    Dim fso As Object, shell As Object, stream As Object, command As String, started As Date, code As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If fso.FileExists(donePath) Then fso.DeleteFile donePath, True
    If fso.FileExists(responsePath) Then fso.DeleteFile responsePath, True
    command = KC_VisionQuote(gKV_Python) & _
              " --request " & KC_VisionQuote(requestPath) & " --response " & KC_VisionQuote(responsePath) & _
              " --done " & KC_VisionQuote(donePath) & " --protocol multi-sheet-v1 " & arguments
    If fso.FileExists(gKV_Config) Then command = command & " --config " & KC_VisionQuote(gKV_Config)
    Set shell = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    shell.Run command, 0, False
    started = Now
    Do While Not fso.FileExists(donePath)
        DoEvents
        KC_VisionSleep 30
        If DateDiff("s", started, Now) > KC28_N("Vision.TimeoutSeconds") Then
            gKV_TimedOut = True
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2879, , "Тайм-аут проверки сдвига."
        End If
    Loop
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(donePath, 1, False)
    code = CLng(stream.ReadLine)
    If code <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2880, , "Ошибка проверки Python: " & stream.ReadAll
    stream.Close
    If Not fso.FileExists(responsePath) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2881, , "Нет ответа проверки."
    KC_ShiftRun = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Проверка сдвига: " & Err.Description & vbCrLf
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
End Function

Private Sub KC_ShiftWriteRequest(ByVal path As String, ByVal pdf As String, ByVal targetID As Long)
    Dim text As String, i As Long, idx As Long, targetSheet As String, rec As Object, sr As Object
    Dim a As Object, n As Object, pos As Variant, cx As Double, cy As Double, radius As Double
    Dim detail As String, movable As Boolean, sx As Double, sy As Double
    Dim sourceGuard As Collection, targetBalloon As Object
    If targetID > 0 Then
        Set rec = gKV_Items(targetID): idx = CLng(rec("Index"))
        targetSheet = positionsData(idx).sheetName
    End If
    text = "id" & vbTab & "pdf" & vbTab & "width_m" & vbTab & "height_m" & vbTab & "kind" & vbTab & _
           "source_x" & vbTab & "source_y" & vbTab & "text" & vbTab & "center_x" & vbTab & _
           "center_y" & vbTab & "radius_m" & vbTab & "movable" & vbTab & "page_index" & vbTab & "page_count" & vbCrLf
    For i = 1 To gKV_Items.count
        Set rec = gKV_Items(i): idx = CLng(rec("Index"))
        Set sr = gKV_Sheets(positionsData(idx).sheetName)
        If CBool(sr("OK")) And (targetID = 0 Or positionsData(idx).sheetName = targetSheet) Then
            If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(positionsData(idx).sheetName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2882, , "Не активирован лист запроса."
            swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
            Set a = rec("Ann"): Set n = rec("Note")
            If Not KC_VisionReadGeometry(n, a, cx, cy, radius, detail) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2883, , detail
            sx = positionsData(idx).posX: sy = positionsData(idx).posY
            movable = False
            If positionsData(idx).AnnotationType = swSelDIMENSIONS Or positionsData(idx).AnnotationType = swSelWELDSYMBOLS Then
                If targetID = 0 Then
                    movable = (CStr(rec("Status")) = "FALLBACK" And CStr(rec("Reason")) = "NO_FREE_CANDIDATE")
                Else
                    movable = (i = targetID)
                End If
                If movable Then
                    Set a = positionsData(idx).SourceAnnotation
                    If a Is Nothing Then
                        movable = False
                    Else
                        pos = a.GetPosition
                        sx = CDbl(pos(0)): sy = CDbl(pos(1))
                        If targetID = 0 Then
                            Set targetBalloon = rec("Ann")
                            Set sourceGuard = KC_ShiftGuard(a, targetBalloon, detail)
                            If sourceGuard Is Nothing Then
                                movable = False
                                gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "SOURCE_SKIP | id=" & i & "; " & detail & vbCrLf
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            text = text & i & vbTab & pdf & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(CDbl(sr("W"))) & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(CDbl(sr("H"))) & vbTab & _
                   positionsData(idx).AnnotationType & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(sx) & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(sy) & vbTab & _
                   KC_VisionClean(positionsData(idx).AnnotationText) & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(cx) & vbTab & KC_VisionNum(cy) & vbTab & _
                   KC_VisionNum(radius) & vbTab & IIf(movable, "1", "0") & vbTab & CStr(sr("PageIndex")) & vbTab & CStr(sr("PageCount")) & vbCrLf
        End If
    Next i
    KC_VisionWriteUTF8 path, text
End Sub

' Snapshot all readable position-bearing annotations on this sheet, not just
' the selected types. Never call SetPosition2 on ANY of these guard objects.
Private Function KC_ShiftGuard(ByVal source As Object, ByVal balloon As Object, ByRef detail As String) As Collection
    Dim result As Collection, views As Variant, annotations As Variant, v As Object, a As Object
    Dim entry As Object, pos As Variant, sourcePos As Variant, i As Long, j As Long, typ As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set result = New Collection
    sourcePos = source.GetPosition
    views = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetViews
    If Not IsArray(views) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2884, , "Не получены виды для защиты допусков."
    For i = LBound(views) To UBound(views)
        Set v = views(i): annotations = v.GetAnnotations
        If IsArray(annotations) Then
            For j = LBound(annotations) To UBound(annotations)
                Set a = annotations(j)
                If Not a Is Nothing Then
                    If Not (a Is source) And Not (a Is balloon) Then
                        typ = CLng(a.GetType)
                        If typ = 2 Or typ = 4 Or typ = 5 Or typ = 6 Or typ = 8 Then
                            pos = a.GetPosition
                            If Not IsArray(pos) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2885, , "Не прочитана защищаемая аннотация."
                            ' A loose/attached nearby GDT is a reason to SKIP this
                            ' source altogether. No detaching or reattaching it.
                            If typ = 5 Then
                                If Sqr((CDbl(pos(0)) - CDbl(sourcePos(0))) ^ 2 + (CDbl(pos(1)) - CDbl(sourcePos(1))) ^ 2) < 0.02 Then
                                    detail = "SOURCE_NEAR_GDT_20MM": Exit Function
                                End If
                            End If
                            Set entry = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
                            entry.Add "Ann", a: entry.Add "X", CDbl(pos(0)): entry.Add "Y", CDbl(pos(1)): entry.Add "Z", CDbl(pos(2))
                            entry.Add "Type", typ
                            result.Add entry
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            Next j
        End If
    Next i
    Set KC_ShiftGuard = result
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = "SOURCE_GUARD_UNAVAILABLE: " & Err.Description
End Function

Private Function KC_ShiftGuardOK(ByVal guard As Collection) As Boolean
    Dim entry As Object, a As Object, pos As Variant
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If guard Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    For Each entry In guard
        Set a = entry("Ann"): pos = a.GetPosition
        If Not IsArray(pos) Then Exit Function
        If Abs(CDbl(pos(0)) - CDbl(entry("X"))) > 0.00002 Or _
           Abs(CDbl(pos(1)) - CDbl(entry("Y"))) > 0.00002 Or _
           Abs(CDbl(pos(2)) - CDbl(entry("Z"))) > 0.00002 Then Exit Function
    Next entry
    KC_ShiftGuardOK = True
Failed:
End Function

Private Function KC_ShiftResponse(ByVal path As String, ByVal targetID As Long, _
                                  ByRef tx As Double, ByRef ty As Double, ByRef reason As String) As Boolean
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, cols As Variant, line As String, seen As Object
    Dim i As Long, idx As Long, rec As Object, sr As Object, targetSheet As String, success As Boolean, expected As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set rec = gKV_Items(targetID): idx = CLng(rec("Index")): targetSheet = positionsData(idx).sheetName
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For i = 1 To gKV_Items.count
        Set rec = gKV_Items(i): idx = CLng(rec("Index"))
        If positionsData(idx).sheetName = targetSheet Then
            Set sr = gKV_Sheets(targetSheet)
            If CBool(sr("OK")) Then seen.Add CStr(i), False: expected = expected + 1
        End If
    Next i
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject"): Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(path, 1, False)
    If stream.ReadLine <> "id" & vbTab & "status" & vbTab & "x" & vbTab & "y" & vbTab & "reason" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2886, , "Формат проверки."
    Do While Not stream.AtEndOfStream
        line = stream.ReadLine
        If Len(line) > 0 Then
            cols = Split(line, vbTab)
            If UBound(cols) <> 4 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2887, , "Повреждена строка проверки."
            If Not seen.Exists(CStr(cols(0))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2888, , "Неизвестный объект проверки."
            If CBool(seen(CStr(cols(0)))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2889, , "Повтор объекта проверки."
            seen(CStr(cols(0))) = True
            If CLng(cols(0)) = targetID Then
                reason = CStr(cols(4))
                If CStr(cols(1)) = "AUTO" Then
                    tx = KC_VisionParseNum(CStr(cols(2))): ty = KC_VisionParseNum(CStr(cols(3)))
                    Set rec = gKV_Items(targetID)
                    Set sr = gKV_Sheets(targetSheet)
                    If tx < CDbl(rec("R")) Or tx > CDbl(sr("W")) - CDbl(rec("R")) Or _
                       ty < CDbl(rec("R")) Or ty > CDbl(sr("H")) - CDbl(rec("R")) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2890, , "Центр вне листа."
                    If InStr(1, reason, "SOURCE_SHIFT_VALIDATED_", vbBinaryCompare) <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2891, , "Сдвиг не подтверждён."
                    success = True
                ElseIf CStr(cols(1)) <> "FALLBACK" Then
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2892, , "Статус проверки."
                End If
            ElseIf CStr(cols(1)) <> "FALLBACK" Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2893, , "Попытка двигать другой круг."
            End If
        End If
    Loop
    stream.Close
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In seen.Keys
        If Not CBool(seen(key)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2894, , "Неполная проверка."
    Next key
    KC_ShiftResponse = success
    Exit Function
Failed:
    reason = "SHIFT_RESPONSE_ERROR: " & Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
End Function

' Return 1=accepted, 0=rolled back / skipped, -1=rollback not confirmed.
Private Function KC_ShiftTry(ByVal id As Long, ByVal dx As Double, ByVal dy As Double, _
                             ByVal serial As Long, ByRef currentPDF As String, ByRef report As String) As Long
    Dim rec As Object, source As Object, a As Object, n As Object, guard As Collection
    Dim idx As Long, pos As Variant, balloonPos As Variant, nowPos As Variant, movedSource As Boolean, movedBalloon As Boolean
    Dim path As String, prefix As String, requestPath As String, responsePath As String, donePath As String
    Dim detail As String, reason As String, tx As Double, ty As Double, cx As Double, cy As Double, radius As Double
    Dim sourceOK As Boolean, balloonOK As Boolean, oldKey As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set rec = gKV_Items(id): idx = CLng(rec("Index"))
    If CStr(rec("Status")) <> "FALLBACK" Or CStr(rec("Reason")) <> "NO_FREE_CANDIDATE" Then Exit Function
    If positionsData(idx).AnnotationType <> swSelDIMENSIONS And positionsData(idx).AnnotationType <> swSelWELDSYMBOLS Then Exit Function
    If Sqr(dx * dx + dy * dy) > 0.004001 Or Sqr(dx * dx + dy * dy) < 0.0001 Then Exit Function
    If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(positionsData(idx).sheetName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2895, , "Не активирован лист сдвига."
    Set source = positionsData(idx).SourceAnnotation: If source Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Set a = rec("Ann"): Set n = rec("Note")
    If Not KC_VisionReadGeometry(n, a, cx, cy, radius, detail) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2896, , detail
    pos = source.GetPosition: balloonPos = a.GetPosition
    oldKey = GetBalloonStableKey(n)
    If Len(oldKey) = 0 Then reason = "SOURCE_KEY_MISSING": GoTo Rejected
    Set guard = KC_ShiftGuard(source, a, detail)
    If guard Is Nothing Then reason = detail: GoTo Rejected
    report = report & "SOURCE_SHIFT TRIAL | id=" & id & "; number=" & CStr(n.GetText) & _
             "; original_x=" & KC_VisionNum(CDbl(pos(0))) & "; original_y=" & KC_VisionNum(CDbl(pos(1))) & _
             "; dx=" & KC_VisionNum(dx) & "; dy=" & KC_VisionNum(dy) & vbCrLf
    movedSource = True ' SetPosition2 can partly act even if it returns False.
    If Not source.SetPosition2(CDbl(pos(0)) + dx, CDbl(pos(1)) + dy, CDbl(pos(2))) Then
        reason = "SOURCE_MOVE_REJECTED": GoTo Rejected
    End If
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    nowPos = source.GetPosition
    If Abs(CDbl(nowPos(0)) - CDbl(pos(0)) - dx) > 0.0002 Or Abs(CDbl(nowPos(1)) - CDbl(pos(1)) - dy) > 0.0002 Then
        reason = "SOURCE_POSITION_NOT_CONFIRMED": GoTo Rejected
    End If
    If Not KC_ShiftGuardOK(guard) Then reason = "SOURCE_MOVED_PROTECTED_ANNOTATION": GoTo Rejected
    prefix = gKV_Folder & "\trial_" & Format$(serial, "000")
    path = prefix & ".pdf": requestPath = prefix & "_request.tsv"
    responsePath = prefix & "_response.tsv": donePath = prefix & "_done.txt"
    If Not KC_ShiftSnapshot(path) Then reason = "SOURCE_NEW_PDF_UNAVAILABLE": GoTo Rejected
    KC_ShiftWriteRequest requestPath, path, id
    If Not KC_ShiftRun(requestPath, responsePath, donePath, "--action validate-shift --before " & KC_VisionQuote(currentPDF) & _
                      " --target " & id & " --dx " & KC_VisionNum(dx) & " --dy " & KC_VisionNum(dy)) Then
        reason = "SOURCE_VALIDATION_FAILED": GoTo Rejected
    End If
    If Not KC_ShiftResponse(responsePath, id, tx, ty, reason) Then GoTo Rejected
    swDraw.ActivateSheet positionsData(idx).sheetName
    movedBalloon = True
    If Not a.SetPosition2(CDbl(balloonPos(0)) + tx - cx, CDbl(balloonPos(1)) + ty - cy, CDbl(balloonPos(2))) Then
        reason = "SHIFT_BALLOON_MOVE_REJECTED": GoTo Rejected
    End If
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    If Not KC_VisionReadGeometry(n, a, cx, cy, radius, detail) Then reason = "SHIFT_BALLOON_GEOMETRY": GoTo Rejected
    If Abs(cx - tx) > 0.0005 Or Abs(cy - ty) > 0.0005 Or Abs(radius - CDbl(rec("R"))) > 0.00025 Then
        reason = "SHIFT_BALLOON_POSITION": GoTo Rejected
    End If
    If Not KC_ShiftGuardOK(guard) Then reason = "SHIFT_PROTECTED_ANNOTATION_CHANGED": GoTo Rejected
    nowPos = source.GetPosition
    If Abs(CDbl(nowPos(0)) - CDbl(pos(0)) - dx) > 0.0002 Or Abs(CDbl(nowPos(1)) - CDbl(pos(1)) - dy) > 0.0002 Then
        reason = "SHIFT_FINAL_SOURCE_POSITION": GoTo Rejected
    End If
    ' Existing stable-key scheme includes source coordinates. Update this NEW
    ' circle's key only after acceptance, otherwise the next run duplicates it.
    positionsData(idx).posX = CDbl(nowPos(0)): positionsData(idx).posY = CDbl(nowPos(1))
    KC_RefreshFallbackKey idx
    SetBalloonStableKey n, BuildAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(idx))
    If GetBalloonStableKey(n) <> BuildAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(idx)) Then
        reason = "SHIFT_KEY_NOT_SAVED": GoTo Rejected
    End If
    rec("Status") = "AUTO": rec("Reason") = reason: rec("TX") = tx: rec("TY") = ty: rec("Moved") = True
    currentPDF = path
    gKV_SourceMovedCount = gKV_SourceMovedCount + 1
    report = report & "SOURCE_SHIFT ACCEPTED | id=" & id & "; number=" & CStr(n.GetText) & _
             "; dx=" & KC_VisionNum(dx) & "; dy=" & KC_VisionNum(dy) & "; " & reason & vbCrLf
    KC_ShiftTry = 1
    Exit Function
Failed:
    reason = "SOURCE_TRIAL_ERROR: " & Err.Description
Rejected:
    sourceOK = True: balloonOK = True
    On Error Resume Next
    If movedSource Then
        Err.Clear
        swDraw.ActivateSheet positionsData(idx).sheetName
        sourceOK = source.SetPosition2(CDbl(pos(0)), CDbl(pos(1)), CDbl(pos(2)))
        If Err.number <> 0 Then sourceOK = False
        swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
        Err.Clear
        nowPos = source.GetPosition
        If Err.number <> 0 Then
            sourceOK = False
        ElseIf Abs(CDbl(nowPos(0)) - CDbl(pos(0))) > 0.00002 Or Abs(CDbl(nowPos(1)) - CDbl(pos(1))) > 0.00002 Then
            sourceOK = False
        End If
        positionsData(idx).posX = CDbl(pos(0)): positionsData(idx).posY = CDbl(pos(1))
        KC_RefreshFallbackKey idx
        If Not KC_ShiftGuardOK(guard) Then sourceOK = False
    End If
    If movedBalloon Then
        Err.Clear
        balloonOK = a.SetPosition2(CDbl(balloonPos(0)), CDbl(balloonPos(1)), CDbl(balloonPos(2)))
        If Err.number <> 0 Then balloonOK = False
        swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
        If Not KC_VisionReadGeometry(n, a, cx, cy, radius, detail) Then balloonOK = False
        If Abs(cx - CDbl(rec("CX"))) > 0.0005 Or Abs(cy - CDbl(rec("CY"))) > 0.0005 Then balloonOK = False
    End If
    If Len(oldKey) > 0 And movedSource Then
        Err.Clear
        SetBalloonStableKey n, oldKey
        If Err.number <> 0 Then balloonOK = False
        If GetBalloonStableKey(n) <> oldKey Then balloonOK = False
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    report = report & "SOURCE_SHIFT REJECTED | id=" & id & "; " & reason & vbCrLf
    If Not sourceOK Or Not balloonOK Then
        gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "ВНИМАНИЕ: откат сдвига НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЁН, позиция " & positionsData(idx).number & _
                   ". Дальнейшие пробы остановлены. Проверьте чертёж, размеры и допуски вручную." & vbCrLf
        KC_ShiftTry = -1
    End If
End Function

Private Sub KC_VisionAdjustSources(ByRef report As String)
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, plans As Collection, plan As Variant, cols As Variant
    Dim requestPath As String, responsePath As String, donePath As String, line As String, currentPDF As String
    Dim id As Long, serial As Long, result As Long, dx As Double, dy As Double
    On Error GoTo Failed
    requestPath = gKV_Folder & "\shift_request.tsv": responsePath = gKV_Folder & "\shift_proposals.tsv": donePath = gKV_Folder & "\shift_done.txt"
    currentPDF = gKV_AllPDFPath
    KC_ShiftWriteRequest requestPath, currentPDF, 0
    If Not KC_ShiftRun(requestPath, responsePath, donePath, "--action propose-shifts") Then Exit Sub
    Set plans = New Collection: Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(responsePath, 1, False)
    If stream.ReadLine <> "id" & vbTab & "trial" & vbTab & "dx" & vbTab & "dy" & vbTab & "reason" Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2897, , "Неизвестный формат предложений сдвига."
    End If
    Do While Not stream.AtEndOfStream
        line = stream.ReadLine
        If Len(line) > 0 Then
            cols = Split(line, vbTab)
            If UBound(cols) <> 4 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2898, , "Повреждены предложения сдвига."
            id = CLng(cols(0)): dx = KC_VisionParseNum(CStr(cols(2))): dy = KC_VisionParseNum(CStr(cols(3)))
            If id < 1 Or id > gKV_Items.count Or Sqr(dx * dx + dy * dy) > 0.004001 Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2899, , "Недопустимый пробный сдвиг."
            End If
            plans.Add Array(id, dx, dy)
            If plans.count > 100 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2900, , "Превышен защитный лимит проб."
        End If
    Loop
    stream.Close
    For Each plan In plans
        serial = serial + 1
        result = KC_ShiftTry(CLng(plan(0)), CDbl(plan(1)), CDbl(plan(2)), serial, currentPDF, report)
        If result < 0 Or gKV_TimedOut Then Exit For
    Next plan
    report = report & "SOURCE_SHIFT SUMMARY | accepted=" & gKV_SourceMovedCount & "; proposed=" & plans.count & vbCrLf
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Дополнительная расстановка остановлена: " & Err.Description & vbCrLf
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
End Sub

Private Sub KC_VisionRemovePDFs(ByRef report As String)
    Dim fso As Object, file As Object, paths As Collection, path As Variant, errors As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Len(gKV_Folder) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If gKV_TimedOut Then
        report = report & "PDF cleanup deferred: Python timeout; files may still be in use: " & gKV_Folder & vbCrLf
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Not fso.FolderExists(gKV_Folder) Then Exit Sub
    Set paths = New Collection
    For Each file In fso.GetFolder(gKV_Folder).files
        If LCase$(fso.GetExtensionName(file.name)) = "pdf" Then paths.Add CStr(file.path)
    Next file
    For Each path In paths
        On Error Resume Next
        Err.Clear
        fso.DeleteFile CStr(path), True
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
        On Error GoTo Failed
    Next path
    report = report & "Служебные PDF удалены; ошибок удаления: " & errors & vbCrLf
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    report = report & "Не завершена очистка PDF: " & Err.Description & vbCrLf
End Sub

' Общий формат обоих макросов: A:E — ВСЕ операции, I7:I9 — три строки ОК1.
Private Function OK1ExcelHeaders() As Variant
    OK1ExcelHeaders = Array("Р", "Контролируемые параметры", _
        "Наименование средств ТО", "Объем и ПК", "То/Тв")
End Function

Private Function OK1ExcelText(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal r As Long, ByVal c As Long) As String
    Dim v As Variant
    v = ws.cells(r, c).Value2
    If IsError(v) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2500, , _
        "Ошибка Excel в " & ws.name & "!" & ws.cells(r, c).address(False, False)
    If Not IsEmpty(v) Then OK1ExcelText = Trim$(CStr(v))
End Function

Private Function HasCardHeaders(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    Dim h As Variant, c As Long
    h = OK1ExcelHeaders()
    For c = 0 To 4
        If OK1ExcelText(ws, 6, c + 1) <> CStr(h(c)) Then Exit Function
    Next c
    HasCardHeaders = True
End Function

Private Function IsUnifiedCard(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    If KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then
        IsUnifiedCard = HasCardHeaders(ws)
        Exit Function
    End If
    If OK1ExcelText(ws, 5, 8) <> UNIFIED_CARD_MARKER And _
        OK1ExcelText(ws, 5, 8) <> "ОК: единый список v9" Then Exit Function
    If Not HasCardHeaders(ws) Then Exit Function
    If OK1ExcelText(ws, 6, 9) <> "Первые три строки ОК1" Then Exit Function
    IsUnifiedCard = True
End Function

Private Function IsLegacyOK1Block(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    Dim h As Variant, c As Long
    If OK1ExcelText(ws, 5, 8) <> "Данные ОК1 (11 строк)" Then Exit Function
    h = OK1ExcelHeaders()
    For c = 0 To 4
        If OK1ExcelText(ws, 6, c + 8) <> CStr(h(c)) Then Exit Function
    Next c
    IsLegacyOK1Block = True
End Function

Private Function IsIntroOperationKey(ByVal text As String) As Boolean
    IsIntroOperationKey = StrComp(Left$(Trim$(text), 4), "ОК1-", vbTextCompare) = 0
End Function

Private Function LastOKContinuationRow(ByVal ws As Object) As Long
    Dim c As Long, r As Long, result As Long
    result = 6
    For c = 1 To 5
        r = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, c).End(-4162).row
        If r > result Then result = r
    Next c
    LastOKContinuationRow = result
End Function

Private Sub DefaultFixedCardText(ByRef fixed() As String)
    ReDim fixed(0 To 2)
    fixed(0) = "Контролер ОТК, мастер, исполнитель."
    fixed(1) = "Костюм для защиты от общих производственных загрязнений и механических воздействий, ботинки кожаные с защитным подноском"
    fixed(2) = "перчатки трикотажные с точечным полимерным покрытием, каска защитная, подшлемник под каску."
End Sub

Private Sub DefaultIntroOperations(ByRef intro() As Variant, ByRef introCount As Long)
    ' ДВА таких же четырёхстрочных блока, как у параметров чертежа.
    introCount = 8
    ReDim intro(1 To introCount, 1 To 5)
    intro(1, 1) = "ОК1-1"
    intro(1, 2) = "1. Клеймо сварщика, мастера, заводской номер. Эскиз 2"
    intro(1, 3) = "Визуально"
    intro(1, 4) = "100%"
    intro(1, 5) = 0.5
    intro(5, 1) = "ОК1-2"
    intro(5, 2) = "2. Отсутствие дефектов сварных швов: прожогов, подрезов, раковин, трещин. Отсутствие сварочных брызг, окалин и побежалости."
    intro(5, 3) = "Визуально"
    intro(5, 4) = "100%"
    intro(5, 5) = 7
    intro(6, 3) = "Лупа ГОСТ 25706-83"
End Sub

Private Sub GetIntroSource(ByVal source As Object, ByRef fixed() As String, _
    ByRef mass As Variant, ByRef intro() As Variant, ByRef introCount As Long)
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, lastRow As Long, key As String
    Dim firstGroup As Boolean, groupNo As Long, text As String, anyValue As Boolean
    Dim rx As Object, prefixData As Variant
    DefaultFixedCardText fixed
    mass = Empty
    If source Is Nothing Then
        DefaultIntroOperations intro, introCount
        Exit Sub
    End If
    key = OK1ExcelText(source, 5, 8)
    If key = UNIFIED_CARD_MARKER Or key = "ОК: единый список v9" Then
        If Not IsUnifiedCard(source) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2527, , _
            "В прежнем Excel есть подпись единого списка, но изменена шапка. Перенос отменён без подстановки образца."
    ElseIf key = "Данные ОК1 (11 строк)" Then
        If Not IsLegacyOK1Block(source) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2528, , _
            "В прежнем Excel есть подпись v8, но изменена шапка ОК1. Перенос отменён."
    End If
    If IsUnifiedCard(source) Or IsLegacyOK1Block(source) Then
        For r = 0 To 2
            fixed(r) = KC23_CellText(KC23_FixedCell(source, r))
        Next r
        mass = KC23_Field(source, "OK_Mass", "I3").Value2
    End If
    If IsUnifiedCard(source) Then
        ' Только префиксные операции, не параметры чертежа и не клеймение в конце.
        introCount = 0
        lastRow = KC15_IntroLast(source)
        If lastRow >= 7 Then introCount = lastRow - 6
        If introCount > 0 Then
            prefixData = source.Range("A7:E" & CStr(lastRow)).Value2
            ReDim intro(1 To introCount, 1 To 5)
            For r = 1 To introCount
                For c = 1 To 5
                    intro(r, c) = prefixData(r, c)
                Next c
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(intro(r, 1)))) > 0 Then
                    groupNo = groupNo + 1
                    intro(r, 1) = "ОК1-" & CStr(groupNo)
                End If
                intro(r, 4) = KC15_PercentText(intro(r, 4))
            Next r
        Else
            ReDim intro(1 To 1, 1 To 5)
        End If
    ElseIf IsLegacyOK1Block(source) Then
        ' Миграция v8: физические строки 04–11 становятся исходными строками операций.
        introCount = 8
        ReDim intro(1 To introCount, 1 To 5)
        Set rx = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
        rx.Pattern = "^\s*[0-9]+[.)]\s*"
        firstGroup = True
        For r = 1 To introCount
            anyValue = False
            For c = 2 To 5
                intro(r, c) = source.cells(9 + r, 7 + c).Value2
                If Len(OK1ExcelText(source, 9 + r, 7 + c)) > 0 Then anyValue = True
            Next c
            If anyValue Then
                text = OK1ExcelText(source, 9 + r, 9)
                If firstGroup Or rx.Test(text) Then
                    groupNo = groupNo + 1
                    intro(r, 1) = "ОК1-" & CStr(groupNo)
                    firstGroup = False
                End If
            End If
        Next r
    Else
        DefaultIntroOperations intro, introCount
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub WriteFixedCardBlock(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef fixed() As String, ByVal mass As Variant)
    Dim r As Long
    KC23_EnsureLayout ws
    KC23_Field(ws, "OK_Mass", "I3").Value2 = mass
    For r = 0 To 2: KC23_FixedCell(ws, r).Value2 = fixed(r): Next r
    ws.Range("H20:I22").rows.AutoFit
End Sub

Private Sub WriteIntroOperations(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef intro() As Variant, ByVal introCount As Long)
    Dim data As Variant, r As Long, c As Long, n As Long
    If introCount > 0 Then
        ReDim data(1 To introCount, 1 To 5)
        For r = 1 To introCount
            For c = 2 To 5: data(r, c) = intro(r, c): Next c
            data(r, 4) = KC15_PercentText(intro(r, 4))
            If Len(Trim$(CStr(intro(r, 1)))) > 0 Then
                n = n + 1: data(r, 1) = Format$(n, "00")
            End If
        Next r
        ws.Range("A7:A" & CStr(6 + introCount)).NumberFormat = "@"
        ws.Range("D7:D" & CStr(6 + introCount)).NumberFormat = "@"
        ws.Range("A7").Resize(introCount, 5).Value2 = data
    End If
    KC15_SetIntroArea ws, 6 + introCount
    If introCount > 0 Then KCPerf_Layout ws, data, 7, introCount
End Sub

Private Function InitializeUnifiedExport(ByVal target As Object, ByVal xl As Object, _
    ByVal sourcePath As String) As Long
    Dim sourceBook As Object, source As Object, ws As Object
    Dim fixed() As String, mass As Variant, intro() As Variant, introCount As Long
    Dim errorNumber As Long, errorText As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Len(Trim$(sourcePath)) > 0 Then
        Set sourceBook = xl.Workbooks.Open(sourcePath, 0, True)
        For Each ws In sourceBook.Worksheets
            If StrComp(ws.name, "Карта контроля", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                Set source = ws
                Exit For
            End If
        Next ws
        If Not source Is Nothing Then source.Calculate
    End If
    GetIntroSource source, fixed, mass, intro, introCount
    NormalizeIntroBlocks intro, introCount
    WriteFixedCardBlock target, fixed, mass
    KC23_CopyPanel source, target
    WriteIntroOperations target, intro, introCount
    InitializeUnifiedExport = 7 + introCount
    If Not sourceBook Is Nothing Then sourceBook.Close False
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number
    errorText = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not sourceBook Is Nothing Then sourceBook.Close False
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errorNumber, "InitializeUnifiedExport", errorText
End Function

Private Sub NormalizeIntroBlocks(ByRef intro() As Variant, ByRef introCount As Long)
    Dim buffer() As Variant, starts() As Long, stops() As Long, lengths() As Long
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, groups As Long, g As Long, lastUsed As Long
    Dim outputCount As Long, outRow As Long, anyValue As Boolean
    If introCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ReDim starts(1 To introCount)
    ReDim stops(1 To introCount)
    ReDim lengths(1 To introCount)
    For r = 1 To introCount
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(intro(r, 1)))) > 0 Then
            If Not IsIntroOperationKey(CStr(intro(r, 1))) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2600, , "В вводных операциях найден неизвестный идентификатор."
            If groups > 0 Then stops(groups) = r - 1
            groups = groups + 1
            starts(groups) = r
        ElseIf groups = 0 Then
            For c = 2 To 5
                If IsError(intro(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2601, , "Ошибка Excel во вводной операции."
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(intro(r, c)))) > 0 Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2602, , "Вводная операция должна начинаться с метки ОК1-... в A."
            Next c
        End If
    Next r
    If groups = 0 Then
        introCount = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    stops(groups) = introCount
    For g = 1 To groups
        lastUsed = starts(g)
        For r = starts(g) To stops(g)
            anyValue = False
            For c = 1 To 5
                If IsError(intro(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2603, , "Ошибка Excel во вводной операции."
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(intro(r, c)))) > 0 Then anyValue = True
            Next c
            If anyValue Then lastUsed = r
        Next r
        lengths(g) = lastUsed - starts(g) + 1
        If lengths(g) < 4 Then lengths(g) = 4
        outputCount = outputCount + lengths(g)
        stops(g) = lastUsed
    Next g
    ReDim buffer(1 To outputCount, 1 To 5)
    outRow = 1
    For g = 1 To groups
        For r = starts(g) To stops(g)
            For c = 1 To 5
                buffer(outRow + r - starts(g), c) = intro(r, c)
            Next c
        Next r
        outRow = outRow + lengths(g)
    Next g
    ReDim intro(1 To outputCount, 1 To 5)
    For r = 1 To outputCount
        For c = 1 To 5
            intro(r, c) = buffer(r, c)
        Next c
    Next r
    introCount = outputCount
End Sub

Private Function EnsureCardToolsList(ByVal book As Object) As Boolean
    Dim nm As Object, rng As Object, ws As Object, candidate As Object
    Dim tools As Variant, i As Long, lastRow As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    Set nm = book.names("ToolsList")
    If Not nm Is Nothing Then Set rng = nm.RefersToRange
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Not rng Is Nothing Then
        If rng.Columns.count = 1 Then
            If rng.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(rng) > 0 Then Exit Function
        End If
    End If
    For Each candidate In book.Worksheets
        If StrComp(candidate.name, "СписокТО", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            Set ws = candidate
            Exit For
        End If
    Next candidate
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = book.Worksheets.Add(After:=book.Worksheets(book.Worksheets.count))
        ws.name = "СписокТО"
    End If
    lastRow = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("A1:A" & CStr(lastRow))) = 0 Then
        tools = Array("Шаблон сварщика УШС-2", "Шаблон сварщика УШС МГ-8", "Визуально", "Рулетка Р5У2П ГОСТ 7502-98", _
            "Угольник поверочный слесарный ГОСТ 7502-77", "Лупа ГОСТ 25706-83", "КИМ", _
            "Линейка ШД-1-1000 ГОСТ 8026-92", "Набор щупов ГОСТ 882-75", _
            "Штангенциркуль ШЦ-I-125-0,05 ГОСТ 166-89", "Правило", "Уровень строительный ГОСТ 9416-83", _
            "Клеймо ОТК", "Молоток 7850-0103 ГОСТ 2310-77")
        For i = 0 To UBound(tools)
            ws.cells(i + 1, 1).Value2 = tools(i)
        Next i
        lastRow = UBound(tools) + 1
    End If
    If Not nm Is Nothing Then nm.Delete
