    book.names.Add name:="ToolsList", RefersTo:="='" & Replace(ws.name, "'", "''") & "'!$A$1:$A$" & CStr(lastRow)
    ws.Visible = False
    EnsureCardToolsList = True
End Function

Private Function IsCardEndRow(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal r As Long) As Boolean
    Dim a As String, b As String, c As String
    a = UCase$(OK1ExcelText(ws, r, 1))
    b = LCase$(OK1ExcelText(ws, r, 2))
    c = LCase$(OK1ExcelText(ws, r, 3))
    If a = "ОК" Or a = "OK" Then
        IsCardEndRow = True
    ElseIf InStr(1, b & " " & c, "операционная карта контроля", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
        IsCardEndRow = True
    End If
End Function

Private Function NeedsCardToolValidation(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long, tp As Long, formula As String, dropDown As Boolean
    lastRow = LastOKContinuationRow(ws)
    For r = 7 To lastRow
        If IsCardEndRow(ws, r) Then Exit For
        tp = 0
        formula = ""
        dropDown = False
        On Error Resume Next
        tp = ws.cells(r, 3).Validation.Type
        formula = ws.cells(r, 3).Validation.Formula1
        dropDown = ws.cells(r, 3).Validation.InCellDropdown
        On Error GoTo 0
        If tp <> 3 Or StrComp(formula, "=ToolsList", vbTextCompare) <> 0 Or Not dropDown Then
            NeedsCardToolValidation = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next r
End Function

Private Sub FormatCardOperationBlock(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal firstRow As Long, ByVal lastRow As Long)
    Dim data As Variant, percents As Variant, r As Long, key As String, length As Long
    length = lastRow - firstRow + 1
    If length < 1 Then Exit Sub
    data = ws.Range("A" & CStr(firstRow) & ":E" & CStr(lastRow)).Value2
    key = CStr(data(1, 1))
    ws.Range("A" & CStr(firstRow) & ":A" & CStr(lastRow)).NumberFormat = "@"
    If IsNumeric(key) Then ws.cells(firstRow, 1).Value2 = Format$(CLng(key), "00")
    ReDim percents(1 To length, 1 To 1)
    For r = 1 To length: percents(r, 1) = KC15_PercentText(data(r, 4)): Next r
    ws.Range("D" & CStr(firstRow) & ":D" & CStr(lastRow)).NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.cells(firstRow, 4).Resize(length, 1).Value2 = percents
    KCPerf_Layout ws, data, firstRow, length
End Sub

Private Sub ApplyCardOperationLayout(ByVal ws As Object)
    Dim data As Variant, numbers As Variant, percents As Variant, r As Long, last As Long, length As Long, key As String
    last = LastOKContinuationRow(ws)
    If last < 7 Then Exit Sub
    data = ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(last)).Value2
    length = UBound(data, 1)
    For r = 1 To UBound(data, 1)
        If KCPerf_EndRow(data, r) Then length = r - 1: Exit For
    Next r
    If length < 1 Then Exit Sub
    ReDim numbers(1 To length, 1 To 1): ReDim percents(1 To length, 1 To 1)
    For r = 1 To length
        key = CStr(data(r, 1))
        If IsNumeric(key) Then key = Format$(CLng(key), "00")
        data(r, 1) = key: numbers(r, 1) = key
        percents(r, 1) = KC15_PercentText(data(r, 4))
    Next r
    ws.Range("A7:A" & CStr(6 + length)).NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("D7:D" & CStr(6 + length)).NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("A7").Resize(length, 1).Value2 = numbers
    ws.Range("D7").Resize(length, 1).Value2 = percents
    KCPerf_Layout ws, data, 7, length
End Sub


' Вводные пункты подтверждаются и сохраняются ДО присвоения номеров баллонам.
Private Sub KC_ResetIntroConfiguration()
    gIntroReady = False
    gIntroRows = 0
    gIntroPointCount = 0
    gIntroFirstBalloon = 1
    gIntroPreviousCount = 0
    gIntroSourcePath = ""
    gIntroConfigPath = ""
    gIntroSourceHasRaw = False
    gIntroMass = Empty
    gIntroTools = Empty
    gIntroToolsRows = 0
    Erase gIntroData
    Erase gIntroFixed
End Sub

Private Function KC_CountIntroGroups(ByRef data() As Variant, ByVal rowCount As Long) As Long
    Dim r As Long, count As Long
    For r = 1 To rowCount
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(data(r, 1)))) > 0 Then
            If Not IsIntroOperationKey(CStr(data(r, 1))) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2700, , "Неверная метка вводного пункта в A."
            count = count + 1
        End If
    Next r
    KC_CountIntroGroups = count
End Function

Private Function KC_NumberedOperationText(ByVal text As String, ByVal number As Long) As String
    Dim rx As Object, matches As Object
    text = Trim$(text)
    Set rx = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    rx.Pattern = "^\s*[0-9]+[.)]\s*(?=[^0-9]|$)"
    Set matches = rx.Execute(text)
    If matches.count > 0 Then text = Trim$(Mid$(text, matches(0).length + 1))
    If Len(text) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2701, , _
        "Заполните текст вводного пункта " & CStr(number) & ". Одного номера недостаточно."
    KC_NumberedOperationText = CStr(number) & ". " & text
End Function

Private Function KC_IntroNumeric(ByVal text As String, ByRef result As Double) As Boolean
    Dim decSep As String, comma As Long, dot As Long
    text = Replace(Replace(Replace(Trim$(text), Chr(160), ""), " ", ""), "'", "")
    If Len(text) = 0 Then Exit Function
    decSep = Mid$(CStr(1.1), 2, 1)
    comma = InStrRev(text, ",")
    dot = InStrRev(text, ".")
    If comma > 0 And dot > 0 Then
        If comma > dot Then
            text = Replace(Replace(text, ".", ""), ",", decSep)
        Else
            text = Replace(Replace(text, ",", ""), ".", decSep)
        End If
    ElseIf comma > 0 Then
        text = Replace(text, ",", decSep)
    ElseIf dot > 0 Then
        text = Replace(text, ".", decSep)
    End If
    If Not IsNumeric(text) Then Exit Function
    result = CDbl(text)
    KC_IntroNumeric = True
End Function



Private Function KC_ReadIntroEditor(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef reason As String) As Boolean
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, lastRow As Long, key As String, groupNo As Long, n As Double, text As String
    Dim prefixData As Variant
    On Error GoTo Invalid
    If Not IsUnifiedCard(ws) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2702, , "Не изменяйте шапку A6:E6 и служебные имена диапазонов панели реквизитов."
    For r = 0 To 2
        If KC23_CellText(KC23_FixedCell(ws, r).Offset(0, -1)) <> Format$(r + 1, "00") Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2711, , "Не изменяйте номера фиксированных строк ОК1 (01, 02, 03). Для удаления пункта очищайте A:E, не всю строку листа."
    Next r
    lastRow = KC15_IntroLast(ws)
    If lastRow >= 7 Then prefixData = ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(lastRow)).Value2
    For r = 7 To lastRow
        key = KCPerf_Text(prefixData(r - 6, 1), r, 1)
        For c = 2 To 5
            text = KCPerf_Text(prefixData(r - 6, c), r, c)
        Next c
        text = KCPerf_Text(prefixData(r - 6, 5), r, 5)
        If Len(text) > 0 And text <> "-" And text <> "—" Then
            If Not KC_IntroNumeric(text, n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2704, , "Время E" & CStr(r) & " должно быть числом."
            If n < 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2705, , "Время E" & CStr(r) & " не может быть отрицательным."
        End If
    Next r
    GetIntroSource ws, gIntroFixed, gIntroMass, gIntroData, gIntroRows
    NormalizeIntroBlocks gIntroData, gIntroRows
    KC_CompactIntroDrafts gIntroData, gIntroRows
    NormalizeIntroBlocks gIntroData, gIntroRows
    gIntroPointCount = KC_CountIntroGroups(gIntroData, gIntroRows)
    If gIntroPointCount > 1000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2706, , "Слишком много вводных пунктов (предел 1000)."
    groupNo = 0
    For r = 1 To gIntroRows
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(gIntroData(r, 1)))) > 0 Then
            groupNo = groupNo + 1
            gIntroData(r, 1) = "ОК1-" & CStr(groupNo)
            gIntroData(r, 2) = KC_NumberedOperationText(CStr(gIntroData(r, 2)), groupNo)
        End If
    Next r
    text = KC23_CellText(KC23_Field(ws, "OK_Mass", "I3"))
    If Len(text) > 0 Then
        If Not KC_IntroNumeric(text, n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2707, , "Масса в панели реквизитов должна быть числом без 'кг'."
        If n <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2708, , "Масса в панели реквизитов должна быть больше нуля либо пока оставаться пустой."
    End If
    gIntroFirstBalloon = gIntroPointCount + 1
    KC_ReadIntroEditor = True
    Exit Function
Invalid:
    reason = Err.Description
End Function

Private Sub KC_WriteIntroSummary(ByVal ws As Object)
    KC23_EnsureLayout ws
    ws.Range("I24").Value2 = gIntroPointCount
    ws.Range("I25").Value2 = gIntroFirstBalloon
End Sub

Private Sub KC_CopyConfirmedTools(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim sheet As Object
    If gIntroToolsRows < 1 Then Exit Sub
    EnsureCardToolsList book
    Set sheet = book.Worksheets("СписокТО")
    sheet.Columns("A").Clear
    If gIntroToolsRows = 1 Then
        sheet.Range("A1").Value2 = gIntroTools
    Else
        sheet.Range("A1:A" & CStr(gIntroToolsRows)).Value2 = gIntroTools
    End If
    book.names("ToolsList").Delete
    book.names.Add name:="ToolsList", RefersTo:="='СписокТО'!$A$1:$A$" & CStr(gIntroToolsRows)
End Sub

Private Function KC_WriteConfirmedIntro(ByVal target As Object) As Long
    If Not gIntroReady Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2710, , "Сначала подтвердите вводные пункты."
    WriteFixedCardBlock target, gIntroFixed, gIntroMass
    WriteIntroOperations target, gIntroData, gIntroRows
    KC_WriteIntroSummary target
    KC_WriteConfirmedIntro = 7 + gIntroRows
End Function

' Проверка соответствий до первого изменения номера.
Private Function KC_ValidateBalloonAssignments(ByRef mapping() As Long, ByRef numbers() As Long, _
    ByRef sheets() As String, ByVal total As Long, ByRef reason As String, ByRef orphanCount As Long) As Boolean
    Dim used() As Boolean, p As Long, b As Long, lastNumber As Long, extra As Long, list As String
    orphanCount = 0
    lastNumber = gIntroFirstBalloon + positionsCount - 1
    If total <= 0 Then
        KC_ValidateBalloonAssignments = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    ReDim used(1 To total)
    For p = 1 To positionsCount
        b = mapping(p)
        If b < 0 Or b > total Then
            reason = "Ошибка сопоставления для позиции " & CStr(gIntroFirstBalloon + p - 1) & "."
            Exit Function
        End If
        If b > 0 Then
            If used(b) Then
                reason = "Один баллон сопоставлен нескольким позициям."
                Exit Function
            End If
            used(b) = True
        End If
    Next p
    For b = 1 To total
        If Not used(b) Then
            If numbers(b) >= gIntroFirstBalloon And numbers(b) <= lastNumber Then
                extra = extra + 1
                If extra <= 15 Then list = list & vbCrLf & "  № " & CStr(numbers(b)) & " — лист '" & sheets(b) & "'"
            Else
                orphanCount = orphanCount + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next b
    If extra > 0 Then
        reason = "Лишние баллоны без аннотации занимают новый диапазон номеров (" & extra & " шт.):" & list & _
            IIf(extra > 15, vbCrLf & "  ...", "") & vbCrLf & _
            "Обычно это дубликаты, созданные прежними версиями макроса после перемещения размеров, " & _
            "или баллоны удалённых/невыбранных по типу аннотаций. Удалите их и повторите режим 1."
        Exit Function
    End If
    KC_ValidateBalloonAssignments = True
End Function

' v15.14: баллоны невыбранного листа собираются в общем проходе по листам.
' Возвращает пустую строку или текст ошибки (как раньше, ошибка блокирует изменения позже).
Private Function KC_ScanOtherSheetBalloons(ByVal sheetName As String, _
    ByRef notes() As Object, ByRef numbers() As Long, ByRef xs() As Double, ByRef ys() As Double, _
    ByRef names() As String, ByRef types() As Long, ByRef pointers() As Long, _
    ByRef total As Long, ByRef capacity As Long, ByVal seen As Object) As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    swDraw.ActivateSheet sheetName
    InitWorkArea
    ScanSheetForBalloons_v2 sheetName, notes, numbers, xs, ys, names, types, pointers, total, capacity, seen
    KC_ScanOtherSheetBalloons = ""
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC_ScanOtherSheetBalloons = "Не удалось проверить номера на остальных листах: " & Err.Description
End Function

Private Function KC_CheckOtherSheetNumbers(ByVal scanError As String, ByRef numbers() As Long, _
    ByRef names() As String, ByVal total As Long, _
    ByVal firstNumber As Long, ByVal lastNumber As Long, ByRef reason As String) As Boolean
    Dim b As Long
    If Len(scanError) > 0 Then
        reason = scanError
        Exit Function
    End If
    For b = 1 To total
        If numbers(b) >= firstNumber And numbers(b) <= lastNumber Then
            reason = "На невыбранном листе '" & names(b) & "' уже есть баллон № " & CStr(numbers(b)) & ". Выберите все нужные листы."
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next b
    KC_CheckOtherSheetNumbers = True
End Function


' Пустой добавленный блок не является вводным пунктом.
Private Sub KC_CompactIntroDrafts(ByRef data() As Variant, ByRef rowCount As Long)
    Dim first As Long, last As Long, r As Long, c As Long, outRow As Long, anyValue As Boolean
    Dim buffer() As Variant
    If rowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ReDim buffer(1 To rowCount, 1 To 5)
    first = 1
    Do While first <= rowCount
        last = first
        Do While last < rowCount
            If Len(Trim$(CStr(data(last + 1, 1)))) > 0 Then Exit Do
            last = last + 1
        Loop
        anyValue = False
        For r = first To last
            For c = 2 To 5
                If IsError(data(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2822, , "Ошибка Excel во вводном пункте."
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(data(r, c)))) > 0 Then
                    If c <> 4 Or KC15_PercentText(data(r, c)) <> "100%" Then anyValue = True
                End If
            Next c
        Next r
        If anyValue Then
            If Len(Trim$(CStr(data(first, 2)))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2823, , _
                "У блока '" & CStr(data(first, 1)) & "' есть данные, но нет текста в первой B. Заполните начало пункта или удалите весь блок."
            For r = first To last
                outRow = outRow + 1
                For c = 1 To 5
                    buffer(outRow, c) = data(r, c)
                Next c
            Next r
        End If
        first = last + 1
    Loop
    rowCount = outRow
    If rowCount = 0 Then
        Erase data
        Exit Sub
    End If
    ReDim data(1 To rowCount, 1 To 5)
    For r = 1 To rowCount
        For c = 1 To 5
            data(r, c) = buffer(r, c)
        Next c
    Next r
End Sub


Private Function KC_EditorMeta(ByVal book As Object) As Object
    Dim sheet As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set sheet = book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If sheet Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2800, , "Нет служебного листа редактора v12. Сначала подготовьте шаблон .xlsm."
    If CStr(sheet.Range("A1").Value2) <> "OK_INTRO_EDITOR_V12" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2801, , "Это не редактор вводных пунктов v12."
    Set KC_EditorMeta = sheet
End Function

Private Function KC_SamePath(ByVal leftPath As String, ByVal rightPath As String) As Boolean
    Dim fso As Object
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    KC_SamePath = (StrComp(fso.GetAbsolutePathName(leftPath), fso.GetAbsolutePathName(rightPath), vbTextCompare) = 0)
End Function

Private Function KC_ReadIntroBinding(ByVal drawingPath As String) As String
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, signature As String, owner As String, path As String
    Dim sidecar As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    sidecar = drawingPath & ".okintro-v12.txt"
    If Not fso.FileExists(sidecar) Then Exit Function
    On Error GoTo Invalid
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(sidecar, 1, False, -1)
    signature = stream.ReadLine
    owner = stream.ReadLine
    path = stream.ReadLine
    stream.Close
    If signature <> "OK_INTRO_BINDING_V12" Then GoTo Invalid
    If Not KC_SamePath(owner, drawingPath) Then GoTo Invalid
    If LCase$(Right$(path, 5)) <> ".xlsm" Then GoTo Invalid
    If Not fso.FileExists(path) Then
        MsgBox "Привязанный файл перемещён или удалён:" & vbCrLf & path & vbCrLf & _
            "Укажите его заново. Другие файлы автоматически не подставляются.", vbExclamation
        Exit Function
    End If
    KC_ReadIntroBinding = path
    Exit Function
Invalid:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
    MsgBox "Не удалось прочитать привязку к этому чертежу. Укажите файл редактора заново.", vbExclamation
End Function

Private Sub KC_WriteIntroBinding(ByVal drawingPath As String, ByVal editorPath As String)
    KC30_WriteBinding drawingPath, editorPath
End Sub

Private Function KC_TemplatePath(ByVal xl As Object) As String
    Dim fso As Object
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Not fso.FileExists(INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH) Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2930, , _
            "Не найден шаблон вводных пунктов:" & vbCrLf & _
            INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH & vbCrLf & _
            "Проверьте имя файла, наличие шаблона и доступ к сетевой папке."
    End If
    KC_TemplatePath = INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH
End Function

Private Sub KC_RunEditorCommand(ByVal xl As Object, ByVal book As Object, ByVal command As String)
    Dim answer As Variant, qualified As String
    Dim originalNumber As Long, originalText As String, originalSource As String
    Dim bookPath As String, security As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    qualified = "'" & Replace(book.name, "'", "''") & "'!" & command
    answer = xl.Run(qualified)
    If command = "OK_EditorPing" Then
        If CStr(answer) <> "OK_INTRO_EDITOR_V19" Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2931, "KC_RunEditorCommand", _
                "Неверная версия Excel-модуля. Ожидалось OK_INTRO_EDITOR_V19, получено: '" & CStr(answer) & "'."
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    originalNumber = Err.number
    originalText = Err.Description
    originalSource = Err.source
    On Error Resume Next
    bookPath = book.FullName
    security = CStr(xl.AutomationSecurity)
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2804, "KC_RunEditorCommand", _
        "Не удалось выполнить команду Excel: " & command & vbCrLf & _
        "Файл: " & bookPath & vbCrLf & _
        "Исходная ошибка: " & CStr(originalNumber) & vbCrLf & _
        "Источник: " & originalSource & vbCrLf & _
        "Описание: " & originalText & vbCrLf & _
        "AutomationSecurity: " & security & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Откройте именно этот .xlsm вручную. Проверьте компиляцию и работу OK_RefreshButtons в Excel." & vbCrLf & _
        "Если код блокируется, действуйте по политике организации; не включайте все макросы глобально."
End Sub

Private Sub KC_ImportPreviousIntro(ByVal xl As Object, ByVal target As Object)
    Dim chosen As Variant, book As Object, source As Object, sheet As Object, rng As Object
    Dim tools As Variant, toolRows As Long, toolSheet As Object, detail As String
    chosen = KC32_PickCard(xl, swModel.GetPathName, "Прежняя карта или настройка v11", "*.xlsx;*.xlsm;*.xls")
    If VarType(chosen) = vbBoolean Then Exit Sub
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If KC_SamePath(CStr(chosen), target.Parent.FullName) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2821, , "Нельзя импортировать редактор в самого себя."
    xl.AutomationSecurity = 3
    Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(CStr(chosen), 0, True)
    For Each sheet In book.Worksheets
        If StrComp(sheet.name, "Карта контроля", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Set source = sheet
        If StrComp(sheet.name, "Данные из чертежа", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then gIntroSourceHasRaw = True
    Next sheet
    If source Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2805, , "В прежнем файле нет 'Карта контроля'."
    If Not IsUnifiedCard(source) And Not IsLegacyOK1Block(source) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2806, , "Отдельный блок вводных пунктов не найден. Подстановка образцов отменена."
    source.Calculate
    GetIntroSource source, gIntroFixed, gIntroMass, gIntroData, gIntroRows
    NormalizeIntroBlocks gIntroData, gIntroRows
    If LastOKContinuationRow(target) >= 7 Then target.Range("A7:E" & CStr(LastOKContinuationRow(target))).Clear
    WriteFixedCardBlock target, gIntroFixed, gIntroMass
    KC23_CopyPanel source, target
    WriteIntroOperations target, gIntroData, gIntroRows
    On Error Resume Next
    Set rng = book.names("ToolsList").RefersToRange
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not rng Is Nothing Then
        If rng.Columns.count = 1 And rng.rows.count <= 10000 Then
            toolRows = rng.rows.count
            tools = rng.Value2
        End If
    End If
    If toolRows > 0 Then
        Set toolSheet = target.Parent.Worksheets("СписокТО")
        toolSheet.Columns("A").Clear
        If toolRows = 1 Then
            toolSheet.Range("A1").Value2 = tools
        Else
            toolSheet.Range("A1:A" & CStr(toolRows)).Value2 = tools
        End If
        target.Parent.names("ToolsList").Delete
        target.Parent.names.Add name:="ToolsList", RefersTo:="='СписокТО'!$A$1:$A$" & CStr(toolRows)
    End If
    ApplyCardOperationLayout target
    gIntroSourcePath = CStr(chosen)
    book.Close False
    xl.AutomationSecurity = 2
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    detail = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
    xl.AutomationSecurity = 2
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2807, , "Импорт прежних пунктов отменён: " & detail
End Sub

Private Function KC_PrepareIntroConfiguration(ByVal editRequested As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim xl As Object, book As Object, ws As Object, meta As Object, rng As Object, fso As Object
    Dim drawing As String, path As String, creating As Boolean, reason As String, detail As String
    Dim previous As Double, editEnd As Long, oldRows As Long, delta As Long
    If gIntroReady Then KC_PrepareIntroConfiguration = True: Exit Function
    On Error GoTo Failed
    drawing = swModel.GetPathName
    If Len(gKC37PreparedPath) > 0 Then
        path = gKC37PreparedPath
    Else
        path = KC14_Resolve(drawing, False)
    End If
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    creating = Not fso.FileExists(path)
    If creating Then
        If Not fso.FileExists(INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3010, , "Нет шаблона: " & INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH
        KC14_Open xl, book, INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH, True
    Else
        KC14_Open xl, book, path, False
        If book.ReadOnly Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3011, , "Рабочая книга открыта только для чтения. Закройте другую копию Excel или проверьте права: " & path
        KC14_Backup path
    End If
    Set meta = KC_EditorMeta(book)
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    KC47_CheckCardFormulas ws
    If creating Then
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2))) > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3012, , "В постоянной папке лежит рабочая книга, а не чистый шаблон."
        meta.Range("B2").Value2 = drawing
        book.SaveAs path, 52
    Else
        If Not KC_SamePath(CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2), drawing) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3013, , "Книга принадлежит другому чертежу: " & path
    End If
    gIntroConfigPath = path
    KC_WriteIntroBinding drawing, path
    KC_RunEditorCommand xl, book, "OK_EditorPing"
    KC_RunEditorCommand xl, book, "OK_RefreshButtons"
    If KC_IntroNumeric(CStr(meta.Range("B4").Value2), previous) Then gIntroPreviousCount = CLng(previous)
    meta.Range("B10").Value2 = "PENDING"
    book.Save
    If editRequested Or creating Then
        xl.Visible = True
        ws.Activate
        Do
            If MsgBox("Заполните карту в Excel (Alt+Tab). После редактирования вернитесь сюда и нажмите ОК." & vbCrLf & _
                "Книгу не закрывайте. Отмена — без изменения номеров баллонов.", vbOKCancel + vbInformation, "Карта контроля") = vbCancel Then Exit Function
            xl.Calculate
            If KC_ReadIntroEditor(ws, reason) Then Exit Do
            MsgBox reason, vbExclamation
        Loop
    Else
        xl.Calculate
        If Not KC_ReadIntroEditor(ws, reason) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3014, , reason
    End If
    KC47_CheckCardFormulas ws
    ' Меняем только префикс вводных пунктов; параметры чертежа ниже сохраняются.
    oldRows = KC15_IntroLast(ws) - 6
    delta = gIntroRows - oldRows
    If delta > 0 Then ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(6 + delta)).Insert -4121
    If delta < 0 Then ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(6 - delta)).Delete -4162
    editEnd = 6 + gIntroRows
    If editEnd >= 7 Then ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(editEnd)).Clear
    WriteIntroOperations ws, gIntroData, gIntroRows
    KC_WriteIntroSummary ws
    Set rng = book.names("ToolsList").RefersToRange
    If rng.Columns.count <> 1 Or rng.rows.count > 10000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3016, , "Некорректный ToolsList."
    gIntroToolsRows = rng.rows.count: gIntroTools = rng.Value2
    meta.Range("B4").Value2 = gIntroPointCount
    book.Save
    gIntroReady = True
    Set gWorkApp = xl: Set gWorkBook = book
    KC_PrepareIntroConfiguration = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = "Подготовка рабочей книги: " & Err.number & " | " & Err.Description
    KC14_Log drawing, detail
    MsgBox detail & vbCrLf & "Книга не закрывается без сохранения. Номера баллонов на этом этапе не менялись.", vbCritical
End Function

Private Sub ResetEditorTemplate()
    SaveSetting "OKCardsV12", "Editor", "TemplatePath", ""
    MsgBox "Запомненный путь к шаблону сброшен. При создании редактора выберите новый чистый шаблон."
End Sub



Private Sub KC14_Open(ByRef xl As Object, ByRef book As Object, ByVal path As String, ByVal creating As Boolean)
    Dim app As Object, item As Object, valid As Boolean, events As Boolean
    Dim originalNumber As Long, originalText As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not gWorkBook Is Nothing Then
        On Error Resume Next
        valid = KC_SamePath(gWorkBook.FullName, path)
        On Error GoTo 0
        If valid Then
            Set xl = gWorkApp: Set book = gWorkBook
            Exit Sub
        End If
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    Set app = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    If Not app Is Nothing And Not creating Then
        For Each item In app.Workbooks
            If KC_SamePath(item.FullName, path) Then
                Set xl = app: Set book = item
                Exit For
            End If
        Next item
    End If
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If book Is Nothing Then
        Set xl = KC53_TakeExcel()
        xl.EnableEvents = True
        xl.Visible = True
        xl.AutomationSecurity = 2
        events = xl.EnableEvents
        xl.EnableEvents = False
        On Error GoTo Failed
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(path, 0, creating)
        xl.EnableEvents = events
    End If
    Set gWorkApp = xl: Set gWorkBook = book
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    originalNumber = Err.number: originalText = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then xl.EnableEvents = events
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise originalNumber, "KC14_Open", originalText
End Sub

Private Sub KC14_Backup(ByVal path As String)
    Dim fso As Object, retained As String, serial As Long, stamp As String
    If gBackupMade Then Exit Sub
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If fso.FileExists(path) Then
        ' v16.5.14: одна копия вместо двух (прежняя .previous.xlsm дублировала .keep). Храним последние 10.
        stamp = Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss")
        retained = fso.BuildPath(KC14_Service(path), fso.GetBaseName(path) & ".previous.xlsm." & stamp & ".keep.xlsm")
        Do While fso.FileExists(retained)
            serial = serial + 1
            retained = fso.BuildPath(KC14_Service(path), fso.GetBaseName(path) & ".previous.xlsm." & stamp & "." & serial & ".keep.xlsm")
        Loop
        fso.CopyFile path, retained, False
        If Not fso.FileExists(retained) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4706, , "Не создана резервная копия карты."
        If fso.GetFile(retained).Size <> fso.GetFile(path).Size Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4706, , "Размер резервной копии не совпал."
        KC46_Trace "Резервная копия карты: " & retained
        KC53_PruneKeep KC14_Service(path), fso.GetBaseName(path) & ".previous.xlsm.", 10
    End If
    gBackupMade = True
End Sub

Private Sub KC14_Snapshot(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim card As Object, raw As Object, rows As Collection, r As Long, first As Long, last As Long, key As String
    Dim i As Long, n As Long, stopRow As Long, values As Variant, c As Long
    Dim cardValues As Variant, rawValues As Variant, introLast As Long
    Set gOldRows = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set card = book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    KC47_CheckCardFormulas card
    On Error Resume Next
    Set raw = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If raw Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    n = raw.cells(raw.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row - 1
    If n <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    last = LastOKContinuationRow(card)
    If last < 7 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3025, , "В карте отсутствуют группы исходных параметров. Обновление отменено."
    cardValues = card.Range("A7:E" & CStr(last)).Value2
    rawValues = raw.Range("A2:B" & CStr(n + 1)).Value2
    introLast = KC15_IntroLast(card)
    i = 0: r = 7
    Do While r <= last
        key = KCPerf_Text(cardValues(r - 6, 1), r, 1)
        If KCPerf_EndRow(cardValues, r - 6) Then Exit Do
        If Len(key) > 0 And r > introLast Then
            first = r: stopRow = r + 1
            Do While stopRow <= last
                If Len(KCPerf_Text(cardValues(stopRow - 6, 1), stopRow, 1)) > 0 Then Exit Do
                stopRow = stopRow + 1
            Loop
            i = i + 1
            If i <= n Then
                If Not IsNumeric(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3022, , "Неверный номер параметра в A" & CStr(first)
                If CLng(key) <> CLng(rawValues(i, 1)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3023, , "Порядок параметров карты изменён вручную. Обновление отменено во избежание переноса правок не к тому пункту."
                key = CStr(CLng(rawValues(i, 1)))
                Set rows = New Collection
                For r = first To stopRow - 1
                    ReDim values(1 To 1, 1 To 4)
                    For c = 1 To 4: values(1, c) = cardValues(r - 6, c + 1): Next c
                    For c = 1 To 4
                        If IsError(values(1, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3026, , "В карте есть ошибка Excel, строка " & CStr(r)
                    Next c
                    rows.Add values
                Next r
                gOldRows.Add key, rows
            Else
                Set rows = New Collection
                For r = first To stopRow - 1
                    ReDim values(1 To 1, 1 To 4)
                    For c = 1 To 4: values(1, c) = cardValues(r - 6, c + 1): Next c
                    For c = 1 To 4
                        If IsError(values(1, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3026, , "В карте есть ошибка Excel, строка " & CStr(r)
                    Next c
                    rows.Add values
                Next r
                If i <> n + 1 Or InStr(1, KCPerf_Text(cardValues(first - 6, 2), first, 2), "Клейм", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3024, , "В карте найдены дополнительные обычные пункты. Добавляйте вводные пункты кнопкой; обновление отменено."
                gOldRows.Add "STAMP", rows
            End If
            r = stopRow
        Else
            r = r + 1
        End If
    Loop
    If i < n Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3025, , "В карте отсутствуют группы исходных параметров. Обновление отменено."
End Sub

Private Function KC14_WriteParameter(ByVal card As Object, ByVal first As Long, ByVal index As Long) As Long
    Dim rows As Collection, data As Variant, r As Long, c As Long, oldIndex As Long, key As String, pd As PositionData
    pd = positionsData(index)
    If gUsePreparedMerge Then
        oldIndex = gPreparedMatchIdx(index)
        If oldIndex > 0 Then
            key = CStr(gPreparedOldData(oldIndex).posNumber)
            If gOldRows.Exists(key) Then Set rows = gOldRows(key)
        End If
    End If
    If Not rows Is Nothing Then
        For r = 1 To rows.count
            data = rows(r)
            For c = 1 To 4
                card.cells(first + r - 1, c + 1).Value2 = data(1, c)
            Next c
        Next r
        card.cells(first, 2).Value2 = KC_NumberedOperationText(CStr(card.cells(first, 2).Value2), pd.number)
        r = rows.count
        If r < 4 Then r = 4
    Else
        card.cells(first, 2).Value2 = BuildParamText(pd)
        card.cells(first, 3).Value2 = pd.ToolName
        card.cells(first + 1, 3).Value2 = GetSecondaryTool(pd)
        card.cells(first, 4).Value2 = KC28_S("Operations.DefaultVolume")
        Select Case pd.AnnotationType
            Case swSelDIMENSIONS
                If pd.NominalValue <= KC28_N("Operations.ShortDimensionLimit") Then card.cells(first, 5).Value2 = KC28_N("Operations.DimensionShortTime") Else card.cells(first, 5).Value2 = KC28_N("Operations.DimensionLongTime")
            Case swSelGTOLS: card.cells(first, 5).Value2 = KC28_N("Operations.GtolTime")
            Case Else: card.cells(first, 5).Value2 = KC28_N("Operations.OtherTime")
        End Select
        r = 4
    End If
    card.cells(first, 1).Value2 = Format$(pd.number, "00")
    FormatCardOperationBlock card, first, first + r - 1
    KC14_WriteParameter = first + r
End Function

' Общие пути v14. Формат привязки v12 сохранён для существующих книг.
Private Function KC14_Key(ByVal drawing As String) As String
    Dim h As Double, i As Long, n As Long
    h = 5381
    For i = 1 To Len(LCase$(drawing))
        n = AscW(Mid$(LCase$(drawing), i, 1))
        If n < 0 Then n = n + 65536
        h = h * 33# + n
        h = h - Fix(h / 2147483629#) * 2147483629#
    Next i
    KC14_Key = "D" & Hex$(CLng(h))
End Function

Private Function KC14_Setting(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal name As String, ByVal fallback As String) As String
    If StrComp(GetSetting("OKCardsV14", KC14_Key(drawing), "Owner", ""), drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
        KC14_Setting = fallback
    Else
        KC14_Setting = GetSetting("OKCardsV14", KC14_Key(drawing), name, fallback)
    End If
End Function

Private Sub KC14_SaveSetting(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal name As String, ByVal value As String)
    SaveSetting "OKCardsV14", KC14_Key(drawing), "Owner", drawing
    SaveSetting "OKCardsV14", KC14_Key(drawing), name, value
End Sub

Private Function KC14_DefaultPath(ByVal drawing As String) As String
    Dim fso As Object, folder As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = KC14_Setting(drawing, "Folder", fso.GetParentFolderName(drawing))
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3000, , "Недоступна рабочая папка: " & folder
    KC14_DefaultPath = fso.BuildPath(folder, fso.GetBaseName(drawing) & " — карта контроля.xlsm")
End Function

Private Function KC14_Resolve(ByVal drawing As String, Optional ByVal required As Boolean = True) As String
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, signature As String, owner As String, path As String, sidecar As String, detail As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Len(drawing) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3001, , "Сначала сохраните чертёж."
    sidecar = drawing & ".okintro-v12.txt"
    On Error GoTo Invalid
    If fso.FileExists(sidecar) Then
        Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(sidecar, 1, False, -1)
        signature = stream.ReadLine: owner = stream.ReadLine: path = stream.ReadLine
        stream.Close: Set stream = Nothing
        If signature <> "OK_INTRO_BINDING_V12" Or StrComp(owner, drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3004, , "Повреждена привязка или указан другой чертёж. Для новой копии не копируйте чужой .okintro-v12.txt; сохраните его отдельно и повторите запуск без него."
        If LCase$(Right$(path, 5)) <> ".xlsm" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3004, , "В привязке должна быть .xlsm."
    Else
        path = KC14_DefaultPath(drawing)
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    If fso.FileExists(path) Then
        KC14_Resolve = path
        Exit Function
    End If
    KC14_Resolve = KC30_RebindMissing(drawing, path, required)
    Exit Function
Invalid:
    detail = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3004, "KC14_Resolve", detail
End Function

Private Function KC14_Service(ByVal path As String) As String
    Dim fso As Object, folder As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = fso.BuildPath(fso.GetParentFolderName(path), ".ok-service")
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then fso.CreateFolder folder
    KC14_Service = folder
End Function

Private Sub KC14_Log(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal text As String)
    If Not KC28_LogOn("Logs.Journal") Then Exit Sub
    On Error Resume Next
    KC53_Add text
End Sub


' ============================================
' v15.14: ЛИСТЫ ДЛЯ БАЛЛОНОВ — выбор при первом запуске, хранение по именам
'   "Sheets"      — выбранные листы (через vbLf) или * (все, включая будущие);
'   "SheetsKnown" — все листы чертежа на момент выбора (чтобы замечать новые).
' ============================================
Private Function KC14_InList(ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal listText As String) As Boolean
    Dim items As Variant, j As Long
    If Len(listText) = 0 Then Exit Function
    items = Split(listText, vbLf)
    For j = LBound(items) To UBound(items)
        If StrComp(CStr(items(j)), sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            KC14_InList = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next j
End Function

Private Function KC14_SelectedNames(ByVal names As Variant, ByRef selected() As Boolean) As String
    Dim i As Long, result As String
    For i = 0 To UBound(names)
        If selected(i) Then
            If Len(result) > 0 Then result = result & vbLf
            result = result & CStr(names(i))
        End If
    Next i
    KC14_SelectedNames = result
End Function

' Текст по умолчанию для поля ввода: "*" или "1,3,4".
Private Function KC14_SelectionText(ByRef selected() As Boolean) As String
    Dim i As Long, result As String, allSel As Boolean
    allSel = True
    For i = LBound(selected) To UBound(selected)
        If selected(i) Then
            If Len(result) > 0 Then result = result & ","
            result = result & CStr(i + 1)
        Else
            allSel = False
        End If
    Next i
    If allSel Or Len(result) = 0 Then result = "*"
    KC14_SelectionText = result
End Function

Private Sub KC14_SaveSheets(ByVal names As Variant, ByVal encoded As String)
    Dim i As Long, known As String
    For i = 0 To UBound(names)
        If i > 0 Then known = known & vbLf
        known = known & CStr(names(i))
    Next i
    KC14_SaveSetting swModel.GetPathName, "Sheets", encoded
    KC14_SaveSetting swModel.GetPathName, "SheetsKnown", known
End Sub

' Текущий сохранённый выбор без вопросов (отсутствующие листы пропускаются).
Private Function KC14_ReadSavedSheets(ByVal names As Variant, ByRef selected() As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim value As String, i As Long
    value = KC14_Setting(swModel.GetPathName, "Sheets", "")
    ReDim selected(0 To UBound(names))
    For i = 0 To UBound(names)
        If Len(value) = 0 Or value = "*" Then
            selected(i) = True
        Else
            selected(i) = KC14_InList(CStr(names(i)), value)
        End If
    Next i
    KC14_ReadSavedSheets = (Len(value) > 0)
End Function

' Диалог выбора листов. В selected — значение по умолчанию и результат. Отмена -> False.
Private Function KC14_AskSheets(ByVal names As Variant, ByRef selected() As Boolean, _
                                ByVal header As String, ByRef encoded As String) As Boolean
    Dim list As String, value As String, defaultText As String, i As Long
    For i = 0 To UBound(names): list = list & CStr(i + 1) & " = " & names(i) & vbCrLf: Next i
    defaultText = KC14_SelectionText(selected)
    Do
        value = InputBox(IIf(Len(header) > 0, header & vbCrLf & vbCrLf, "") & list & vbCrLf & _
            "Номера листов для баллонов и перенумерации (например 1,3-5) или * (все):", _
            "Карта контроля: листы", defaultText)
        If Len(Trim$(value)) = 0 Then Exit Function
        ReDim selected(0 To UBound(names))
        If Trim$(value) = "*" Then
            For i = 0 To UBound(names): selected(i) = True: Next i
            encoded = "*"
            KC14_AskSheets = True
            Exit Function
        End If
        ParseSheetSelection value, selected, UBound(names) + 1
        encoded = KC14_SelectedNames(names, selected)
        If Len(encoded) > 0 Then
            KC14_AskSheets = True
            Exit Function
        End If
        MsgBox "Не выбран ни один лист. Повторите ввод.", vbExclamation
        defaultText = value
    Loop
End Function

Private Function KC14_LoadSelection(ByVal names As Variant, ByRef selected() As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim drawing As String, value As String, known As String, items As Variant
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, found As Boolean, anySel As Boolean
    Dim missing As String, added As String, encoded As String, msg As String
    drawing = swModel.GetPathName
    value = KC14_Setting(drawing, "Sheets", "")
    ReDim selected(0 To UBound(names))

    ' Первый запуск для чертежа: спросить и запомнить.
    If Len(value) = 0 Then
        For i = 0 To UBound(names): selected(i) = True: Next i
        If Not KC14_AskSheets(names, selected, _
            "Первый запуск для этого чертежа: выберите листы для баллонов." & vbCrLf & _
            "Выбор запомнится по именам листов (изменить: режим 3 -> 5).", encoded) Then Exit Function
        KC14_SaveSheets names, encoded
        KC14_LoadSelection = True
        Exit Function
    End If

    If value = "*" Then
        For i = 0 To UBound(names): selected(i) = True: Next i
        KC14_LoadSelection = True
        Exit Function
    End If

    items = Split(value, vbLf)
    For j = LBound(items) To UBound(items)
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(items(j)))) > 0 Then
            found = False
            For i = 0 To UBound(names)
                If StrComp(CStr(names(i)), CStr(items(j)), vbTextCompare) = 0 Then selected(i) = True: found = True
            Next i
            If Not found Then missing = missing & vbCrLf & "   " & CStr(items(j))
        End If
    Next j
    known = KC14_Setting(drawing, "SheetsKnown", "")
    For i = 0 To UBound(names)
        If selected(i) Then
            anySel = True
        ElseIf Not KC14_InList(CStr(names(i)), known) Then
            added = added & vbCrLf & "   " & CStr(names(i))
        End If
    Next i
    If Len(missing) = 0 And Len(added) = 0 Then
        KC14_LoadSelection = True
        Exit Function
    End If

    ' Листы изменились: спросить один раз и запомнить ответ.
    If Len(missing) > 0 Then msg = msg & "Сохранённые листы не найдены (удалены или переименованы):" & missing & vbCrLf
    If Len(added) > 0 Then msg = msg & "Листы, которых нет в сохранённом списке:" & added & vbCrLf
    If Not anySel Then
        If Not KC14_AskSheets(names, selected, msg & vbCrLf & "Ни один сохранённый лист не найден. Выберите листы заново.", encoded) Then Exit Function
    Else
        Select Case MsgBox(msg & vbCrLf & _
            "Да — выбрать листы заново." & vbCrLf & _
            "Нет — продолжить с найденными сохранёнными листами (больше не спрашивать об этих листах)." & vbCrLf & _
            "Отмена — остановить.", vbYesNoCancel + vbQuestion, "Карта контроля: листы")
            Case vbYes
                If Not KC14_AskSheets(names, selected, "", encoded) Then Exit Function
            Case vbNo
                encoded = KC14_SelectedNames(names, selected)
            Case Else
                Exit Function
        End Select
    End If
    KC14_SaveSheets names, encoded
    KC14_LoadSelection = True
End Function

' Режим 3 -> 5: выбрать листы заново.
Private Sub KC14_SelectSheets()
    Dim names As Variant, selected() As Boolean, encoded As String
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    KC14_ReadSavedSheets names, selected
    If Not KC14_AskSheets(names, selected, "Листы для постановки и перенумерации баллонов.", encoded) Then Exit Sub
    KC14_SaveSheets names, encoded
    MsgBox "Листы сохранены: " & IIf(encoded = "*", "все", Replace(encoded, vbLf, ", ")) & ".", vbInformation
End Sub

Private Function KC14_LoadTypes() As Boolean
    processDimensions = False: processGtols = False: processWelds = False: processNotes = True
    ParseCustomTypes KC14_Setting(swModel.GetPathName, "Types", "14,13,38")
    KC14_LoadTypes = (processDimensions Or processGtols Or processWelds)
End Function

Private Sub KC14_Settings()
    Dim names As Variant, selected() As Boolean, value As String, list As String, encoded As String
    Dim i As Long, folder As String, picked As Variant, xl As Object, book As Object, meta As Object
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    KC14_ReadSavedSheets names, selected
    If Not KC14_AskSheets(names, selected, "Настройки: листы для баллонов.", encoded) Then Exit Sub
    If Not SelectAnnotationTypes() Then Exit Sub
    value = ""
    If processDimensions Then value = value & "14,"
    If processGtols Then value = value & "13,"
    If processWelds Then value = value & "38,"
    KC14_SaveSheets names, encoded
    KC14_SaveSetting swModel.GetPathName, "Types", value
    value = InputBox("Папка рабочей книги (пустой ввод — оставить без изменения):", "Настройки: папка", _
        KC14_Setting(swModel.GetPathName, "Folder", CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject").GetParentFolderName(swModel.GetPathName)))
    If Len(value) > 0 Then
        If Not CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject").FolderExists(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3032, , "Папка не найдена."
        KC14_SaveSetting swModel.GetPathName, "Folder", value
    End If
    value = InputBox("После какого листа вставлять ОК (пустой ввод — по умолчанию после титульного):", "Настройки: ОК", _
        KC14_Setting(swModel.GetPathName, "Anchor", ""))
    KC14_SaveSetting swModel.GetPathName, "Anchor", value
    ' Перепривязка предлагается автоматически только при отсутствии связанной книги.
    MsgBox "Настройки сохранены. В обычных запусках повторные вопросы отключены.", vbInformation
End Sub

Private Sub KC14_Advanced()
    Dim value As String
    value = InputBox("1 = Настройки" & vbCrLf & "2 = Импорт прежней полной карты (однократно)" & vbCrLf & _
        "3 = Необязательная копия .xlsx" & vbCrLf & _
        "4 = Пересоздание КЭ швов при следующем заполнении" & vbCrLf & _
        "5 = Листы для баллонов (выбрать заново)" & vbCrLf & _
        "6 = Архивы и резервные копии", "Дополнительные действия", "1")
    Select Case Trim$(value)
        Case "1": KC14_Settings
        Case "2": KC14_Import
        Case "3": KC14_OptionalXlsx
        Case "4": KC22_RequestWeldRebuild
        Case "5": KC14_SelectSheets
        Case "6": KA_Service
        Case "": Exit Sub
        Case Else: MsgBox "Неверный выбор.", vbExclamation
    End Select
End Sub

' Только явные дополнительные команды; обычные режимы новых .xlsx не создают.
Private Sub KC14_Import()
    Dim picker As Object, sourceBook As Object, source As Object, target As Object, raw As Object, dest As Object
    Dim chosen As Variant, data As Variant, rawData As Variant, fixed() As String, mass As Variant, intro() As Variant
    Dim introRows As Long, last As Long, rawLast As Long, i As Long, c As Long, tools As Variant, rng As Object, toolRows As Long
    Dim detail As String, variantEnabled As Boolean, variantText As String
    Dim importRoles() As String, importNames() As String, haveHeader As Boolean
    Dim importPrimary As String, havePrimary As Boolean
    Dim importKD As String, importItem As String, importEquipment As String, haveProduct As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set picker = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
    picker.Visible = True: picker.AutomationSecurity = 3: picker.EnableEvents = False
    chosen = KC32_PickCard(picker, swModel.GetPathName, "Однократно: прежняя полная карта контроля", "*.xlsx;*.xlsm")
    If VarType(chosen) = vbBoolean Then picker.Quit: Exit Sub
    If KC_SamePath(CStr(chosen), KC14_Resolve(swModel.GetPathName, False)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3070, , "Выбрана текущая рабочая книга. Импорт не нужен."
    Set sourceBook = picker.Workbooks.Open(CStr(chosen), 0, True)
    Set source = sourceBook.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    Set raw = sourceBook.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    If Not IsUnifiedCard(source) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3071, , "Нужна полная карта с единым списком v10, а не отдельный редактор."
    last = LastOKContinuationRow(source)
    If last < 7 Or last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3072, , "Некорректное число строк."
    data = source.Range("A7:E" & CStr(last)).Value2
    rawLast = raw.cells(raw.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If rawLast < 2 Or rawLast > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3073, , "Нет исходных параметров чертежа."
    rawData = raw.Range("A1:Z" & CStr(rawLast)).Value2
    If CStr(raw.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "№ поз." Or CStr(raw.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Текст аннотации" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3074, , "Неизвестная структура исходных данных."
    For i = 1 To UBound(data, 1)
        For c = 1 To 5
            If IsError(data(i, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3075, , "В прежней карте есть ошибка Excel. Исправьте её до импорта."
        Next c
    Next i
    GetIntroSource source, fixed, mass, intro, introRows
    KC15_ReadVariants source, variantEnabled, variantText
    haveHeader = KC23_ReadHeader(source, importRoles, importNames)
    havePrimary = KC27_ReadPrimary(source, importPrimary)
    haveProduct = KC29_ReadHeaderData(source, importKD, importItem, importEquipment)
    Set rng = sourceBook.names("ToolsList").RefersToRange
    If rng.Columns.count <> 1 Or rng.rows.count > 10000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3076, , "Некорректный список средств ТО."
    toolRows = rng.rows.count: tools = rng.Value2
    sourceBook.Close False: picker.Quit
    Set sourceBook = Nothing: Set picker = Nothing
    If MsgBox("Заменить содержимое текущей карты прежним файлом? После импорта нужен режим 1. Предыдущая сохранённая версия останется в .ok-service.", vbYesNo + vbQuestion) <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
    If Not KC_PrepareIntroConfiguration(False) Then Exit Sub
    Set target = gWorkBook.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    gWorkBook.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2 = "PENDING"
    gWorkBook.Save
    last = LastOKContinuationRow(target)
    If last >= 7 Then target.Range("A7:E" & CStr(last)).Clear
    target.Range("A7").Resize(UBound(data, 1), 5).Value2 = data
    KC15_SetIntroArea target, 6 + introRows
    KC23_Field(target, "OK_VariantEnabled", "I16").Value2 = variantEnabled
    KC23_Field(target, "OK_VariantText", "I17").Value2 = variantText
    WriteFixedCardBlock target, fixed, mass
    If havePrimary Then target.Range("L6").Value2 = importPrimary
    If haveProduct Then
        target.Range("L4").Value2 = importKD
        target.Range("L5").Value2 = importItem
        target.Range("I18").Value2 = importEquipment
    End If
    If haveHeader Then
        For i = 1 To 5
            target.cells(3 + i, 8).Value2 = importRoles(i)
            target.cells(3 + i, 9).Value2 = importNames(i)
        Next i
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    Set dest = gWorkBook.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If dest Is Nothing Then
        Set dest = gWorkBook.Worksheets.Add(After:=target)
        dest.name = "Данные из чертежа"
    End If
    dest.cells.Clear: dest.Range("A1").Resize(rawLast, 26).Value2 = rawData
    dest.Visible = True
    Set dest = gWorkBook.Worksheets("СписокТО")
    dest.Columns("A").Clear
    dest.Range("A1").Resize(toolRows, 1).Value2 = tools
    gWorkBook.names("ToolsList").Delete
    gWorkBook.names.Add name:="ToolsList", RefersTo:="='СписокТО'!$A$1:$A$" & CStr(toolRows)
    ApplyCardOperationLayout target
    KC_RunEditorCommand gWorkApp, gWorkBook, "OK_RefreshButtons"
    gWorkBook.Save
    MsgBox "Импорт выполнен. Запустите режим 1 для согласования баллонов и карты.", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    detail = "Импорт: " & Err.number & " | " & Err.Description
    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, detail
    MsgBox detail & vbCrLf & "Книги оставлены открытыми. При частичном импорте восстановите предыдущую версию из .ok-service.", vbCritical
End Sub

Private Sub KC14_OptionalXlsx()
    Dim path As String, app As Object, book As Object, copyBook As Object, ws As Object, button As Object
    Dim picked As Variant, detail As String, rng As Object, toolRows As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    path = KC14_Resolve(swModel.GetPathName)
    KC14_Open app, book, path, False
    If CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2) <> "READY" Or Not book.saved Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3077, , "Сначала обновите и сохраните рабочую карту режимом 1."
    If Not KC_SamePath(CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B2").Value2), swModel.GetPathName) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3078, , "Выбрана книга другого чертежа."
    picked = app.GetSaveAsFilename("Копия карты.xlsx", "Excel (*.xlsx),*.xlsx", , "Необязательная копия без кнопок")
    If VarType(picked) = vbBoolean Then Exit Sub
    If LCase$(Right$(CStr(picked), 5)) <> ".xlsx" Then picked = CStr(picked) & ".xlsx"
    If CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject").FileExists(CStr(picked)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3079, , "Файл существует. Выберите новое имя."
    Set rng = book.names("ToolsList").RefersToRange: toolRows = rng.rows.count
    Set copyBook = app.Workbooks.Add
    Set ws = copyBook.Worksheets(1): ws.name = "Карта контроля"
    book.Worksheets("Карта контроля").UsedRange.Copy ws.Range("A1")
    If KC23_NewLayout(book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")) Then KC23_RegisterNames ws
    KC15_SetIntroArea ws, KC15_IntroLast(book.Worksheets("Карта контроля"))
    For Each button In ws.Buttons: button.Delete: Next button
    For Each button In ws.CheckBoxes: button.Delete: Next button
    ws.Columns("A").ColumnWidth = 12: ws.Columns("B").ColumnWidth = 65
    ws.Columns("C").ColumnWidth = 45: ws.Columns("D:E").ColumnWidth = 13
    ws.Columns("H").ColumnWidth = 26: ws.Columns("I").ColumnWidth = 75
    ws.Columns("K").ColumnWidth = 26: ws.Columns("L").ColumnWidth = 60
    Set ws = copyBook.Worksheets.Add: ws.name = "Данные из чертежа"
    book.Worksheets(ws.name).UsedRange.Copy ws.Range("A1")
    ws.Visible = True
    Set ws = copyBook.Worksheets.Add: ws.name = "СписокТО"
    ws.Range("A1").Resize(toolRows, 1).Value2 = rng.Value2
    ws.Visible = False
    On Error Resume Next
    copyBook.names("ToolsList").Delete
    On Error GoTo Failed
    copyBook.names.Add name:="ToolsList", RefersTo:="='СписокТО'!$A$1:$A$" & CStr(toolRows)
    Set ws = copyBook.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    ApplyCardOperationLayout ws
    ws.Activate
    copyBook.SaveAs CStr(picked), 51
    MsgBox "Дополнительная копия сохранена. Рабочей остаётся исходная .xlsm.", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    detail = "Дополнительная копия: " & Err.number & " | " & Err.Description
    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, detail
    MsgBox detail & vbCrLf & "Рабочая книга не закрывалась.", vbCritical
End Sub



Private Function KC15_IntroLast(ByVal ws As Object) As Long
    Dim nm As Object, area As Object, r As Long, c As Long, last As Long, key As String
    Dim tailStart As Long, hasRegular As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    Set nm = ws.Parent.names("OK_IntroArea")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Not nm Is Nothing Then
        If nm.RefersTo = "=0" Then KC15_IntroLast = 6: Exit Function
        On Error GoTo Invalid
        Set area = nm.RefersToRange
        If Not area.Parent Is ws Then GoTo Invalid
        If area.Areas.count <> 1 Or area.row <> 7 Or area.Column <> 1 Or area.Columns.count <> 5 Then GoTo Invalid
        last = area.row + area.rows.count - 1
        If last > 100000 Then GoTo Invalid
        KC15_IntroLast = last
        Exit Function
    End If
    ' Безопасная миграция старой книги: только буквальные ОК1-... до первого параметра.
    last = 6
    For c = 1 To 5
        r = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, c).End(-4162).row
        If r > last Then last = r
    Next c
    KC15_IntroLast = 6
    For r = 7 To last
        If IsError(ws.cells(r, 1).Value2) Then GoTo Invalid
        key = Trim$(CStr(ws.cells(r, 1).Value2))
        If Len(key) > 0 Then
            If StrComp(Left$(key, 4), "ОК1-", vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
                hasRegular = True
                Exit For
            End If
            tailStart = r
        End If
        KC15_IntroLast = r
    Next r
    If Not hasRegular And tailStart > 0 Then
        If KC15_IntroLast < tailStart + 3 Then KC15_IntroLast = tailStart + 3
    End If
    Exit Function
Invalid:
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3150, "KC15_IntroLast", "Повреждён диапазон вводных OK_IntroArea. Не меняйте его вручную; восстановите книгу из резервной копии."
End Function


Private Function KC15_IsIntroRow(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal row As Long) As Boolean
    KC15_IsIntroRow = (row >= 7 And row <= KC15_IntroLast(ws))
End Function


Private Sub KC15_SetIntroArea(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal last As Long)
    Dim refers As String
    If last < 6 Or last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3151, , "Неверная граница вводных пунктов."
    refers = "=0"
    If last >= 7 Then refers = "='" & Replace(ws.name, "'", "''") & "'!$A$7:$E$" & CStr(last)
    On Error Resume Next
    ws.Parent.names("OK_IntroArea").Delete
    On Error GoTo 0
    ws.Parent.names.Add name:="OK_IntroArea", RefersTo:=refers
End Sub


Private Function KC15_PercentText(ByVal value As Variant) As String
    Dim text As String, n As Double, explicitPercent As Boolean
    If IsError(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3152, , "Ошибка Excel в столбце Объём и ПК."
    text = Trim$(Replace(CStr(value), ChrW(160), " "))
    If Len(text) = 0 Then Exit Function
    explicitPercent = (InStr(text, "%") > 0)
    text = Replace(Replace(text, "%", ""), " ", "")
    If IsNumeric(text) Then
        n = CDbl(text)
        If Not explicitPercent And n >= 0 And n <= 1 Then n = n * 100#
        KC15_PercentText = CStr(n) & "%"
    Else
        KC15_PercentText = Trim$(CStr(value))
    End If
End Function


Private Sub KC15_ReadVariants(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef enabled As Boolean, ByRef text As String)
    Dim flag As String
    flag = UCase$(KC23_CellText(KC23_Field(ws, "OK_VariantEnabled", "I16")))
    enabled = (flag = "TRUE" Or flag = "ИСТИНА" Or flag = "1" Or flag = "ДА")
    If Not enabled And flag <> "" And flag <> "FALSE" And flag <> "ЛОЖЬ" And flag <> "0" And flag <> "НЕТ" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3155, , "Неизвестное значение флажка исполнений."
    text = KC23_CellText(KC23_Field(ws, "OK_VariantText", "I17"))
    If enabled And Len(text) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3154, , "Включена таблица исполнений: заполните текст в новой панели (I12)."
End Sub

' v15.1 PERF: данные собираются в памяти; Excel оформляется диапазонами.
Private Function KCPerf_OldParameter(ByVal index As Long) As Collection
    Dim oldIndex As Long, key As String
    If Not gUsePreparedMerge Then Exit Function
    oldIndex = gPreparedMatchIdx(index)
    If oldIndex <= 0 Then Exit Function
    key = CStr(gPreparedOldData(oldIndex).posNumber)
    If gOldRows Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If gOldRows.Exists(key) Then Set KCPerf_OldParameter = gOldRows(key)
End Function

Private Function KCPerf_WriteParameter(ByRef buffer As Variant, ByVal first As Long, ByVal index As Long) As Long
    Dim saved As Collection, data As Variant, r As Long, c As Long, length As Long, pd As PositionData
    Dim extras As Collection
    pd = positionsData(index)
    Set saved = KCPerf_OldParameter(index)
    length = 4
    If Not saved Is Nothing Then
        For r = 1 To saved.count
            data = saved(r)
            For c = 1 To 4
                buffer(first + r - 1, c + 1) = data(1, c)
            Next c
        Next r
        buffer(first, 2) = KC_NumberedOperationText(CStr(buffer(first, 2)), pd.number)
        buffer(first, 2) = KC50_RenameViewInText(CStr(buffer(first, 2)), index)
        If saved.count > length Then length = saved.count
        If KC28_B("Tools.AddToSavedBlocks") Then KCPerf_AddMissingTools buffer, first, length, pd
    Else
        buffer(first, 2) = BuildParamText(pd)
        buffer(first, 3) = pd.ToolName
        ' v16.2: до трёх дополнительных средств (строки 2..4 блока).
        Set extras = KC28_ExtraTools(pd)
        For r = 1 To extras.count
            buffer(first + r, 3) = extras(r)
        Next r
        buffer(first, 4) = KC28_S("Operations.DefaultVolume")
        Select Case pd.AnnotationType
            Case swSelDIMENSIONS
                If pd.NominalValue <= KC28_N("Operations.ShortDimensionLimit") Then buffer(first, 5) = KC28_N("Operations.DimensionShortTime") Else buffer(first, 5) = KC28_N("Operations.DimensionLongTime")
            Case swSelGTOLS: buffer(first, 5) = KC28_N("Operations.GtolTime")
            Case Else: buffer(first, 5) = KC28_N("Operations.OtherTime")
        End Select
    End If
    buffer(first, 1) = Format$(pd.number, "00")
    KCPerf_WriteParameter = first + length
End Function

' Только известный автоматически сформированный формат; пользовательский текст сохраняется.
Private Function KC_NormalizeStampLocation(ByVal text As String) As String
    Dim re As Object, matches As Object, viewText As String, prefix As String
    KC_NormalizeStampLocation = text
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True: re.Global = False
    re.Pattern = "^(\s*[0-9]+\.\s*)?Клеймить ударным способом\.\s*Место клеймения:\s*(.+?)\s*\(лист\s+КЭ[^)]*\)\.\s*$"
    Set matches = re.Execute(text)
    If matches.count <> 1 Then Exit Function
    prefix = CStr(matches(0).SubMatches(0))
    viewText = Trim$(CStr(matches(0).SubMatches(1)))
    If Len(viewText) = 0 Then Exit Function
    KC_NormalizeStampLocation = prefix & "Клеймить ударным способом. " & viewText & "."
End Function

Private Function KCPerf_BuildCard(ByRef buffer As Variant) As Long
    Dim total As Long, index As Long, length As Long, r As Long, c As Long, at As Long, n As Long
    Dim saved As Collection, stamp As Collection, data As Variant, maxNumber As Long
    total = gIntroRows + 4 + 1
    maxNumber = gIntroFirstBalloon - 1
    If Not gOldRows Is Nothing Then
        If gOldRows.Exists("STAMP") Then Set stamp = gOldRows("STAMP")
    End If
    If Not stamp Is Nothing Then
        If stamp.count > 4 Then total = total + stamp.count - 4
    End If
    For index = 1 To positionsCount
        Set saved = KCPerf_OldParameter(index)
        length = 4
        If Not saved Is Nothing Then
            If saved.count > length Then length = saved.count
        End If
        total = total + length
        If positionsData(index).number > maxNumber Then maxNumber = positionsData(index).number
    Next index
    If total > 1048570 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3300, , "Карта превышает число строк Excel."
    ReDim buffer(1 To total, 1 To 5)
    For r = 1 To gIntroRows
        For c = 2 To 5: buffer(r, c) = gIntroData(r, c): Next c
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(gIntroData(r, 1)))) > 0 Then
            n = n + 1: buffer(r, 1) = Format$(n, "00")
        End If
    Next r
    at = gIntroRows + 1
    For index = 1 To positionsCount
        at = KCPerf_WriteParameter(buffer, at, index)
    Next index
    length = 4
    If Not stamp Is Nothing Then
        For r = 1 To stamp.count
            data = stamp(r)
            For c = 1 To 4: buffer(at + r - 1, c + 1) = data(1, c): Next c
        Next r
        buffer(at, 2) = KC_NormalizeStampLocation(CStr(buffer(at, 2)))
        buffer(at, 2) = KC41_StampText(CStr(buffer(at, 2)), maxNumber + 1, gKC_StampView)
        If stamp.count > length Then length = stamp.count
    Else
        buffer(at, 2) = CStr(maxNumber + 1) & ". Клеймить ударным способом. " & gKC_StampView & "."
        buffer(at, 3) = "Клеймо ОТК"
        buffer(at + 1, 3) = "Рулетка Р5У2П ГОСТ 7502-98"
        buffer(at + 2, 3) = "Молоток 7850-0103 ГОСТ 2310-77"
        buffer(at, 4) = "100%": buffer(at, 5) = 1.5
    End If
    buffer(at, 1) = Format$(gIntroFirstBalloon + positionsCount, "00")
    at = at + length
    If at <> total Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3301, , "Внутренняя ошибка размера буфера карты."
    For r = 1 To total - 1
        buffer(r, 4) = KC15_PercentText(buffer(r, 4))
    Next r
    buffer(at, 1) = "ОК": buffer(at, 2) = "Операционная карта контроля"
    KCPerf_BuildCard = total
End Function

Private Sub KCPerf_Layout(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef data As Variant, ByVal firstRow As Long, ByVal length As Long)
    Dim area As Object, r As Long, first As Long, lastRow As Long
    If length < 1 Then Exit Sub
    lastRow = firstRow + length - 1
    Set area = ws.Range("A" & CStr(firstRow) & ":E" & CStr(lastRow))
    area.Font.Bold = False: area.Interior.Pattern = -4142: area.VerticalAlignment = -4160
    ws.Range("B" & CStr(firstRow) & ":C" & CStr(lastRow)).WrapText = True
    With ws.Range("A" & CStr(firstRow) & ":A" & CStr(lastRow))
        .NumberFormat = "@": .HorizontalAlignment = -4108: .VerticalAlignment = -4108
    End With
    ws.Range("D" & CStr(firstRow) & ":D" & CStr(lastRow)).NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("D" & CStr(firstRow) & ":E" & CStr(lastRow)).HorizontalAlignment = -4108
    ws.rows(CStr(firstRow) & ":" & CStr(lastRow)).RowHeight = 22
    With ws.Range("C" & CStr(firstRow) & ":C" & CStr(lastRow)).Validation
        .Delete
        .Add Type:=3, AlertStyle:=2, Operator:=1, Formula1:="=ToolsList"
        .IgnoreBlank = True: .InCellDropdown = True: .ShowError = True
    End With
    For r = 1 To length
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(data(r, 1)))) > 0 Then
            If first > 0 Then KCPerf_GroupBorders ws, firstRow + first - 1, firstRow + r - 2
            first = r
            ws.rows(firstRow + r - 1).RowHeight = 30
        End If
    Next r
    If first > 0 Then KCPerf_GroupBorders ws, firstRow + first - 1, lastRow
End Sub

Private Sub KCPerf_GroupBorders(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal first As Long, ByVal last As Long)
    Dim block As Object
    Set block = ws.Range("A" & CStr(first) & ":E" & CStr(last))
    block.Borders(9).LineStyle = 1: block.Borders(9).Weight = 2
    If last > first Then
        block.Borders(12).LineStyle = 2: block.Borders(12).Weight = 1
        block.Borders(12).Color = RGB(180, 180, 180)
    End If
End Sub

Private Function KCPerf_EndRow(ByRef data As Variant, ByVal r As Long) As Boolean
    Dim a As String, b As String, c As String
    If IsError(data(r, 1)) Or IsError(data(r, 2)) Or IsError(data(r, 3)) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3302, , "Ошибка Excel в строке карты " & CStr(r + 6)
    a = UCase$(Trim$(CStr(data(r, 1))))
    b = LCase$(Trim$(CStr(data(r, 2)))): c = LCase$(Trim$(CStr(data(r, 3))))
    KCPerf_EndRow = (a = "ОК" Or a = "OK" Or InStr(1, b & " " & c, "операционная карта контроля", vbTextCompare) > 0)
End Function

Private Sub KCPerf_RestoreExcel(ByVal app As Object, ByVal calculation As Long, ByVal events As Boolean, ByVal screen As Boolean)
    On Error Resume Next
    app.calculation = calculation
    app.EnableEvents = events
    app.ScreenUpdating = screen
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function KCPerf_Seconds(ByVal started As Double) As String
    Dim seconds As Double
    seconds = Timer - started
    If seconds < 0 Then seconds = seconds + 86400#
    KCPerf_Seconds = Format$(seconds, "0.000") & " с"
End Function

Private Sub KC46_Trace(ByVal text As String)
    ' v16.5.14: буфер в памяти, одна запись в журнал папки карты (нет записи в TEMP и Debug).
    On Error Resume Next
    KC53_Add text
End Sub

Private Sub KC46_Mark(ByRef started As Double, ByVal phase As String)
    KC46_Trace phase & ": " & KCPerf_Seconds(started)
    started = Timer
End Sub

Private Function KCPerf_Text(ByVal value As Variant, ByVal row As Long, ByVal col As Long) As String
    If IsError(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2500, , "Ошибка Excel в карте: строка " & CStr(row) & ", колонка " & CStr(col)
    If Not IsEmpty(value) Then KCPerf_Text = Trim$(CStr(value))
End Function


' Подтверждение принадлежит документу, не листу и не конфигурации.
' Значение READY записывается только после успешной перенумерации и экспорта.
Private Sub KC20_WriteIntroSync(ByVal model As Object, ByVal value As String)
    Dim manager As Object, raw As String, resolved As String, result As Long
    Set manager = model.Extension.CustomPropertyManager("")
    If manager Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3220, "KC20_WriteIntroSync", "Нет доступа к служебному свойству чертежа."
    ' 30 = swCustomInfoText; 2 = swCustomPropertyReplaceValue.
    result = manager.Add3("KC_IntroSync_v1", 30, value, 2)
    manager.Get2 "KC_IntroSync_v1", raw, resolved
    If raw <> value Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3221, "KC20_WriteIntroSync", _
        "Не записано подтверждение вводных пунктов. Результат Add3: " & CStr(result) & ". Повторите режим 1."
    model.SetSaveFlag
End Sub

Private Sub KC20_ConfirmIntroSync(ByVal model As Object, ByVal count As Long, ByVal firstBalloon As Long)
    If count < 0 Or count > 1000 Or firstBalloon <> count + 1 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3222, "KC20_ConfirmIntroSync", "Неверные данные подтверждения вводных пунктов."
    KC20_WriteIntroSync model, "READY|" & CStr(count) & "|" & CStr(firstBalloon) & "|" & model.GetPathName
End Sub


Private Sub KC22_RequestWeldRebuild()
    Dim answer As VbMsgBoxResult, enabled As Boolean
    enabled = (KC14_Setting(swModel.GetPathName, "ForceKEWeldRebuild", "0") = "1")
    answer = MsgBox("Принудительная пересборка КЭ швов при следующем заполнении: " & IIf(enabled, "включена", "выключена") & "." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Да — включить однократную пересборку вторым макросом." & vbCrLf & _
        "Нет — отменить запрос пересборки." & vbCrLf & _
        "Отмена — ничего не менять." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "При пересборке прежние листы швов будут архивированы вместе с ручным заполнением.", _
        vbYesNoCancel + vbQuestion, "КЭ швов: принудительная пересборка")
    If answer = vbCancel Then Exit Sub
    If answer = vbYes Then
        KC14_SaveSetting swModel.GetPathName, "ForceKEWeldRebuild", "1"
        MsgBox "Запрос сохранён. Запустите второй макрос. Запрос снимется после успешного создания и проверки комплекта.", vbInformation
    Else
        KC14_SaveSetting swModel.GetPathName, "ForceKEWeldRebuild", "0"
        MsgBox "Запрос принудительной пересборки отменён.", vbInformation
    End If
End Sub






' Общая разметка v16: A:E остаётся на прежних строках; справа новая панель реквизитов.
Private Function KC23_NewLayout(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    Dim nm As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set nm = ws.Parent.names("OK_HeaderLayout")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If nm Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    KC23_NewLayout = (nm.RefersTo = "=""OK_HEADER_V16""")
End Function

Private Function KC23_Field(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal legacyAddress As String) As Object
    Dim nm As Object, area As Object
    If KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then
        On Error Resume Next
        Set nm = ws.Parent.names(fieldName)
        On Error GoTo 0
        If nm Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3300, , "Не найден диапазон " & fieldName & ". Обновите Excel-модуль и выполните OK_UpdateEditor."
        Set area = nm.RefersToRange
        If Not area.Parent Is ws Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3301, , "Диапазон " & fieldName & " относится к другому листу."
        Set KC23_Field = area
    Else
        Set KC23_Field = ws.Range(legacyAddress)
    End If
End Function

Private Function KC23_CellText(ByVal area As Object) As String
    Dim value As Variant
    value = area.cells(1, 1).Value2
    If IsError(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3302, , "Ошибка Excel в " & area.address(False, False) & "."
    KC23_CellText = Trim$(CStr(value))
End Function

Private Function KC23_FixedCell(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal index As Long) As Object
    Dim area As Object
    Set area = KC23_Field(ws, "OK_FixedRows", "I7:I9")
    If area.rows.count <> 3 Or area.Columns.count <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3303, , "Повреждён диапазон OK_FixedRows."
    Set KC23_FixedCell = area.cells(index + 1, 1)
End Function

Private Sub KC23_Name(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal addressText As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    ws.Parent.names(fieldName).Delete
    On Error GoTo 0
    ws.Parent.names.Add name:=fieldName, RefersTo:="='" & Replace(ws.name, "'", "''") & "'!" & ws.Range(addressText).address
End Sub

Private Sub KC23_RegisterNames(ByVal ws As Object)
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_HeaderRoles", "H4:H8"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_HeaderNames", "I4:I8"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_VariantEnabled", "I11"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_VariantText", "I12"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_Mass", "I17"
    If KC23_CellText(ws.Range("H18")) = "Первичное применение" Then KC23_Name ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "I18"
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then KC23_Name ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "L6"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_FixedRows", "I20:I22"
    If KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K3")) = "Реквизиты изделия" Then
        KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductData", "L4:L6"
        KC23_Name ws, "OK_KDNumber", "L4"
        KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductName", "L5"
        If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
            KC23_Name ws, "OK_Equipment", "I18"
        Else
            KC23_Name ws, "OK_Equipment", "L6"
        End If
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    ws.Parent.names("OK_HeaderLayout").Delete
    On Error GoTo 0
    ws.Parent.names.Add name:="OK_HeaderLayout", RefersTo:="=""OK_HEADER_V16"""
End Sub

Private Sub KC23_Band(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal addressText As String, ByVal label As String)
    With ws.Range(addressText)
        .Merge
        .cells(1, 1).Value2 = label
        .Font.Bold = True
        .Interior.Color = RGB(217, 229, 241)
        .Borders.LineStyle = 1
        .WrapText = True
    End With
End Sub

Private Sub KC23_EnsureLayout(ByVal ws As Object)
    Dim mass As Variant, fixedRows As Variant, flag As Variant, variantText As Variant, summary As Variant
    Dim roles As Variant, i As Long, box As Object
    If Not KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then
        If KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K3")) = "Реквизиты изделия" Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3407, , "Повреждена метка панели реквизитов. Восстановите именованный диапазон OK_HeaderLayout из резервной копии; данные не очищались."
        ' До первого Clear сохраняем все старые поля, включая выключенный текст исполнений.
        mass = ws.Range("I3").Value2
        fixedRows = ws.Range("I7:I9").Value2
        flag = ws.Range("I16").Value2
        variantText = ws.Range("I17").Value2
        summary = ws.Range("I11:I14").Value2
        If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("H22:I29")) > 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3304, , "В H22:I29 есть посторонние данные. Перенесите их до обновления панели реквизитов."
        On Error Resume Next
        ws.CheckBoxes("okVariants").Delete
        On Error GoTo 0
        ws.Range("H3:I29").UnMerge
        ws.Range("H3:I29").Clear
        KC23_RegisterNames ws
        roles = Array("Разраб.", "Пров.", "Согласовано", "Нач. ЛТК", "Н. контр.")
        For i = 0 To 4
            ws.cells(4 + i, 8).Value2 = roles(i)
        Next i
        ws.Range("I17").Value2 = mass
        ws.Range("I20:I22").Value2 = fixedRows
        If IsEmpty(flag) Or Len(CStr(flag)) = 0 Then flag = False
        ws.Range("I11").Value2 = flag
        ws.Range("I12").Value2 = variantText
        ws.Range("I24:I27").Value2 = summary
    End If
    KC29_EnsureHeaderData ws
    ws.Columns("H").ColumnWidth = 26
    ws.Columns("I").ColumnWidth = 75
    KC23_Band ws, "H3:I3", "Шапка ОК1 / КЭ1 — должность и фамилия"
    With ws.Range("H4:I8")
        .NumberFormat = "@"
        .Font.name = "GOST type A"
        .Font.size = 14
        .Font.Italic = True
        .Font.Bold = False
        .Borders.LineStyle = 1
        .VerticalAlignment = -4108
        .WrapText = True
    End With
    ws.Range("I4:I8").Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
    For i = 4 To 8
        If ws.rows(i).RowHeight < 22 Then ws.rows(i).RowHeight = 22
    Next i
    KC23_Band ws, "H10:I10", "Исполнения — таблица в рамках ОК / КЭ"
    ws.Range("I11").NumberFormat = ";;;"
    ws.Range("H12").Value2 = "Текст исполнений"
    With ws.Range("I12:I14")
        .Merge
        .NumberFormat = "@"
        .WrapText = True
        .VerticalAlignment = -4160
        .Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
        .BorderAround LineStyle:=1, Weight:=2
    End With
    With ws.Range("H15:I15")
        .Merge
        .Value2 = "Флажок выключен — собственная таблица исполнений удаляется при заполнении."
        .WrapText = True
        .Font.size = 9
    End With
    ws.Range("H17").Value2 = "Масса детали, кг"
    With ws.Range("I17")
        .NumberFormat = "0.###"
        .Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
        .Font.Bold = True
        .BorderAround LineStyle:=1, Weight:=4, Color:=RGB(31, 63, 94)
    End With
    ws.Range("H19").Value2 = "Строка"
    ws.Range("I19").Value2 = "Первые три строки ОК1"
    ws.Range("H19:I19").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("H19:I19").Interior.Color = RGB(217, 229, 241)
    ws.Range("H19:I22").Borders.LineStyle = 1
    ws.Range("H19:I22").WrapText = True
    ws.Range("H20:I22").NumberFormat = "@"
    For i = 0 To 2: ws.cells(20 + i, 8).Value2 = Format$(i + 1, "00"): Next i
    ws.Range("H24").Value2 = "Заполненных пунктов"
    ws.Range("H25").Value2 = "Первый баллон"
    ws.Range("H26").Value2 = "Пустых блоков"
    ws.Range("H27").Value2 = "Нет начального текста"
    ws.Range("H24:I27").Borders.LineStyle = 1
    ws.Range("I24:I27").NumberFormat = "0"
    ws.Range("H20:I22").rows.AutoFit
    ws.Range("H29").Value2 = "ОК: единый список v10"
    ws.Range("H29").Font.size = 9
    ws.Range("H29").Font.Color = RGB(110, 110, 110)
End Sub
