Private Function KC23_ReadHeader(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef roles() As String, ByRef surnames() As String) As Boolean
    Dim roleArea As Object, nameArea As Object, i As Long
    ReDim roles(1 To 5): ReDim surnames(1 To 5)
    If Not KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then Exit Function
    Set roleArea = KC23_Field(ws, "OK_HeaderRoles", "H4:H8")
    Set nameArea = KC23_Field(ws, "OK_HeaderNames", "I4:I8")
    If roleArea.rows.count <> 5 Or roleArea.Columns.count <> 1 Or nameArea.rows.count <> 5 Or nameArea.Columns.count <> 1 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3305, , "Шапка должна содержать пять должностей и пять фамилий."
    For i = 1 To 5
        roles(i) = KC23_CellText(roleArea.cells(i, 1))
        surnames(i) = KC23_CellText(nameArea.cells(i, 1))
    Next i
    KC23_ReadHeader = True
End Function

Private Sub KC23_CopyPanel(ByVal source As Object, ByVal target As Object)
    Dim roles() As String, surnames() As String, haveHeader As Boolean
    Dim flag As Variant, text As Variant, i As Long
    Dim primaryText As String, havePrimary As Boolean
    Dim kd As String, itemName As String, equipment As String, haveProduct As Boolean
    If source Is Nothing Then
        KC23_EnsureLayout target
        Exit Sub
    End If
    havePrimary = KC27_ReadPrimary(source, primaryText)
    haveProduct = KC29_ReadHeaderData(source, kd, itemName, equipment)
    haveHeader = KC23_ReadHeader(source, roles, surnames)
    flag = KC23_Field(source, "OK_VariantEnabled", "I16").Value2
    text = KC23_Field(source, "OK_VariantText", "I17").Value2
    KC23_EnsureLayout target
    If havePrimary Then target.Range("L6").Value2 = primaryText
    If haveProduct Then
        target.Range("L4").Value2 = kd
        target.Range("L5").Value2 = itemName
        target.Range("I18").Value2 = equipment
    End If
    KC23_Field(target, "OK_VariantEnabled", "I16").Value2 = flag
    KC23_Field(target, "OK_VariantText", "I17").Value2 = text
    If haveHeader Then
        For i = 1 To 5
            target.cells(3 + i, 8).Value2 = roles(i)
            target.cells(3 + i, 9).Value2 = surnames(i)
        Next i
    End If
End Sub


' v15.17: проверяем именно GetName2 — это имя макрос записывает в карту.
' Подпись на поле чертежа может отличаться от имени в дереве FeatureManager.
Private Function KC26_IsAutomaticViewName(ByVal viewName As String) As Boolean
    Dim text As String
    text = LCase$(RemoveViewScale(viewName))
    text = Replace(text, "ё", "е")
    text = Replace(text, " ", "")
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(160), "")
    text = Replace(text, vbTab, "")
    KC26_IsAutomaticViewName = (text = "чертежныйвид" Or text = "drawingview" Or _
        text Like "чертежныйвид#*" Or text Like "drawingview#*")
End Function

Private Sub KC26_AddViewWarning(ByRef pages As Collection, ByRef currentPage As String, ByVal warningLine As String)
    If Len(currentPage) > 0 And Len(currentPage) + Len(warningLine) > 600 Then
        pages.Add currentPage
        currentPage = ""
    End If
    currentPage = currentPage & warningLine & vbCrLf
End Sub

Private Function KC26_CheckViewNames(ByVal sheetNames As Variant, ByRef selected() As Boolean) As Boolean
    Dim originalSheet As String, sheetName As String, viewName As String
    Dim sheet As Object, view As Object, pages As Collection
    Dim si As Long, pi As Long, found As Long, currentPage As String, detail As String
    Dim phase As String, message As String
    Set pages = New Collection
    On Error GoTo Failed
    originalSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    For si = LBound(sheetNames) To UBound(sheetNames)
        If selected(si) Then
            sheetName = CStr(sheetNames(si))
            phase = "Активация листа"
            If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(sheetName) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3260, , "Не удалось активировать лист."
            Set sheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
            If StrComp(CStr(sheet.GetName), sheetName, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3261, , "Активирован другой лист."
            phase = "Чтение видов"
            Set view = swDraw.GetFirstView
            If view Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3262, , "Не получен вид листа."
            ' Первый IView — служебный вид самого листа, его не проверяем.
            Set view = view.GetNextView
            Do While Not view Is Nothing
                If KC35_ViewTouchesWorkArea(view, sheet) Then
                viewName = ""
                phase = "Чтение имени вида"
                viewName = CStr(view.GetName2)
                If Len(Trim$(viewName)) = 0 Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3263, , "Получено пустое имя вида."
                If KC26_IsAutomaticViewName(viewName) Then
                    found = found + 1
                    KC26_AddViewWarning pages, currentPage, _
                        "Лист «" & sheetName & "» — «" & viewName & "»"
                End If
                End If
                phase = "Переход к следующему виду"
                Set view = view.GetNextView
            Loop
        End If
    Next si
    phase = "Восстановление исходного листа"
    If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(originalSheet) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3264, , "Не удалось восстановить исходный лист."
    Set sheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    If StrComp(CStr(sheet.GetName), originalSheet, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3265, , "После проверки активен другой лист."
    If found = 0 Then
        KC26_CheckViewNames = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    If Len(currentPage) > 0 Then pages.Add currentPage
    ' Длинный список не обрезаем. Отмена на любой странице останавливает режим 1.
    For pi = 1 To pages.count - 1
        message = "Найдены автоматические имена видов в рабочей области: " & CStr(found) & "." & vbCrLf & _
            "Список " & CStr(pi) & " из " & CStr(pages.count) & ":" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            CStr(pages(pi)) & vbCrLf & "ОК — следующая часть. Отмена — остановить."
        If MsgBox(message, vbOKCancel + vbExclamation + vbDefaultButton2 + vbSystemModal, _
            "Карта контроля: имена видов") <> vbOK Then Exit Function
    Next pi
    message = "Найдены автоматические имена видов в рабочей области: " & CStr(found) & "." & vbCrLf & _
        "Список " & CStr(pages.count) & " из " & CStr(pages.count) & ":" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        CStr(pages(pages.count)) & vbCrLf & _
        "Да — продолжить с этими именами." & vbCrLf & _
        "Нет — остановить, переименовать виды вручную и запустить снова." & vbCrLf & _
        "Проверяется имя в дереве, а не отдельная надпись «Вид А»."
    KC26_CheckViewNames = (MsgBox(message, vbYesNo + vbExclamation + vbDefaultButton2 + vbSystemModal, _
        "Карта контроля: продолжить?") = vbYes)
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = "Проверка имён видов не завершена." & vbCrLf & _
        "Лист: " & sheetName & "; вид: " & viewName & vbCrLf & _
        phase & ": " & Err.Description & vbCrLf & _
        "Режим 1 остановлен до работы с баллонами и Excel."
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then swDraw.ActivateSheet originalSheet
    On Error GoTo 0
    MsgBox detail, vbExclamation + vbSystemModal, "Карта контроля: проверка видов"
End Function


' v17: первичное применение хранится только в панели Excel, не в имени файла.





' v18: реквизиты изделия в Excel; имя файла больше не источник шапки.





' Единый снимок настроек на запуск. Неизвестные ключи и пропуски — ошибка.
Private Function KC28_S(ByVal key As String) As String
    If gKC28 Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3450, , "Настройки не загружены."
    If Not gKC28.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3451, , "Нет настройки " & key
    KC28_S = CStr(gKC28(key))
End Function

Private Function KC28_N(ByVal key As String) As Double
    KC28_N = CDbl(gKC28(key))
End Function

Private Function KC28_B(ByVal key As String) As Boolean
    KC28_B = (KC28_N(key) <> 0)
End Function

Private Function KC28_LogOn(ByVal category As String) As Boolean
    KC28_LogOn = True
    If gKC28 Is Nothing Then KC28_LogOn = False: Exit Function
    If gKC28.Exists("Service.LogEnabled") Then
        If Not CBool(gKC28("Service.LogEnabled")) Then KC28_LogOn = False: Exit Function
    End If
    If gKC28.Exists(category) Then KC28_LogOn = CBool(gKC28(category))
End Function

Private Function KC28_Number(ByVal text As String) As Double
    Dim re As Object, value As String
    value = Replace(Trim$(text), ",", ".")
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Pattern = "^[+-]?[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?([Ee][+-]?[0-9]+)?$"
    If Not re.Test(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3452, , "Ожидается число: " & text
    KC28_Number = val(value)
End Function

Private Sub KC28_Def(ByVal defs As Object, ByVal key As String, ByVal kind As String, ByVal minimum As Double, ByVal maximum As Double, Optional ByVal displayScale As Double = 1, Optional ByVal defaultValue As Variant)
    ' v16.2: defaultValue задан -> строку можно не заводить в Excel (или оставить значение пустым).
    Dim d As Variant
    If IsMissing(defaultValue) Then d = Empty Else d = defaultValue
    defs.Add key, Array(kind, minimum, maximum, displayScale, d)
End Sub

Private Sub KC28_Load(ByVal app As Object)
    Dim defs As Object, seen As Object, xl As Object, book As Object, ws As Object, item As Object
    Dim values As Variant, spec As Variant, key As String, value As String, n As Double
    Dim last As Long, r As Long, c As Long, fso As Object, candidate As String, kind As String
    Dim owned As Boolean, errorText As String, defKey As Variant
    Dim schema As String, pageList As Variant, pageName As Variant, keyColumn As Long

    If KC53_SettingsCached(app) Then Exit Sub
    Set gKC28Sizes = Nothing

    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")

    Set defs = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")

    KC28_Def defs, "M1.INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH", "String", 0, 0

    KC28_Def defs, "M1.FRAME_RIGHT", "Double", -10000, 10000, 0.001

    KC28_Def defs, "M1.FRAME_BOTTOM", "Double", -10000, 10000, 0.001

    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE1_Y4", "Double", -10000, 10000

    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE1_X5", "Double", -10000, 10000

    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_X1", "Double", -10000, 10000

    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_H1", "Double", 0.000001, 10000

    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_W3", "Double", 0.000001, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_H3", "Double", 0.000001, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_X4", "Double", -10000, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_Y4", "Double", -10000, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_W4", "Double", 0.000001, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_H4", "Double", 0.000001, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_X5", "Double", -10000, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_Y5", "Double", -10000, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_W5", "Double", 0.000001, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_H5", "Double", 0.000001, 10000

    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_Y6", "Double", -10000, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_W6", "Double", 0.000001, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.OK1_H6", "Double", 0.000001, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_TABLE_X", "Double", -10000, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_TABLE_Y", "Double", -10000, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE", "Long", 1, 10000
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_HDR_ROW_H", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_ROW_DATA_H", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_COL_NUM_W", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_COL_OBZ_W", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_COL_PIC_W", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_COL_SIZE_W", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_PIC_FOLDER", "String", 0, 0
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_PIC_MARGIN", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_PIC_SIDE_GAP", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_PIC_VARIANT_GAP", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "M2.KE_PIC_CAPTION_H", "Double", 0.000001, 10000, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "Codes.CompactPrefixLength", "Long", 1, 20
    KC28_Def defs, "Codes.02190", "String", 0, 0
    KC28_Def defs, "Codes.20102", "String", 0, 0
    KC28_Def defs, "Codes.60102", "String", 0, 0
    KC28_Def defs, "Codes.02102", "String", 0, 0
    KC28_Def defs, "Service.LogEnabled", "Boolean", 0, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Logs.Journal", "Boolean", 0, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Logs.Balloons", "Boolean", 0, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Logs.MarkerConflicts", "Boolean", 0, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Logs.MarkerActions", "Boolean", 0, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Logs.Pictures", "Boolean", 0, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Logs.KeepVisionDiagnostics", "Boolean", 0, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Paths.Reports", "String", 0, 0
    KC28_Def defs, "Paths.VisionRoot", "String", 0, 0
    KC28_Def defs, "Vision.Enabled", "Boolean", 0, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Vision.TimeoutSeconds", "Long", 1, 3600
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.RolesX", "Double", 0.000001, 1, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.NamesX", "Double", 0.000001, 1, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.RolesWidth", "Double", 0.000001, 1, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.NamesWidth", "Double", 0.000001, 1, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.RowHeight", "Double", 0.000001, 1, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.OK1Y", "Double", 0.000001, 1, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.KE1Y", "Double", 0.000001, 1, 0.001
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.Template", "String", 0, 0
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.FontName", "String", 0, 0
    KC28_Def defs, "Sign.FontPoints", "Long", 1, 72
    KC28_Def defs, "Header.EquipmentX", "Double", 0.001, 1000
    KC28_Def defs, "Header.EquipmentY", "Double", 0.001, 1000
    KC28_Def defs, "Header.EquipmentW", "Double", 0.001, 1000
    KC28_Def defs, "Header.EquipmentH", "Double", 0.001, 1000
    KC28_Def defs, "Variant.FontPoints", "Long", 1, 72
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.ShortDimensionLimit", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.DimensionShortTime", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.DimensionLongTime", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.GtolTime", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.OtherTime", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.DefaultVolume", "String", 0, 0
    ' v16.2: необязательные параметры (есть значение по умолчанию).
    KC28_Def defs, "Tools.MaxExtraTools", "Long", 0, 3, 1, 3
    KC28_Def defs, "Tools.AddToSavedBlocks", "Boolean", 0, 1, 1, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Refs.Enabled", "Boolean", 0, 1, 1, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Refs.BorderStyle", "Long", -1, 50, 1, 2
    ' v16.5.3: optional settings; old settings books retain safe defaults.
    KC28_Def defs, "Backup.Subfolder", "String", 0, 0, 1, "Резерв КК"
    KC28_Def defs, "Backup.KeepCompleted", "Long", 0, 1000, 1, 5
    KC28_Def defs, "Archive.HideCompleted", "Boolean", 0, 1, 1, 1
    On Error Resume Next
    Set xl = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then
        For Each item In xl.Workbooks
            If StrComp(item.FullName, candidate, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Set book = item: Exit For
        Next item
    End If
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If book Is Nothing Then
        Set xl = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
        xl.AutomationSecurity = 3: xl.EnableEvents = False
        owned = True
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(candidate, 0, True)
    ElseIf Not book.saved Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3454, , "Сначала сохраните файл настроек."
    End If
    schema = CStr(book.Worksheets("Версия").Range("B1").Value2)
    If schema <> "OK_SETTINGS_V1" And schema <> "OK_SETTINGS_V2" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3455, , "Неподдерживаемая версия настроек."
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): seen.CompareMode = 1
    If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1" Then
        pageList = Array("Параметры")
    Else
        pageList = Array("Файлы и папки", "Баллоны", "Нормы контроля", "Шифры", "Подписи", "Сервис и логи", "Таблицы ОК", "Таблицы КЭ", "Рамки ОК", "Рамки КЭ", "?Свои параметры")
    End If
    For Each pageName In pageList
        If Left$(CStr(pageName), 1) = "?" Then
            ' Необязательный лист: нет его или он пуст — не ошибка.
            If Not KC28_HasSheet(book, Mid$(CStr(pageName), 2)) Then GoTo NextPage
            Set ws = book.Worksheets(Mid$(CStr(pageName), 2))
        Else
            Set ws = book.Worksheets(CStr(pageName))
        End If
        last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, IIf(schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1", 1, 6)).End(-4162).row
        If last < 2 And Left$(CStr(pageName), 1) = "?" Then GoTo NextPage
        If last < 2 Or last > 5000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3456, , "Некорректный лист настроек: " & ws.name
        values = ws.Range("A2:F" & CStr(last)).Value2
        For r = 1 To UBound(values, 1)
            keyColumn = IIf(schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1", 1, 6)
            If IsError(values(r, keyColumn)) Or IsError(values(r, 2)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3457, , "Ошибка Excel: " & ws.name & ", строка " & CStr(r + 1)
            key = Trim$(CStr(values(r, keyColumn))): value = Trim$(CStr(values(r, 2)))
            location = ws.name & "!B" & CStr(r + 1)
            If Len(key) = 0 And Len(value) = 0 Then GoTo NextRow   ' v16.2: пустая строка-разделитель
            If Len(key) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3458, , "Пустой служебный ключ: " & location
            If seen.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3460, , "Параметр повторяется: " & location
            If Not defs.Exists(key) Then
                ' v16.2: свой параметр — не ошибка. Хранится как текст, читается KC28_S("ключ").
                seen.Add key, value
                If Left$(key, 5) <> "Свои." And Left$(key, 5) <> "User." Then _
                    gKC28Warn = gKC28Warn & "• " & key & " (" & location & ")" & vbCrLf
                GoTo NextRow
            End If
            spec = defs(key): kind = CStr(spec(0))
            If Len(value) = 0 And Not IsEmpty(spec(4)) Then GoTo NextRow   ' возьмётся значение по умолчанию
            If Len(value) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3461, , "Заполните значение: " & location
            If kind = "String" Then
                If Left$(key, 6) = "Codes." Then
                    If Len(value) <> 5 Or value Like "*[!0-9]*" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3462, , "Шифр должен содержать пять цифр: " & location
                End If
                seen.Add key, value
            Else
                If kind = "Boolean" Then n = KC32_Flag(value) Else n = KC28_Number(value)
                If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V2" Then n = n * CDbl(spec(3))
                If n < CDbl(spec(1)) Or n > CDbl(spec(2)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3463, , "Значение вне допустимого диапазона: " & location
                If kind = "Long" Or kind = "Boolean" Then
                    If n <> Fix(n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3464, , "Введите целое значение: " & location
                End If
                seen.Add key, n
            End If
NextRow:
        Next r
NextPage:
    Next pageName
    ' Старый файл сохраняет пользовательские значения; новый параметр имеет совместимый default.
    If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1" And Not seen.Exists("Codes.CompactPrefixLength") Then seen.Add "Codes.CompactPrefixLength", 4
    For Each defKey In defs.Keys
        If Not seen.Exists(CStr(defKey)) Then
            spec = defs(CStr(defKey))
            If IsEmpty(spec(4)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3465, , "Пропущен параметр " & CStr(defKey) & ". Восстановите строку из нового файла настроек."
            If CStr(spec(0)) = "String" Then seen.Add CStr(defKey), CStr(spec(4)) Else seen.Add CStr(defKey), CDbl(spec(4))
        End If
    Next defKey
    Set gKC28 = seen
    If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1" Then
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Средства ТО")
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last < 2 Or last > 1000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3466, , "Некорректное число правил средств ТО."
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "Enabled" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: Enabled"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 2).Value2) <> "Type" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: Type"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "StandardContains" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: StandardContains"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 4).Value2) <> "WeldPrefixes" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: WeldPrefixes"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 5).Value2) <> "GdtEquals" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: GdtEquals"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 6).Value2) <> "LegMin" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: LegMin"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 7).Value2) <> "RoundMin" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: RoundMin"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 8).Value2) <> "RoundMax" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: RoundMax"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 9).Value2) <> "RoundedLegs" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: RoundedLegs"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 10).Value2) <> "Parity" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: Parity"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 11).Value2) <> "NominalMin" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: NominalMin"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 12).Value2) <> "NominalMax" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: NominalMax"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 13).Value2) <> "MainTool" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: MainTool"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 14).Value2) <> "ExtraTool" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: ExtraTool"
    gKC28Rules = KC28_Widen(ws.Range("A2:N" & CStr(last)).Value2)
    Else
        ' v16.2: столбцы ищутся по заголовкам; порядок свободный, лишние столбцы (комментарии) игнорируются,
        ' «Прерывистый шов» и «Текст содержит» — необязательные столбцы.
        Set ws = book.Worksheets("Выбор инструментов")
        gKC28Rules = KC28_ReadRuleTable(ws, "Основное средство", "Дополнительное средство")
        If IsEmpty(gKC28Rules) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3466, , "Лист «Выбор инструментов» пуст."
    End If
    KC28_NormalizeRules gKC28Rules
    KC28_ValidateRules
    ' v16.2: необязательный лист — КАЖДАЯ подходящая строка добавляет своё средство.
    If KC28_HasSheet(book, "Добавочные средства") Then
        gKC28Extra = KC28_ReadRuleTable(book.Worksheets("Добавочные средства"), "Добавить средство", "")
        If Not IsEmpty(gKC28Extra) Then
            KC28_NormalizeRules gKC28Extra
            KC28_ValidateRuleTable gKC28Extra, "Добавочные средства", False
        End If
    End If
    KC33_LoadRefs book
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Шаблоны размеров")
    If (CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "Стандарт" And CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "Standard") Or _
       (CStr(ws.cells(1, 2).Value2) <> "Тип шва" And CStr(ws.cells(1, 2).Value2) <> "WeldType") Or _
       (CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Надпись в графе Размеры" And CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Template") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3483, , "Изменены заголовки шаблонов размеров."
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last < 2 Or last > 1000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3484, , "Некорректная таблица шаблонов размеров."
    values = ws.Range("A2:C" & CStr(last)).Value2
    Set gKC28Sizes = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): gKC28Sizes.CompareMode = 1
    For r = 1 To UBound(values, 1)
        For c = 1 To 3
            If IsError(values(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3485, , "Ошибка Excel в шаблоне размеров."
        Next c
        value = Trim$(CStr(values(r, 1)))
        If value <> "14771" And value <> "23518" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3486, , "Standard шаблона: 14771 или 23518."
        key = value & "|" & KC28_WeldKey(CStr(values(r, 2)))
        If Len(KC28_WeldKey(CStr(values(r, 2)))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3487, , "Пустой тип шва в шаблоне размеров."
        If gKC28Sizes.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3488, , "Повтор шаблона размеров " & key
        value = Replace(CStr(values(r, 3)), Chr(13), "")
        value = Replace(value, "|", Chr(10))
        value = Replace(value, "… ", "")
        value = Replace(value, "…", "")
        gKC28Sizes.Add key, value
    Next r
    If owned Then book.Close False: KC53_KeepSpare xl
    Set book = Nothing: Set xl = Nothing
    gKC53SettingsStamp = KC53_FileStamp(gKC28Path)
    INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH = KC28_S("M1.INTRO_TEMPLATE_PATH")
    KC_DISTANCE_MATCH_MAX_M = KC28_N("M1.KC_DISTANCE_MATCH_MAX_M")
    BALLOON_OFFSET_X = KC28_N("M1.BALLOON_OFFSET_X")
    BALLOON_OFFSET_Y = KC28_N("M1.BALLOON_OFFSET_Y")
    FRAME_LEFT = KC28_N("M1.FRAME_LEFT")
    FRAME_RIGHT = KC28_N("M1.FRAME_RIGHT")
    FRAME_TOP = KC28_N("M1.FRAME_TOP")
    FRAME_BOTTOM = KC28_N("M1.FRAME_BOTTOM")
    If Len(gKC28Warn) > 0 And gKC28WarnShown <> gKC28Path Then
        gKC28WarnShown = gKC28Path
        MsgBox "В настройках есть параметры, которые макрос пока не использует (это не ошибка):" & vbCrLf & _
               Left$(gKC28Warn, 900) & vbCrLf & "Чтобы не видеть это сообщение, начинайте свои ключи с «Свои.».", _
               vbInformation, "Настройки"
    End If
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errorText = "Настройки: " & Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If owned Then
        If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
        If Not xl Is Nothing Then xl.Quit
    End If
    Set gKC28 = Nothing
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3469, "KC28_Load", errorText
End Sub

Private Sub KC28_ValidateRules()
    KC28_ValidateRuleTable gKC28Rules, "Выбор инструментов", True
End Sub

Private Sub KC28_ValidateRuleTable(ByRef rules As Variant, ByVal label As String, ByVal requireFallback As Boolean)
    Dim re As Object, at As String
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, n As Double, text As String, parts As Variant, p As Variant
    Dim haveFallback As Boolean
    For r = 1 To UBound(rules, 1)
        at = " (" & label & ", строка " & CStr(rules(r, 17)) & ")"
        For c = 1 To 16
            If IsError(rules(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3470, , "Ошибка Excel в правиле" & at
            If VarType(rules(r, c)) = vbString Then rules(r, c) = Trim$(CStr(rules(r, c)))
        Next c
        n = KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 1)))
        If n <> 0 And n <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3471, , "В колонке «Использовать» выберите Да или Нет."
        text = CStr(rules(r, 2))
        If InStr("|DIMENSION|GTOL|WELD|NOTE|*|", "|" & text & "|") = 0 Or Len(text) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3472, , "Неверное значение «Что контролируем»" & at
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 13))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3473, , "Не задано средство ТО" & at
        For c = 6 To 8
            If Len(CStr(rules(r, c))) > 0 Then
                n = KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, c)))
                If n < 0 Or n > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Неверная граница катета в правиле."
                If c > 6 And n <> Fix(n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Границы округлённого катета должны быть целыми."
            End If
        Next c
        For c = 11 To 12
            If Len(CStr(rules(r, c))) > 0 Then
                n = KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, c)))
                If n < 0 Or n > 1000000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Неверная граница номинала в правиле."
            End If
        Next c
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 7))) > 0 And Len(CStr(rules(r, 8))) > 0 Then
            If KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 7))) > KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 8))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Нижняя граница катета больше верхней."
        End If
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 11))) > 0 And Len(CStr(rules(r, 12))) > 0 Then
            If KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 11))) > KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 12))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Нижняя граница номинала больше верхней."
        End If
        text = CStr(rules(r, 10))
        If text <> "" And text <> "ODD" And text <> "EVEN" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3475, , "Чётность катета: пусто, Нечётный или Чётный."
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 9))) > 0 Then
            parts = Split(CStr(rules(r, 9)), ",")
            For Each p In parts
                n = KC28_Number(CStr(p))
                If n < 0 Or n > 100000 Or n <> Fix(n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3476, , "В списке катетов нужны целые значения через запятую."
            Next p
        End If
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 16))) > 0 Then
            Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
            re.Pattern = CStr(rules(r, 16))
            On Error Resume Next
            re.Test "x"
            If Err.number <> 0 Then
                Err.Clear
                On Error GoTo 0
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3479, , "Неверное выражение в «Текст содержит»" & at
            End If
            On Error GoTo 0
        End If
        If CStr(rules(r, 2)) = "*" And CStr(rules(r, 1)) = "1" Then
            text = ""
            For c = 3 To 12: text = text & CStr(rules(r, c)): Next c
            text = text & CStr(rules(r, 15)) & CStr(rules(r, 16))
            If Len(text) = 0 Then haveFallback = True
        End If
    Next r
    If requireFallback And Not haveFallback Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3477, , "Нужно включённое запасное правило «Любой» без условий."
End Sub


Private Sub KC28_SelectTools(ByRef pd As PositionData, ByRef mainTool As String, ByRef extraTool As String)
    Dim typ As String, r As Long
    typ = KC28_TypeCode(pd)
    For r = 1 To UBound(gKC28Rules, 1)
        If KC28_RuleMatch(gKC28Rules, r, pd, typ) Then
            mainTool = CStr(gKC28Rules(r, 13)): extraTool = CStr(gKC28Rules(r, 14))
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next r
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3478, , "Нет подходящего правила средств ТО."
End Sub


' Перепривязка вызывается только если сохранённый/ожидаемый файл отсутствует.
' Никаких операций над аннотациями, баллонами или геометрией в этих процедурах нет.
Private Sub KC30_WriteBinding(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal path As String)
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, target As String, temp As String, detail As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    target = drawing & ".okintro-v12.txt": temp = target & ".tmp"
    Set stream = fso.CreateTextFile(temp, True, True)
    stream.WriteLine "OK_INTRO_BINDING_V12"
    stream.WriteLine drawing
    stream.WriteLine path
    stream.Close
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(temp, 1, False, -1)
    If stream.ReadLine <> "OK_INTRO_BINDING_V12" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3500, , "Ошибка записи привязки."
    If StrComp(stream.ReadLine, drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3500, , "Ошибка владельца привязки."
    If StrComp(stream.ReadLine, path, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3500, , "Ошибка пути привязки."
    stream.Close
    fso.CopyFile temp, target, True
    fso.DeleteFile temp
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    detail = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
    If Not fso Is Nothing Then
        If Len(temp) > 0 Then
            If fso.FileExists(temp) Then fso.DeleteFile temp
        End If
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3501, "KC30_WriteBinding", "Не записана привязка: " & detail & ". Баллоны не изменены."
End Sub

Private Sub KC30_ValidateSource(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawing As String)
    Dim card As Object, raw As Object, meta As Object, app As Object, model As Object
    Dim sheets As Variant, known As Object, data As Variant, last As Long, r As Long, i As Long
    Set card = book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    If Not IsUnifiedCard(card) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3502, , "Не распознана полная карта контроля."
    Set meta = book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное")
    If Len(Trim$(CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3503, , "Выбран чистый шаблон, а не рабочая карта."
    Set raw = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    If CStr(raw.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "№ поз." Or CStr(raw.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Текст аннотации" Or _
       CStr(raw.cells(1, 23).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(raw.cells(1, 24).Value2) <> "Чертёжный вид" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3504, , "Не распознана структура исходных данных карты."
    last = raw.cells(raw.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last < 2 Or last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3505, , "В карте нет корректного списка параметров."
    Set app = Application.SldWorks: Set model = app.ActiveDoc
    If StrComp(CStr(model.GetPathName), drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3506, , "Активный чертёж сменился."
    sheets = model.GetSheetNames
    Set known = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): known.CompareMode = 1
    For i = LBound(sheets) To UBound(sheets): known(CStr(sheets(i))) = True: Next i
    data = raw.Range("W2:X" & CStr(last)).Value2
    For r = 1 To UBound(data, 1)
        If IsError(data(r, 1)) Or IsError(data(r, 2)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3507, , "Ошибка Excel в исходных данных."
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(data(r, 1)))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3508, , "Пустое имя исходного листа."
        If Not known.Exists(CStr(data(r, 1))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3509, , "В текущем чертеже нет исходного листа: " & CStr(data(r, 1)) & ". Требуется отдельный разбор соответствий, привязка не изменена."
    Next r
End Sub

Private Function KC30_RebindMissing(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal expected As String, ByVal required As Boolean) As String
    Dim answer As VbMsgBoxResult, xl As Object, book As Object, sourceBook As Object, picked As Variant, dest As Variant
    Dim fso As Object, oldOwner As String, target As String, meta As Object, item As Variant, text As String
    Dim existingApp As Object, openBook As Object, detail As String, sourcePath As String
    answer = MsgBox("Связанная рабочая карта не найдена:" & vbCrLf & expected & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Да — выбрать существующую .xlsm. Для другого чертежа будет создана отдельная копия; исходная карта не изменится." & vbCrLf & _
        "Нет — новая карта первым макросом." & vbCrLf & "Отмена — остановиться без изменения баллонов.", vbYesNoCancel + vbQuestion, "Карта контроля не найдена")
    If answer = vbCancel Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3510, , "Выбор рабочей карты отменён. Баллоны не изменены."
    If answer = vbNo Then
        If required Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3511, , "Создайте новую карту первым макросом."
        target = KC14_DefaultPath(drawing)
        If CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject").FileExists(target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3514, , "Новая книга по умолчанию уже существует. Выберите Да и укажите её явно. Перезаписи нет."
        KC30_RebindMissing = target
        Exit Function
    End If
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    Set xl = CreateObject("Excel.Application"): xl.Visible = True
    xl.AutomationSecurity = 3: xl.EnableEvents = False
    picked = KC32_PickCard(xl, drawing, "Выберите существующую полную карту", "*.xlsm")
    If VarType(picked) = vbBoolean Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3510, , "Выбор карты отменён."
    sourcePath = CStr(picked)
    If LCase$(fso.GetExtensionName(sourcePath)) <> "xlsm" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3512, , "Нужна рабочая .xlsm."
    On Error Resume Next
    Set existingApp = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not existingApp Is Nothing Then
        For Each openBook In existingApp.Workbooks
            If StrComp(openBook.FullName, sourcePath, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                If Not openBook.saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3513, , "Сначала сохраните выбранную карту Excel."
            End If
        Next openBook
    End If
    Set sourceBook = xl.Workbooks.Open(sourcePath, 0, True)
    KC30_ValidateSource sourceBook, drawing
    Set meta = sourceBook.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное")
    oldOwner = CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2)
    If StrComp(oldOwner, drawing, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        target = sourcePath
        sourceBook.Close False: Set sourceBook = Nothing
    Else
        dest = xl.GetSaveAsFilename(KC14_DefaultPath(drawing), "Excel с макросами (*.xlsm),*.xlsm", , "Независимая копия карты для текущего чертежа")
        If VarType(dest) = vbBoolean Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3510, , "Создание копии отменено."
        target = CStr(dest)
        If LCase$(Right$(target, 5)) <> ".xlsm" Then target = target & ".xlsm"
        If fso.FileExists(target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3514, , "Файл уже существует. Выберите новое имя, перезапись запрещена."
        sourceBook.SaveCopyAs target
        sourceBook.Close False: Set sourceBook = Nothing
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(target, 0, False)
        If book.ReadOnly Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3515, , "Копия доступна только для чтения."
        Set meta = book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное")
        meta.Range("A12").Value2 = "CardCopyId"
        meta.Range("B12").Value2 = Format$(Now, "yyyymmddhhnnss") & "_" & fso.GetTempName
        meta.Range("A13").Value2 = "CopiedFrom"
        meta.Range("B13").Value2 = sourcePath
        KC37_InvalidateCopiedLayout book, oldOwner, drawing
        meta.Range("B2").Value2 = drawing
        meta.Range("B10").Value2 = "PENDING"
        book.Save
        book.Close False: Set book = Nothing
        For Each item In Array("Sheets", "SheetsKnown", "Types", "Anchor")
            text = KC14_Setting(oldOwner, CStr(item), "")
            If Len(text) > 0 Then KC14_SaveSetting drawing, CStr(item), text
        Next item
    End If
    xl.Quit: Set xl = Nothing
    KC30_WriteBinding drawing, target
    KC14_Log drawing, "Перепривязка: " & sourcePath & " -> " & target
    If StrComp(oldOwner, drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        MsgBox "Независимая копия карты создана и привязана. Исходная книга и баллоны не изменены." & vbCrLf & _
        "Перед заполнением чертежа выполните режим 1 первого макроса: он сверит параметры, используя существующие баллоны.", vbInformation
    KC30_RebindMissing = target
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not sourceBook Is Nothing Then sourceBook.Close False
    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then xl.Quit
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3516, "KC30_RebindMissing", detail & vbCrLf & "Привязка не подтверждена; баллоны не изменены. Созданная копия, если есть, оставлена на диске."
End Function

Private Sub KC28_AppendRuleTools(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim area As Object, ws As Object, known As Object, cell As Object
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, text As String, length As Long
    Set area = book.names("ToolsList").RefersToRange
    Set ws = area.Parent
    If Not ws.Parent Is book Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3480, , "ToolsList относится к другой книге."
    If area.Areas.count <> 1 Or area.Columns.count <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3481, , "ToolsList должен быть одним столбцом."
    Set known = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): known.CompareMode = 1
    For Each cell In area.cells
        text = KC23_CellText(cell)
        If Len(text) > 0 Then known(text) = True
    Next cell
    length = area.rows.count
    For r = 1 To UBound(gKC28Rules, 1)
        If CStr(gKC28Rules(r, 1)) = "1" Then
            For c = 13 To 14
                KC28_AddToolName area, ws, known, length, CStr(gKC28Rules(r, c))
            Next c
        End If
    Next r
    If Not IsEmpty(gKC28Extra) Then
        For r = 1 To UBound(gKC28Extra, 1)
            If CStr(gKC28Extra(r, 1)) = "1" Then KC28_AddToolName area, ws, known, length, CStr(gKC28Extra(r, 13))
        Next r
    End If
    book.names("ToolsList").Delete
    book.names.Add name:="ToolsList", RefersTo:="='" & Replace(ws.name, "'", "''") & "'!" & area.cells(1, 1).Resize(length, 1).address
End Sub


Private Function KC28_WeldKey(ByVal value As String) As String
    Dim text As String
    text = UCase$(Trim$(value))
    text = Replace(text, " ", "")
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(160), "")
    text = Replace(text, vbTab, "")
    text = Replace(text, "C", "С")
    text = Replace(text, "T", "Т")
    text = Replace(text, "H", "Н")
    text = Replace(text, "Y", "У")
    KC28_WeldKey = text
End Function


' v19: семантическое чтение старой и новой панели без подмены значений местами.
Private Function KC29_NewFields(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    KC29_NewFields = (KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K6")) = "Первичное применение" And KC23_CellText(ws.Range("H18")) = "Оборудование ОК1")
End Function

Private Function KC29_TextField(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal name As String, ByVal address As String) As String
    Dim nm As Object, area As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set nm = ws.Parent.names(name)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If nm Is Nothing Then
        Set area = ws.Range(address)
    Else
        Set area = nm.RefersToRange
        If Not area.Parent Is ws Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3400, , "Диапазон " & name & " относится к другому листу."
        If area.address <> ws.Range(address).address Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3401, , "Диапазон " & name & " должен указывать на " & address & ". Выполните OK_UpdateEditor."
    End If
    If area.MergeCells Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3402, , "Не объединяйте ячейку " & address & "."
    KC29_TextField = KC23_CellText(area)
End Function

Private Function KC27_ReadPrimary(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef value As String) As Boolean
    value = ""
    If Not KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then Exit Function
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        value = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "L6")
    ElseIf KC23_CellText(ws.Range("H18")) = "Первичное применение" Then
        value = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "I18")
    Else
        Exit Function
    End If
    KC27_ReadPrimary = True
End Function

Private Function KC29_ReadHeaderData(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef kd As String, ByRef itemName As String, ByRef equipment As String) As Boolean
    kd = "": itemName = "": equipment = ""
    If KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K3")) <> "Реквизиты изделия" Then Exit Function
    kd = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_KDNumber", "L4")
    itemName = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_ProductName", "L5")
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        equipment = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_Equipment", "I18")
    ElseIf KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K6")) = "Оборудование ОК1" Then
        equipment = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_Equipment", "L6")
    Else
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3403, , "Не распознана панель реквизитов. Выполните OK_UpdateEditor."
    End If
    KC29_ReadHeaderData = True
End Function

Private Sub KC29_EnsureHeaderData(ByVal ws As Object)
    Dim kd As String, itemName As String, equipment As String, primary As String
    Dim haveProduct As Boolean, havePrimary As Boolean, cell As Object, fresh As Boolean
    ' До первой записи читаем оба старых поля. Повторное обновление не меняет их местами.
    haveProduct = KC29_ReadHeaderData(ws, kd, itemName, equipment)
    havePrimary = KC27_ReadPrimary(ws, primary)
    For Each cell In ws.Range("K3:L6").cells
        If cell.MergeCells Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3404, , "Освободите K3:L6 от объединений перед обновлением панели."
    Next cell
    If ws.Range("H18").MergeCells Or ws.Range("I18").MergeCells Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3404, , "Освободите H18:I18 от объединений."
    If Not haveProduct Then
        If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("K3:L6")) > 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3405, , "В K3:L6 есть посторонние данные. Перенесите их до обновления."
    End If
    If Not havePrimary And Not KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("H18:I18")) > 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3406, , "В H18:I18 есть посторонние данные. Перенесите их до обновления."
    End If
    ws.Range("K3").Value2 = "Реквизиты изделия"
    ws.Range("K4").Value2 = "Номер КД"
    ws.Range("K5").Value2 = "Наименование изделия"
    ws.Range("K6").Value2 = "Первичное применение"
    ws.Range("H18").Value2 = "Оборудование ОК1"
    ws.Range("L4:L6").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("I18").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("L4").Value2 = kd
    ws.Range("L5").Value2 = itemName
    ws.Range("L6").Value2 = primary
    ws.Range("I18").Value2 = equipment
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductData", "L4:L6"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_KDNumber", "L4"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductName", "L5"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "L6"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_Equipment", "I18"
    ws.Columns("K").ColumnWidth = 26
    ws.Columns("L").ColumnWidth = 60
    ws.Range("K3:L3").Interior.Color = RGB(217, 229, 241)
    ws.Range("K3:L3").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("K4:L6").Borders.LineStyle = 1
    ws.Range("K4:L6").WrapText = True
    ws.Range("L4:L6").Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
    ws.Range("H18").WrapText = True
    With ws.Range("I18")
        .Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
        .Font.Bold = False
        .BorderAround LineStyle:=1, Weight:=2
    End With
    If ws.rows(5).RowHeight < 40 Then ws.rows(5).RowHeight = 40
    If ws.rows(18).RowHeight < 30 Then ws.rows(18).RowHeight = 30
End Sub


Private Function KC32_PickCard(ByVal xl As Object, ByVal drawing As String, ByVal title As String, ByVal filter As String) As Variant
    Dim dialog As Object, fso As Object, folder As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = fso.GetParentFolderName(drawing)
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3550, , "Папка активного документа недоступна: " & folder
    Set dialog = xl.FileDialog(3)
    With dialog
        .title = title
        .AllowMultiSelect = False
        .Filters.Clear
        .Filters.Add "Карты контроля Excel", filter
        .InitialFileName = folder & "\"
        KC32_PickCard = False
        If .Show = -1 Then KC32_PickCard = CStr(.SelectedItems(1))
    End With
End Function


Private Function KC32_Flag(ByVal value As String) As Double
    Select Case UCase$(Trim$(value))
        Case "ДА": KC32_Flag = 1
        Case "НЕТ": KC32_Flag = 0
        Case Else: KC32_Flag = KC28_Number(value)
    End Select
End Function

' ============================================
' v16.2: ГИБКОЕ ЧТЕНИЕ НАСТРОЕК (заголовки вместо позиций столбцов)
' ============================================
Private Function KC28_HasSheet(ByVal book As Object, ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim ws As Object
    For Each ws In book.Worksheets
        If StrComp(Trim$(CStr(ws.name)), sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then KC28_HasSheet = True: Exit Function
    Next ws
End Function

Private Function KC28_HeaderKey(ByVal value As Variant) As String
    Dim t As String
    If IsError(value) Then Exit Function
    t = LCase$(Trim$(CStr(value)))
    t = Replace(t, "ё", "е")
    t = Replace(t, ChrW(160), " ")
    t = Replace(t, vbCr, " ")
    t = Replace(t, vbLf, " ")
    Do While InStr(t, "  ") > 0: t = Replace(t, "  ", " "): Loop
    KC28_HeaderKey = t
End Function

' Внутренний формат строки правила (1..17):
' 1 Использовать, 2 Что контролируем, 3 ГОСТ содержит, 4 Типы швов, 5 Геом. допуск,
' 6 Катет от, 7 Окр. катет от, 8 Окр. катет до, 9 Катеты из списка, 10 Чётность,
' 11 Номинал от, 12 Номинал до, 13 Средство, 14 Доп. средство, 15 Прерывистый шов,
' 16 Текст содержит (регулярное выражение), 17 номер строки на листе (для сообщений).
Private Function KC28_ReadRuleTable(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal toolHeader As String, ByVal extraHeader As String) As Variant
    Dim names As Variant, cols(1 To 16) As Long, lastCol As Long, lastRow As Long, rowLast As Long
    Dim c As Long, k As Long, r As Long, n As Long, h As String, v As Variant
    Dim values As Variant, result As Variant, blank As Boolean
    names = Array("Использовать", "Что контролируем", "ГОСТ содержит", "Типы швов", "Геометрический допуск", _
        "Катет от, мм", "Округлённый катет от", "Округлённый катет до", "Катеты из списка", "Чётность катета", _
        "Номинал от, мм", "Номинал до, мм", toolHeader, extraHeader, "Прерывистый шов", "Текст содержит")
    lastCol = ws.cells(1, ws.Columns.count).End(-4159).Column
    For c = 1 To lastCol
        h = KC28_HeaderKey(ws.cells(1, c).Value2)
        If Len(h) > 0 Then
            For k = 1 To 16
                If Len(CStr(names(k - 1))) > 0 Then
                    If h = KC28_HeaderKey(names(k - 1)) Then
                        If cols(k) > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Столбец «" & names(k - 1) & "» повторяется на листе " & ws.name
                        cols(k) = c
                    End If
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next c
    If cols(1) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Лист «" & ws.name & "»: нет столбца «Использовать»."
    If cols(2) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Лист «" & ws.name & "»: нет столбца «Что контролируем»."
    If cols(13) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Лист «" & ws.name & "»: нет столбца «" & toolHeader & "»."
    lastRow = 1
    For k = 1 To 16
        If cols(k) > 0 Then
            rowLast = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, cols(k)).End(-4162).row
            If rowLast > lastRow Then lastRow = rowLast
        End If
    Next k
    If lastRow > 1001 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3466, , "Слишком много правил на листе " & ws.name
    If lastRow < 2 Then KC28_ReadRuleTable = Empty: Exit Function
    values = ws.Range(ws.cells(2, 1), ws.cells(lastRow, lastCol)).Value2
    ReDim result(1 To lastRow - 1, 1 To 17)
    For r = 1 To lastRow - 1
        blank = True
        For k = 1 To 16
            If cols(k) > 0 Then
                v = values(r, cols(k))
                If IsError(v) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3457, , "Ошибка Excel: " & ws.name & ", строка " & CStr(r + 1)
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(v))) > 0 Then blank = False
            End If
        Next k
        If Not blank Then          ' пустые строки-разделители разрешены
            n = n + 1
            For k = 1 To 16
                If cols(k) > 0 Then result(n, k) = Trim$(CStr(values(r, cols(k)))) Else result(n, k) = ""
            Next k
            result(n, 17) = r + 1
        End If
    Next r
    If n = 0 Then KC28_ReadRuleTable = Empty: Exit Function
    KC28_ReadRuleTable = KC28_Shrink(result, n, 17)
End Function

Private Function KC28_Shrink(ByRef src As Variant, ByVal n As Long, ByVal width As Long) As Variant
    Dim result As Variant, r As Long, c As Long
    ReDim result(1 To n, 1 To width)
    For r = 1 To n
        For c = 1 To width: result(r, c) = src(r, c): Next c
    Next r
    KC28_Shrink = result
End Function

' Старая схема V1 (14 столбцов) -> внутренний формат 17 столбцов.
Private Function KC28_Widen(ByVal v As Variant) As Variant
    Dim result As Variant, r As Long, c As Long
    ReDim result(1 To UBound(v, 1), 1 To 17)
    For r = 1 To UBound(v, 1)
        For c = 1 To 14: result(r, c) = v(r, c): Next c
        result(r, 15) = "": result(r, 16) = "": result(r, 17) = r + 1
    Next r
    KC28_Widen = result
End Function

Private Sub KC28_NormalizeRules(ByRef rules As Variant)
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To UBound(rules, 1)
        If IsError(rules(r, 1)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3470, , "Ошибка Excel в правиле, строка " & CStr(rules(r, 17))
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 1)))) = 0 Then rules(r, 1) = 0 Else rules(r, 1) = KC32_Flag(CStr(rules(r, 1)))
        Select Case UCase$(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 2))))
            Case "РАЗМЕР": rules(r, 2) = "DIMENSION"
            Case "ГЕОМЕТРИЧЕСКИЙ ДОПУСК": rules(r, 2) = "GTOL"
            Case "СВАРНОЙ ШОВ": rules(r, 2) = "WELD"
            Case "ЗАМЕТКА": rules(r, 2) = "NOTE"
            Case "ЛЮБОЙ": rules(r, 2) = "*"
        End Select
        Select Case UCase$(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 10))))
            Case "НЕЧЁТНЫЙ", "НЕЧЕТНЫЙ": rules(r, 10) = "ODD"
            Case "ЧЁТНЫЙ", "ЧЕТНЫЙ": rules(r, 10) = "EVEN"
        End Select
        Select Case UCase$(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 15))))
            Case "": rules(r, 15) = ""
            Case "ДА", "1": rules(r, 15) = "1"
            Case "НЕТ", "0": rules(r, 15) = "0"
            Case Else: Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3479, , "«Прерывистый шов»: пусто, Да или Нет (строка " & CStr(rules(r, 17)) & ")."
        End Select
    Next r
End Sub

Private Function KC28_TypeCode(ByRef pd As PositionData) As String
    Select Case pd.AnnotationType
        Case swSelDIMENSIONS: KC28_TypeCode = "DIMENSION"
        Case swSelGTOLS: KC28_TypeCode = "GTOL"
        Case swSelWELDSYMBOLS: KC28_TypeCode = "WELD"
        Case swSelNOTES: KC28_TypeCode = "NOTE"
        Case Else: KC28_TypeCode = "OTHER"
    End Select
End Function

' Одна строка правила против одной позиции. Пустая ячейка условия = условие не проверяется.
Private Function KC28_RuleMatch(ByRef rules As Variant, ByVal r As Long, ByRef pd As PositionData, ByVal typ As String) As Boolean
    Dim match As Boolean, rounded As Long, parts As Variant, p As Variant, found As Boolean, re As Object
    rounded = CLng(pd.weldLeg)
    match = (CStr(rules(r, 1)) = "1")
    If match And CStr(rules(r, 2)) <> "*" And CStr(rules(r, 2)) <> typ Then match = False
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 3))) > 0 Then match = (InStr(1, pd.WeldStandard, CStr(rules(r, 3)), vbTextCompare) > 0)
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 4))) > 0 Then
        found = False: parts = Split(CStr(rules(r, 4)), ",")
        For Each p In parts
            If StrComp(Left$(pd.weldType, Len(Trim$(CStr(p)))), Trim$(CStr(p)), vbTextCompare) = 0 Then found = True
        Next p
        match = found
    End If
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 5))) > 0 Then match = (StrComp(pd.GDTSymbolName, CStr(rules(r, 5)), vbTextCompare) = 0)
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 6))) > 0 Then match = (pd.weldLeg >= KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 6))))
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 7))) > 0 Then match = (rounded >= KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 7))))
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 8))) > 0 Then match = (rounded <= KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 8))))
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 9))) > 0 Then
        found = False: parts = Split(CStr(rules(r, 9)), ",")
        For Each p In parts
            If rounded = CLng(KC28_Number(CStr(p))) Then found = True
        Next p
        match = found
    End If
    If match And CStr(rules(r, 10)) = "ODD" Then match = (rounded Mod 2 = 1)
    If match And CStr(rules(r, 10)) = "EVEN" Then match = (rounded Mod 2 = 0)
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 11))) > 0 Then match = (pd.NominalValue >= KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 11))))
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 12))) > 0 Then match = (pd.NominalValue <= KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 12))))
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 15))) > 0 Then
        match = ((Len(pd.WeldIntermittentSpec) > 0) = (CStr(rules(r, 15)) = "1"))
    End If
    If match And Len(CStr(rules(r, 16))) > 0 Then
        Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
        re.Pattern = CStr(rules(r, 16)): re.IgnoreCase = True
        match = re.Test(pd.AnnotationText)
    End If
    KC28_RuleMatch = match
End Function

' Все дополнительные средства позиции: из сработавшего основного правила
' и со ВСЕХ подходящих строк листа «Добавочные средства» (без повторов).
Private Function KC28_ExtraTools(ByRef pd As PositionData, Optional ByVal onlyAdditive As Boolean = False) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, seen As Object, mainTool As String, extraTool As String
    Dim typ As String, r As Long, limit As Long, t As String
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): seen.CompareMode = 1
    limit = CLng(KC28_N("Tools.MaxExtraTools"))
    KC28_SelectTools pd, mainTool, extraTool
    seen(mainTool) = True
    If Len(extraTool) > 0 Then
        seen(extraTool) = True
        If Not onlyAdditive Then result.Add extraTool
    End If
    If Not IsEmpty(gKC28Extra) Then
        typ = KC28_TypeCode(pd)
        For r = 1 To UBound(gKC28Extra, 1)
            t = CStr(gKC28Extra(r, 13))
            If Len(t) > 0 And Not seen.Exists(t) Then
                If KC28_RuleMatch(gKC28Extra, r, pd, typ) Then seen(t) = True: result.Add t
            End If
        Next r
    End If
    If result.count > limit And limit > 0 Then _
        gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Поз. " & pd.number & ": дополнительных средств " & result.count & _
                    ", лимит " & limit & "; часть не добавлена. "
    Do While result.count > limit: result.remove result.count: Loop
    Set KC28_ExtraTools = result
End Function

' Повторный экспорт: в сохранённый блок дописываем только недостающие добавочные средства
' и только в пустые строки столбца «Средства» (ручные правки не затираются).
Private Sub KCPerf_AddMissingTools(ByRef buffer As Variant, ByVal first As Long, ByVal length As Long, ByRef pd As PositionData)
    Dim extras As Collection, r As Long, k As Long, have As Boolean, t As String
    Set extras = KC28_ExtraTools(pd)
    For k = 1 To extras.count
        t = CStr(extras(k)): have = False
        For r = first To first + length - 1
            If StrComp(Trim$(CStr(buffer(r, 3))), t, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then have = True: Exit For
        Next r
        If Not have Then
            For r = first + 1 To first + length - 1
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(buffer(r, 3)))) = 0 Then buffer(r, 3) = t: have = True: Exit For
            Next r
            If Not have Then gKV_Notes = gKV_Notes & "Поз. " & pd.number & ": нет свободной строки для «" & t & "». "
        End If
    Next k
End Sub

Private Sub KC28_AddToolName(ByVal area As Object, ByVal ws As Object, ByVal known As Object, ByRef length As Long, ByVal text As String)
    Dim cell As Object
    If Len(text) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If known.Exists(text) Then Exit Sub
    Set cell = area.cells(1, 1).Offset(length, 0)
    If cell.MergeCells Or ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(cell) > 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3482, , "Под ToolsList есть данные или объединение; нельзя добавить средства из настроек."
    cell.NumberFormat = "@": cell.Value2 = text
    length = length + 1: known(text) = True
End Sub

' ============================================
' v16.2: ССЫЛКИ НА ПУНКТЫ КАРТЫ В ЗАМЕТКАХ ("Клеймо ОТК#" -> п.N, связь через свойство чертежа)
' ============================================
' Текст заметки с сохранением ссылок $PRP:"..." (GetText возвращает уже вычисленный текст).
Private Function KC33_NoteRaw(ByVal note As Object) As String
    Dim t As String
    On Error Resume Next
    t = CStr(note.PropertyLinkedText)
    If Err.number <> 0 Then t = ""
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
    If InStr(1, t, "$PRP", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then t = CStr(note.GetText)
    KC33_NoteRaw = t
End Function

Private Function KC33_Clean(ByVal t As String) As String
    t = Replace(t, "#", ""): t = Replace(t, "@", "")
    t = Replace(t, vbCr, " "): t = Replace(t, vbLf, " "): t = Replace(t, vbTab, " ")
    t = Replace(t, ChrW(160), " ")
    Do While InStr(t, "  ") > 0: t = Replace(t, "  ", " "): Loop
    KC33_Clean = LCase$(Trim$(t))
End Function

Private Function KC33_Style(ByVal note As Object) As Long
    KC33_Style = -1
    On Error Resume Next
    KC33_Style = note.GetBalloonStyle
End Function

Private Function KC33_TrySetLinked(ByVal note As Object, ByVal linkText As String) As Boolean
    KC33_TrySetLinked = KC34_SetNoteRaw(note, linkText)
End Function

' Лист «Ссылки на пункты» необязателен. Без него действует одна строка:
' Клеймо ОТК -> пункт «Клеймение» -> свойство КК_П_Клеймо -> формат «п.{N}».
Private Sub KC33_LoadRefs(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim ws As Object, lastRow As Long, lastCol As Long, r As Long, n As Long, c As Long, k As Long
    Dim cols(1 To 5) As Long, names As Variant, h As String, v(1 To 5) As String, raw As Variant
    Dim result As Variant, tokens As Object, properties As Object
    gKC28Refs = Empty
    names = Array("Использовать", "Текст в заметке", "Пункт карты", "Свойство чертежа", "Формат")
    If Not KC28_HasSheet(book, "Ссылки на пункты") Then
        ReDim result(1 To 1, 1 To 5)
        result(1, 1) = "1": result(1, 2) = "Клеймо ОТК": result(1, 3) = "STAMP"
        result(1, 4) = "КК_П_Клеймо": result(1, 5) = "п.{N}"
        gKC28Refs = result
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Ссылки на пункты")
    lastCol = ws.cells(1, ws.Columns.count).End(-4159).Column
    For c = 1 To lastCol
        h = KC28_HeaderKey(ws.cells(1, c).Value2)
        For k = 1 To 5
            If h = KC28_HeaderKey(names(k - 1)) Then
                If cols(k) > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3490, , "Повтор заголовка: " & names(k - 1)
                cols(k) = c
            End If
        Next k
    Next c
    For k = 1 To 4
        If cols(k) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3490, , "Лист «Ссылки на пункты»: нет столбца «" & names(k - 1) & "»."
    Next k
    lastRow = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, cols(2)).End(-4162).row
    If lastRow < 2 Then Exit Sub
    If lastRow > 200 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3490, , "Лист «Ссылки на пункты»: слишком много строк."
    Set tokens = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): tokens.CompareMode = 1
    Set properties = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): properties.CompareMode = 1
    ReDim result(1 To lastRow - 1, 1 To 5)
    For r = 2 To lastRow
        For k = 1 To 5
            v(k) = ""
            If cols(k) > 0 Then
                raw = ws.cells(r, cols(k)).Value2
                If IsError(raw) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3491, , "Ошибка Excel: Ссылки на пункты, строка " & r
                v(k) = Trim$(CStr(raw))
            End If
        Next k
        If Len(v(2)) > 0 Then
            n = n + 1
            If Len(v(1)) = 0 Then result(n, 1) = "0" Else result(n, 1) = CStr(KC32_Flag(v(1)))
            result(n, 2) = v(2)
            If StrComp(v(3), "Клеймение", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                result(n, 3) = "STAMP"
            ElseIf Len(v(3)) > 0 And Not (v(3) Like "*[!0-9]*") Then
                If Len(v(3)) > 6 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3492, , "Слишком большой номер пункта."
                If CLng(v(3)) < 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3492, , "Номер пункта должен быть положительным."
                result(n, 3) = v(3)
            Else
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3492, , "Ссылки на пункты, строка " & r & ": «Пункт карты» = Клеймение или номер пункта."
            End If
            If Len(v(4)) = 0 Or InStr(v(4), """") > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3493, , "Ссылки на пункты, строка " & r & ": укажите имя свойства без кавычек."
            If Len(v(5)) = 0 Then v(5) = "п.{N}"
            If InStr(1, v(5), "{N}") = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3494, , "Ссылки на пункты, строка " & r & ": в формате нужен {N}."
            result(n, 4) = v(4): result(n, 5) = v(5)
            If tokens.Exists(KC33_Clean(v(2))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3495, , "Ссылки на пункты: текст «" & v(2) & "» повторяется."
            If Len(KC33_Clean(v(2))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3495, , "Пустой текст для поиска."
            tokens.Add KC33_Clean(v(2)), True
            If CStr(result(n, 1)) = "1" Then
                If properties.Exists(v(4)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3495, , "Свойство используется в нескольких включённых ссылках: " & v(4)
                properties.Add v(4), True
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    If n > 0 Then gKC28Refs = KC28_Shrink(result, n, 5)
End Sub

' Вызывается после успешного экспорта, когда номера пунктов окончательные.
' Заметка «Клеймо ОТК#» превращается в «п.$PRP:"КК_П_Клеймо"», значение свойства = номер пункта.
' Уже связанные заметки обновляются сами через свойство при каждом запуске.
Private Function KC33_ApplyNoteRefs(ByRef summary As String) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, sheets As Variant, si As Long, origSheet As String, view As Object, note As Object
    Dim raw As String, token As String, prefix As String, suffix As String, fmt As String, prop As String
    Dim number As Long, linkText As String, expected As String, style As Long, wantStyle As Long
    Dim replaced As Long, linked As Long, literal As Long, skipped As Long, report As String
    Dim cpm As Object, p As Long, gfxWasOff As Boolean, propResult As Long
    summary = ""
    KC33_ApplyNoteRefs = True
    If Not KC28_B("Refs.Enabled") Then Exit Function
    If IsEmpty(gKC28Refs) Then Exit Function
    KC33_ApplyNoteRefs = False
    On Error GoTo Failed
    gfxWasOff = gKC_GfxOff
    KC_GraphicsOff
    wantStyle = CLng(KC28_N("Refs.BorderStyle"))
    Set cpm = swModel.Extension.CustomPropertyManager("")
    origSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    sheets = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = 1 To UBound(gKC28Refs, 1)
        If CStr(gKC28Refs(i, 1)) = "1" Then
            If CStr(gKC28Refs(i, 3)) = "STAMP" Then number = gIntroFirstBalloon + positionsCount Else number = CLng(gKC28Refs(i, 3))
            token = KC33_Clean(CStr(gKC28Refs(i, 2)))
            prop = CStr(gKC28Refs(i, 4)): fmt = CStr(gKC28Refs(i, 5))
            p = InStr(1, fmt, "{N}")
            prefix = Left$(fmt, p - 1): suffix = Mid$(fmt, p + 3)
            propResult = cpm.Add3(prop, 30, CStr(number), 2)
            If propResult <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3540, , _
                "Не записано свойство " & prop & ": Add3=" & propResult
            linkText = prefix & "$PRP:""" & prop & """" & suffix
            expected = prefix & CStr(number) & suffix
            For si = LBound(sheets) To UBound(sheets)
                If KC_InMarkerScope(gKC34RefScope, CStr(sheets(si))) Then
                If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(CStr(sheets(si))) Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3541, , "Не активирован лист " & CStr(sheets(si))
                Set view = swDraw.GetFirstView
                Do While Not view Is Nothing
                    Set note = view.GetFirstNote
                    Do While Not note Is Nothing
                        raw = KC33_NoteRaw(note)
                        If InStr(1, raw, "$PRP:""" & prop & """", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
                            linked = linked + 1
                        ElseIf InStr(1, raw, "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
                            If KC33_Clean(raw) = token Then
                                style = KC33_Style(note)
                                If wantStyle >= 0 And style <> wantStyle Then
                                    skipped = skipped + 1
                                    report = report & CStr(sheets(si)) & ": «" & raw & "» — граница " & style & _
                                             ", в настройке Refs.BorderStyle = " & wantStyle & vbCrLf
                                ElseIf KC33_TrySetLinked(note, linkText) Then
                                    replaced = replaced + 1
                                Else
                                    literal = literal + 1
                                    If Not KC34_SetNoteRaw(note, raw) Then _
                                        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3542, , "Не восстановлен исходный текст заметки."
                                    report = report & CStr(sheets(si)) & ": «" & raw & "» — связь не подтверждена; исходный текст сохранён." & vbCrLf
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                        Set note = note.GetNext
                    Loop
                    Set view = view.GetNextView
                Loop
                End If
            Next si
        End If
    Next i
    swDraw.ActivateSheet origSheet
    swModel.ForceRebuild3 False
    If Not gfxWasOff Then KC_GraphicsOn
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    If replaced + linked + literal + skipped > 0 Then
        summary = "Ссылки на пункты: заменено " & replaced & ", уже связано " & linked
        If literal > 0 Then summary = summary & ", не заменено (исходный текст сохранён) " & literal
        If skipped > 0 Then summary = summary & ", пропущено из-за границы " & skipped
        summary = summary & "."
        If Len(report) > 0 Then summary = summary & " Отчёт: " & KC_WriteMarkerReport(report, "note_refs")
    End If
    KC33_ApplyNoteRefs = (literal = 0 And skipped = 0)
    Exit Function
Failed:
    summary = "Ссылки на пункты: ошибка — " & Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(origSheet) > 0 Then swDraw.ActivateSheet origSheet
    If Not gfxWasOff Then KC_GraphicsOn
End Function


' v16.3-review: распознаём положительные длину/шаг, включая отдельное 60/100.
' Это разбор текста символа, а не OCR. Нечисловые/неполные записи не принимаются.
Private Function KC34_IntermittentSpec(ByVal text As String) As String
    Dim re As Object, matches As Object, m As Object, t As String
    Dim lengthText As String, pitchText As String
    t = Replace(Replace(Replace(text, ChrW(160), " "), vbCr, " "), vbLf, " ")
    t = Replace(t, "|", " ")
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Global = True
    re.Pattern = "<[^>]+>"
    t = re.Replace(t, " ")
    ' Исключаем пары предельных отклонений +a/-b и +a/b.
    re.Pattern = "\+\s*[0-9]+([.,][0-9]+)?\s*/\s*-?\s*[0-9]+([.,][0-9]+)?"
    t = re.Replace(t, " ")
    re.Pattern = "([0-9]+([.,][0-9]+)?)\s*[/Zz]\s*([0-9]+([.,][0-9]+)?)"
    Set matches = re.Execute(t)
    For Each m In matches
        lengthText = CStr(m.SubMatches(0)): pitchText = CStr(m.SubMatches(2))
        If val(Replace(lengthText, ",", ".")) > 0 And val(Replace(pitchText, ",", ".")) > 0 Then
            KC34_IntermittentSpec = "-" & lengthText & "/" & pitchText
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next m
End Function

' Удаляем служебный маркер только ВНЕ ссылок на свойства.
Private Function KC34_WithoutMarker(ByVal text As String, ByVal marker As String) As String
    Dim re As Object, matches As Object, m As Object, at As Long, result As String
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Global = True: re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "\$PRP[A-Z]*\s*:\s*""[^""]*"""
    Set matches = re.Execute(text)
    at = 1
    For Each m In matches
        result = result & Replace(Mid$(text, at, m.FirstIndex + 1 - at), marker, "") & m.value
        at = m.FirstIndex + m.length + 1
    Next m
    KC34_WithoutMarker = result & Replace(Mid$(text, at), marker, "")
End Function

' Запись подтверждается точным чтением выражения, а не наличием любого $PRP.
Private Function KC34_SetNoteRaw(ByVal note As Object, ByVal text As String) As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    If InStr(1, text, "$PRP", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
        note.PropertyLinkedText = text
        If Err.number = 0 Then
            If StrComp(KC33_NoteRaw(note), text, vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then
                KC34_SetNoteRaw = True
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
        Err.Clear
    End If
    note.SetText text
    If Err.number <> 0 Then Exit Function
    KC34_SetNoteRaw = (StrComp(KC33_NoteRaw(note), text, vbBinaryCompare) = 0)
End Function


' v16.5.3-review: исключаем только виды, целиком находящиеся за рабочей областью.
' Неизвестная/некорректная геометрия НЕ является разрешением скрыть предупреждение.
Private Function KC35_ViewTouchesWorkArea(ByVal view As Object, ByVal sheet As Object) As Boolean
    Dim outline As Variant, props As Variant, lo As Long, p As Long
    Dim boxLeft As Double, boxRight As Double, boxBottom As Double, boxTop As Double
    Dim minX As Double, maxX As Double, minY As Double, maxY As Double
    Const eps As Double = 0.0000001
    KC35_ViewTouchesWorkArea = True
    On Error GoTo UnknownBounds
    props = sheet.GetProperties
    If Not IsArray(props) Then Exit Function
    If UBound(props) < 6 Then Exit Function
    minX = FRAME_LEFT: maxX = CDbl(props(5)) - FRAME_RIGHT
    minY = FRAME_BOTTOM: maxY = CDbl(props(6)) - FRAME_TOP
    If maxX <= minX Or maxY <= minY Then Exit Function
    outline = view.GetOutline
    If Not IsArray(outline) Then Exit Function
    lo = LBound(outline)
    If UBound(outline) - lo <> 3 Then Exit Function
    For p = lo To lo + 3
        If Not IsNumeric(outline(p)) Then Exit Function
    Next p
    boxLeft = CDbl(outline(lo)): boxBottom = CDbl(outline(lo + 1))
    boxRight = CDbl(outline(lo + 2)): boxTop = CDbl(outline(lo + 3))
    If boxRight <= boxLeft Or boxTop <= boxBottom Then Exit Function
    KC35_ViewTouchesWorkArea = Not (boxRight < minX - eps Or boxLeft > maxX + eps Or _
                                    boxTop < minY - eps Or boxBottom > maxY + eps)
UnknownBounds:
End Function

' Для старого списка с устаревшим именем листа доверяем только разрешённой
' persistent reference, а не новому владельцу того же текстового имени.
Private Sub KC35_RefreshMarkerOwners(ByVal rows As Collection, ByVal current As Collection)
    Dim saved As Object, rec As Object, target As Object, ann As Object, owner As Object
    Dim hits As Long
    For Each saved In rows
        If Len(CStr(saved("Ref"))) > 0 Then
            Set target = KC_ResolvePersistentHex(CStr(saved("Ref")))
            If Not target Is Nothing Then
                hits = 0: Set owner = Nothing
                For Each rec In current
                    Set ann = rec("Ann")
                    If KC39_SameObject(ann, target) Then
                        hits = hits + 1
                        Set owner = rec
                    End If
                Next rec
                If hits = 1 Then
                    If CStr(saved("Kind")) <> "" And CStr(saved("Kind")) <> CStr(owner("Kind")) Then _
                        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3560, , "Persistent reference маркера указывает на другой тип объекта."
                    saved("Sheet") = CStr(owner("Sheet"))
                    saved("View") = CStr(owner("View"))
                    saved("Name") = CStr(owner("Name"))
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next saved
End Sub




Private Function RegularKENumber(ByVal sheetName As String) As Long
    Dim re As Object, matches As Object, value As Double
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^КЭ([0-9]+)$"
    Set matches = re.Execute(Trim$(sheetName))
    If matches.count = 0 Then Exit Function
    value = CDbl(matches(0).SubMatches(0))
    If value < 1 Or value > 2147483647# Then Exit Function
    RegularKENumber = CLng(value)
End Function

Private Function SheetExistsExact(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        If StrComp(CStr(names(i)), sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            SheetExistsExact = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

Private Function KC15_TryArrayBounds(ByVal values As Variant, ByRef first As Long, ByRef last As Long) As Boolean
    ' API может вернуть Empty или пустой SAFEARRAY. IsArray само по себе
    ' не гарантирует, что у массива можно прочитать границы.
    On Error GoTo NoItems
    If Not IsArray(values) Then Exit Function
    first = LBound(values)
    last = UBound(values)
    KC15_TryArrayBounds = (last >= first)
    Exit Function
NoItems:
    Err.Clear
    first = 0: last = -1
    KC15_TryArrayBounds = False
End Function

Private Function ActivateSheetForMacro(ByVal swDraw As Object, _
    ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim actualSheet As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(sheetName) Then GoTo Failed
    Set actualSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    If actualSheet Is Nothing Then GoTo Failed
    If StrComp(actualSheet.GetName, sheetName, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then GoTo Failed
    ActivateSheetForMacro = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC37_Warning "Не удалось активировать лист: " & sheetName
End Function

Private Function KC21_IsNewKE(ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim re As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^КЭ новый( [1-9][0-9]*)?$"
    KC21_IsNewKE = re.Test(Trim$(sheetName))
End Function

Private Sub KC21_PlanRegularKE(ByVal drawing As Object, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByRef total As Long, ByRef changed As Long)
    Dim names As Variant, first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, n As String
    Dim candidates As Object
    Erase oldNames: Erase newNames
    total = 0: changed = 0
    KC37_AssertKE1 drawing
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3240, , "Не получен список листов для упорядочения КЭ."
    Set candidates = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    candidates.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    ReDim oldNames(1 To last - first + 1): ReDim newNames(1 To last - first + 1)
    For i = first To last
        n = CStr(names(i))
        If RegularKENumber(n) > 1 Or KC21_IsNewKE(n) Then
            total = total + 1
            oldNames(total) = n
            newNames(total) = "КЭ" & CStr(total + 1)
            candidates.Add n, True
            If StrComp(n, newNames(total), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then changed = changed + 1
        End If
    Next i
    If total = 0 Then
        Erase oldNames: Erase newNames
        Exit Sub
    End If
    ReDim Preserve oldNames(1 To total): ReDim Preserve newNames(1 To total)
    For i = 1 To total
        If SheetExistsExact(drawing, newNames(i)) And Not candidates.Exists(newNames(i)) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3241, , "Имя " & newNames(i) & " занято посторонним листом."
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function KC21_MappedName(ByVal name As String, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As String
    Dim i As Long
    KC21_MappedName = name
    For i = 1 To total
        If StrComp(name, oldNames(i), vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            KC21_MappedName = newNames(i)
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

Private Function KC21_MappedSelection(ByVal value As String, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As String
    Dim items As Variant, i As Long
    KC21_MappedSelection = value
    If Len(value) = 0 Or value = "*" Then Exit Function
    items = Split(value, vbLf)
    For i = LBound(items) To UBound(items)
