        items(i) = KC21_MappedName(CStr(items(i)), oldNames, newNames, total)
    Next i
    KC21_MappedSelection = Join(items, vbLf)
End Function

Private Function KC21_FreeTempName(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal prefix As String, ByVal ordinal As Long) As String
    Dim candidate As String, suffix As Long
    candidate = prefix & CStr(ordinal)
    Do While SheetExistsExact(drawing, candidate)
        suffix = suffix + 1
        candidate = prefix & CStr(ordinal) & "_" & CStr(suffix)
    Loop
    KC21_FreeTempName = candidate
End Function

Private Sub KC21_SetSheetName(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal sheet As Object, ByVal target As String)
    If StrComp(sheet.GetName, target, vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If StrComp(CStr(sheet.GetName), "КЭ1", vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Or StrComp(target, "КЭ1", vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , "Смена имени фиксированного КЭ1 запрещена."
    If SheetExistsExact(drawing, target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3242, , "Имя листа занято: " & target
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, CStr(sheet.GetName)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3254, , _
        "Не активирован лист для переименования: " & CStr(sheet.GetName)
    sheet.SetName target
    If StrComp(sheet.GetName, target, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3243, , "Не подтверждено имя листа: " & target
    If Not SheetExistsExact(drawing, target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3244, , "Не найден переименованный лист: " & target
End Sub

Private Sub KC21_RenameRegularKE(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal model As Object, _
    ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long, _
    ByRef insertAfterSheet As String, ByVal bookPath As String, ByRef reviewRequired As Boolean)
    Dim sheets As New Collection, sheet As Object, original As Variant, actual As Variant
    Dim i As Long, first As Long, last As Long, af As Long, al As Long
    Dim tempNames() As String, oldAnchor As String, currentName As String, mappedCurrent As String
    Dim savedAnchor As String, savedSelection As String, savedKnown As String
    Dim drawingPath As String, started As Boolean, settingsAttempted As Boolean
    Dim failure As String, rollbackInfo As String, expected As String, candidate As String
    Dim xl As Object, book As Object, owns As Boolean, events As Boolean, eventsCaptured As Boolean
    Dim sources As Collection, balloons As Collection, markers As Collection, changes As New Collection
    Dim affectedSheets As New Collection
    Dim backup As String, saveAttempted As Boolean, rollbackErrors As Long
    Dim current As Object, missing As Object, uncertain As Object, allSheets As New Collection
    Dim allSources As Collection, orphans As New Collection, archive As Object, item As Variant
    Dim selectedNew As String, knownNew As String, anchorBase As String, identityOld As String
    Dim layoutCreated As Object, autoDelete As Boolean, auditPlans As Collection
    Dim changedNames As Boolean, reconcile As Boolean, sheetNamesAttempted As Boolean, balloonNamesAttempted As Boolean
    Dim kc46Inner As Double: kc46Inner = Timer
    On Error GoTo Failed
    reviewRequired = False
    oldAnchor = insertAfterSheet
    currentName = CStr(drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName)
    original = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(original, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3570, , "Нет списка листов."
    drawingPath = CStr(model.GetPathName)
    savedAnchor = KC14_Setting(drawingPath, "Anchor", "")
    savedSelection = KC14_Setting(drawingPath, "Sheets", "")
    savedKnown = KC14_Setting(drawingPath, "SheetsKnown", "")
    identityOld = KC14_Setting(drawingPath, "KEIdentityRequired", "0")
    Set current = KC36_CurrentNames(drawing)
    If total > 0 Then ReDim tempNames(1 To total)
    For i = 1 To total
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, oldNames(i)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3570, , "Не найден лист " & oldNames(i)
        Set sheet = drawing.GetCurrentSheet
        sheets.Add sheet
        If oldNames(i) <> newNames(i) Then
            affectedSheets.Add sheet: changedNames = True
        End If
        tempNames(i) = KC21_FreeTempName(drawing, "__KC_KE_TMP_", i)
    Next i
    ' Всё, что можно проверить без изменения документа, проверяем до первого SetName.
    ' v16.5.14: та же книга и тот же Excel далее используются подготовкой карты (без повторного открытия).
    KC14_Open xl, book, bookPath, False: owns = False
    KC37_CheckMigrationBook book, drawingPath
    KD_LoadPending book, drawing
    KC36_CheckReview book
    events = xl.EnableEvents: eventsCaptured = True: xl.EnableEvents = False
    KC35_CheckLegacyMarkerFiles book, drawingPath

    KC35_ValidateRaw book
    Set missing = KC36_Missing(book, current, savedKnown)
    Set uncertain = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): uncertain.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    ' Ищем реального владельца на ВСЕХ листах, даже если сохранённое имя исчезло.
    For Each item In current.Keys
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, CStr(item)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3600, , "Не активирован лист для сверки."
        Set sheet = drawing.GetCurrentSheet: allSheets.Add sheet
    Next item
    KC46_Mark kc46Inner, "КЭ: книга, история и подготовка листов (включая диалоги)"
    Set allSources = KC35_CollectSources(drawing, model, allSheets, "KE all current sheets")
    KC46_Mark kc46Inner, "КЭ: сбор источников"
    KR_CheckRegistryHint book, current, missing, savedKnown
    Set sources = KR_AffectedSources(allSources, oldNames, newNames, total)
    Set balloons = KC35_PlanBalloons(model, sources, oldNames, newNames, total)
    KC46_Mark kc46Inner, "КЭ: план привязок баллонов"
    Set gKC50Rebind = KC50_ResolveMovedMarkers(book, model, allSources, current)
    Set markers = KC36_PlanMarkers(book, model, allSources, current, missing, oldNames, newNames, total, orphans, uncertain)
    Set gKC50Rebind = Nothing
    KC46_Mark kc46Inner, "КЭ: план маркеров (включая диалог)"
    KC36_PlanRaw book, missing, uncertain, orphans
    autoDelete = KD_CanRebuild(book, missing, uncertain, orphans, markers)
    If autoDelete Or gKDForceRebuild Then
        KD_CaptureScope book, drawing
        Set auditPlans = KD_Audit(drawing, model)
    End If
    anchorBase = KC36_Anchor(drawing, current, oldAnchor)
    selectedNew = KC36_Selection(savedSelection, current, oldNames, newNames, total)
    knownNew = KC36_AllKnown(original, oldNames, newNames, total)
    reconcile = (changedNames Or missing.count > 0 Or orphans.count > 0 Or anchorBase <> oldAnchor Or _
        KC37_MarkerOwnersChanged(markers))
    If Not reconcile Then
        ' Никаких SetName/Save/архива, если состав и владельцы не требуют изменения.
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, currentName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3600, , "Не восстановлен активный лист."
        ' Без изменений КЭ можно безопасно использовать уже собранные объекты.
        KC46_Mark kc46Inner, "КЭ: проверки согласования"
        Set gKRBalloonPlans = KR_CurrentBalloonPlans(model, allSources, True)
        Set gKRSourceCounts = KR_CountSources(allSources)
        KC46_Mark kc46Inner, "КЭ: привязки существующих баллонов"
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, currentName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3600, , "Не восстановлен активный лист."
        GoTo SuccessCleanup
    End If
    If missing.count > 0 Or orphans.count > 0 Then
        If Not KC37_YesNo("Не найдены прежние имена КЭ: " & missing.count & "; потерянных/неподтверждённых записей: " & orphans.count & "." & vbCrLf & _
            "Создать резервную копию и архив, исключить потерянные привязки и согласовать оставшиеся листы?" & vbCrLf & _
            IIf(autoDelete, "После согласования будет пересобрана ВСЯ активная карта по оставшимся КЭ. Старые строки исчезнут только после успешного экспорта. Подбор баллонов по расстоянию отключён. Подтвердите: номера существующих баллонов вручную отдельно от Excel не менялись.", _
                "Владение потерянными записями не доказано. Строки карты НЕ удаляются; режим 1 остановится до ручной проверки."), "Удаление/потеря ссылок КЭ") Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3608, , "Согласование отменено до изменений."
    End If
    If (changedNames Or missing.count > 0) And Not autoDelete And Not gKDForceRebuild Then
        selectedNew = KR_AskSelection(drawing, savedSelection, oldNames, newNames, total, missing)
    End If
    backup = KC35_BackupBook(book)
    started = True
    If missing.count > 0 Or orphans.count > 0 Then
        KC36_CreateArchive book, model, missing, orphans, archive
        KC36_Quarantine orphans, changes
        If autoDelete Then
            KD_Register book, archive
            KC35_WriteCell archive.Range("B4"), "AUTO_REBUILD_PENDING", changes
            KC35_WriteCell archive.Range("A5"), "Ожидается полная пересборка режимом 1. Архив не удалять. Не вводите ПРОВЕРЕНО вместо пересборки.", changes
        Else
            reviewRequired = True
        End If
    End If
    If changedNames Then
    sheetNamesAttempted = True
    For i = 1 To total
        Set sheet = sheets(i)
        KC21_SetSheetName drawing, sheet, tempNames(i)
    Next i
    For i = 1 To total
        Set sheet = sheets(i)
        KC21_SetSheetName drawing, sheet, newNames(i)
    Next i
    End If
    actual = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(actual, af, al) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3571, , "Не прочитаны листы после переименования."
    If al - af <> last - first Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3571, , "Изменилось количество листов."
    For i = first To last
        expected = KC21_MappedName(CStr(original(i)), oldNames, newNames, total)
        If StrComp(CStr(actual(af + i - first)), expected, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3571, , "Не подтверждён порядок вкладок."
    Next i
    ' Ключи вычисляются заново по тому же исходному объекту, а не по номеру баллона.
    balloonNamesAttempted = True
    KC35_RekeyBalloons model, balloons
    KC35_WriteMarkerOwners model, markers, changes
    KC35_MapRaw book, oldNames, newNames, total, changes
    If Not gKDForceRebuild Or autoDelete Then
        KR_WriteLayout book, drawing, drawingPath, oldNames, newNames, total, changes, layoutCreated
    ElseIf changedNames Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Сначала завершите предыдущую пересборку, затем меняйте состав КЭ."
    End If
    ' Recollect after SetName: subsequent mode1 assignments use the same actual source/balloon objects.
    Set gKRBalloonPlans = KR_CurrentBalloonPlans(model, allSources)
    Set gKRSourceCounts = KR_CountSources(allSources)
    If gKDForceRebuild Then Set auditPlans = KD_Audit(drawing, model)
    settingsAttempted = True
    KC35_Setting drawingPath, "Anchor", KC21_MappedName(anchorBase, oldNames, newNames, total)
    KC35_Setting drawingPath, "Sheets", selectedNew
    KC35_Setting drawingPath, "SheetsKnown", knownNew
    KC35_Setting drawingPath, "KEIdentityRequired", "0"
    insertAfterSheet = KC21_MappedName(anchorBase, oldNames, newNames, total)
    mappedCurrent = KC21_MappedName(currentName, oldNames, newNames, total)
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, mappedCurrent) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3572, , "Не восстановлен активный лист."
    KC35_WriteCell book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10"), "PENDING", changes
    saveAttempted = True
    book.Save
    If Not book.saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3573, , "Книга с обновлёнными привязками не сохранена."
    model.SetSaveFlag
    KC37_Warning "Состав КЭ согласован с привязками. Резервная книга: " & backup & _
        ". Сохраните чертёж вместе с обновлённой картой."
    If reviewRequired Then
        KC37_Message "Состав КЭ согласован; потерянные привязки исключены из активного списка и сохранены в «" & archive.name & "»." & vbCrLf & _
            "Карта контроля оставлена без удаления строк. Режим 1 остановлен до восстановления маркеров и обработки баллонов." & vbCrLf & _
            "Сохраните чертёж. Проверьте/исправьте карту, введите ПРОВЕРЕНО в B4 архива и сохраните книгу." & vbCrLf & _
            "Затем повторите режим 1 первого макроса. Резервная книга: " & backup, "Проверка после удаления КЭ", vbExclamation
    End If
SuccessCleanup:
    On Error Resume Next
    If eventsCaptured Then xl.EnableEvents = events
    If owns Then book.Close False: xl.Quit
    Set book = Nothing: Set xl = Nothing
    On Error GoTo 0
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    failure = Err.Description
    KD_Reset
    Set gKRBalloonPlans = Nothing: Set gKRSourceCounts = Nothing
    On Error Resume Next
    If started Then
        ' Сначала освобождаем имена ключей; затем возвращаем физическим листам старые имена.
        If balloonNamesAttempted Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + KC35_RestoreBalloonNames(balloons)
        If sheetNamesAttempted Then
        For i = 1 To sheets.count
            Err.Clear
            Set sheet = sheets(i)
            candidate = KC21_FreeTempName(drawing, "__KC_KE_ROLLBACK_", i)
            If Err.number = 0 Then KC21_SetSheetName drawing, sheet, candidate
            If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
        Next i
        For i = 1 To sheets.count
            Err.Clear
            Set sheet = sheets(i)
            KC21_SetSheetName drawing, sheet, oldNames(i)
            If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
        Next i
        End If
        rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + KC35_RestoreCells(changes)
        rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + KC36_RemoveArchive(book, archive)
        rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + KC36_RemoveArchive(book, layoutCreated)
        If settingsAttempted Then
            Err.Clear: KC35_Setting drawingPath, "Anchor", savedAnchor
            If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
            Err.Clear: KC35_Setting drawingPath, "Sheets", savedSelection
            If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
            Err.Clear: KC35_Setting drawingPath, "SheetsKnown", savedKnown
            If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
            Err.Clear: KC35_Setting drawingPath, "KEIdentityRequired", identityOld
            If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
        End If
        If saveAttempted And Not book Is Nothing Then
            Err.Clear: book.Save
            If Err.number <> 0 Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
        End If
    End If
    insertAfterSheet = oldAnchor
    Err.Clear
    If Len(currentName) > 0 Then
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, currentName) Then rollbackErrors = rollbackErrors + 1
    End If
    If eventsCaptured Then xl.EnableEvents = events
    If owns And rollbackErrors = 0 Then
        If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
        If Not xl Is Nothing Then xl.Quit
    End If
    If rollbackErrors > 0 Then rollbackInfo = " Ошибок возврата: " & rollbackErrors & "; Excel оставлен открытым."
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3574, "KC21_RenameRegularKE", _
        "Переименование КЭ с привязками остановлено: " & failure & "." & rollbackInfo & _
        IIf(Len(backup) > 0, " Резервная книга: " & backup, "") & _
        " Основная обработка режима 1 ещё не начата. Проверьте копию чертежа."
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_OpenBook(ByVal path As String, ByRef xl As Object, ByRef book As Object, ByRef owns As Boolean)
    Dim item As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set xl = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then
        For Each item In xl.Workbooks
            If StrComp(CStr(item.FullName), path, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Set book = item: Exit For
        Next item
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    If book Is Nothing Then
        Set xl = KC53_TakeExcel()
        owns = True
        xl.AutomationSecurity = 3: xl.EnableEvents = False: xl.Visible = False
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(path, 0, False)
    End If
    If book.ReadOnly Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3580, , "Книга открыта только для чтения. Для миграции нужна запись: " & path
    If Not book.saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3580, , "Сначала сохраните пользовательские изменения рабочей книги."
End Sub

Private Function KC35_BackupBook(ByVal book As Object) As String
    KC35_BackupBook = KA_CreateBackup(book)
End Function

Private Sub KC35_CheckLegacyMarkerFiles(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawingPath As String)
    Dim ws As Object, fso As Object, suffix As Variant
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    For Each suffix In Array("_markers.xlsx", "_hashes.xlsx", "_at_notes.xlsx")
        If fso.FileExists(drawingPath & CStr(suffix)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3582, , _
            "Сначала перенесите прежние маркеры в лист «Маркеры КК» первым макросом. Старые внешние файлы не переписываются."
    Next suffix
End Sub

Private Function KC35_Sheet(ByVal book As Object, ByVal name As String) As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set KC35_Sheet = book.Worksheets(name)
End Function

Private Function KC35_GetRef(ByVal model As Object, ByVal ann As Object) As String
    Dim text As String, restored As Object, specific As Object
    On Error GoTo Unsupported
    If ann Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    text = KC40_RawRef(model, ann)
    If Len(text) > 0 Then
        Set restored = KC35_ResolveRef(model, text)
        If KC39_SameObject(restored, ann) Then KC35_GetRef = text: Exit Function
    End If
    Set specific = ann.GetSpecificAnnotation
    text = KC40_RawRef(model, specific)
    If Len(text) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Set restored = KC35_ResolveRef(model, text)
    If KC39_SameObject(restored, ann) Then KC35_GetRef = text
Unsupported:
End Function

Private Function KC35_ResolveRef(ByVal model As Object, ByVal text As String) As Object
    Dim bytes() As Byte, i As Long, state As Long, obj As Object
    On Error GoTo Unresolved
    If Len(text) = 0 Or Len(text) Mod 2 <> 0 Then Exit Function
    ReDim bytes(0 To Len(text) \ 2 - 1)
    For i = 0 To UBound(bytes)
        bytes(i) = CByte(CLng("&H" & Mid$(text, i * 2 + 1, 2)))
    Next i
    Set obj = model.Extension.GetObjectByPersistReference3(bytes, state)
    If state = 0 And Not obj Is Nothing Then Set KC35_ResolveRef = KC40_AsAnnotation(obj)
Unresolved:
End Function

Private Sub KC35_AddSource(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal ann As Object, ByVal kind As Long, _
                          ByVal sheet As Object, ByVal view As Object, ByVal note As Object, _
                          ByVal byName As Object, ByRef comparisons As Long)
    Dim rec As Object, have As Object, candidates As Collection, bucketName As String
    If ann Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    ' Совпадение имён не считается совпадением объектов; проверяем всех
    ' одноимённых кандидатов через прежнюю проверку идентичности KC39_SameObject.
    bucketName = "N|" & Trim$(CStr(ann.GetName))
    If Not byName.Exists(bucketName) Then byName.Add bucketName, New Collection
    Set candidates = byName.Item(bucketName)
    For Each rec In candidates
        comparisons = comparisons + 1
        Set have = rec("Ann")
        If KC39_SameObject(have, ann) Then
            If StrComp(CStr(rec("OldSheet")), CStr(sheet.GetName), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3901, , "Одна аннотация обнаружена на разных листах; перенос заблокирован."
            If Not view Is Nothing Then
                If Len(CStr(rec("OldView"))) > 0 Then
                    If StrComp(CStr(rec("OldView")), CStr(view.GetName2), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3901, , "Одна аннотация обнаружена в разных видах; владелец не определён."
                End If
                Set rec("View") = view
                rec("OldView") = CStr(view.GetName2)
            End If
            If Not note Is Nothing Then Set rec("Note") = note
            If CLng(rec("Type")) <> kind Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3901, , "Не совпал тип повторной аннотации."
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next rec
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "Ann", ann: rec.Add "Sheet", sheet: rec.Add "View", view: rec.Add "Note", note
    rec.Add "Type", kind: rec.Add "OldSheet", CStr(sheet.GetName): rec.Add "OldName", Trim$(CStr(ann.GetName))
    rec.Add "OldView", ""
    If Not view Is Nothing Then rec("OldView") = CStr(view.GetName2)
    result.Add rec
    candidates.Add rec
End Sub

Private Function KC35_CollectSources(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal sheets As Collection, Optional ByVal reason As String = "unspecified") As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, sheet As Variant, sel As Object, obj As Object, ann As Object
    Dim view As Object, dimObj As Object, note As Object, nothingObj As Object, i As Long, kind As Long
    Dim byName As Object, comparisons As Long, kc46Started As Double
    Dim sheetStarted As Double, phaseStarted As Double, sheetName As String, beforeCount As Long
    Dim activationTime As String, selectionTime As String, viewTime As String
    kc46Started = Timer
    Set byName = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): byName.CompareMode = vbBinaryCompare
    For Each sheet In sheets
        sheetStarted = Timer: phaseStarted = sheetStarted: beforeCount = result.count
        sheetName = CStr(sheet.GetName)
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, sheetName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3583, , "Не активирован исходный лист."
        activationTime = KCPerf_Seconds(phaseStarted): phaseStarted = Timer
        model.ClearSelection2 True
        model.Extension.SelectAll
        Set sel = model.SelectionManager
        For i = 1 To sel.GetSelectedObjectCount2(-1)
            kind = sel.GetSelectedObjectType3(i, -1)
            ' Размеры (14) ниже собираются только из видов. Здесь только GDT/сварка.
            If kind = 13 Or kind = 38 Then
                Set obj = sel.GetSelectedObject6(i, -1)
                Set ann = obj.GetAnnotation
                KC35_AddSource result, ann, kind, sheet, nothingObj, nothingObj, byName, comparisons
            End If
        Next i
        model.ClearSelection2 True
        selectionTime = KCPerf_Seconds(phaseStarted): phaseStarted = Timer
        Set view = drawing.GetFirstView
        Do While Not view Is Nothing
            Set dimObj = view.GetFirstDisplayDimension5
            Do While Not dimObj Is Nothing
                Set ann = dimObj.GetAnnotation
                KC35_AddSource result, ann, 14, sheet, view, nothingObj, byName, comparisons
                Set dimObj = dimObj.GetNext5
            Loop
            Set note = view.GetFirstNote
            Do While Not note Is Nothing
                Set ann = note.GetAnnotation
                KC35_AddSource result, ann, 15, sheet, view, note, byName, comparisons
                Set note = note.GetNext
            Loop
            Set view = view.GetNextView
        Loop
        viewTime = KCPerf_Seconds(phaseStarted)
        KC46_Trace "SOURCE_SHEET | " & reason & " | " & sheetName & "; новых объектов=" & (result.count - beforeCount) & _
            "; активация=" & activationTime & "; выделение=" & selectionTime & "; виды=" & viewTime & _
            "; всего=" & KCPerf_Seconds(sheetStarted)
    Next sheet
    KC46_Trace "Сбор источников [" & reason & "]: " & KCPerf_Seconds(kc46Started) & _
        "; листов=" & sheets.count & "; объектов=" & result.count & "; сравнений=" & comparisons
    Set KC35_CollectSources = result
End Function

Private Function KC35_Normalize(ByVal text As String) As String
    text = LCase$(Trim$(text))
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(216), "d"): text = Replace(text, ChrW(248), "d")
    text = Replace(text, Chr(177), "+-"): text = Replace(text, ",", ".")
    Do While InStr(text, "  ") > 0: text = Replace(text, "  ", " "): Loop
    KC35_Normalize = text
End Function

Private Function KC35_Hash(ByVal text As String) As String
    Dim a As Double, b As Double, code As Long, i As Long
    Const modulus As Double = 2147483647#
    a = 5381#: b = 7#
    For i = 1 To Len(text)
        code = AscW(Mid$(text, i, 1))
        If code < 0 Then code = code + 65536
        a = a * 33# + code: a = a - Int(a / modulus) * modulus
        b = b * 131# + code: b = b - Int(b / modulus) * modulus
    Next i
    KC35_Hash = Right$("00000000" & Hex$(CLng(a)), 8) & Right$("00000000" & Hex$(CLng(b)), 8)
End Function

Private Function KC35_SourceKey(ByVal model As Object, ByVal rec As Object, ByVal old As Boolean, ByVal extended As Boolean) As String
    Dim sheetName As String, sourceName As String, raw As String, ref As String, ann As Object, sheet As Object
    Set ann = rec("Ann"): Set sheet = rec("Sheet")
    If old Then
        sheetName = CStr(rec("OldSheet")): sourceName = CStr(rec("OldName"))
    Else
        sheetName = CStr(sheet.GetName): sourceName = Trim$(CStr(ann.GetName))
    End If
    If Len(sourceName) = 0 Then Exit Function
    raw = "KC_ID2|" & CStr(rec("Type")) & "|" & KC35_Normalize(sheetName) & "|" & LCase$(Trim$(sourceName))
    If extended Then
        ref = KC35_GetRef(model, ann)
        If Len(ref) = 0 Then Exit Function
        raw = raw & "|" & ref
    End If
    KC35_SourceKey = "KC_B2_" & KC35_Hash(raw)
End Function

Private Sub KC35_AddKeyCandidate(ByVal index As Object, ByVal key As String, ByVal rec As Object, ByVal extended As Boolean)
    Dim list As Collection, item As Object
    If Len(key) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not index.Exists(key) Then
        Set list = New Collection
        index.Add key, list
    End If
    Set list = index(key)
    Set item = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    item.Add "Source", rec: item.Add "Extended", extended
    list.Add item
End Sub

Private Function KC35_PlanBalloons(ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection, _
    ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, index As Object, used As Object, rec As Object, candidate As Object, plan As Object
    Dim name As String, key As String, note As Object, list As Collection, source As Object
    Dim legacy As Object, needLegacy As Boolean, extended As Boolean
    Set index = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each rec In sources
        name = CStr(rec("OldName"))
        If Left$(name, 5) = "KC_B_" Then needLegacy = True
        If Left$(name, 5) <> "KC_B_" And Left$(name, 6) <> "KC_B2_" Then
            KC35_AddKeyCandidate index, KC35_SourceKey(model, rec, True, False), rec, False
            KC35_AddKeyCandidate index, KC35_SourceKey(model, rec, True, True), rec, True
        End If
    Next rec
    If needLegacy Then Set legacy = KR_LegacyPositions(sources, oldNames, newNames, total)
    For Each rec In sources
        Set note = rec("Note")
        If Not note Is Nothing Then
            name = CStr(rec("OldName"))
            If Left$(name, 5) = "KC_B_" Or Left$(name, 6) = "KC_B2_" Then
                Set source = Nothing: extended = False
                If Left$(name, 5) = "KC_B_" Then
                    If Not legacy.Exists(name) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4103, , _
                        "Не воспроизведён точный исходный хеш старого баллона " & name & " на " & rec("OldSheet") & _
                        ". Листы не переименованы. Нужна ручная проверка этого баллона, а не повторный запуск режима 1."
                    Set list = legacy(name)
                    If list.count <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4103, , "Неоднозначный исходный хеш " & name
                    Set source = list(1)
                Else
                    If Not index.Exists(name) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , _
                        "Не найден исходный объект ключа " & name & " на листе " & rec("OldSheet") & ". Переименование не начато."
                    Set list = index(name)
                    If list.count <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , _
                        "Неоднозначный ключ " & name & KC39_CandidateDetails(list)
                    Set candidate = list(1): Set source = candidate("Source"): extended = CBool(candidate("Extended"))
                End If
                If used.Exists(name) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , "Несколько баллонов используют ключ " & name
                used.Add name, True
                If StrComp(CStr(source("OldSheet")), CStr(rec("OldSheet")), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , "Ключ баллона указывает на исходный объект другого листа."
                Set plan = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
                plan.Add "Ann", rec("Ann"): plan.Add "Source", source
                plan.Add "OldName", name: plan.Add "Extended", extended
                plan.Add "NewName", "": plan.Add "TempName", ""
                plan.Add "Legacy", (Left$(name, 5) = "KC_B_")
                result.Add plan
            End If
        End If
    Next rec
    Set KC35_PlanBalloons = result
End Function

Private Function KC35_PlanMarkers(ByVal book As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection, _
    ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, ws As Object, rec As Object, target As Object, ann As Object, owner As Object
    Dim row As Long, lastRow As Long, savedSheet As String, ref As String, hits As Long, plan As Object
    Dim affected As Boolean, expectedKind As String
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Set KC35_PlanMarkers = result: Exit Function
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "KC_MARKERS_1" Or _
       StrComp(CStr(ws.cells(2, 2).Value2), CStr(model.GetPathName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Неверный формат или владелец листа «Маркеры КК»."
    If CStr(ws.cells(5, 3).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 4).Value2) <> "Вид" Or _
       CStr(ws.cells(5, 5).Value2) <> "Имя" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 9).Value2) <> "PersistentReferenceHex" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Изменены заголовки листа маркеров."
    lastRow = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    For row = 6 To lastRow
        savedSheet = CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2)
        affected = (KC21_MappedName(savedSheet, oldNames, newNames, total) <> savedSheet)
        ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
        Set target = Nothing: Set owner = Nothing: hits = 0
        If Len(ref) > 0 Then Set target = KC35_ResolveRef(model, ref)
        If Not target Is Nothing Then
            For Each rec In sources
                Set ann = rec("Ann")
                If KC39_SameObject(ann, target) Then hits = hits + 1: Set owner = rec
            Next rec
        End If
        If hits > 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Неоднозначный владелец маркера в строке " & row
        If hits = 0 And affected Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , _
            "Маркер, строка " & row & ": нет подтверждённой persistent reference. Переименование не начато; проверьте/пересохраните список на исходных листах."
        If hits = 1 Then
            expectedKind = ""
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 14 Then expectedKind = "DIM"
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 15 Then expectedKind = "NOTE"
            If CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2) <> expectedKind Or Len(expectedKind) = 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Не совпал тип объекта маркера, строка " & row
            Set ann = owner("View")
            If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Не найден вид владельца маркера, строка " & row
            Set plan = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            plan.Add "Sheet", ws: plan.Add "Row", row: plan.Add "Owner", owner
            result.Add plan
        End If
    Next row
    Set KC35_PlanMarkers = result
End Function

Private Sub KC35_SetAnnName(ByVal ann As Object, ByVal name As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    ann.SetName2 name
    If StrComp(CStr(ann.GetName), name, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then
        Err.Clear
        ann.SetName name
    End If
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
    If StrComp(CStr(ann.GetName), name, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3586, , "Не подтверждено имя аннотации " & name
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_RekeyBalloons(ByVal model As Object, ByVal plans As Collection)
    Dim plan As Object, source As Object, ann As Object, seen As Object, i As Long, key As String
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each plan In plans
        Set source = plan("Source")
        If CBool(plan("Legacy")) Then
            key = CStr(source("LegacyNewKey"))
        Else
            key = KC35_SourceKey(model, source, False, CBool(plan("Extended")))
        End If
        If Len(key) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3586, , "Не вычислен новый ключ баллона."
        If seen.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3586, , "Совпали новые ключи разных баллонов."
        seen.Add key, True: plan("NewName") = key
    Next plan
    For Each plan In plans
        i = i + 1: Set ann = plan("Ann")
        plan("TempName") = "KC_RN_" & Format$(Now, "hhnnss") & "_" & CStr(i)
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, CStr(plan("TempName"))
    Next plan
    For Each plan In plans
        Set ann = plan("Ann")
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, CStr(plan("NewName"))
    Next plan
End Sub

Private Function KC35_RestoreBalloonNames(ByVal plans As Collection) As Long
    Dim plan As Object, ann As Object, i As Long, errors As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    For Each plan In plans
        i = i + 1: Set ann = plan("Ann"): Err.Clear
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, "KC_RB_" & Format$(Now, "hhnnss") & "_" & CStr(i)
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
    Next plan
    For Each plan In plans
        Set ann = plan("Ann"): Err.Clear
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, CStr(plan("OldName"))
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
    Next plan
    KC35_RestoreBalloonNames = errors
End Function

Private Sub KC35_WriteCell(ByVal cell As Object, ByVal text As String, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim rec As Object
    If cell.HasFormula Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3587, , "Формула в служебной ячейке " & cell.address & "; замена запрещена."
    If CStr(cell.Value2) = text Then Exit Sub
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "Cell", cell: rec.Add "Formula", cell.formula
    changes.Add rec
    cell.Value2 = text
    If CStr(cell.Value2) <> text Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3587, , "Не подтверждена запись " & cell.address
End Sub

Private Function KC35_RestoreCells(ByVal changes As Collection) As Long
    Dim i As Long, rec As Object, cell As Object, errors As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    For i = changes.count To 1 Step -1
        Set rec = changes(i): Set cell = rec("Cell"): Err.Clear
        cell.formula = rec("Formula")
        If Err.number <> 0 Then
            errors = errors + 1
        ElseIf CStr(cell.formula) <> CStr(rec("Formula")) Then
            errors = errors + 1
        End If
    Next i
    KC35_RestoreCells = errors
End Function

Private Sub KC35_WriteMarkerOwners(ByVal model As Object, ByVal plans As Collection, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim plan As Object, owner As Object, ws As Object, ann As Object, sheet As Object, view As Object
    Dim row As Long, ref As String
    For Each plan In plans
        Set owner = plan("Owner"): Set ws = plan("Sheet"): row = CLng(plan("Row"))
        Set ann = owner("Ann"): Set sheet = owner("Sheet"): Set view = owner("View")
        ' Live owner was captured before SetName. Persistent reference is an optional enhancement.
        ref = KC35_GetRef(model, ann)
        If Len(ref) = 0 Then ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 3), CStr(sheet.GetName), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 4), CStr(view.GetName2), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 5), CStr(ann.GetName), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 9), ref, changes
        ' F:H and J:K (original text, coordinates, #/@ flags) are untouched.
    Next plan
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_ValidateRaw(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim ws As Object
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Данные из чертежа")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3589, , "Нет листа «Данные из чертежа»."
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 23).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(ws.cells(1, 24).Value2) <> "Чертёжный вид" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3589, , "Изменена схема исходных данных W:X."
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_MapRaw(ByVal book As Object, ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, _
                       ByVal total As Long, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim ws As Object, row As Long, old As String, mapped As String, oldView As String
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    For row = 2 To ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        old = CStr(ws.cells(row, 23).Value2)
        mapped = KC21_MappedName(old, oldNames, newNames, total)
        If mapped <> old Then
            oldView = CStr(ws.cells(row, 24).Value2)
            KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 23), mapped, changes
            ' Меняем только имя служебного вида листа, а не произвольную подпись вида.
            If oldView = old Then KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 24), mapped, changes
        End If
    Next row
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_Setting(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal key As String, ByVal value As String)
    KC14_SaveSetting drawing, key, value
    If KC14_Setting(drawing, key, ChrW(1)) <> value Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3590, , "Не сохранён параметр " & key
End Sub

Private Function KC36_IsRegular(ByVal name As String) As Boolean
    KC36_IsRegular = (RegularKENumber(name) > 0 Or KC21_IsNewKE(name))
End Function

Private Function KC36_CurrentNames(ByVal drawing As Object) As Object
    Dim result As Object, names As Variant, i As Long, first As Long, last As Long
    Set result = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): result.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3600, , "Нет списка текущих листов."
    For i = first To last: result.Add CStr(names(i)), True: Next i
    Set KC36_CurrentNames = result
End Function

Private Sub KC36_CheckReview(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim ws As Object
    For Each ws In book.Worksheets
        If Left$(CStr(ws.name), 9) = "КЭ_Архив_" Then
            If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_ARCHIVE_1" Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3601, , "Неизвестный формат архива КЭ: " & ws.name
            If CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2) <> CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B2").Value2) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3601, , "Архив относится к другому чертежу: " & ws.name
            If Not KD_CompletedArchive(ws) And UCase$(Trim$(CStr(ws.Range("B4").Value2))) <> "ПРОВЕРЕНО" Then
                If Not KD_Allowed(book, ws) Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3601, , "Требуется проверка архива " & ws.name & ". Автопродолжение не подтверждено. ОК не обновлены."
            End If
        End If
    Next ws
End Sub

Private Sub KC36_AddMissing(ByVal missing As Object, ByVal current As Object, ByVal name As String)
    If KC36_IsRegular(name) And Not current.Exists(name) Then missing(name) = True
End Sub

Private Function KC36_Missing(ByVal book As Object, ByVal current As Object, ByVal known As String) As Object
    Dim result As Object, ws As Object, row As Long, last As Long, count As Long, value As Variant, owner As String
    Set result = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): result.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    ' Registry SheetsKnown is only advisory. It may belong to a deleted earlier workbook.
    ' Actual historical ownership comes from the CURRENT validated workbook, not the registry.
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Данные из чертежа")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Слишком большой список исходных данных."
        For row = 2 To last: KC36_AddMissing result, current, CStr(ws.cells(row, 23).Value2): Next row
    End If
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Слишком большой список маркеров."
        For row = 6 To last: KC36_AddMissing result, current, CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2): Next row
    End If
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        owner = CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B2").Value2)
        If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_LAYOUT_1" Or StrComp(CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2), owner, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Не подтверждён владелец/формат истории состава КЭ."
        If CStr(ws.Range("B3").Value2) <> "NEEDS_MODE1" Then
            value = ws.Range("B4").Value2
            If Not IsNumeric(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Повреждён счётчик истории КЭ."
            If CDbl(value) < 1 Or CDbl(value) > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Некорректный счётчик истории КЭ."
            count = CLng(value)
            If CDbl(value) <> CDbl(count) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Счётчик истории КЭ должен быть целым."
            For row = 5 To count + 4
                If RegularKENumber(CStr(ws.cells(row, 1).Value2)) < 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Повреждено имя в истории КЭ."
                KC36_AddMissing result, current, CStr(ws.cells(row, 1).Value2)
            Next row
        Else
            If CStr(ws.Range("B4").Value2) <> "0" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Некорректно сброшена история копии карты."
        End If
    End If
    Set KC36_Missing = result
End Function

Private Sub KC36_AddOrphan(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal ws As Object, ByVal row As Long, _
                           ByVal width As Long, ByVal reason As String, ByVal marker As Boolean)
    Dim rec As Object, c As Long
    ' Формулы в очищаемых ячейках нельзя заменять константами даже при откате.
    If marker Then
        For c = 1 To width
            If ws.cells(row, c).HasFormula Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3602, , "Формула в строке маркера " & row
        Next c
    Else
        For c = 23 To 24
            If ws.cells(row, c).HasFormula Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3602, , "Формула W:X в исходной строке " & row
        Next c
    End If
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "Sheet", ws: rec.Add "Row", row: rec.Add "Width", width
    rec.Add "Reason", reason: rec.Add "Marker", marker
    result.Add rec
End Sub

Private Function KC36_PlanMarkers(ByVal book As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection, _
    ByVal current As Object, ByVal missing As Object, ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, _
    ByVal total As Long, ByVal orphans As Collection, ByVal uncertain As Object) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, ws As Object, row As Long, last As Long, savedSheet As String, ref As String
    Dim target As Object, owner As Object, rec As Object, ann As Object, view As Object, plan As Object
    Dim hits As Long, expectedKind As String, affected As Boolean, namesUsed As Long, namesDetail As String
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Set KC36_PlanMarkers = result: Exit Function
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "KC_MARKERS_1" Or _
       StrComp(CStr(ws.cells(2, 2).Value2), CStr(model.GetPathName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Не совпал формат/владелец маркеров."
    If CStr(ws.cells(5, 3).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 4).Value2) <> "Вид" Or _
       CStr(ws.cells(5, 5).Value2) <> "Имя" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 9).Value2) <> "PersistentReferenceHex" Or _
       CStr(ws.cells(5, 10).Value2) <> "#" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 11).Value2) <> "@" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Повреждены заголовки маркеров."
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Слишком много строк маркеров."
    For row = 6 To last
        ' Пусты только полностью исключённые ранее строки A:K; неполные строки не скрываем.
        If book.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range(ws.cells(row, 1), ws.cells(row, 11))) = 0 Then GoTo NextMarker
        savedSheet = CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2): ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
        affected = (KC21_MappedName(savedSheet, oldNames, newNames, total) <> savedSheet)
        Set target = Nothing: Set owner = Nothing: hits = 0
        If Len(ref) > 0 Then Set target = KC35_ResolveRef(model, ref)
        If Len(ref) = 0 And current.Exists(savedSheet) Then
            Set owner = KR_ExactOwner(sources, savedSheet, CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2), _
                CStr(ws.cells(row, 5).Value2), CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2), hits)
            If hits = 0 And KC50_HasRebind(row) Then
                Set owner = gKC50Rebind(CStr(row)): hits = 1
            End If
            If hits <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4102, , _
                KC49_MarkerMismatch(model, sources, ws, row, hits)
            If affected Or missing.count > 0 Then
                namesUsed = namesUsed + 1
                namesDetail = namesDetail & vbCrLf & "Строка " & row & ": " & savedSheet & " / " & _
                    CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2) & " / " & CStr(ws.cells(row, 5).Value2)
            End If
        End If
        If Not target Is Nothing Then
            For Each rec In sources
                Set ann = rec("Ann")
                If KC39_SameObject(ann, target) Then hits = hits + 1: Set owner = rec
            Next rec
        End If
        If hits > 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Неоднозначный владелец строки маркера " & row
        If hits = 1 Then
            expectedKind = ""
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 14 Then expectedKind = "DIM"
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 15 Then expectedKind = "NOTE"
            If expectedKind = "" Or CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2) <> expectedKind Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Не совпал тип владельца маркера " & row
            Set view = owner("View")
            If view Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Не определён вид владельца маркера " & row
            Set plan = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            plan.Add "Sheet", ws: plan.Add "Row", row: plan.Add "Owner", owner
            result.Add plan
        ElseIf Not target Is Nothing Then
            ' Разрешившийся, но не собранный объект НЕ считается удалённым.
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Объект маркера существует, но владелец не подтверждён: строка " & row
        ElseIf missing.Exists(savedSheet) Then
            KC36_AddOrphan orphans, ws, row, 11, "Исходное имя листа отсутствует; ссылка не подтверждена: " & savedSheet, True
        ElseIf Len(ref) > 0 And KC36_IsRegular(savedSheet) Then
            ' Это может быть удалённый объект или отказ API. Не утверждаем, что лист удалён.
            KC36_AddOrphan orphans, ws, row, 11, "Persistent reference не разрешается; владелец неизвестен: " & savedSheet, True
            uncertain(savedSheet) = True
        ElseIf affected Or (missing.count > 0 And KC36_IsRegular(savedSheet)) Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Не подтверждён исходный владелец маркера " & row & _
                "; перенос без единственного исходного объекта запрещён."
        End If
NextMarker:
    Next row
    If namesUsed > 0 Then
        KC47_Decision "AUTO_YES_MARKERS", "Однозначных совпадений по старым именам=" & namesUsed & _
            "; книга=" & CStr(book.FullName) & namesDetail & _
            "; отсутствие подмены одноимённых объектов НЕ доказано; применена разрешённая пользователем политика."
    End If
    Set KC36_PlanMarkers = result
End Function

Private Sub KC36_PlanRaw(ByVal book As Object, ByVal missing As Object, ByVal uncertain As Object, ByVal orphans As Collection)
    Dim ws As Object, row As Long, last As Long, name As String
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3604, , "Слишком много исходных строк."
    For row = 2 To last
        name = CStr(ws.cells(row, 23).Value2)
        If missing.Exists(name) Then
            KC36_AddOrphan orphans, ws, row, 26, "Исходное имя листа отсутствует: " & name, False
        ElseIf uncertain.Exists(name) Then
            KC36_AddOrphan orphans, ws, row, 26, "Неоднозначная история владельца; W:X не переносятся по имени: " & name, False
        End If
    Next row
End Sub

Private Function KC36_Selection(ByVal value As String, ByVal current As Object, _
    ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As String
    Dim name As Variant, result As String, used As Object, mapped As String
    If value = "*" Then KC36_Selection = "*": Exit Function
    Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): used.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    For Each name In Split(value, vbLf)
        If current.Exists(CStr(name)) Then
            mapped = KC21_MappedName(CStr(name), oldNames, newNames, total)
            If Not used.Exists(mapped) Then
                If Len(result) > 0 Then result = result & vbLf
                result = result & mapped: used.Add mapped, True
            End If
        End If
    Next name
    KC36_Selection = result
End Function

Private Function KC36_AllKnown(ByVal names As Variant, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As String
    Dim i As Long, first As Long, last As Long, result As String
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3605, , "Нет списка для SheetsKnown."
    For i = first To last
        If i > first Then result = result & vbLf
        result = result & KC21_MappedName(CStr(names(i)), oldNames, newNames, total)
    Next i
    KC36_AllKnown = result
End Function

Private Function KC36_Anchor(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal current As Object, ByVal old As String) As String
    Dim name As String
    If current.Exists(old) Then KC36_Anchor = old: Exit Function
    If current.Exists("Титульный лист") Then
        name = "Титульный лист"
    ElseIf current.Exists("ОК1") Then
        name = "ОК1"
    Else
        name = CStr(drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName)
    End If
    If Not KC37_YesNo("Прежний ориентир вставки отсутствует: " & old & "." & vbCrLf & _
        "Использовать существующий лист «" & name & "»?", "Ориентир после удаления КЭ") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3605, , "Новый ориентир не подтверждён. Ничего не изменено."
    KC36_Anchor = name
End Function

Private Sub KC36_ArchiveText(ByVal cell As Object, ByVal text As String)
    ' Даже строка =... сохраняется как текст, а не выполняемая формула.
    cell.NumberFormat = "@": cell.Value2 = "'" & text
    If cell.HasFormula Or CStr(cell.Value2) <> text Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3606, , "Не подтверждён текст архива."
End Sub

Private Sub KC36_ArchiveRow(ByVal archive As Object, ByRef destRow As Long, ByVal source As Object, _
    ByVal sourceRow As Long, ByVal width As Long, ByVal reason As String)
    Dim c As Long, cell As Object, text As String
    For c = 1 To width
        Set cell = source.cells(sourceRow, c)
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 1), CStr(source.name)
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 2), CStr(sourceRow)
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 3), reason
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 4), CStr(cell.address(False, False))
        If IsError(cell.Value2) Then text = CStr(cell.text) Else text = CStr(cell.Value2)
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 5), text
        If cell.HasFormula Then KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 6), CStr(cell.formula)
        destRow = destRow + 1
    Next c
End Sub

Private Sub KC36_CreateArchive(ByVal book As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal missing As Object, _
    ByVal orphans As Collection, ByRef archive As Object)
    Dim name As String, suffix As Long, probe As Object, rec As Object, source As Object
    Dim destRow As Long, row As Long, last As Long, item As Variant, headers As Variant, c As Long
    Dim estimated As Double
    Set source = book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    last = LastOKContinuationRow(source)
    estimated = 8# + missing.count + CDbl(last) * 5#
    For Each rec In orphans: estimated = estimated + CDbl(rec("Width")): Next rec
    If last > 100000 Or estimated > source.rows.count Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3607, , "Архив слишком велик."
    name = "КЭ_Архив_" & Format$(Now, "yymmdd_hhnnss")
    Do
        Set probe = KC35_Sheet(book, name)
        If probe Is Nothing Then Exit Do
        suffix = suffix + 1: name = "КЭ_Архив_" & Format$(Now, "yymmdd_hhnnss") & "_" & suffix
        If Len(name) > 31 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3607, , "Не найдено свободное имя архива."
    Loop
    Set archive = book.Worksheets.Add(After:=book.Worksheets(book.Worksheets.count))
    archive.name = name
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A1"), "KC_KE_ARCHIVE_1"
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A2"), "Чертёж": KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("B2"), CStr(model.GetPathName)
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A3"), "Создан": KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("B3"), Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A4"), "Проверка": KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("B4"), "ТРЕБУЕТ ПРОВЕРКИ"
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A5"), "После проверки/исправления карты введите ПРОВЕРЕНО в B4 и сохраните книгу."
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A6"), "Карточные строки НЕ удалены. Значения и формулы ниже — текстовый снимок до изменений."
    headers = Array("Лист книги", "Строка", "Причина", "Ячейка", "Значение", "Формула (текст)")
    For c = 0 To 5: KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(7, c + 1), CStr(headers(c)): Next c
    destRow = 8
    For Each item In missing.Keys
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 1), "Отсутствующее имя листа"
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 3), CStr(item): destRow = destRow + 1
    Next item
    For Each rec In orphans
        Set source = rec("Sheet")
        KC36_ArchiveRow archive, destRow, source, CLng(rec("Row")), CLng(rec("Width")), CStr(rec("Reason"))
    Next rec
    Set source = book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    last = LastOKContinuationRow(source)
    If last > 100000 Or destRow + last * 5 > archive.rows.count Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3607, , "Архив слишком велик."
    For row = 1 To last
        KC36_ArchiveRow archive, destRow, source, row, 5, "Карта ДО согласования; снимок для ручной сверки, не активные маркеры"
    Next row
    archive.Columns("A:F").ColumnWidth = 24
    archive.Columns("C").ColumnWidth = 55: archive.Columns("E:F").ColumnWidth = 55
    archive.Range("A1:F7").Font.Bold = True
    archive.Range("B4").Interior.Color = RGB(255, 230, 140)
    archive.Range("A7:F" & CStr(destRow - 1)).WrapText = True
    archive.Range("A7:F" & CStr(destRow - 1)).AutoFilter
End Sub

Private Sub KC36_Quarantine(ByVal orphans As Collection, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim rec As Object, ws As Object, row As Long, c As Long
    For Each rec In orphans
        Set ws = rec("Sheet"): row = CLng(rec("Row"))
        If CBool(rec("Marker")) Then
            ' Не удаляем Rows: номера прочих строк и формулы карты остаются на месте.
            For c = 1 To 11: KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, c), "", changes: Next c
        Else
            ' A:V,Y:Z остаются для сопоставления прежних ручных блоков; только связь W:X исключается.
            KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 23), "", changes
            KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 24), "", changes
        End If
    Next rec
End Sub

Private Function KC36_RemoveArchive(ByVal book As Object, ByVal archive As Object) As Long
    Dim alerts As Boolean
    If archive Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    On Error GoTo Failed
    alerts = book.Application.DisplayAlerts
    book.Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    archive.Delete
    book.Application.DisplayAlerts = alerts
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC36_RemoveArchive = 1
    On Error Resume Next
    book.Application.DisplayAlerts = alerts
End Function

' v16.5.3-review: КЭ1 — фиксированный обычный лист, новые КЭ допускаются только после него.
Private Sub KC37_AssertKE1(ByVal drawing As Object)
    Dim names As Variant, first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, name As String, found As Boolean, seenRegular As Boolean
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , "Нет списка листов."
    For i = first To last
        name = CStr(names(i))
        If RegularKENumber(name) > 0 Or KC21_IsNewKE(name) Then
            If Not seenRegular Then
                If StrComp(name, "КЭ1", vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , _
                    "Первым обычным КЭ должен быть КЭ1. Восстановите его или переместите новый лист после КЭ1. Автозамены КЭ1 нет."
                seenRegular = True
            End If
            If RegularKENumber(name) = 1 Then
                If StrComp(name, "КЭ1", vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , "Неоднозначное имя КЭ1: " & name
                found = True
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If Not found Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , "Нет обязательного КЭ1. Восстановите лист из копии; другой КЭ не назначается вместо него."
End Sub

Private Function KC37_RegularSnapshot(ByVal drawing As Object) As String
    Dim names As Variant, first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, name As String, result As String
    KC37_AssertKE1 drawing
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3701, , "Не прочитан состав КЭ."
    For i = first To last
        name = CStr(names(i))
        If RegularKENumber(name) > 0 Or KC21_IsNewKE(name) Then
            If Len(result) > 0 Then result = result & vbLf
            result = result & name
        End If
    Next i
    KC37_RegularSnapshot = result
End Function

Private Sub KC37_CheckLayoutSlot(ByVal book As Object, ByVal owner As String)
    Dim ws As Object
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_LAYOUT_1" Or _
       StrComp(CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2), owner, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3702, , "Лист КЭ_Состав занят другим форматом/владельцем. Автоматическая перезапись запрещена."
End Sub

' Только новая независимая копия книги: прежний состав НЕ считается подтверждённым для другого чертежа.
Private Sub KC37_InvalidateCopiedLayout(ByVal book As Object, ByVal oldOwner As String, ByVal newOwner As String)
    Dim ws As Object
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_LAYOUT_1" Or _
       StrComp(CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2), oldOwner, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3702, , "Не подтверждён владелец состава в копируемой книге."
    ws.Range("B2").Value2 = newOwner
    ws.Range("B3").Value2 = "NEEDS_MODE1"
    ws.Range("B4").Value2 = 0
End Sub


' Вызывается ТОЛЬКО режимом 1, до LoadSelection, PENDING и восстановления #/@.
Private Function KC37_PrepareRegularKE() As Boolean
    Dim oldNames() As String, newNames() As String, total As Long, changed As Long
    Dim path As String, anchor As String, review As Boolean, fso As Object
    KD_Reset
    gKC37PreparedPath = "": gKC37PreparedLayout = ""
    Set gKRBalloonPlans = Nothing: Set gKRSourceCounts = Nothing
    KC21_PlanRegularKE swDraw, oldNames, newNames, total, changed
    path = KC14_Resolve(swModel.GetPathName, False)
    gKC37PreparedPath = path
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Not fso.FileExists(path) Then
        ' Новая карта ещё не создана. Не подменяем потерянную книгу пустой без проверок истории.
        If changed > 0 Then KC37_Bootstrap swDraw, swModel, oldNames, newNames, total
        gKC37PreparedLayout = KC37_RegularSnapshot(swDraw)
        KC37_PrepareRegularKE = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    anchor = KC14_Setting(swModel.GetPathName, "Anchor", "")
    If Len(anchor) = 0 Then
        If SheetExistsExact(swDraw, "Титульный лист") Then
            anchor = "Титульный лист"
        ElseIf SheetExistsExact(swDraw, "ОК1") Then
            anchor = "ОК1"
        Else
            anchor = CStr(swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName)
        End If
    End If
    KC21_RenameRegularKE swDraw, swModel, oldNames, newNames, total, anchor, path, review
    If review Then Exit Function
    gKC37PreparedLayout = KC37_RegularSnapshot(swDraw)
    KC37_PrepareRegularKE = True
End Function

' Ранний этап НЕ требует READY: он выполняется до подготовки редактора и допускает PENDING.
Private Sub KC37_CheckMigrationBook(ByVal book As Object, ByVal owner As String)
    Dim meta As Object, status As String
    Set meta = KC35_Sheet(book, "ОК_Служебное")
    If meta Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3703, , "Нет служебного листа рабочей карты."
    If StrComp(CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2), owner, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3703, , "Рабочая карта относится к другому чертежу."
    status = CStr(meta.Range("B10").Value2)
    If status <> "READY" And status <> "PENDING" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3703, , "Неизвестное состояние рабочей карты: " & status
    If book.ReadOnly Or Not book.saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3703, , "Нужна сохранённая рабочая карта с доступом на запись."
    KC37_CheckLayoutSlot book, owner
End Sub

' Ограниченный первый запуск без рабочей книги: только если нет истории, требующей миграции.
Private Sub KC37_Bootstrap(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
                          ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long)
    Dim oldSelection As String, oldKnown As String, oldAnchor As String, oldIdentity As String, drawingPath As String
    Dim current As Object, sources As Collection, sheets As New Collection, changedSheets As New Collection
    Dim sheet As Object, rec As Object, item As Variant, name As String, tempNames() As String
    Dim i As Long, originalCurrent As String, started As Boolean, settingsAttempted As Boolean
    Dim fso As Object, failure As String, errors As Long, original As Variant, actual As Variant
    Dim first As Long, last As Long, af As Long, al As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    drawingPath = CStr(model.GetPathName): originalCurrent = CStr(drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName)
    Set current = KC36_CurrentNames(drawing): original = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(original, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , "Нет списка листов."
    oldSelection = KC14_Setting(drawingPath, "Sheets", "")
    oldKnown = KC14_Setting(drawingPath, "SheetsKnown", "")
    oldAnchor = KC14_Setting(drawingPath, "Anchor", "")
    oldIdentity = KC14_Setting(drawingPath, "KEIdentityRequired", "0")
    For Each item In Split(oldKnown, vbLf)
        If KC36_IsRegular(CStr(item)) And Not current.Exists(CStr(item)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , _
            "Рабочей книги нет, а прежний КЭ отсутствует: " & CStr(item) & ". Сначала восстановите/привяжите карту для архивации истории."
    Next item
    If Len(oldAnchor) > 0 And Not current.Exists(oldAnchor) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , _
        "Нет прежнего ориентира вставки. Сначала задайте существующий ориентир в настройках."
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    For Each item In Array("_markers.xlsx", "_hashes.xlsx", "_at_notes.xlsx")
        If fso.FileExists(drawingPath & CStr(item)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , _
            "Найдены прежние маркеры без рабочей карты. Сначала восстановите книгу и выполните миграцию; переименование не начато."
    Next item
    If total > 0 Then ReDim tempNames(1 To total)
    For i = 1 To total
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, oldNames(i)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , "Не активирован исходный КЭ."
        Set sheet = drawing.GetCurrentSheet: sheets.Add sheet
        If oldNames(i) <> newNames(i) Then changedSheets.Add sheet
        tempNames(i) = KC21_FreeTempName(drawing, "__KC_KE_TMP_", i)
    Next i
    Set sources = KC35_CollectSources(drawing, model, changedSheets, "bootstrap changed names")
    For Each rec In sources
        name = CStr(rec("OldName"))
        If Left$(name, 5) = "KC_B_" Or Left$(name, 6) = "KC_B2_" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , _
            "Есть прежние служебные ключи, но нет рабочей карты. Сначала восстановите привязку книги."
    Next rec
    If Not KC37_YesNo("Рабочей карты пока нет. Упорядочить обычные КЭ перед созданием новой карты?" & vbCrLf & _
        "КЭ1 остаётся неизменным. История известных листов и служебные ключи проверены; книгу ещё нельзя резервировать." & vbCrLf & _
        "Используйте копию чертежа.", "Первый запуск: КЭ") Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , "Первичное согласование отменено."
    started = True
    For i = 1 To total
        Set sheet = sheets(i): KC21_SetSheetName drawing, sheet, tempNames(i)
    Next i
    For i = 1 To total
        Set sheet = sheets(i): KC21_SetSheetName drawing, sheet, newNames(i)
    Next i
    actual = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(actual, af, al) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , "Не подтверждены имена."
    If al - af <> last - first Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , "Изменилось число листов."
    For i = first To last
        If CStr(actual(af + i - first)) <> KC21_MappedName(CStr(original(i)), oldNames, newNames, total) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , "Не подтверждён порядок вкладок."
    Next i
    settingsAttempted = True
    KC35_Setting drawingPath, "Sheets", KC36_Selection(oldSelection, current, oldNames, newNames, total)
    KC35_Setting drawingPath, "SheetsKnown", KC36_AllKnown(original, oldNames, newNames, total)
    KC35_Setting drawingPath, "Anchor", KC21_MappedName(oldAnchor, oldNames, newNames, total)
    KC35_Setting drawingPath, "KEIdentityRequired", "0"
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, KC21_MappedName(originalCurrent, oldNames, newNames, total)) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , "Не восстановлен активный лист."
    model.SetSaveFlag
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    failure = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If started Then
        For i = 1 To sheets.count
            Err.Clear: Set sheet = sheets(i)
            name = KC21_FreeTempName(drawing, "__KC_KE_ROLLBACK_", i)
            If Err.number = 0 Then KC21_SetSheetName drawing, sheet, name
            If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
        Next i
        For i = 1 To sheets.count
            Err.Clear: Set sheet = sheets(i): KC21_SetSheetName drawing, sheet, oldNames(i)
            If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
        Next i
    End If
    If settingsAttempted Then
        Err.Clear: KC35_Setting drawingPath, "Sheets", oldSelection
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
        Err.Clear: KC35_Setting drawingPath, "SheetsKnown", oldKnown
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
        Err.Clear: KC35_Setting drawingPath, "Anchor", oldAnchor
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
        Err.Clear: KC35_Setting drawingPath, "KEIdentityRequired", oldIdentity
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
    End If
    Err.Clear
    If Len(originalCurrent) > 0 Then
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, originalCurrent) Then errors = errors + 1
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3704, , failure & ". Ошибок возврата: " & errors
End Sub

' Состав заверяется только при успешном экспорте; до READY и Save рабочей книги.
Private Sub KC37_StampLayout(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal owner As String)
    Dim ws As Object, names As Variant, i As Long, oldNames() As String, newNames() As String, total As Long, changed As Long
    If Len(gKC37PreparedLayout) = 0 Or KC37_RegularSnapshot(drawing) <> gKC37PreparedLayout Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3705, , "Состав КЭ изменился после раннего согласования. READY не установлен; повторите режим 1."
    KC21_PlanRegularKE drawing, oldNames, newNames, total, changed
    If changed > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3705, , "Состав КЭ изменился во время режима 1. Повторите согласование; READY не установлен."
    KC37_CheckLayoutSlot book, owner
    KC36_CheckReview book
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = book.Worksheets.Add(After:=book.Worksheets(book.Worksheets.count))
        ws.name = "КЭ_Состав"
    End If
    ws.Range("A1:B100004").ClearContents
    names = Split(KC37_RegularSnapshot(drawing), vbLf)
    ws.Range("A1").Value2 = "KC_KE_LAYOUT_1"
    ws.Range("A2").Value2 = "Чертёж": ws.Range("B2").Value2 = owner
    ws.Range("A3").Value2 = "Экспорт": ws.Range("B3").Value2 = "TS: " & Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")
    ws.Range("A4").Value2 = "Обычных КЭ": ws.Range("B4").Value2 = UBound(names) + 1
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names): ws.cells(5 + i, 1).Value2 = CStr(names(i)): Next i
    ws.Visible = 2
End Sub

Private Function KC37_YesNo(ByVal text As String, ByVal title As String) As Boolean
    KC37_YesNo = (MsgBox(text, vbYesNo + vbQuestion, title) = vbYes)
End Function

Private Sub KC37_Message(ByVal text As String, ByVal title As String, ByVal icon As Long)
    MsgBox text, icon, title
End Sub

Private Sub KC37_Warning(ByVal text As String)
    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, text
End Sub

Private Function KC37_MarkerOwnersChanged(ByVal plans As Collection) As Boolean
    Dim plan As Object, owner As Object, ws As Object, row As Long
    For Each plan In plans
        Set owner = plan("Owner"): Set ws = plan("Sheet"): row = CLng(plan("Row"))
        If CStr(owner("OldSheet")) <> CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2) Or _
           CStr(owner("OldView")) <> CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2) Or _
           CStr(owner("OldName")) <> CStr(ws.cells(row, 5).Value2) Then
            KC37_MarkerOwnersChanged = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next plan
End Function


Private Function KC39_SameObject(ByVal leftObj As Object, ByVal rightObj As Object) As Boolean
    Dim app As Object, state As Long, leftSpecific As Object, rightSpecific As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    If leftObj Is Nothing Or rightObj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If leftObj Is rightObj Then KC39_SameObject = True: Exit Function
    Err.Clear: Set app = Application.SldWorks: state = 2
    state = CLng(app.IsSame(leftObj, rightObj))
    If Err.number = 0 Then
        If state = 1 Then KC39_SameObject = True: Exit Function
        If state = 0 Then Exit Function
    End If
    Err.Clear
    Set leftSpecific = leftObj.GetSpecificAnnotation
    Set rightSpecific = rightObj.GetSpecificAnnotation
    If Err.number <> 0 Then Exit Function
    If leftSpecific Is Nothing Or rightSpecific Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If leftSpecific Is rightSpecific Then KC39_SameObject = True: Exit Function
    Err.Clear: state = 2
    state = CLng(app.IsSame(leftSpecific, rightSpecific))
    KC39_SameObject = (Err.number = 0 And state = 1)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function KC39_CandidateDetails(ByVal candidates As Collection) As String
    Dim item As Object, source As Object, text As String, i As Long
    For Each item In candidates
        i = i + 1: If i > 8 Then Exit For
        Set source = item("Source")
        text = text & vbCrLf & CStr(i) & ") " & CStr(source("OldSheet")) & " | " & _
            CStr(source("OldView")) & " | " & CStr(source("OldName")) & " | тип " & CStr(source("Type"))
    Next item
    If candidates.count > 8 Then text = text & vbCrLf & "..."
    KC39_CandidateDetails = text
End Function

Private Function KC40_AsAnnotation(ByVal obj As Object) As Object
    Dim ann As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    If obj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Err.Clear: Set ann = obj.GetSpecificAnnotation
    If Err.number = 0 Then Set KC40_AsAnnotation = obj: Exit Function
    Err.Clear: Set ann = obj.GetAnnotation
    If Err.number = 0 And Not ann Is Nothing Then Set KC40_AsAnnotation = ann
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function KC40_RawRef(ByVal model As Object, ByVal obj As Object) As String
    Dim bytes As Variant, i As Long, text As String
    On Error GoTo Unsupported
    If obj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    bytes = model.Extension.GetPersistReference3(obj)
    If Not IsArray(bytes) Then Exit Function
    For i = LBound(bytes) To UBound(bytes)
        text = text & Right$("0" & Hex$(CLng(bytes(i))), 2)
    Next i
    KC40_RawRef = text
Unsupported:
End Function


' v16.5.1: names are matched only in the OLD namespace, before any sheet rename.
Private Function KR_ExactOwner(ByVal sources As Collection, ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal viewName As String, _
    ByVal objectName As String, ByVal kind As String, ByRef hits As Long) As Object
    Dim rec As Object, found As Object, expected As Long
    hits = 0
    If kind = "DIM" Then expected = 14
    If kind = "NOTE" Then expected = 15
    If expected = 0 Or Len(objectName) = 0 Or Len(sheetName) = 0 Or Len(viewName) = 0 Then Exit Function
    For Each rec In sources
        If CLng(rec("Type")) = expected And CStr(rec("OldSheet")) = sheetName And _
           CStr(rec("OldView")) = viewName And CStr(rec("OldName")) = objectName Then
            hits = hits + 1: Set found = rec
        End If
    Next rec
    If hits = 1 Then Set KR_ExactOwner = found
End Function

Private Function KR_PinnedKey(ByVal plans As Collection, ByVal ann As Object) As String
    Dim plan As Object, source As Object, sourceAnn As Object, hits As Long, value As String
    If plans Is Nothing Or ann Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    For Each plan In plans
        Set source = plan("Source"): Set sourceAnn = source("Ann")
        If KC39_SameObject(sourceAnn, ann) Then
            hits = hits + 1: value = CStr(plan("NewName"))
        End If
    Next plan
    If hits > 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4101, , "У источника несколько баллонов; выбор по расстоянию запрещён."
    If hits = 1 Then KR_PinnedKey = value
End Function


Private Function KR_LegacyPositions(ByVal sources As Collection, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As Object
    Dim result As Object, rec As Object, ann As Object, specific As Object, list As Collection
    Dim saved() As PositionData, prior As Long, i As Long, counter As Long, before As Long
    Dim oldD As Boolean, oldG As Boolean, oldW As Boolean, oldN As Boolean, current As String, active As String
    Dim key As String, fail As String, noteText As String, pos As Variant
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set result = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    prior = positionsCount
    If prior > 0 Then
        ReDim saved(1 To prior)
        For i = 1 To prior: saved(i) = positionsData(i): Next i
    End If
    oldD = processDimensions: oldG = processGtols: oldW = processWelds: oldN = processNotes
    current = CStr(swDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName)
    processDimensions = True: processGtols = True: processWelds = True: processNotes = True
    positionsCount = 0: ReDim positionsData(1 To 500): counter = 1
    For Each rec In sources
        If Left$(CStr(rec("OldName")), 5) <> "KC_B_" And Left$(CStr(rec("OldName")), 6) <> "KC_B2_" Then
            If active <> CStr(rec("OldSheet")) Then
                active = CStr(rec("OldSheet"))
                If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, active) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4001, , "Не активирован исходный лист старого ключа."
InitWorkArea:                 KC_ViewCacheReset
            End If
            Set ann = rec("Ann"): Set specific = ann.GetSpecificAnnotation
            before = positionsCount
            If Not specific Is Nothing Then
                If CLng(rec("Type")) = swSelNOTES Then
                    ' @ may already have been removed after a successful earlier run.
                    ' Reconstruct the same note payload without editing or restoring its text.
                    noteText = GetMarkedNoteText(specific): pos = ann.GetPosition
                    If Len(noteText) > 0 And IsArray(pos) Then
                        If IsInWorkArea(pos(0), pos(1)) Then
                            positionsCount = positionsCount + 1
                            If positionsCount > UBound(positionsData) Then ReDim Preserve positionsData(1 To positionsCount + 200)
                            With positionsData(positionsCount)
                                .AnnotationType = swSelNOTES
                                .sheetName = active: .viewName = RemoveViewScale(CStr(rec("OldView")))
                                .sourceName = CStr(rec("OldName")): Set .SourceAnnotation = ann
                                .posX = CDbl(pos(0)): .posY = CDbl(pos(1)): .AnnotationText = noteText
                            End With
                        End If
                    End If
                Else
                    ProcessAnnotation CLng(rec("Type")), specific, active, counter, CStr(rec("OldView"))
                End If
                If positionsCount > before Then
                    key = BuildLegacyAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(positionsCount))
                    positionsData(positionsCount).sheetName = KC21_MappedName(CStr(rec("OldSheet")), oldNames, newNames, total)
                    If CStr(rec("OldView")) = CStr(rec("OldSheet")) Then _
                        positionsData(positionsCount).viewName = RemoveViewScale(positionsData(positionsCount).sheetName)
                    rec("LegacyNewKey") = BuildLegacyAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(positionsCount))
