                    If Not result.Exists(key) Then Set list = New Collection: result.Add key, list
                    Set list = result(key): list.Add rec
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next rec
Restore:
    processDimensions = oldD: processGtols = oldG: processWelds = oldW: processNotes = oldN
    positionsCount = prior
    If prior > 0 Then
        ReDim positionsData(1 To prior)
        For i = 1 To prior: positionsData(i) = saved(i): Next i
    Else
        Erase positionsData
    End If
    KC_ViewCacheReset
    If Len(current) > 0 Then
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, current) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4001, , "Не восстановлен лист после проверки старых ключей."
    End If
    If Len(fail) > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4001, , fail
    Set KR_LegacyPositions = result
    Exit Function
Failed:
    fail = Err.Description
    ' Restore globals before propagating; no drawing/Excel writes occurred here.
    On Error GoTo 0
    GoTo Restore
End Function


' All helpers below are called by macro 1 only.
Private Function KR_AffectedSources(ByVal sources As Collection, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, rec As Object, name As String
    For Each rec In sources
        name = CStr(rec("OldSheet"))
        If KC21_MappedName(name, oldNames, newNames, total) <> name Then result.Add rec
    Next rec
    Set KR_AffectedSources = result
End Function

Private Function KR_CountSources(ByVal sources As Collection) As Object
    Dim result As Object, rec As Object, sheet As Object, ann As Object, name As String, raw As String
    Set result = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each rec In sources
        Set sheet = rec("Sheet"): Set ann = rec("Ann")
        If KC36_IsRegular(CStr(sheet.GetName)) Then
            name = Trim$(CStr(ann.GetName))
            If Len(name) > 0 And Left$(name, 5) <> "KC_B_" And Left$(name, 6) <> "KC_B2_" Then
                raw = "KC_ID2|" & CStr(rec("Type")) & "|" & KC35_Normalize(CStr(sheet.GetName)) & "|" & LCase$(name)
                If result.Exists(raw) Then result(raw) = CLng(result(raw)) + 1 Else result.Add raw, 1
            End If
        End If
    Next rec
    Set KR_CountSources = result
End Function

' Preserve existing key form (base/extended/legacy), independent of selected sheets or source types.
Private Function KR_CurrentBalloonPlans(ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection, _
                                        Optional ByVal reuseSources As Boolean = False) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, index As Object, used As Object, rec As Object, source As Object, ann As Object
    Dim note As Object, sheet As Object, list As Collection, candidate As Object, plan As Object, name As String
    Dim currentSources As Collection, sheets As New Collection, seenSheets As Object, legacy As Object
    Dim oldNames() As String, newNames() As String, needLegacy As Boolean
    Set index = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set seenSheets = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each rec In sources
        Set sheet = rec("Sheet")
        name = CStr(sheet.GetName)
        If KC36_IsRegular(name) And Not seenSheets.Exists(name) Then sheets.Add sheet: seenSheets.Add name, True
    Next rec
    If reuseSources Then
        Set currentSources = New Collection
        For Each rec In sources
            Set sheet = rec("Sheet")
            If KC36_IsRegular(CStr(sheet.GetName)) Then currentSources.Add rec
        Next rec
    Else
        ' После переименования обязательно заново считываем имена и ключи.
        Set currentSources = KC35_CollectSources(swDraw, model, sheets, "balloon plans refresh")
    End If
    For Each rec In currentSources
        name = CStr(rec("OldName"))
        If Left$(name, 5) = "KC_B_" Then needLegacy = True
        If Left$(name, 5) <> "KC_B_" And Left$(name, 6) <> "KC_B2_" Then
            KC35_AddKeyCandidate index, KC35_SourceKey(model, rec, True, False), rec, False
            KC35_AddKeyCandidate index, KC35_SourceKey(model, rec, True, True), rec, True
        End If
    Next rec
    If needLegacy Then Set legacy = KR_LegacyPositions(currentSources, oldNames, newNames, 0)
    For Each rec In currentSources
        Set note = rec("Note"): name = CStr(rec("OldName")): Set source = Nothing
        If Not note Is Nothing Then
            If Left$(name, 6) = "KC_B2_" Then
                If index.Exists(name) Then
                    Set list = index(name)
                    If list.count = 1 Then Set candidate = list(1): Set source = candidate("Source")
                End If
            ElseIf Left$(name, 5) = "KC_B_" Then
                If legacy.Exists(name) Then
                    Set list = legacy(name)
                    If list.count = 1 Then Set source = list(1)
                End If
            End If
            If Not source Is Nothing Then
                If used.Exists(name) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4101, , "Повтор ключа существующих баллонов: " & name
                used.Add name, True
                Set plan = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
                plan.Add "Ann", rec("Ann"): plan.Add "Source", source
                plan.Add "OldName", name: plan.Add "NewName", name
                result.Add plan
            End If
        End If
    Next rec
    Set KR_CurrentBalloonPlans = result
End Function

Private Sub KR_CheckRegistryHint(ByVal book As Object, ByVal current As Object, ByVal missing As Object, ByVal known As String)
    Dim name As Variant, text As String, count As Long
    If Len(known) = 0 Or known = "*" Then Exit Sub
    For Each name In Split(known, vbLf)
        If KC36_IsRegular(CStr(name)) And Not current.Exists(CStr(name)) And Not missing.Exists(CStr(name)) Then
            count = count + 1
            If count <= 12 Then text = text & vbCrLf & CStr(name)
        End If
    Next name
    If count = 0 Then Exit Sub
    KC47_Decision "AUTO_YES_HISTORY", "Неподтверждённых имён=" & count & _
        "; книга=" & CStr(book.FullName) & "; имена (первые 12):" & text & _
        "; учитывается только история книги; полнота истории не доказана; реестр не основание удаления."
End Sub

Private Function KR_AskSelection(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal saved As String, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long, ByVal missing As Object) As String
    Dim names As Variant, selected() As Boolean, encoded As String, msg As String, i As Long, name As Variant
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames: ReDim selected(0 To UBound(names))
    For i = 0 To UBound(names)
        selected(i) = (saved = "*" Or Len(saved) = 0 Or KC14_InList(CStr(names(i)), saved))
        If KC21_IsNewKE(CStr(names(i))) Then selected(i) = True
    Next i
    For i = 1 To total
        If oldNames(i) <> newNames(i) Then msg = msg & oldNames(i) & " -> " & newNames(i) & vbCrLf
    Next i
    If missing.count > 0 Then
        msg = msg & "Отсутствующие прежние листы: "
        For Each name In missing.Keys: msg = msg & CStr(name) & " ": Next name
        msg = msg & vbCrLf
    End If
    msg = msg & "Выберите листы для обновления баллонов по ТЕКУЩИМ именам (до переименования)." & vbCrLf & _
        "Привязки всех переименованных КЭ будут перенесены независимо от выбора."
    If Not KC14_AskSheets(names, selected, msg, encoded) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4104, , _
        "Выбор листов отменён до изменения имён."
    If Len(encoded) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4104, , "Нужно выбрать хотя бы один лист."
    KR_AskSelection = KC21_MappedSelection(encoded, oldNames, newNames, total)
End Function

' Transactional snapshot: also saved when deletion requires a review stop, preventing repeated deletion detection.
' D:F is the latest transition table, not a second independent source of ownership.
Private Sub KR_WriteLayout(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal owner As String, _
    ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long, _
    ByVal changes As Collection, ByRef created As Object)
    Dim ws As Object, names As Variant, i As Long, row As Long, last As Long, c As Long
    KC37_CheckLayoutSlot book, owner
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = book.Worksheets.Add(After:=book.Worksheets(book.Worksheets.count))
        Set created = ws
        ws.name = "КЭ_Состав": ws.Visible = 2
    End If
    last = ws.UsedRange.row + ws.UsedRange.rows.count - 1
    If last > 100004 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4105, , "Слишком большая история состава КЭ."
    For row = 1 To last
        For c = 1 To 6
            If c <> 3 Then KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, c), "", changes
        Next c
    Next row
    names = Split(KC37_RegularSnapshot(drawing), vbLf)
    KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("A1"), "KC_KE_LAYOUT_1", changes
    KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("A2"), "Чертёж", changes: KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("B2"), owner, changes
    KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("A3"), "Согласовано", changes
    KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("B3"), "TS: " & Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss"), changes
    KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("A4"), "Обычных КЭ", changes: KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("B4"), CStr(UBound(names) + 1), changes
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names): KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(5 + i, 1), CStr(names(i)), changes: Next i
    KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("D4"), "До перехода", changes: KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("E4"), "После перехода", changes
    KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("F4"), "Время", changes
    For i = 1 To total
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(4 + i, 4), oldNames(i), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(4 + i, 5), newNames(i), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(4 + i, 6), "TS: " & Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss"), changes
    Next i
End Sub

' v16.5.2: deletion is complete ONLY after full audited export and Save.
Private Sub KD_Reset()
    gKDForceRebuild = False: gKDBookPath = "": gKDRemovedCount = 0
    Set gKDArchives = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set gKDInclude = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    gKDInclude.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
End Sub

Private Function KD_Allowed(ByVal book As Object, ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    If gKDArchives Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If StrComp(CStr(book.FullName), gKDBookPath, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    KD_Allowed = gKDArchives.Exists(CStr(ws.name))
End Function

Private Sub KD_Register(ByVal book As Object, ByVal ws As Object)
    gKDBookPath = CStr(book.FullName)
    gKDArchives(CStr(ws.name)) = True
    gKDForceRebuild = True
End Sub

Private Function KD_CanRebuild(ByVal book As Object, ByVal missing As Object, ByVal uncertain As Object, _
    ByVal orphans As Collection, ByVal markers As Collection) As Boolean
    Dim rec As Object, ws As Object, owner As String, r As Long, last As Long
    If missing.count = 0 Or uncertain.count > 0 Then Exit Function
    For Each rec In orphans
        Set ws = rec("Sheet")
        If CBool(rec("Marker")) Then
            owner = CStr(ws.cells(CLng(rec("Row")), 3).Value2)
        Else
            owner = CStr(ws.cells(CLng(rec("Row")), 23).Value2)
        End If
        If Not missing.Exists(owner) Then Exit Function
    Next rec
    For Each rec In markers
        Set ws = rec("Sheet")
        owner = CStr(ws.cells(CLng(rec("Row")), 3).Value2)
        ' A surviving marker moved FROM a deleted owner needs manual raw-row migration.
        If missing.Exists(owner) Then Exit Function
    Next rec
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last > 100000 Then Exit Function
    For r = 2 To last
        If Len(CStr(ws.cells(r, 23).Value2)) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Next r
    KD_CanRebuild = True
End Function

Private Sub KD_CaptureScope(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawing As Object)
    Dim ws As Object, r As Long, last As Long, owner As String
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4200, , "Слишком много исходных строк."
    For r = 2 To last
        owner = CStr(ws.cells(r, 23).Value2)
        If Len(owner) > 0 And Not KC36_IsRegular(owner) Then
            If Not SheetExistsExact(drawing, owner) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4200, , _
                "Нельзя исключить прежний лист вне КЭ без проверки: " & owner
            gKDInclude(owner) = True
        End If
    Next r
End Sub

Private Sub KD_SelectAll(ByVal names As Variant, ByRef selected() As Boolean)
    Dim i As Long, name As String
    ReDim selected(LBound(names) To UBound(names))
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        name = CStr(names(i))
        selected(i) = KC36_IsRegular(name) Or gKDInclude.Exists(name)
    Next i
End Sub

' Strict audit uses the SAME exact-key planner as rename, with an identity mapping.
Private Function KD_Audit(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal model As Object) As Collection
    Dim sheets As New Collection, names As Variant, i As Long, sh As Object, source As Collection
    Dim oldNames() As String, newNames() As String, active As String, fail As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    active = CStr(drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName)
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        If KC36_IsRegular(CStr(names(i))) Or gKDInclude.Exists(CStr(names(i))) Then
            If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, CStr(names(i))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4200, , "Не активирован лист для проверки ключей."
            Set sh = drawing.GetCurrentSheet: sheets.Add sh
        End If
    Next i
    Set source = KC35_CollectSources(drawing, model, sheets, "KD audit regular plus included")
    Set KD_Audit = KC35_PlanBalloons(model, source, oldNames, newNames, 0)
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, active) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4200, , "Не восстановлен активный лист."
    Exit Function
Failed:
    fail = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(active) > 0 Then drawing.ActivateSheet active
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4200, , "Проверка ключей при удалении КЭ: " & fail
End Function

Private Sub KD_CheckMerge()
    Dim used As Object, i As Long, ix As Long, owner As String
    If Not gKDForceRebuild Then Exit Sub
    If Not gUsePreparedMerge Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4201, , "Не подготовлено сохранение прежних ручных блоков."
    Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        ix = gPreparedMatchIdx(i)
        If ix > 0 Then
            If used.Exists(CStr(ix)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4201, , "Повтор прежнего блока карты при пересборке."
            used.Add CStr(ix), True
        End If
    Next i
    For i = 1 To gPreparedOldCount
        owner = gPreparedOldData(i).sheetName
        If Len(owner) > 0 And Not used.Exists(CStr(i)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4201, , _
            "Не сопоставлен прежний пункт оставшегося листа " & owner & ", позиция " & gPreparedOldData(i).posNumber & _
            ". Автопересборка остановлена до перенумерации: проверьте изменённые размеры/виды и ручные правки."
    Next i
End Sub

Private Sub KD_CheckResult()
    Dim plans As Collection, plan As Object, source As Object, sourceAnn As Object, noteAnn As Object, note As Object
    Dim i As Long, hits As Long, actualKey As String, used As Object
    If Not gKDForceRebuild Then Exit Sub
    Set plans = KD_Audit(swDraw, swModel)
    Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        hits = 0
        For Each plan In plans
            Set source = plan("Source"): Set sourceAnn = source("Ann")
            If KD_IsPositionSource(source, i) Then
                hits = hits + 1: actualKey = CStr(plan("OldName"))
                Set noteAnn = plan("Ann"): Set note = noteAnn.GetSpecificAnnotation
                If CStr(note.GetText) <> CStr(positionsData(i).number) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4202, , _
                    "Не совпал номер баллона, позиция " & positionsData(i).number
            End If
        Next plan
        If hits <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4202, , "Источник позиции " & positionsData(i).number & ": баллонов " & hits
        If actualKey <> BuildAnnotationStableKey(positionsData(i)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4202, , "Не подтверждён ключ позиции " & positionsData(i).number
        If used.Exists(actualKey) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4202, , "Повтор итогового ключа: " & actualKey
        used.Add actualKey, True
    Next i
    If plans.count <> positionsCount Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4202, , "После обработки остались лишние баллоны. Экспорт заблокирован."
End Sub

Private Function KD_SameValue(ByVal current As Variant, ByVal archived As String) As Boolean
    Dim text As String, a As String, b As String
    If IsError(current) Or IsNull(current) Then Exit Function
    text = CStr(current)
    If text = archived Then KD_SameValue = True: Exit Function
    ' Archive stores CStr numeric values as text; tolerate decimal separator only.
    If VarType(current) <> vbString And IsNumeric(current) Then
        a = Replace(text, ",", "."): b = Replace(archived, ",", ".")
        KD_SameValue = (a = b)
    End If
End Function

' Read-only proof of the OLD v16.5.1 stopped transaction. Never infer a deleted
' owner from the current name (old KE4 can now belong to surviving old KE5).
Private Function KD_ValidatePending(ByVal book As Object, ByVal archive As Object, ByVal drawing As Object, _
    ByVal occupied As Object) As Long
    Dim layout As Object, raw As Object, markers As Object, card As Object, ws As Object
    Dim missing As Object, oldMap As Object, targets As Object, groups As Object, cells As Object
    Dim names As Variant, r As Long, last As Long, i As Long, c As Long, width As Long, n As Long
    Dim name As String, old As String, target As String, groupKey As String, address As String, item As Variant
    Dim sourceName As String, reason As String, key As Variant, originalRow As Long, owner As String, record As Object
    Set layout = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If layout Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Для продолжения PENDING нет снимка КЭ_Состав."
    If CStr(layout.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_LAYOUT_1" Or _
       CStr(layout.Range("B2").Value2) <> CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B2").Value2) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Не подтверждён владелец снимка состава."
    names = Split(KC37_RegularSnapshot(drawing), vbLf)
    If CStr(layout.Range("B4").Value2) <> CStr(UBound(names) + 1) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Состав КЭ изменился после остановки."
    For i = 0 To UBound(names)
        If CStr(layout.cells(5 + i, 1).Value2) <> CStr(names(i)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Не совпал порядок КЭ после остановки."
    Next i
    Set missing = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): missing.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    Set oldMap = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): oldMap.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    Set targets = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): targets.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    last = layout.cells(layout.rows.count, 4).End(-4162).row
    If last > 100004 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Слишком большая таблица переходов."
    For r = 5 To last
        old = CStr(layout.cells(r, 4).Value2): target = CStr(layout.cells(r, 5).Value2)
        If Not KC36_IsRegular(old) Or RegularKENumber(target) < 2 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Повреждена таблица старое/новое имя КЭ."
        If oldMap.Exists(old) Or targets.Exists(target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Неоднозначная таблица переходов КЭ."
        If Not SheetExistsExact(drawing, target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Нет целевого листа таблицы переходов."
        oldMap.Add old, target: targets.Add target, True
    Next r
    If targets.count <> UBound(names) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Неполная таблица переходов КЭ."
    Set groups = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    last = archive.cells(archive.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last > 1000000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Слишком большой архив."
    For r = 8 To last
        sourceName = CStr(archive.cells(r, 1).Value2): reason = CStr(archive.cells(r, 3).Value2)
        If sourceName = "Отсутствующее имя листа" Then
            If Not KC36_IsRegular(reason) Or reason = "КЭ1" Or oldMap.Exists(reason) Or missing.Exists(reason) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Не доказано удаление старого имени: " & reason
            missing.Add reason, True
        Else
            If sourceName <> "Данные из чертежа" And sourceName <> "Маркеры КК" And sourceName <> "Карта контроля" Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Неизвестная запись архива: " & sourceName
            If Not IsNumeric(archive.cells(r, 2).Value2) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Неверная строка архива."
            originalRow = CLng(archive.cells(r, 2).Value2)
            If originalRow < 1 Or originalRow > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Строка архива вне диапазона."
            groupKey = sourceName & "|" & CStr(originalRow)
            If Not groups.Exists(groupKey) Then
                Set record = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
                Set cells = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
                record.Add "Sheet", sourceName: record.Add "Row", originalRow: record.Add "Cells", cells
                record.Add "Reason", reason: groups.Add groupKey, record
            End If
            Set record = groups(groupKey): Set cells = record("Cells")
            address = CStr(archive.cells(r, 4).Value2)
            If cells.Exists(address) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Повтор ячейки архива."
            If CStr(record("Reason")) <> reason Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Смешанные причины архивирования строки."
            If Len(CStr(archive.cells(r, 6).Value2)) > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Архив содержит формулы: нужна ручная проверка."
            cells.Add address, CStr(archive.cells(r, 5).Value2)
        End If
    Next r
    If missing.count = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "В архиве нет подтверждённых удалённых КЭ."
    Set raw = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    Set markers = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    Set card = book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    For Each key In groups.Keys
        Set record = groups(key): sourceName = CStr(record("Sheet")): originalRow = CLng(record("Row"))
        Set cells = record("Cells"): Set ws = book.Worksheets(sourceName)
        width = 5
        If sourceName = "Данные из чертежа" Then width = 26
        If sourceName = "Маркеры КК" Then width = 11
        If cells.count <> width Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Неполный снимок строки архива."
        For c = 1 To width
            address = ColLetter(c) & CStr(originalRow)
            If Not cells.Exists(address) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Нет ячейки архива " & address
        Next c
        If sourceName <> "Карта контроля" Then
            If occupied.Exists(CStr(key)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Несколько архивов ссылаются на одну активную строку."
            occupied.Add CStr(key), True
            If width = 11 Then
                owner = CStr(cells("C" & originalRow))
            Else
                owner = CStr(cells("W" & originalRow))
            End If
            If Not missing.Exists(owner) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Архивирован не удалённый лист: " & owner
            reason = CStr(record("Reason"))
            If InStr(1, reason, "Исходное имя листа отсутствует", vbTextCompare) <> 1 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Не подтверждена причина удаления строки: " & reason
            If width = 26 Then n = n + 1
        End If
        For c = 1 To width
            address = ColLetter(c) & CStr(originalRow)
            If ws.cells(originalRow, c).HasFormula Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Формулы активной строки требуют ручной проверки."
            If width = 11 Or (width = 26 And (c = 23 Or c = 24)) Then
                If Len(CStr(ws.cells(originalRow, c).Value2)) > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Строка больше не изолирована: " & sourceName & "!" & address
            Else
                If Not KD_SameValue(ws.cells(originalRow, c).Value2, CStr(cells(address))) Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Данные изменились после архивирования: " & sourceName & "!" & address
            End If
        Next c
    Next key
    KD_ValidatePending = n
End Function

Private Sub KD_LoadPending(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawing As Object)
    Dim ws As Object, pending As New Collection, occupied As Object, status As String, count As Long
    Dim backup As String, raw As Object, r As Long, last As Long, owner As String
    Set occupied = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each ws In book.Worksheets
        If Left$(CStr(ws.name), 9) = "КЭ_Архив_" Then
            status = UCase$(Trim$(CStr(ws.Range("B4").Value2)))
            If status = "ТРЕБУЕТ ПРОВЕРКИ" Or status = "AUTO_REBUILD_PENDING" Then
                If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_ARCHIVE_1" Or _
                   CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2) <> CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B2").Value2) Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Не подтверждён формат/владелец архива."
                If CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2) <> "PENDING" Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Автопродолжение архива разрешено только из PENDING."
                count = count + KD_ValidatePending(book, ws, drawing, occupied)
                pending.Add ws
            End If
        End If
    Next ws
    If pending.count = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Set raw = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    last = raw.cells(raw.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Слишком много исходных строк."
    For r = 2 To last
        owner = CStr(raw.cells(r, 23).Value2)
        If Len(owner) = 0 Then
            If Not occupied.Exists("Данные из чертежа|" & r) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Неизвестная строка без владельца: " & r
        ElseIf Not SheetExistsExact(drawing, owner) Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Изменился состав после архива: " & owner
        End If
    Next r
    If Not KC37_YesNo("Найдена незавершённая пересборка после удаления КЭ. Снимок состава и изолированные строки сверены с архивом." & vbCrLf & _
        "Удалённых позиций: " & count & ". Продолжить полную пересборку по всем оставшимся КЭ с проверкой ключей?" & vbCrLf & _
        "Подтвердите: номера существующих баллонов вручную отдельно от Excel не менялись. Подбор по расстоянию отключён. До успешного экспорта — PENDING.", "Продолжение удаления КЭ") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4203, , "Продолжение отменено; карта не изменена."
    backup = KC35_BackupBook(book)
    KD_CaptureScope book, drawing
    For Each ws In pending: KD_Register book, ws: Next ws
    KC37_Warning "Резерв перед продолжением PENDING: " & backup
End Sub

Private Sub KD_Finish(ByVal book As Object, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim raw As Object, ws As Object, r As Long, key As Variant, last As Long
    If Not gKDForceRebuild Then Exit Sub
    If StrComp(CStr(book.FullName), gKDBookPath, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4204, , "Сменилась книга пересборки."
    Set raw = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    last = raw.cells(raw.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last <> positionsCount + 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4204, , "Не совпало число записей после пересборки."
    For r = 1 To positionsCount
        If CStr(raw.cells(r + 1, 23).Value2) <> positionsData(r).sheetName Or _
           CStr(raw.cells(r + 1, 24).Value2) <> positionsData(r).viewName Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4204, , "Не совпал владелец итоговой строки " & r + 1
        If Not SheetExistsExact(swDraw, positionsData(r).sheetName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4204, , "Итоговая строка ссылается на отсутствующий лист."
    Next r
    For Each key In gKDArchives.Keys
        Set ws = book.Worksheets(CStr(key))
        KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("H1"), "KC_DELETE_REBUILD_1", changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("H2"), CStr(positionsCount), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("I2"), "TS: " & Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss"), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("H3"), gKC37PreparedLayout, changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("B4"), "AUTO_REBUILT", changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("A5"), "Активная карта пересобрана по оставшимся листам. Архив — резерв, не активные позиции.", changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.Range("A6"), "Значения и формулы ниже — текстовый снимок ДО согласования, не текущая карта.", changes
    Next key
End Sub

Private Function KD_RowMatches(ByVal index As Long, ByVal oldIndex As Long) As Boolean
    Dim kind As String
    Select Case positionsData(index).AnnotationType
        Case swSelDIMENSIONS: kind = "Размер"
        Case swSelGTOLS: kind = "Геом.допуск"
        Case swSelWELDSYMBOLS: kind = "Сварка"
        Case swSelNOTES: kind = "Заметка"
    End Select
    With gPreparedOldData(oldIndex)
        KD_RowMatches = (.AnnotationType = kind And Len(.sheetName) > 0 And _
            StrComp(.sheetName, positionsData(index).sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 And _
            KC50_ViewOK(index, oldIndex) And _
            NormalizeForCompare(.AnnotationText) = NormalizeForCompare(positionsData(index).AnnotationText) And _
            Abs(.NominalValue - positionsData(index).NominalValue) < 0.000001 And _
            NormalizeForCompare(.DimPrefix) = NormalizeForCompare(positionsData(index).DimPrefix))
    End With
End Function

' Identical dimensions/welds within ONE view are common. For manual blocks only,
' resolve them by a native audited source -> balloon -> old number -> exact row.
' This does NOT assign a balloon by Excel number: balloon ownership is already
' proven by its full key. Never assume enumeration order equals row identity.
Private Sub KD_PrepareMerge()
    Dim plans As Collection, plan As Object, source As Object, sourceAnn As Object, ann As Object, note As Object
    Dim used As Object, i As Long, j As Long, hits As Long, number As Long, rowHits As Long, ix As Long
    Dim matches() As Long, value As String
    If Not gKDForceRebuild Then
        If gPreparedOldCount <= 0 Then Exit Sub
        ' v16.5.13: одинаковые параметры (например, два 30±2 на одном виде) не различаются по атрибутам.
        ' Тогда и при обычном запуске включаем сопоставление через ключ баллона источника.
        If gIntroPointCount = gIntroPreviousCount And gMergeSkipped = 0 Then Exit Sub
        If gIntroPointCount = gIntroPreviousCount Then KC46_Trace "CARD_MERGE_BY_BALLOON | неоднозначных по параметрам строк: " & gMergeSkipped
    End If
    If Not gUsePreparedMerge Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4201, , "Не прочитана прежняя рабочая карта."
    Set plans = KD_Audit(swDraw, swModel)
    Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    ReDim matches(1 To positionsCount)
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        hits = 0: value = "": rowHits = 0: ix = 0
        For Each plan In plans
            Set source = plan("Source"): Set sourceAnn = source("Ann")
            If KD_IsPositionSource(source, i) Then
                hits = hits + 1
                Set ann = plan("Ann"): Set note = ann.GetSpecificAnnotation
                value = Trim$(CStr(note.GetText))
            End If
        Next plan
        If hits > 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4201, , "Несколько баллонов у одного исходного объекта."
        If hits = 1 Then
            If Len(value) = 0 Or Len(value) > 9 Or value Like "*[!0-9]*" Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4201, , "Не прочитан прежний номер проверенного баллона."
            number = CLng(value)
            For j = 1 To gPreparedOldCount
                If gPreparedOldData(j).posNumber = number Then
                    If KD_RowMatches(i, j) Then rowHits = rowHits + 1: ix = j
                End If
            Next j
            If rowHits <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4201, , _
                "Ключ баллона найден, но его прежний номер " & number & " не совпал с единственной строкой Excel по листу/виду/параметру." & _
                " Номера или параметры менялись отдельно от карты; автоматический перенос ручного блока остановлен."
            If used.Exists(CStr(ix)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4201, , "Два исходных объекта претендуют на один ручной блок карты."
            used.Add CStr(ix), True: matches(i) = ix
        End If
    Next i
    ' Sources without an existing balloon: retain only the pre-existing UNIQUE exact match.
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        If matches(i) = 0 Then
            ix = gPreparedMatchIdx(i)
            If ix > 0 Then
                If Not used.Exists(CStr(ix)) Then
                    If KD_RowMatches(i, ix) Then used.Add CStr(ix), True: matches(i) = ix
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    For j = 1 To gPreparedOldCount: gPreparedOldData(j).matched = False: Next j
    For i = 1 To positionsCount
        ix = matches(i): gPreparedMatchIdx(i) = ix: gPreparedMatchResults(i) = 0
        If ix > 0 Then
            gPreparedOldData(ix).matched = True: gPreparedMatchResults(i) = 1
        End If
    Next i
    gMergeSkipped = 0: gKDRemovedCount = 0
    For j = 1 To gPreparedOldCount
        If Not gPreparedOldData(j).matched Then
            If Len(gPreparedOldData(j).sheetName) = 0 Then
                gKDRemovedCount = gKDRemovedCount + 1
            Else
                gMergeSkipped = gMergeSkipped + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next j
End Sub

Private Function KD_IsPositionSource(ByVal source As Object, ByVal index As Long) As Boolean
    Dim ann As Object
    If CStr(source("OldSheet")) <> positionsData(index).sheetName Then Exit Function
    If CLng(source("Type")) <> positionsData(index).AnnotationType Then Exit Function
    If Len(positionsData(index).sourceName) > 0 Then
        If StrComp(Trim$(CStr(source("OldName"))), Trim$(positionsData(index).sourceName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    End If
    Set ann = source("Ann")
    KD_IsPositionSource = KC39_SameObject(ann, positionsData(index).SourceAnnotation)
End Function

Private Function KD_CompletedArchive(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    If CStr(ws.Range("B4").Value2) <> "AUTO_REBUILT" Then Exit Function
    If CStr(ws.Range("H1").Value2) <> "KC_DELETE_REBUILD_1" Then Exit Function
    If Not IsNumeric(ws.Range("H2").Value2) Then Exit Function
    If CDbl(ws.Range("H2").Value2) < 1 Then Exit Function
    If Left$(CStr(ws.Range("I2").Value2), 4) <> "TS: " Then Exit Function
    If Len(CStr(ws.Range("H3").Value2)) = 0 Then Exit Function
    KD_CompletedArchive = True
End Function

' v16.5.3: managed backups are isolated per workbook; unknown/PENDING/LEGACY copies are never pruned.
Private Sub KA_Reset()
    Set gKABackups = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    gKABackups.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
End Sub

Private Function KA_Owner(ByVal book As Object) As String
    KA_Owner = CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B2").Value2)
    If Len(KA_Owner) = 0 Or StrComp(KA_Owner, CStr(swModel.GetPathName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "Не подтверждён владелец книги резервов."
End Function

Private Function KA_Folder(ByVal book As Object, ByVal create As Boolean) As String
    Dim fso As Object, root As String, folder As String, leaf As String, ownerFile As String, stream As Object
    Dim expected As String, actual As String, bad As Variant, stem As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    leaf = Trim$(KC28_S("Backup.Subfolder"))
    If Len(leaf) = 0 Or Len(leaf) > 64 Or leaf = "." Or leaf = ".." Or Right$(leaf, 1) = "." Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "Укажите простое имя папки резервов, не путь (до 64 символов)."
    For Each bad In Array("\", "/", ":", "*", "?", Chr$(34), "<", ">", "|")
        If InStr(leaf, CStr(bad)) > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "Недопустимый символ имени папки резервов."
    Next bad
    stem = UCase$(Split(leaf, ".")(0))
    If stem = "CON" Or stem = "PRN" Or stem = "AUX" Or stem = "NUL" Or _
       stem Like "COM[1-9]" Or stem Like "LPT[1-9]" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "Зарезервированное имя папки резервов."
    expected = "KC_BACKUP_OWNER_1" & vbCrLf & CStr(book.FullName) & vbCrLf & KA_Owner(book) & vbCrLf
    root = fso.BuildPath(fso.GetParentFolderName(CStr(book.FullName)), leaf)
    folder = fso.BuildPath(root, "Карта_" & KC35_Hash(LCase$(CStr(book.FullName))))
    ownerFile = fso.BuildPath(folder, "Владелец.kcinfo")
    If create Then
        If Not fso.FolderExists(root) Then fso.CreateFolder root
        If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then fso.CreateFolder folder
        If Not fso.FileExists(ownerFile) Then
            Set stream = fso.CreateTextFile(ownerFile, False, True)
            stream.Write expected: stream.Close
        End If
    End If
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then KA_Folder = folder: Exit Function
    If Not fso.FileExists(ownerFile) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "Папка резервов без подтверждения владельца: " & folder
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(ownerFile, 1, False, -1)
    actual = stream.ReadAll: stream.Close
    If StrComp(actual, expected, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "Папка резервов принадлежит другой карте."
    KA_Folder = folder
End Function

Private Function KA_NewPath(ByVal book As Object) As String
    Dim fso As Object, folder As String, candidate As String, i As Long, timestamp As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = KA_Folder(book, True): timestamp = Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss")
    For i = 1 To 9999
        candidate = fso.BuildPath(folder, "КК_" & timestamp & "_" & Format$(i, "0000") & ".xlsm")
        If Not fso.FileExists(candidate) And Not fso.FileExists(candidate & ".kcmeta") And _
           Not fso.FileExists(candidate & ".kcmeta.new") Then KA_NewPath = candidate: Exit Function
    Next i
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "Не найдено свободное имя резервной копии."
End Function

Private Sub KA_WriteMeta(ByVal book As Object, ByVal path As String, ByVal status As String, ByVal size As Double, Optional ByVal originalPath As String = "")
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, temp As String, metadata As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Len(originalPath) = 0 Then originalPath = CStr(book.FullName)
    metadata = "KC_BACKUP_COPY_1" & vbCrLf & CStr(book.FullName) & vbCrLf & KA_Owner(book) & vbCrLf & _
        fso.GetFileName(path) & vbCrLf & status & vbCrLf & CStr(size) & vbCrLf & _
        "TS: " & Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss") & vbCrLf & "Source: " & originalPath & vbCrLf
    temp = path & ".kcmeta.new"
    Set stream = fso.CreateTextFile(temp, False, True)
    stream.Write metadata: stream.Close
    ' A failed metadata replacement leaves the actual XLSM untouched and unprunable.
    If fso.FileExists(path & ".kcmeta") Then fso.DeleteFile path & ".kcmeta", False
    fso.MoveFile temp, path & ".kcmeta"
End Sub

Private Function KA_ReadMeta(ByVal book As Object, ByVal path As String, ByRef status As String) As Boolean
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, lines As Variant, re As Object, text As String, folder As String
    On Error GoTo Unmanaged
    status = "": Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = KA_Folder(book, False)
    If StrComp(fso.GetParentFolderName(path), folder, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Pattern = "^КК_[0-9]{8}_[0-9]{6}_[0-9]{4}\.xlsm$": re.IgnoreCase = True
    If Not re.Test(fso.GetFileName(path)) Then Exit Function
    If Not fso.FileExists(path) Or Not fso.FileExists(path & ".kcmeta") Then Exit Function
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(path & ".kcmeta", 1, False, -1)
    text = stream.ReadAll: stream.Close
    lines = Split(text, vbCrLf)
    If UBound(lines) <> 8 Then Exit Function
    If lines(0) <> "KC_BACKUP_COPY_1" Or lines(8) <> "" Then Exit Function
    If StrComp(CStr(lines(1)), CStr(book.FullName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    If StrComp(CStr(lines(2)), KA_Owner(book), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    If StrComp(CStr(lines(3)), fso.GetFileName(path), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    If lines(4) <> "PENDING" And lines(4) <> "COMPLETE" And lines(4) <> "LEGACY" Then Exit Function
    If Not IsNumeric(lines(5)) Then Exit Function
    If CDbl(lines(5)) <= 0 Or CDbl(fso.GetFile(path).size) <> CDbl(lines(5)) Then Exit Function
    re.Pattern = "^TS: [0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2} [0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}$"
    If Not re.Test(CStr(lines(6))) Then Exit Function
    If Left$(CStr(lines(7)), 8) <> "Source: " Then Exit Function
    status = CStr(lines(4)): KA_ReadMeta = True
    Exit Function
Unmanaged:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
End Function

Private Function KA_CreateBackup(ByVal book As Object) As String
    Dim fso As Object, path As String, size As Double
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    path = KA_NewPath(book)
    book.SaveCopyAs path
    If Not fso.FileExists(path) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "Резервная копия не создана."
    size = CDbl(fso.GetFile(path).size)
    If size <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "Резервная копия пуста."
    KA_WriteMeta book, path, "PENDING", size
    If gKABackups Is Nothing Then KA_Reset
    gKABackups(path) = CStr(book.FullName)
    KA_CreateBackup = path
End Function

Private Function KA_Archive(ByVal book As Object, ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    If Left$(CStr(ws.name), 9) <> "КЭ_Архив_" Then Exit Function
    If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_ARCHIVE_1" Then Exit Function
    KA_Archive = (StrComp(CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2), KA_Owner(book), vbTextCompare) = 0)
End Function

Private Function KA_Completed(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    KA_Completed = KD_CompletedArchive(ws) Or UCase$(Trim$(CStr(ws.Range("B4").Value2))) = "ПРОВЕРЕНО"
End Function

Private Sub KA_SetVisibility(ByVal book As Object, ByVal showAll As Boolean, ByVal previous As Object)
    Dim ws As Object, value As Long
    book.Worksheets("Карта контроля").Visible = -1
    book.Worksheets("Карта контроля").Activate
    For Each ws In book.Worksheets
        If KA_Archive(book, ws) Then
            value = CLng(ws.Visible)
            If showAll Then
                If value <> -1 Then previous.Add CStr(ws.name), value: ws.Visible = -1
            ElseIf KA_Completed(ws) Then
                If value <> 2 Then previous.Add CStr(ws.name), value: ws.Visible = 2
            End If
        End If
    Next ws
End Sub

Private Sub KA_RestoreVisibility(ByVal book As Object, ByVal previous As Object)
    Dim name As Variant
    On Error Resume Next
    For Each name In previous.Keys: book.Worksheets(CStr(name)).Visible = CLng(previous(name)): Next name
End Sub

Private Sub KA_Prune(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim fso As Object, folder As String, file As Object, candidates As New Collection, paths() As String
    Dim item As Variant, status As String, keep As Long, i As Long, j As Long, tmp As String, deleted As Long
    If CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2) <> "READY" Or Not book.saved Then Exit Sub
    keep = CLng(KC28_N("Backup.KeepCompleted"))
    If keep = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject"): folder = KA_Folder(book, False)
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then Exit Sub
    For Each file In fso.GetFolder(folder).files
        If LCase$(fso.GetExtensionName(CStr(file.name))) = "xlsm" Then
            If KA_ReadMeta(book, CStr(file.path), status) Then
                If status = "COMPLETE" Then candidates.Add CStr(file.path)
            End If
        End If
    Next file
    If candidates.count <= keep Then Exit Sub
    ReDim paths(1 To candidates.count)
    For i = 1 To candidates.count: paths(i) = CStr(candidates(i)): Next i
    ' Time-coded names, newest first; never use date of last user access/edit.
    For i = 2 To UBound(paths)
        tmp = paths(i): j = i - 1
        Do While j >= 1
            If StrComp(paths(j), tmp, vbBinaryCompare) >= 0 Then Exit Do
            paths(j + 1) = paths(j): j = j - 1
        Loop
        paths(j + 1) = tmp
    Next i
    For i = keep + 1 To UBound(paths)
        ' Recheck READY and metadata immediately before each deletion.
        If CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2) <> "READY" Or Not book.saved Then Exit Sub
        If KA_ReadMeta(book, paths(i), status) Then
            If status = "COMPLETE" Then
                ' Even with a backwards Windows clock, never prune this run's fresh backup.
                If Not gKABackups.Exists(paths(i)) Then
                    fso.DeleteFile paths(i), False
                    fso.DeleteFile paths(i) & ".kcmeta", False
                    deleted = deleted + 1
                    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, "Удалён старый завершённый резерв: " & paths(i)
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If deleted > 0 Then KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, "Очистка резервов: удалено " & deleted & "; лимит завершённых " & keep
End Sub

' Called ONLY after export, reference update and marker cleanup all succeeded.
Private Sub KA_AfterSuccess(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim previous As Object, fso As Object, path As Variant, status As String, failure As String
    Dim events As Boolean, captured As Boolean, saved As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set previous = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    If CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2) <> "READY" Then Exit Sub
    If gKABackups Is Nothing Then KA_Reset
    Call KA_Owner(book)
    events = book.Application.EnableEvents: captured = True: book.Application.EnableEvents = False
    If KC28_B("Archive.HideCompleted") Then KA_SetVisibility book, False, previous
    If previous.count > 0 Or Not book.saved Then book.Save
    If Not book.saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4301, , "Не сохранена книга после скрытия архивов."
    saved = True
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    For Each path In gKABackups.Keys
        If StrComp(CStr(gKABackups(path)), CStr(book.FullName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4301, , "Сменилась книга в текущей сессии резервов."
        If Not KA_ReadMeta(book, CStr(path), status) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4301, , "Не подтверждена служебная запись резерва. Копия сохранена: " & path
        If status <> "PENDING" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4301, , "Неожиданный статус текущего резерва; очистка не запущена."
        KA_WriteMeta book, CStr(path), "COMPLETE", CDbl(fso.GetFile(CStr(path)).size)
    Next path
    KA_Prune book
    book.Application.EnableEvents = events
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    failure = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not saved And Not previous Is Nothing Then KA_RestoreVisibility book, previous
    If captured Then book.Application.EnableEvents = events
    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, "Обслуживание резервов/архивов: " & failure
    MsgBox "Карта уже обновлена, но обслуживание резервов/архивов выполнено не полностью: " & failure & vbCrLf & _
        "Проверки и данные карты не отключались. Не удаляйте резервы вручную до проверки.", vbExclamation
End Sub

' Explicit migration only. Legacy files are moved, NOT deleted, and stay protected.
Private Sub KA_MoveLegacy(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim fso As Object, file As Object, files As New Collection, path As Variant, re As Object
    Dim prefix As String, suffix As String, sourceFolder As String, target As String, size As Double, moved As Long
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    sourceFolder = fso.GetParentFolderName(CStr(book.FullName))
    prefix = fso.GetBaseName(CStr(book.FullName)) & ".before-ke-rename_"
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp"): re.Pattern = "^[0-9]{8}_[0-9]{6}(_[0-9]+)?\.xlsm$": re.IgnoreCase = True
    For Each file In fso.GetFolder(sourceFolder).files
        If StrComp(Left$(CStr(file.name), Len(prefix)), prefix, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            suffix = Mid$(CStr(file.name), Len(prefix) + 1)
            If re.Test(suffix) Then files.Add CStr(file.path)
        End If
    Next file
    If files.count = 0 Then MsgBox "Старые before-ke-rename этой карты рядом с книгой не найдены.", vbInformation: Exit Sub
    If MsgBox("Найдены старые резервы по точному имени текущей карты: " & files.count & "." & vbCrLf & _
        "Переместить их в отдельную папку? Файлы получат новые короткие имена. Они НЕ удаляются и НЕ участвуют в автоочистке." & vbCrLf & _
        "Перед переносом закройте резервные книги Excel. Внутреннее содержимое этих старых копий не проверяется.", _
        vbYesNo + vbQuestion, "Перенос старых резервов") <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
    For Each path In files
        target = KA_NewPath(book): size = CDbl(fso.GetFile(CStr(path)).size)
        If size <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4302, , "Старый резерв пуст: " & path
        KA_WriteMeta book, target, "LEGACY", size, CStr(path)
        fso.MoveFile CStr(path), target
        If Not fso.FileExists(target) Or fso.FileExists(CStr(path)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4302, , "Не подтверждён перенос старого резерва."
        If CDbl(fso.GetFile(target).size) <> size Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4302, , "Изменился размер перенесённого резерва."
        moved = moved + 1
        KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, "Старый резерв перемещён (защищён от очистки): " & path & " -> " & target
    Next path
    MsgBox "Перемещено резервов: " & moved & ". Статус LEGACY: автоочистка к ним не применяется.", vbInformation
End Sub

Private Sub KA_Service()
    Dim choice As String, path As String, xl As Object, book As Object, owns As Boolean, events As Boolean
    Dim captured As Boolean, leaveOpen As Boolean, previous As Object, ws As Object, first As Object
    Dim folder As String, shell As Object, count As Long, failure As String, saved As Boolean
    choice = InputBox("1 = Открыть папку резервов этой карты" & vbCrLf & _
        "2 = Показать архивные листы в Excel" & vbCrLf & _
        "3 = Скрыть завершённые архивы (VeryHidden)" & vbCrLf & _
        "4 = Перенести старые before-ke-rename в папку резервов" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Незавершённые архивы не скрываются командой 3. Архивные листы не удаляются.", "Архивы и резервные копии", "1")
    If Len(choice) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If choice <> "1" And choice <> "2" And choice <> "3" And choice <> "4" Then MsgBox "Неверный выбор.", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Set previous = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    On Error GoTo Failed
    path = KC14_Resolve(swModel.GetPathName, False)
    If Not CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject").FileExists(path) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4303, , "Рабочая карта ещё не создана."
    KC35_OpenBook path, xl, book, owns
    KC37_CheckMigrationBook book, CStr(swModel.GetPathName)
    events = xl.EnableEvents: captured = True: xl.EnableEvents = False
    Select Case choice
        Case "1"
            folder = KA_Folder(book, True)
            Set shell = CreateObject("Shell.Application"): shell.Open folder
        Case "2"
            KA_SetVisibility book, True, previous
            For Each ws In book.Worksheets
                If KA_Archive(book, ws) Then
                    count = count + 1
                    If first Is Nothing Then Set first = ws
                    If Not KA_Completed(ws) Then Set first = ws
                End If
            Next ws
            If previous.count > 0 Then book.Save
            If Not book.saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4303, , "Не сохранена видимость архивов."
            saved = True
            If count > 0 Then
                xl.Visible = True: book.Activate: first.Activate: leaveOpen = True
                MsgBox "Показано архивных листов: " & count & ". Это резервные снимки, не активная карта." & vbCrLf & _
                    "Для скрытия завершённых архивов: первый макрос > 3 > 6 > 3.", vbInformation
            Else
                MsgBox "Архивных листов этой карты нет.", vbInformation
            End If
        Case "3"
            KA_SetVisibility book, False, previous
            If previous.count > 0 Then book.Save
            If Not book.saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4303, , "Не сохранена видимость архивов."
            saved = True
            MsgBox "Скрыто завершённых архивов: " & previous.count & ". Незавершённые не скрывались.", vbInformation
        Case "4"
            KA_MoveLegacy book
    End Select
Cleanup:
    On Error Resume Next
    If captured Then
        If owns And leaveOpen Then xl.EnableEvents = True Else xl.EnableEvents = events
    End If
    If owns And Not leaveOpen Then book.Close False: xl.Quit
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    failure = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not saved And Not book Is Nothing Then KA_RestoreVisibility book, previous
    MsgBox "Архивы/резервы: " & failure & vbCrLf & "Автоматическая очистка не запускалась этой командой.", vbExclamation
    GoTo Cleanup
End Sub






' v16.5.4: место клейма берём только из владельца сохранённой заметки;
' координаты и ближайший вид не считаются подтверждением привязки.
Private Function KC41_PrepareStampView() As Boolean
    Dim rec As Object, token As String, candidate As String, viewText As String
    Dim count As Long, i As Long, answer As String
    gKC_StampView = ""
    token = KC33_Clean("Клеймо ОТК")
    If Not IsEmpty(gKC28Refs) Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(gKC28Refs, 1)
            If CStr(gKC28Refs(i, 1)) = "1" And CStr(gKC28Refs(i, 3)) = "STAMP" Then
                token = KC33_Clean(CStr(gKC28Refs(i, 2)))
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    If Not gKC_AutoRows Is Nothing Then
        For Each rec In gKC_AutoRows
            If CStr(rec("Kind")) = "NOTE" And KC_InMarkerScope(gKC_AutoScope, CStr(rec("Sheet"))) Then
                If KC33_Clean(CStr(rec("Text"))) = token Then
                    count = count + 1
                    candidate = Trim$(RemoveViewScale(CStr(rec("View"))))
                    If StrComp(candidate, CStr(rec("Sheet")), vbTextCompare) = 0 Then candidate = ""
                    viewText = candidate
                End If
            End If
        Next rec
    End If
    If count = 1 And Len(viewText) > 0 Then
        gKC_StampView = viewText
    Else
        answer = InputBox("Не удалось однозначно определить вид заметки клейма по списку маркеров." & vbCrLf & _
            "Укажите точное название вида, разреза или эскиза для операции клеймения." & vbCrLf & _
            "Пустое значение или Отмена остановит обработку до перенумерации баллонов.", "Место клеймения")
        answer = Trim$(answer)
        If Len(answer) = 0 Then Exit Function
        gKC_StampView = answer
    End If
    Do While Right$(gKC_StampView, 1) = "."
        gKC_StampView = Trim$(Left$(gKC_StampView, Len(gKC_StampView) - 1))
        If Len(gKC_StampView) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Loop
    KC41_PrepareStampView = True
End Function

' Перезаписываются только типовые автоматически созданные формулировки;
' произвольный пользовательский текст сохраняется.
Private Function KC41_StampText(ByVal text As String, ByVal number As Long, ByVal viewText As String) As String
    Dim re As Object, cleaned As String
    cleaned = Trim$(text)
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True: re.Global = False
    re.Pattern = "^\s*([0-9]+\.\s*)?Клеймить ударным способом\.\s*((Эскиз|Вид|Разрез|Сечение|Местный вид|Выносной элемент)[^.]*\.?)?\s*$"
    If re.Test(cleaned) Then
        KC41_StampText = CStr(number) & ". Клеймить ударным способом. " & viewText & "."
    Else
        KC41_StampText = KC_NumberedOperationText(text, number)
    End If
End Function


Private Function KC42_GetLower(ByVal obj As Object, ByRef value As String) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    value = CStr(obj.GetLowerText())
    KC42_GetLower = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    value = ""
End Function

Private Function KC42_IsHole(ByVal obj As Object, ByRef value As Boolean) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    value = CBool(obj.IsHoleCallout())
    KC42_IsHole = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    value = False
End Function

' Только чтение; не используем отображаемый текст для перезаписи размера.
Private Function KC42_Diagnostic(ByVal obj As Object) As String
    Dim p As Long, text As String, result As String, low As String, hole As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    If KC42_IsHole(obj, hole) Then result = "IsHoleCallout=" & CStr(hole) Else result = "IsHoleCallout=?"
    If KC42_GetLower(obj, low) Then result = result & "; нижний=" & Left$(low, 100)
    For p = 5 To 8
        Err.Clear: text = CStr(obj.GetText(p))
        If Err.number = 0 Then
            If InStr(1, text, "#", vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then result = result & "; # в определении " & CStr(p)
        End If
    Next p
    KC42_Diagnostic = result
End Function


' v16.5.7-review: обязательный журнал только для двух разрешённых автоответов.
Private Sub KC47_Decision(ByVal decision As String, ByVal detail As String)
    ' Решения пишутся сразу: без записи автоматическое продолжение запрещено.
    KC53_Add "РЕШЕНИЕ | " & decision & " | " & detail
    If Not KC53_Flush() Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4707, "KC47_Decision", _
        "Автопродолжение отменено: не записан журнал в папке карты контроля."
End Sub

' Не заменяем формулы значениями. Полноценный перенос формул требует карты строк.
Private Sub KC47_CheckCardFormulas(ByVal card As Object)
    Dim last As Long, state As Variant
    last = card.UsedRange.Row + card.UsedRange.Rows.Count - 1
    If last < 7 Then Exit Sub
    state = card.Range("A7:E" & CStr(last)).HasFormula
    If IsNull(state) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4708, , _
        "В A7:E" & last & " есть формулы. Пересборка остановлена до очистки: перенос формул ещё не реализован. Не заменяйте их значениями."
    If CBool(state) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4708, , _
        "В A7:E" & last & " есть формулы. Пересборка остановлена до очистки: нужен проверенный перенос формул."
End Sub

Private Sub KC47_RequireSheet(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal name As String)
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, name) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4709, , _
        "Не подтверждён активный лист " & name & ". Дальнейшее изменение баллонов остановлено."
End Sub

Private Function KC47_RestoreTextPart(ByVal obj As Object, ByVal part As Long, ByVal value As String) As Boolean
    Dim actual As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If part = -1 Then
        obj.SetLowerText value
        If Not KC42_GetLower(obj, actual) Then Exit Function
    Else
        obj.SetText part, value
        actual = CStr(obj.GetText(part))
    End If
    KC47_RestoreTextPart = (StrComp(actual, value, vbBinaryCompare) = 0)
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC47_RestoreTextPart = False
End Function

' READY означает успешные этапы в текущем сеансе, НЕ атомарный commit двух файлов.
Private Sub KC47_CommitCard()
    Dim fail As String, recoveryFailed As Boolean, oldEvents As Boolean, captured As Boolean
    Dim app As Object, meta As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set app = gWorkBook.Application
    oldEvents = app.EnableEvents: captured = True
    app.EnableEvents = False
    If gWorkBook.ReadOnly Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4710, , "Книга доступна только для чтения."
    Set meta = gWorkBook.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное")
    KC20_ConfirmIntroSync swModel, gIntroPointCount, gIntroFirstBalloon
    meta.Range("B10").Value2 = "READY"
    gWorkBook.Save
    If Not gWorkBook.Saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4710, , "Не подтверждено сохранение READY."
    If CStr(meta.Range("B10").Value2) <> "READY" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4710, , "Не подтверждён статус READY."
    app.EnableEvents = oldEvents: captured = False
    KC46_Trace "Этапы режима 1 завершены; Excel READY сохранён; сохранение чертежа пользователем НЕ подтверждено."
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    fail = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    Err.Clear
    KC20_WriteIntroSync swModel, "PENDING"
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then recoveryFailed = True
    Err.Clear
    If Not app Is Nothing Then app.EnableEvents = False
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then recoveryFailed = True
    Err.Clear
    gWorkBook.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2 = "PENDING"
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then recoveryFailed = True
    Err.Clear
    gWorkBook.Save
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then recoveryFailed = True
    Err.Clear
    If Not gWorkBook.Saved Then recoveryFailed = True
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then recoveryFailed = True
    Err.Clear
    If CStr(gWorkBook.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B10").Value2) <> "PENDING" Then recoveryFailed = True
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then recoveryFailed = True
    Err.Clear
    If captured Then app.EnableEvents = oldEvents
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then recoveryFailed = True
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4710, "KC47_CommitCard", "Завершение не подтверждено: " & fail & _
        IIf(recoveryFailed, "; сохранение PENDING или восстановление Excel не подтверждено. Проверьте оба файла и резервы.", _
        "; Excel PENDING сохранён, чертёж изменён только в памяти. Общего отката нет.")
End Sub


' v16.5.9-review: только диагностика при отсутствии старого владельца.
' Схожие имена на новом виде НЕ являются подтверждением прежней идентичности.
Private Function KC49_MarkerMismatch(ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection, _
    ByVal ws As Object, ByVal row As Long, ByVal oldHits As Long) As String
    Dim rec As Object, ann As Object, kind As String
    Dim sheetName As String, oldView As String, objectName As String, newView As String
    Dim count As Long, withRef As Long, details As String, testRef As String, expected As Long
    On Error GoTo DiagnosticFailed
    sheetName = CStr(ws.Cells(row, 3).Value2)
    oldView = CStr(ws.Cells(row, 4).Value2)
    objectName = CStr(ws.Cells(row, 5).Value2)
    kind = CStr(ws.Cells(row, 2).Value2)
    If kind = "DIM" Then expected = 14
    If kind = "NOTE" Then expected = 15
    For Each rec In sources
        If CLng(rec("Type")) = expected And expected <> 0 Then
            If CStr(rec("OldSheet")) = sheetName And CStr(rec("OldName")) = objectName Then
                newView = CStr(rec("OldView"))
                If StrComp(newView, oldView, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then
                    count = count + 1
                    If count <= 6 Then details = details & vbCrLf & "Возможный новый вид: " & newView
                    Set ann = rec("Ann")
                    testRef = KC35_GetRef(model, ann)
                    If Len(testRef) > 0 Then withRef = withRef + 1
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next rec
    KC49_MarkerMismatch = "Не найден единственный исходный объект маркера, строка " & row & _
        ": " & sheetName & " / " & oldView & " / " & objectName & "; совпадений по старому виду: " & oldHits & "." & _
        vbCrLf & "Одноимённых объектов того же типа на других видах листа: " & count & _
        "; текущие проверяемые ссылки для них: " & withRef & "." & details & vbCrLf & _
        "В сохранённой строке нет прежней persistent reference. Имя и нынешняя ссылка нового кандидата " & _
        "не доказывают, что это прежний объект. Автоматическая перепривязка остановлена ДО изменения книги и баллонов." & _
        " Сохраните копии и проверьте историю вида; не исправляйте старое имя в Excel вручную."
    KC46_Trace "VIEW_IDENTITY_UNPROVEN | строка=" & row & "; лист=" & sheetName & _
        "; прежний вид=" & oldView & "; объект=" & objectName & "; возможных одноимённых=" & count & _
        "; текущих ссылок=" & withRef & "; миграция НЕ выполнялась"
    Exit Function
DiagnosticFailed:
    KC49_MarkerMismatch = "Не найден прежний владелец маркера, строка " & row & _
        "; дополнительная диагностика не удалась: " & Err.Description & _
        ". Перепривязка не выполнялась, режим 1 остановлен до изменения баллонов."
End Function


' ============================================
' v16.5.10-review: автоматическое согласование смены вида в режиме 1.
' Повод: строка маркера без persistent reference не найдена по старому виду.
' Признаки (в порядке силы):
'   NOTE: внутреннее имя заметки единственное во всём чертеже (клеймо и ссылки п.N).
'   DIM:  тот же лист, то же внутреннее имя и тот же текст размера, на другом виде.
'   Имя изменилось: текст взаимно однозначен (одна старая запись <-> один свободный объект).
' Объекты, уже точно занятые другими записями, не рассматриваются. Конфликт двух записей за один
' объект или несколько кандидатов = остановка с диагностикой KC49 до изменений.
' Запись в книгу делает существующий путь согласования (резерв, журнал ячеек, откат, PENDING).
' ============================================
Private Function KC50_Key(ByVal kind As String, ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal viewName As String, ByVal objectName As String) As String
    KC50_Key = kind & "|" & sheetName & "|" & viewName & "|" & objectName
End Function

Private Function KC50_HasRebind(ByVal row As Long) As Boolean
    If gKC50Rebind Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    KC50_HasRebind = gKC50Rebind.Exists(CStr(row))
End Function

Private Function KC50_ResolveMovedMarkers(ByVal book As Object, ByVal model As Object, _
    ByVal sources As Collection, ByVal current As Object) As Object
    Dim result As Object, ws As Object, claimed As Object, choice As Object, votes As Object, why As Object
    Dim pending As New Collection, live As Collection, rec As Object, found As Object, owner As Object
    Dim item As Variant, peer As Variant, row As Long, last As Long, hits As Long, n As Long, peers As Long
    Dim kind As String, sh As String, vw As String, nm As String, txt As String, ref As String, k As String
    Dim target As Object, src As Object, srcAnn As Object
    Set result = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set KC50_ResolveMovedMarkers = result
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "KC_MARKERS_1" Then Exit Function ' формат проверяет KC36_PlanMarkers
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last > 100000 Then Exit Function
    Set claimed = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): claimed.CompareMode = vbBinaryCompare
    ' 1. Объекты, уже занятые точными записями или ссылками, нельзя отдать другой записи.
    For row = 6 To last
        If book.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range(ws.cells(row, 1), ws.cells(row, 11))) > 0 Then
            kind = CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2): sh = CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2)
            vw = CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2): nm = CStr(ws.cells(row, 5).Value2)
            ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
            If kind = "DIM" Or kind = "NOTE" Then
                If Len(ref) > 0 Then
                    Set target = KC35_ResolveRef(model, ref)
                    If Not target Is Nothing Then
                        For Each src In sources
                            Set srcAnn = src("Ann")
                            If KC39_SameObject(srcAnn, target) Then claimed(KC50_Key(kind, CStr(src("OldSheet")), CStr(src("OldView")), CStr(src("OldName")))) = row
                        Next src
                    End If
                ElseIf current.Exists(sh) Then
                    Set owner = KR_ExactOwner(sources, sh, vw, nm, kind, hits)
                    If hits = 1 Then
                        claimed(KC50_Key(kind, sh, vw, nm)) = row
                    ElseIf hits = 0 Then
                        pending.Add row
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next row
    If pending.count = 0 Then Exit Function
    ' 2. Текущие объекты читаются тем же способом, каким сохранялся список (текст, имя, вид).
    Set live = KC_CurrentMarkerObjects(Nothing)
    Set choice = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): Set votes = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set why = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each item In pending
        row = CLng(item)
        kind = CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2): sh = CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2)
        vw = CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2): nm = CStr(ws.cells(row, 5).Value2)
        txt = KC_CleanHashText(CStr(ws.cells(row, 6).Value2))
        Set found = Nothing: n = 0
        If kind = "NOTE" And Len(nm) > 0 Then
            For Each rec In live
                If CStr(rec("Kind")) = "NOTE" And CStr(rec("Name")) = nm Then n = n + 1: Set found = rec
            Next rec
            If n = 1 Then
                If claimed.Exists(KC50_Key("NOTE", CStr(found("Sheet")), CStr(found("View")), nm)) Then Set found = Nothing
            Else
                Set found = Nothing
            End If
            If Not found Is Nothing Then why(CStr(row)) = "имя заметки единственное в чертеже"
        ElseIf kind = "DIM" And Len(nm) > 0 Then
            For Each rec In live
                If CStr(rec("Kind")) = "DIM" And CStr(rec("Sheet")) = sh And CStr(rec("Name")) = nm And CStr(rec("View")) <> vw Then
                    If KC_CleanHashText(CStr(rec("Text"))) = txt Then
                        If Not claimed.Exists(KC50_Key("DIM", sh, CStr(rec("View")), nm)) Then n = n + 1: Set found = rec
                    End If
                End If
            Next rec
            If n <> 1 Then Set found = Nothing
            If Not found Is Nothing Then why(CStr(row)) = "тот же лист, имя и текст размера"
        End If
        ' Имя изменилось (например, вырезали и вставили): только взаимно однозначный текст.
        If found Is Nothing And Len(txt) > 0 Then
            n = 0
            For Each rec In live
                If CStr(rec("Kind")) = kind And (kind = "NOTE" Or CStr(rec("Sheet")) = sh) Then
                    If KC_CleanHashText(CStr(rec("Text"))) = txt Then
                        If Not claimed.Exists(KC50_Key(kind, CStr(rec("Sheet")), CStr(rec("View")), CStr(rec("Name")))) Then n = n + 1: Set found = rec
                    End If
                End If
            Next rec
            peers = 0
            For Each peer In pending
                If CStr(ws.cells(CLng(peer), 2).Value2) = kind And (kind = "NOTE" Or CStr(ws.cells(CLng(peer), 3).Value2) = sh) Then
                    If KC_CleanHashText(CStr(ws.cells(CLng(peer), 6).Value2)) = txt Then peers = peers + 1
                End If
            Next peer
            If n <> 1 Or peers <> 1 Then Set found = Nothing
            If Not found Is Nothing Then why(CStr(row)) = "имя изменилось; текст взаимно однозначен"
        End If
        If Not found Is Nothing Then
            k = KC50_Key(kind, CStr(found("Sheet")), CStr(found("View")), CStr(found("Name")))
            Set choice(CStr(row)) = found
            votes(k) = CLng(votes(k)) + 1
        End If
    Next item
    ' 3. Принимаем только пары «одна запись - один объект».
    For Each item In pending
        row = CLng(item)
        If choice.Exists(CStr(row)) Then
            Set found = choice(CStr(row))
            kind = CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2)
            k = KC50_Key(kind, CStr(found("Sheet")), CStr(found("View")), CStr(found("Name")))
            If CLng(votes(k)) = 1 Then
                Set owner = KR_ExactOwner(sources, CStr(found("Sheet")), CStr(found("View")), CStr(found("Name")), kind, hits)
                If hits = 1 Then
                    result.Add CStr(row), owner
                    KC47_Decision "AUTO_REBIND_MARKER", "строка=" & row & "; " & CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2) & " / " & _
                        CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2) & " / " & CStr(ws.cells(row, 5).Value2) & " -> " & CStr(found("Sheet")) & _
                        " / " & CStr(found("View")) & " / " & CStr(found("Name")) & "; основание: " & CStr(why(CStr(row))) & _
                        "; текст: " & KC_CleanHashText(CStr(ws.cells(row, 6).Value2)) & " | " & KC_CleanHashText(CStr(found("Text")))
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next item
End Function

' Карта: прежний ручной блок переносится на позицию другого вида только при взаимной однозначности
' (тот же лист, тип, текст, номинал, префикс). Иначе остаётся существующая остановка.
Private Function KC50_KindName(ByVal annType As Long) As String
    Select Case annType
        Case swSelDIMENSIONS: KC50_KindName = "Размер"
        Case swSelGTOLS: KC50_KindName = "Геом.допуск"
        Case swSelWELDSYMBOLS: KC50_KindName = "Сварка"
        Case swSelNOTES: KC50_KindName = "Заметка"
    End Select
End Function

Private Function KC50_SameParam(ByVal pr As Long, ByVal j As Long) As Boolean
    With gPreparedOldData(j)
        If Len(.sheetName) = 0 Then Exit Function
        If StrComp(.viewName, positionsData(pr).viewName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Exit Function
        KC50_SameParam = (.AnnotationType = KC50_KindName(positionsData(pr).AnnotationType) And _
            StrComp(.sheetName, positionsData(pr).sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 And _
            NormalizeForCompare(.AnnotationText) = NormalizeForCompare(positionsData(pr).AnnotationText) And _
            Abs(.NominalValue - positionsData(pr).NominalValue) < 0.000001 And _
            NormalizeForCompare(.DimPrefix) = NormalizeForCompare(positionsData(pr).DimPrefix))
    End With
End Function

Private Sub KC50_MatchMovedOldRows()
    Dim pr As Long, j As Long, other As Long, hits As Long, found As Long, back As Long
    If gPreparedOldCount <= 0 Or positionsCount <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    For pr = 1 To positionsCount
        If gPreparedMatchIdx(pr) = 0 Then
            hits = 0: found = 0
            For j = 1 To gPreparedOldCount
                If Not gPreparedOldData(j).matched Then
                    If KC50_SameParam(pr, j) Then hits = hits + 1: found = j
                End If
            Next j
            If hits = 1 Then
                back = 0
                For other = 1 To positionsCount
                    If gPreparedMatchIdx(other) = 0 Then
                        If KC50_SameParam(other, found) Then back = back + 1
                    End If
                Next other
                If back = 1 Then
                    gPreparedOldData(found).matched = True
                    gPreparedMatchIdx(pr) = found: gPreparedMatchResults(pr) = 1
                    KC47_Decision "AUTO_REBIND_CARD", "прежний п." & gPreparedOldData(found).posNumber & "; " & _
                        gPreparedOldData(found).sheetName & " / " & gPreparedOldData(found).viewName & " -> " & _
                        positionsData(pr).viewName & "; " & positionsData(pr).AnnotationText & "; ручные данные сохраняются"
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next pr
End Sub

Private Function KC50_ViewOK(ByVal index As Long, ByVal oldIndex As Long) As Boolean
    If StrComp(gPreparedOldData(oldIndex).viewName, positionsData(index).viewName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        KC50_ViewOK = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    KC50_ViewOK = (gPreparedMatchIdx(index) = oldIndex)
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then KC50_ViewOK = False
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

' В сохранённом тексте меняется только точное окончание « <старый вид>.»; прочий ручной текст не трогаем.
Private Function KC50_RenameViewInText(ByVal text As String, ByVal index As Long) As String
    Dim oldIndex As Long, oldView As String, newView As String
    KC50_RenameViewInText = text
    If Not gUsePreparedMerge Then Exit Function
    oldIndex = gPreparedMatchIdx(index)
    If oldIndex <= 0 Then Exit Function
    oldView = gPreparedOldData(oldIndex).viewName: newView = positionsData(index).viewName
    If Len(oldView) = 0 Or Len(newView) = 0 Then Exit Function
    If StrComp(oldView, newView, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Exit Function
    If Len(text) > Len(oldView) + 2 And Right$(text, Len(oldView) + 2) = " " & oldView & "." Then
        KC50_RenameViewInText = Left$(text, Len(text) - Len(oldView) - 1) & newView & "."
    Else
        KC46_Trace "VIEW_TEXT_KEPT | п." & positionsData(index).number & ": ручная формулировка не изменена; старый вид=" & oldView & "; новый=" & newView
    End If
End Function


' v16.5.11-review: сколько сохранённых правил указывают на существующие объекты без видимых маркеров.
' Только чтение. Ненайденные объекты не считаются (они могли быть удалены намеренно).
Private Function KC51_LostRules(ByVal book As Object, ByVal current As Collection, ByRef detail As String) As Long
    Dim saved As Collection, rec As Object, live As Object, found As Long, reason As String, n As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    detail = ""
    KC_ParseMarkerBook book, saved, True
    For Each rec In saved
        found = KC_FindCombinedObject(rec, current, reason)
        If found > 0 Then
            Set live = current(found)
            If Not CBool(live("Hash")) And Not CBool(live("At")) Then
                n = n + 1
                If n <= 8 Then detail = detail & vbCrLf & "  " & CStr(rec("Sheet")) & " / " & CStr(rec("View")) & " / " & CStr(rec("Name"))
            End If
        End If
    Next rec
    If n > 8 Then detail = detail & vbCrLf & "  ... и ещё " & (n - 8)
    KC51_LostRules = n
    Exit Function
Failed:
    ' Нечитаемый список не заменяем без явного предупреждения.
    detail = vbCrLf & "  Сохранённый список не прочитан: " & Err.Description
    KC51_LostRules = 1
End Function


' v16.5.12-review: по каждому несопоставленному прежнему блоку показывает ближайшую текущую позицию
' того же листа и типа и какие поля отличаются. Ничего не сопоставляет и не меняет.
Private Function KC52_MergeReport() As String
    Dim j As Long, pr As Long, best As Long, score As Long, bestScore As Long, kind As String
