    Dim diff As String, report As String, shortList As String, shown As Long
    Dim cText As Long, cView As Long, cNom As Long, cPref As Long, cNoSheet As Long, cAmb As Long, freePos As Long
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, path As String, folder As String, used As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For pr = 1 To positionsCount
        If gPreparedMatchIdx(pr) > 0 Then used(CStr(pr)) = True Else freePos = freePos + 1
    Next pr
    report = "KC v16.5.12 | Несопоставленные прежние блоки карты | " & Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss") & vbCrLf & _
        "Чертёж: " & swModel.GetPathName & vbCrLf & "Прежних строк: " & gPreparedOldCount & "; текущих позиций: " & positionsCount & _
        "; текущих без прежнего блока: " & freePos & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
    For j = 1 To gPreparedOldCount
        If Not gPreparedOldData(j).matched Then
            best = 0: bestScore = -1
            For pr = 1 To positionsCount
                kind = KC50_KindName(positionsData(pr).AnnotationType)
                If kind = gPreparedOldData(j).AnnotationType And _
                   StrComp(gPreparedOldData(j).sheetName, positionsData(pr).sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                    score = 0
                    If NormalizeForCompare(gPreparedOldData(j).AnnotationText) = NormalizeForCompare(positionsData(pr).AnnotationText) Then score = score + 8
                    If StrComp(gPreparedOldData(j).viewName, positionsData(pr).viewName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then score = score + 4
                    If Abs(gPreparedOldData(j).NominalValue - positionsData(pr).NominalValue) < 0.000001 Then score = score + 2
                    If NormalizeForCompare(gPreparedOldData(j).DimPrefix) = NormalizeForCompare(positionsData(pr).DimPrefix) Then score = score + 1
                    If score > bestScore Then bestScore = score: best = pr
                End If
            Next pr
            diff = ""
            If best = 0 Then
                diff = "на этом листе нет текущих позиций этого типа": cNoSheet = cNoSheet + 1
            ElseIf bestScore = 15 Then
                diff = "все поля совпадают с поз. " & positionsData(best).number & " (несколько одинаковых параметров или строка занята)": cAmb = cAmb + 1
            Else
                If (bestScore And 8) = 0 Then diff = diff & " текст[" & gPreparedOldData(j).AnnotationText & " | " & positionsData(best).AnnotationText & "]": cText = cText + 1
                If (bestScore And 4) = 0 Then diff = diff & " вид[" & gPreparedOldData(j).viewName & " | " & positionsData(best).viewName & "]": cView = cView + 1
                If (bestScore And 2) = 0 Then diff = diff & " номинал[" & gPreparedOldData(j).NominalValue & " | " & positionsData(best).NominalValue & "]": cNom = cNom + 1
                If (bestScore And 1) = 0 Then diff = diff & " префикс[" & gPreparedOldData(j).DimPrefix & " | " & positionsData(best).DimPrefix & "]": cPref = cPref + 1
                diff = "ближайшая поз. " & positionsData(best).number & ";" & diff
            End If
            report = report & "п." & gPreparedOldData(j).posNumber & " | " & gPreparedOldData(j).sheetName & " | " & _
                gPreparedOldData(j).viewName & " | " & gPreparedOldData(j).AnnotationType & " | " & _
                gPreparedOldData(j).AnnotationText & " -> " & diff & vbCrLf
            shown = shown + 1
            If shown <= 5 Then shortList = shortList & vbCrLf & "п." & gPreparedOldData(j).posNumber & " " & _
                gPreparedOldData(j).sheetName & "/" & gPreparedOldData(j).viewName & ": " & Left$(diff, 140)
        End If
    Next j
    report = report & vbCrLf & "Итого отличается: текст " & cText & ", вид " & cView & ", номинал " & cNom & _
        ", префикс " & cPref & ", нет позиций на листе " & cNoSheet & ", неоднозначно " & cAmb & vbCrLf
    KC53_Add "ОТЧЁТ сопоставления карты" & vbCrLf & report
    If KC53_Flush() Then path = KC53_JournalPath() Else path = "журнал в папке карты недоступен"
    KC52_MergeReport = "Расхождения: текст " & cText & ", вид " & cView & ", номинал " & cNom & ", префикс " & cPref & _
        ", нет позиций на листе " & cNoSheet & ", неоднозначно " & cAmb & "." & shortList & vbCrLf & "Полный отчёт: " & path
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC52_MergeReport = "Отчёт о расхождениях не составлен: " & Err.Description
End Function


' ============================================
' v16.5.14-review: журнал и повторное использование Excel.
' Один файл «<имя карты> - журнал макроса.txt» рядом с картой контроля.
' ============================================
Private Function KC53_JournalPath() As String
    Dim card As String, fso As Object, folder As String
    On Error GoTo NoPath
    card = gIntroConfigPath
    If Len(card) = 0 Then card = gKC37PreparedPath
    If Len(card) = 0 Then card = gKC53Card
    If Len(card) = 0 And Not gWorkBook Is Nothing Then card = CStr(gWorkBook.FullName)
    If Len(card) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = fso.GetParentFolderName(card)
    If Len(folder) = 0 Then Exit Function
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then Exit Function
    KC53_JournalPath = fso.BuildPath(folder, fso.GetBaseName(card) & " - журнал макроса.txt")
NoPath:
End Function

Private Sub KC53_Add(ByVal text As String)
    gKC53Buf = gKC53Buf & Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss") & " | " & text & vbCrLf
    If Len(gKC53Buf) > 32000 Then KC53_Flush
    ' Карта ещё не определена: буфер не растёт бесконечно.
    If Len(gKC53Buf) > 200000 Then gKC53Buf = Right$(gKC53Buf, 100000)
End Sub

Private Function KC53_Flush() As Boolean
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, path As String
    If Len(gKC53Buf) = 0 Then KC53_Flush = True: Exit Function
    On Error GoTo Failed
    path = KC53_JournalPath()
    If Len(path) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(path, 8, True, -1)
    stream.Write gKC53Buf
    stream.Close
    gKC53Buf = ""
    KC53_Flush = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
End Function

Private Sub KC53_Finish()
    On Error Resume Next
    KC53_Flush
    If Not gKC53SpareXl Is Nothing Then
        If gKC53SpareXl.Workbooks.Count = 0 Then gKC53SpareXl.Quit
        Set gKC53SpareXl = Nothing
    End If
    Err.Clear
End Sub

' Уже запущенный макросом пустой Excel (после чтения настроек) вместо запуска нового.
Private Function KC53_TakeExcel() As Object
    Dim spare As Object, ok As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not gKC53SpareXl Is Nothing Then
        Set spare = gKC53SpareXl: Set gKC53SpareXl = Nothing
        ok = (spare.Workbooks.Count = 0)
        If Err.Number <> 0 Then ok = False: Err.Clear
        If ok Then Set KC53_TakeExcel = spare Else spare.Quit
    End If
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
    If KC53_TakeExcel Is Nothing Then Set KC53_TakeExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
End Function

Private Sub KC53_KeepSpare(ByVal xl As Object)
    On Error Resume Next
    If gKC53SpareXl Is Nothing Then
        Set gKC53SpareXl = xl
    Else
        xl.Quit
    End If
    Err.Clear
End Sub

Private Function KC53_FileStamp(ByVal path As String) As String
    Dim fso As Object, f As Object
    On Error GoTo NoFile
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    Set f = fso.GetFile(path)
    KC53_FileStamp = CStr(f.DateLastModified) & "|" & CStr(f.Size)
NoFile:
End Function

' Настройки повторно не читаются в том же запуске, если файл не изменился.
Private Function KC53_SettingsCached(ByVal app As Object) As Boolean
    Dim fso As Object, candidate As String, stamp As String
    On Error GoTo NoCache
    If gKC28 Is Nothing Or Len(gKC53SettingsStamp) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    candidate = fso.BuildPath(fso.GetParentFolderName(CStr(app.GetCurrentMacroPathName)), "Настройки_ОК.xlsx")
    If StrComp(candidate, gKC28Path, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    stamp = KC53_FileStamp(candidate)
    KC53_SettingsCached = (Len(stamp) > 0 And stamp = gKC53SettingsStamp)
NoCache:
End Function

Private Sub KC53_PruneKeep(ByVal folder As String, ByVal prefix As String, ByVal keep As Long)
    Dim fso As Object, f As Object, names() As String, n As Long, i As Long, j As Long, t As String
    On Error GoTo Done
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    For Each f In fso.GetFolder(folder).Files
        If LCase$(Left$(f.Name, Len(prefix))) = LCase$(prefix) And LCase$(Right$(f.Name, 10)) = ".keep.xlsm" Then
            n = n + 1: ReDim Preserve names(1 To n): names(n) = f.Name
        End If
    Next f
    If n <= keep Then Exit Sub
    ' Имена содержат метку yyyymmdd_hhnnss: сортировка по имени = по времени.
    For i = 1 To n - 1
        For j = i + 1 To n
            If names(j) < names(i) Then t = names(i): names(i) = names(j): names(j) = t
        Next j
    Next i
    For i = 1 To n - keep
        fso.DeleteFile fso.BuildPath(folder, names(i)), False
        KC46_Trace "Удалена старая резервная копия: " & names(i)
    Next i
Done:
End Sub


' v16.5.15-review: набор листов для PDF — только листы с новыми баллонами, в порядке вкладок чертежа.
' Если нужны все листы, остаётся прежний экспорт всех листов.
Private Sub KC54_PdfSubset()
    Dim allNames As Variant, subset() As String, i As Long, n As Long, total As Long
    gKV_ExportNames = Empty
    If gKV_NeedSheets Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If gKV_NeedSheets.Count = 0 Then Exit Sub
    allNames = gKV_AllPDFNames
    total = UBound(allNames) - LBound(allNames) + 1
    ReDim subset(0 To total - 1)
    For i = LBound(allNames) To UBound(allNames)
        If gKV_NeedSheets.Exists(CStr(allNames(i))) Then subset(n) = CStr(allNames(i)): n = n + 1
    Next i
    If n = 0 Or n = total Then Exit Sub
    ReDim Preserve subset(0 To n - 1)
    gKV_ExportNames = subset
    gKV_AllPDFNames = subset
    gKV_Diagnostics = gKV_Diagnostics & "PDF SUBSET | листов " & n & " из " & total & vbCrLf
    KC46_Trace "PDF для расстановки: листов " & n & " из " & total
End Sub
